
### [1] SAPO: Single-Rollout Autoregressive Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning  (id=2608.19842, 2026-08-21, rl=47, score=6.6)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用
- ABS: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has become a critical stage in the post-training of large language models. Existing critic-free, group-relative methods estimate policy advantages from multiple rollouts, avoiding the substantial memory overhead of conventional proximal policy optimization (PPO) and achieving strong performance on long-horizon interactive tasks. Despite their success, recent studies revealed three limitations: (1) Lack explicit value generalization and effective temporal credit assignment; (2) Suffer from potential advantage collapse in long-horizon complex tasks; (3) Require a costly trade-off between sampling budget and policy performance. In this work, we propose Single-rollout Autoregressive Policy Optimization (SAPO), a low-memory and compute-efficient framework in which the policy and value functions share a single autoregressive backbone. SAPO exploits the autoregressive structure of LLMs to produce policy and value predictions at distinct causal boundaries with shared parameters, while independently optimizing the PPO objectives and auxiliary on-policy SARSA objectives. To robustly estimate the contribution of each turn, we further introduce a trajectory-level generalized advantage estimator that combines lambda-returns with batch normalization. Experiments across ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B show that SAPO trains stably and outperforms PPO and GRPO by mean +15.1 and +12.1 percentage points, respectively, while eliminating the memory cost of a separate critic model and reducing per-iteration runtime by 33.2% over PPO.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对智能体强化学习中的现有方法（如GRPO）在长期任务中缺乏价值泛化、优势估计崩溃以及采样成本高的问题，提出了SAPO框架。SAPO让策略和值函数共享单一自回归骨干，在不同因果边界分别输出策略和值预测，并联合优化PPO目标和在线SARSA目标。此外，引入轨迹级广义优势估计器，结合lambda-return和批归一化来稳健估计每轮贡献。在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B进行实验，SAPO训练稳定，平均性能比PPO和GRPO分别提升15.1和12.1个百分点，同时消除了独立评论家模型的内存开销，并比PPO减少33.2%的每轮运行时间。
- innovations_zh: 共享单自回归骨干，策略与值函数在不同因果边界输出，降低内存开销; 联合优化PPO和在线SARSA目标，实现显式价值泛化与时间信用分配; 提出轨迹级广义优势估计器，结合lambda-return和批归一化，避免优势崩溃
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在ALFWorld和WebShop两个环境上的结果，未涉及更广泛或更复杂的任务，泛化性有待验证。此外，性能提升基于特定模型规模（1.5B/7B），对更大模型或不同架构的适用性未知。优势估计器的设计可能引入额外超参数（如lambda和批归一化参数），其敏感性未在摘要中讨论。
- potential: 未来可探索SAPO在更多长期交互任务（如机器人控制、多轮对话）中的表现，并扩展到更大规模模型。其共享骨干和单次采样思想可能启发更高效的强化学习算法，尤其在资源受限场景。长期来看，该框架有望成为智能体后训练的标准工具，并推动在线学习与离线学习的融合。

### [2] Reward Shaping to Mitigate Reward Hacking in RLHF  (id=2502.18770, 2026-09-21, rl=46, score=7.2)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) is widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, RLHF remains vulnerable to \emph{reward hacking}, whereby a policy exploits imperfections in the reward function instead of learning the intended behavior, thereby undermining alignment. Although reward shaping can stabilize RLHF training and partially mitigate reward hacking, shaping methods and their underlying design principles have not been systematically investigated. To address this gap, we conduct a comprehensive study of prevalent reward-shaping techniques. Our analysis identifies two key design principles: (1) the reinforcement-learning reward should be bounded, and (2) it should grow rapidly at first and then gradually saturate. Motivated by these principles, we propose Preference as Reward (PAR), a novel method that uses the latent preferences encoded in the reward model as the reinforcement-learning signal. We further show that PAR possesses two variance-reduction properties that stabilize RLHF training and substantially widen the practical window for early stopping. Our evaluation consists of two parts. First, we compare PAR with several other reward-shaping strategies using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) as the reinforcement-learning algorithm and Gemma2-2B as the base model. Second, we compare PAR with the vanilla baseline (i.e., unshaped reward) across four base models and four reinforcement-learning algorithms. In the first set of experiments, PAR consistently outperforms other reward-shaping methods and also reflects h
- summary_zh: 该研究针对RLHF中奖励黑客问题，系统分析了现有奖励塑形技术，提炼出奖励应有界且先快速增长后逐渐饱和两条设计原则。基于此提出Preference as Reward（PAR）方法，将奖励模型中的潜在偏好作为强化学习信号。实验以PPO和Gemma2-2B比较多种奖励塑形策略，并在四种基座模型和四种强化学习算法上与原始基线对比，显示PAR优于其他塑形方法，且对actor-critic算法在价值估计不稳定时尤其有效。
- innovations_zh: 系统分析奖励塑形技术，提炼出有界和先快增后饱和两条设计原则; 提出PAR方法，将奖励模型中的潜在偏好作为强化学习信号; 证明PAR具有两个方差缩减性质，稳定训练并拓宽早停窗口
- limitations: 摘要未说明PAR在何种任务或偏好分布下可能失效，也未报告与人类偏好对齐的最终效果。实验虽覆盖四种基座模型和四种算法，但未提及模型规模、训练成本及与更大模型的可扩展性。此外，PAR依赖奖励模型中的潜在偏好，若奖励模型本身有偏，其缓解奖励黑客的效果可能受限。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究PAR在更大规模模型和更复杂任务中的表现，探索其与不同奖励模型训练方式的结合。还可从理论层面分析方差缩减性质与早停窗口的关系，并研究PAR在在线RLHF或迭代对齐中的长期价值。

### [3] Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake  (id=2609.02817, 2026-09-03, rl=44, score=6.6)
- cats: 推理与数学, 推理优化与效率
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出Cliff方法，利用现成LLM作为教师识别推理过程中的首个错误，将轨迹分解为正确前缀和错误后缀，并据此分配token级优势以提供更细粒度的奖励信号。在12个场景中，Cliff相比标准GRPO和在线蒸馏方法分别提升7%和15%，且对教师模型能力要求不高。
- innovations_zh: 首次利用首个错误位置分解推理轨迹，实现细粒度奖励塑形; 无需专门奖励模型或师生同构假设，仅需现成LLM教师; 将过程信号转化为token级优势，简单通用且显著提升RLVR性能
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体任务类型和评测细节，难以判断其在不同推理任务（如数学、代码）上的泛化边界。方法依赖教师识别首个错误的准确性，若教师能力不足或错误定位有误，可能影响奖励信号质量。此外，实验仅报告平均提升幅度，未分析方差或失败场景。
- potential: 未来可探索更鲁棒的首个错误识别机制，或结合自适应教师选择以进一步提升性能。该方法可作为通用奖励塑形框架，与多种RL算法结合，并可能扩展到多步推理、工具使用等更复杂场景。长期看，其细粒度监督思想有望推动LLM推理过程的可解释性和可控性研究。

### [4] PACT: From Credit Assignment to Critic Alignment  (id=2609.26355, 2026-09-23, rl=43, score=7.3)
- cats: 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning has become a central component of large language model (LLM) post-training, yet token-level credit lacks a generally accepted mathematical definition, leaving its relationship to commonly used training signals unclear. We formulate three regularity conditions, namely Completeness, Prefix Consistency, and Neutrality, and prove that they uniquely determine token-level credit. This characterization provides a unified basis for explaining phenomena across existing algorithms and guides the development of an improved actor-critic training procedure. Through this lens, an ideal teacher in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) acts as an implicit critic, yielding an expected policy gradient proportional to that induced by token-level credit. Response-level REINFORCE Leave-One-Out (RLOO) signals match the expected policy-gradient contribution of token-level credit despite their coarser granularity. We further establish approximate credit sparsity under bounded outcome rewards and show how intermediate critic errors in Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) can become comparable to the underlying credit. These motivate Policy Aligned Critic Training (PACT), which adopts an Actor-then-Critic update order to apply importance sampling correction to critic training and better align the critic with the updated policy. In agentic mathematical reasoning, PACT achieves 72.87% average accuracy across four benchmarks, outperforming GRPO and PPO by 8.80 and 13.16 percentage points, respectively. On SWE-bench Verified, PACT achieves a pass rate of 67.4%, outperforming PPO, GRPO, a
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型后训练中token级信用缺乏公认数学定义的问题，提出完整性、前缀一致性和中性三个正则条件，并证明它们唯一确定token级信用。基于此统一视角，论文解释了在线策略蒸馏中理想教师作为隐式评论家、RLOO信号与token级信用在期望策略梯度上的一致性，以及GAE中评论家误差的影响。据此提出PACT方法，采用先演员后评论家的更新顺序并对评论家训练施加重要性采样校正。在智能体数学推理四个基准上平均准确率达72.87%，分别超过GRPO和PPO 8.80和13.16个百分点；在SWE-bench Verified上通过率为67.4%，分别超过PPO、GRPO和SAO 2.4、2.0和3.8个百分点。
- innovations_zh: 提出三个正则条件并证明其唯一确定token级信用; 揭示OPD教师与RLOO信号同token级信用的梯度联系; 提出PACT：先演员后评论家并加重要性采样校正
- limitations: 摘要仅给出理论刻画与实验结果，未说明三个正则条件在何种假设下成立及是否覆盖所有实际场景。评测集中于数学推理和SWE-bench，尚不清楚在其他任务或模型规模上的泛化性。PACT相对基线带来的提升幅度与训练开销、超参数敏感性的关系也未在摘要中交代。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证三个正则条件在更广泛任务与模型上的适用性，探索PACT与不同评论家架构或离线数据的结合。该公理化视角有望启发更高效、更稳定的信用分配与评论家对齐算法，推动LLM强化学习后训练的理论与实践发展。

### [5] Back to the Definition: Estimating Step-Level Advantages via Trajectory Graphs for Agentic Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.28963, 2026-09-25, rl=40, score=7.3)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) methods, such as GRPO and its variants, have become a leading paradigm for training reasoning and agentic large language models (LLMs). While their group-normalized advantage estimation is reliable at the response level, it becomes systematically biased at the step level, since coarse-grained trajectory-level advantages are hard to accurately reflect the contribution of individual steps (i.e, failed trajectories may contain valuable steps). Revisiting the foundational RL definition, we notice that GRPO's success on single-turn tasks stems from its advantage estimation strategy, which adheres to the basic definition: the mean reward of multiple actions sampled from the same state constitutes a credible state-value estimate. Extending the faithful estimation to step-level would in principle demand sampling multiple actions from each intermediate state, which is too costly on a per-state basis. To mitigate this issue, we propose a Graph-based Faithful sTep-level credit-assignment framework (GRAFT) that grafts all rollout trajectories into a trajectory graph, recovering node state-values via Bellman iteration on the graph, and assigning credit to each edge by the node value difference. Theoretically, the estimated step-level advantage faithfully adheres to the basic advantage definition in RL. To further ensure the reliability of step-level advantage estimation, we further propose Graph GAE, which extends GAE to the trajectory graph for reducing the impact of state-value estimation bias. Experiments across a range of multi-turn agentic be
- summary_zh: 该论文指出，GRPO等基于组归一化的强化学习方法在响应级优势估计上可靠，但在步骤级存在系统性偏差，因为粗粒度的轨迹级优势难以准确反映单个步骤的贡献。为此，作者提出GRAFT框架，将多条轨迹拼接为轨迹图，通过图上的贝尔曼迭代恢复节点状态值，并利用节点值差为每条边分配信用，从而在理论上忠实遵循强化学习的基本优势定义。作者进一步提出Graph GAE，将GAE扩展到轨迹图以降低状态值估计偏差。在多轮智能体基准测试中，该方法相比GRPO和近期智能体强化学习算法取得了持续增益和更优性能。
- innovations_zh: 将多条轨迹构建为轨迹图，通过贝尔曼迭代恢复节点状态值; 基于节点值差为边分配信用，实现步骤级忠实优势估计; 提出Graph GAE，将GAE扩展到轨迹图以降低状态值估计偏差
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在多轮智能体基准上的实验增益，未说明轨迹图构建与贝尔曼迭代的计算开销及可扩展性。Graph GAE对状态值估计偏差的降低效果缺乏理论保证或消融分析。此外，方法在非智能体或单轮任务上的适用性未经验证，与GRPO等基线相比的统计显著性和超参数敏感性也未提及。
- potential: 未来可探索轨迹图构建的高效近似方法，以降低计算成本并扩展到更长轨迹或更大规模智能体任务。可进一步分析Graph GAE的理论收敛性与偏差-方差权衡，并研究该框架与不同策略优化算法的结合。长期看，忠实步骤级信用分配有望提升智能体在复杂多轮决策中的学习效率与可解释性。

### [6] HISPO: Hierarchical Importance-Sampling Policy Optimization with Entropy-Derived Segments  (id=2609.15471, 2026-09-15, rl=40, score=6.5)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a central approach for improving mathematical reasoning in language models, but long-form completions introduce a difficult credit-assignment problem: different parts of a solution trace may contribute unevenly to final correctness. Existing policyoptimization objectives for RLVR commonly apply importance-sampling correction at either the token level (GRPO, DAPO) or the sequence level (GSPO), imposing different granularities for assigning credit across a response. We introduce Hierarchical Importance-Sampling Policy Optimization (HISPO), a segment-level policy-optimization method that constructs rollout-time entropy-derived contiguous segments, assigns soft entropy-based saliency weights, and applies clipped importance-sampling correction at the segment granularity. This provides an intermediate correction unit between token-level GRPO/DAPO and sequence-level GSPO. We evaluate HISPO by fine-tuning Qwen3-1.7B-Base on mathematical reasoning tasks. Across six benchmarks, HISPO improves Pass@8 over the strongest baseline on all benchmarks and matches or exceeds the strongest baseline in Acc@8 on five of them. On AIME25, HISPO improves over GRPO by +3.75 Acc@8 and +3.78 Pass@8, and over GSPO by +2.50 Acc@8 and +1.27 Pass@8. These results suggest that segment-level correction is a promising granularity for RLVR in long-form mathematical reasoning.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对RLVR中长文本数学推理的信用分配难题，指出现有方法仅在token级或序列级进行重要性采样校正。作者提出HISPO，一种基于熵导出的连续片段、赋予软熵显著性权重并在片段粒度进行裁剪重要性采样校正的策略优化方法。在Qwen3-1.7B-Base上微调后，HISPO在六个数学推理基准上均提升Pass@8，并在五个基准上Acc@8匹配或超越最强基线。
- innovations_zh: 提出片段级策略优化，在token级与序列级之间引入中间校正单元; 利用rollout时熵导出连续片段，并赋予软熵显著性权重; 在片段粒度应用裁剪重要性采样校正，实现分层信用分配
- limitations: 摘要仅报告在Qwen3-1.7B-Base和数学推理任务上的结果，未说明方法在其他模型规模、非数学任务或非RLVR场景的泛化性。评测仅涉及六个基准的Pass@8与Acc@8，未提供训练稳定性、计算开销或与更多基线对比的细节。片段构造依赖rollout时熵，其鲁棒性与超参数敏感性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索片段级校正在不同模型规模与任务领域的适用性，研究熵导出片段构造的替代方案或自适应粒度。长期看，分层重要性采样思路可能推广到更广泛的RLVR与长文本生成场景，推动更精细的信用分配机制。

### [7] SLCA-GRPO: Resolving Cross-Segment Credit Misattribution in Tool-Calling RL  (id=2609.29050, 2026-09-25, rl=37, score=7.2)
- cats: 评测与基准, Agent 与工具使用
- ABS: Tool-calling agents produce heterogeneous outputs, interleaving structured tool invocations with user-facing natural language summaries. This output heterogeneity presents a structural failure mode in standard on-policy Reinforcement Learning (RL): algorithms like GRPO indiscriminately broadcast a homogeneous trajectory-level scalar advantage to all tokens. Consequently, gradient noise from summary generation leaks into tool-decision tokens, causing cross-segment credit misattribution and brittle optimization. In this work, we propose SLCA-GRPO, a framework incorporating Segment-Locked Credit Assignment (SLCA). To enable scalable exploration without costly real APIs and stable training, we first construct the Schema-Guided LLM Simulator (SGLS) as foundational training infrastructure. Building on this, SLCA decouples advantage estimation at the structural segment level within a single group of rollouts, without requiring additional rollouts from intermediate states. Supported by Hierarchical Rewards (HierR), SLCA routes execution advantages to tool tokens and preference advantages to summary tokens, eliminating advantage contamination (the dominant cross-segment credit misattribution channel) within each policy update. On a 7B backbone, SLCA-GRPO accelerates convergence and outperforms standard GRPO, ToolPO, and RLTR by +2.53 pp on in-domain evaluation, +1.36 pp on the Berkeley Function-Calling Leaderboard (BFCL), and +9.15 pp on $τ^2$-Bench under the same training budgets, achieving higher accuracy with reduced tool redundancy and costs.
- summary_zh: 工具调用智能体的输出混合了结构化工具调用与自然语言总结，标准GRPO将轨迹级标量优势广播给所有token，导致总结生成的梯度噪声污染工具决策token，产生跨段信用错配。作者提出SLCA-GRPO，通过Segment-Locked Credit Assignment在单组rollout内按结构段解耦优势估计，并构建Schema-Guided LLM Simulator支持可扩展探索。配合Hierarchical Rewards，将执行优势路由给工具token、偏好优势路由给总结token，消除优势污染。在7B模型上，该方法收敛更快，域内评测提升2.53个百分点，BFCL提升1.36个百分点，τ²-Bench提升9.15个百分点，同时降低工具冗余与成本。
- innovations_zh: 提出段锁定信用分配SLCA，在单组rollout内按结构段解耦优势估计; 构建Schema-Guided LLM Simulator，支持无需真实API的可扩展探索; 设计分层奖励HierR，将执行与偏好优势分别路由至工具与总结token; 消除优势污染，缓解工具调用RL中的跨段信用错配问题
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了7B骨干上的结果，未说明方法在更大或更小模型上的可扩展性。评测集中于域内、BFCL和τ²-Bench，未覆盖更广泛的任务类型或真实API环境。此外，SGLS作为模拟器可能引入与真实工具调用分布的偏差，摘要未讨论该偏差对最终性能的影响。
- potential: 未来可探索SLCA在更多异构输出结构（如多轮工具调用、多模态输出）中的泛化能力，并研究段级信用分配与其他RL算法的结合。长期看，若能在真实API或更复杂环境中验证并降低模拟器偏差，该方法有望成为工具调用智能体RL训练中信用分配的标准组件。

### [8] SignalReasoner: Assessing the Upper Bound of 3B Models for Signal Mathematical Reasoning  (id=2608.17301, 2026-08-19, rl=37, score=6.2)
- cats: 推理与数学, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准, 推理优化与效率
- ABS: Post-training with supervised chain-of-thought fine-tuning and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, their application to signal processing problems remains relatively under-explored. This report investigates reinforcement fine-tuning strategies for adapting Qwen2.5-3B-Base to graduate-level signal mathematical problems from WirelessMATHBench-XL, a comprehensive benchmark for mathematical reasoning in this domain. We examine two training paradigms: (i) direct reinforcement learning (RL) on WirelessMATHBench-XL with verifiable rewards; and (ii) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a distilled wireless-domain chain-of-thought corpus, followed by the same domain-specific RL stage. Across both paradigms, we benchmark Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), and Geometric-Mean Policy Optimization (GMPO). We aim to assess whether domain-aware CoT SFT serves as an effective initialization for subsequent RL, and whether GSPO or GMPO offer advantages in stability or accuracy over GRPO for signal reasoning tasks. Our best model achieves an overall accuracy of 39.12\%, representing a more than threefold improvement over the untrained Base model (12.37\%).
- summary_zh: 该研究针对无线信号处理领域的数学推理问题，探索了基于可验证奖励的强化学习微调策略，以适配Qwen2.5-3B-Base模型。研究比较了直接强化学习与先进行领域蒸馏的思维链监督微调再强化学习两种范式，并评估了GRPO、GSPO和GMPO三种策略。最佳模型在WirelessMATHBench-XL上达到39.12%的准确率，相比未训练基线的12.37%提升超过三倍。
- innovations_zh: 首次将强化微调应用于无线信号数学推理，填补领域空白; 系统比较三种策略，评估领域思维链监督微调作为强化学习初始化的有效性
- limitations: 摘要未提供模型在不同子任务或难度上的详细表现，泛化性未知。评测仅基于单一基准，且未与更大规模模型或非强化方法对比，结论的普适性有限。
- potential: 未来可探索将领域知识蒸馏与更高效的强化策略结合，进一步提升小模型上限。该研究为信号处理、通信等专业领域构建轻量级推理模型提供了基础，长期可推动边缘设备上的智能信号分析。

- ABS: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and other RL-style post-training paradigms have been used for aligning large language models (LLMs) with reasoning standards. The resulting recent Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) such as DeepSeek-R1, o3, and Kimi k1.5 show that such RL-style post-training ("RL-for-LLMs") can substantially improve chain-of-thought reasoning, long-horizon planning, and self-correction. However, the computational footprint of these systems is massive: state-of-the-art RLM training requires millions of GPU-hours and tightly coupled multi-model pipelines that stress modern hardware far beyond classical supervised LLM training. This makes RLM training as much a parallel and distributed systems problem as an algorithmic one. In this work, to facilitate developing RLMs that are simultaneously high-performance, scalable, and cost-effective, we first systematize the RL-for-LLM paradigm and provide a compute-centric analysis of prominent post-training algorithmic frameworks: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), as well as their variants. Second, we develop a taxonomy of intra- and inter-model parallelism strategies for RL-for-LLMs, covering both traditional techniques (data, tensor, pipeline, sequence, context, and expert parallelism) as well as novel forms of parallelism and optimization techniques for multi-model RLM training, for example disaggregated placement, stage fusion, hybrid parallelism, and asynchronous execution. We harness the work-depth model of parallel computing to make our taxonomy and it
- summary_zh: 该论文针对强化学习后训练范式（如RLVR）在推理语言模型（RLM）中带来的巨大计算开销问题，系统化了RL-for-LLM框架，并基于计算中心视角分析了PPO、GRPO等算法。作者提出了一个涵盖传统与新型并行策略的分类体系，利用工作-深度模型进行严格化分析，并总结了现有RLM框架的实践指南与开放研究方向。最终目标是促进RLM训练在性能、可扩展性和成本效益上的平衡。
- innovations_zh: 提出计算中心视角的RL-for-LLM算法分析框架; 构建涵盖传统与新型并行策略的RLM训练分类体系; 利用工作-深度模型使并行策略分析严格且可移植
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验验证或量化结果，分类体系的有效性缺乏实证支持。分析主要基于算法框架，可能未覆盖所有实际工程细节，且未讨论不同策略在真实硬件上的性能差异。
- potential: 未来可结合具体硬件基准测试验证分类体系的实用性，并探索异步执行、混合并行等新技术的优化空间。该工作为构建大规模RLM训练系统提供了理论框架，长期有望促进更高效、更经济的推理模型开发。

### [10] Teach the Magnitude, Not the Direction: Verifier-Bounded Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn Multi-step LLM Agents  (id=2608.13179, 2026-08-14, rl=36, score=6.6)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) offers a verifier-bounded performance ceiling for training multi-turn tool-use agents, yet its trajectory-level credit assignment conflates heterogeneous per-turn outcomes into a single reward signal. On-policy distillation provides dense per-token supervision but is either teacher-bounded or prone to gradient concentration collapse. We introduce $\textbf{CrEST}$, a hierarchical credit assignment framework that retains RL's verifier-bounded ceiling while incorporating dense token-level signals from a privileged self-teacher. $\textbf{CrEST}$ resolves credit at two levels: turn-segmented verified advantages address inter-turn dilution, while entropy-gated self-teacher modulation refines intra-turn token contributions. Experiments on BFCL V3 and WildToolBench show that $\textbf{CrEST}$ consistently outperforms both RL and distillation baselines across two model scales, with the largest gains on long-trajectory and strict session-level metrics. Our work demonstrates that the teacher's role in policy optimization can be reduced from determining update directions to modulating update magnitudes, unlocking dense credit assignment without sacrificing the verifier-bounded ceiling.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对多轮多步工具使用智能体在强化学习（RLVR）中轨迹级信用分配混淆不同轮次结果的问题，以及在线蒸馏方法受教师限制或梯度集中崩溃的缺陷，提出了分层信用分配框架CrEST。CrEST通过轮次分割的验证优势解决轮间稀释，并通过熵门控的自我教师调制细化轮内令牌贡献。在BFCL V3和WildToolBench上的实验表明，CrEST在两个模型规模上均优于RL和蒸馏基线，尤其在长轨迹和严格会话级指标上提升最大。
- innovations_zh: 提出分层信用分配框架，结合验证器奖励与密集令牌级信号，保留RL的验证器上限。; 轮次分割的验证优势解决跨轮信用稀释，熵门控自我教师调制细化轮内贡献。; 将教师角色从决定更新方向降为调节更新幅度，实现密集信用分配而不牺牲验证器上限。
- limitations: 摘要未提供实验细节，如基线配置、超参数敏感性或计算开销，难以评估其实际部署成本。评测仅基于两个基准，且未提及在更广泛任务或真实场景中的泛化性。未讨论熵门控阈值的选择依据及其对性能的敏感度。
- potential: 未来可探索将CrEST扩展到更复杂的多智能体协作或长时程规划任务，并研究自适应熵门控机制。长期价值在于推动信用分配理论的发展，可能为在线蒸馏与强化学习的融合提供新范式。

### [11] Debate Training Reduces Reward Hacking in RLAIF  (id=2608.17776, 2026-08-19, rl=35, score=6.4)
- cats: 安全与可靠性, Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学
- ABS: We demonstrate that RL finetuning an LLM using debate, a two-player adversarial game between a generator and a critic adjudicated by a weaker LLM judge, reduces reward hacking compared to a reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) baseline. Reward hacking is a central obstacle in RLAIF: as training progresses, the policy learns to exploit systematic errors in its AI judge, degrading task performance, a problem that worsens precisely when the judge is weaker than the policy, the setting most relevant to overseeing increasingly capable AI systems. We study mathematics tasks, where final-answer correctness is verifiable, allowing us to measure reward hacking dynamics. We train a Gemini~2.5 Flash-class policy with a frozen, weaker Gemini~2.5 Flash Lite judge, comparing a single-player RLAIF baseline against debate. While the baseline quickly hacks the judge, debate maintains judge performance throughout training, leading to a higher peak validation accuracy (45\% performance gap recovered) that persists through many RL steps. Additional experiments show that: 1) further weakening the judge leads to faster hacking, but this can be compensated by adding an additional debate round; 2) debate incentives override prompted misalignment; 3) RL using an LLM judge has a smaller train/validation reward gap than RL from verifiable rewards; 4) learning to critique to convince the judge using ground truth labels is possible but slow. Taken together, our results are a positive update on the feasibility of debate, while highlighting that balancing multi-agent training is critical: with
- summary_zh: 该研究针对RLAIF中奖励黑客问题，提出使用辩论训练方法，即生成器与评论者进行双人对抗游戏，并由较弱的LLM裁判裁决。在数学任务上，与单智能体RLAIF基线相比，辩论训练显著减少了奖励黑客现象，峰值验证准确率提升45%，且该优势在多个RL步骤中持续存在。实验还发现，进一步削弱裁判会加速黑客攻击，但增加辩论轮次可补偿；辩论激励能覆盖提示错位；基于LLM裁判的RL训练/验证奖励差距小于可验证奖励。
- innovations_zh: 提出辩论训练框架，通过双人对抗游戏减少RLAIF中的奖励黑客; 系统研究裁判强度、辩论轮次和批评字数限制对训练平衡的影响; 发现批评字数限制（150词内）能有效平衡游戏并避免裁判黑客
- limitations: 研究仅基于数学任务，其结论在其他领域（如开放式对话）的适用性未知。评测依赖可验证的最终答案，可能无法完全反映真实任务中的奖励黑客动态。此外，辩论训练的计算开销和实际部署成本未在摘要中提及。
- potential: 未来可探索辩论训练在更广泛任务和更强AI模型上的表现，并优化多智能体训练策略以减少对人工约束的依赖。长期来看，该方法可能成为监督超人类AI的关键技术，但需解决其可扩展性和稳定性问题。

### [12] Revisiting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards from a Contrastive Perspective  (id=2605.12969, 2026-09-19, rl=34, score=9.6)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is one of the most widely adopted RLVR algorithms for post-training large language models on reasoning tasks. We first show that GRPO admits an equivalent discriminative reformulation, in which policy optimization maximizes the expected score gap between verified positive and negative rollouts. This reformulation reveals two objective-level limitations: likelihood-misaligned surrogate scores, in which clipped ratio-based scores are optimized rather than the sequence likelihoods that govern generation, and score-insensitive credit assignment, in which rollout-level credit does not reflect the current score gaps between positive and negative rollouts. To address these limitations, we propose ConSPO, a Contrastive Sequence-level Policy Optimization method that uses length-normalized sequence log-probabilities as rollout scores and contrasts verified positive rollouts against negative distractors within the same group. ConSPO optimizes a group-wise InfoNCE-style objective to adaptively strengthen updates for poorly separated positives and high-scoring negatives, together with a curriculum-scheduled margin that preserves separation pressure as training progresses. Experiments across diverse settings show that ConSPO outperforms strong baselines on challenging reasoning benchmarks.
- summary_zh: 该论文重新审视了用于大语言模型推理任务后训练的GRPO算法，指出其可等价改写为判别式形式，即最大化正负rollout之间的期望分数差。作者据此揭示两个目标层面的局限：基于裁剪比值的代理分数与生成所依赖的序列似然不一致，以及rollout级信用分配对当前正负分数差不敏感。为此提出ConSPO，采用长度归一化序列对数概率作为rollout分数，在同组内对比验证正例与负例干扰项，并优化组级InfoNCE式目标，配合课程调度边界。实验表明ConSPO在多种设置下优于强基线。
- innovations_zh: 将GRPO等价改写为判别式形式，揭示其目标层面的两个局限; 提出ConSPO，用长度归一化序列对数概率作为rollout分数; 在同组内对比验证正例与负例干扰项，优化组级InfoNCE目标; 引入课程调度边界，随训练推进保持分离压力
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在挑战性推理基准上的实验，未说明具体基准、模型规模、训练成本及与基线的统计显著性，泛化性尚不明确。ConSPO引入组内对比、InfoNCE目标与课程调度边界，可能带来额外超参数和计算开销，摘要未讨论其敏感性或效率。此外，理论分析仅针对GRPO的等价改写，未证明ConSPO目标本身的收敛性或最优性。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究ConSPO在不同模型规模、任务类型和RLVR算法上的通用性，并分析其超参数敏感性与训练效率。可探索将序列级对比目标与过程级奖励或更细粒度信用分配结合，以提升长链推理的稳定性。长期看，该对比视角可能推动更原则化、可验证奖励的强化学习后训练算法设计。

### [13] Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization: Teacher in Prompts, Not Gradients  (id=2606.18216, 2026-09-24, rl=34, score=7.3)
- cats: 多模态, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Knowledge distillation transfers a teacher's competence to a small student but is brittle in the small-student regime: forcing the student to imitate logits from a much larger teacher hurts generalization on benchmark families beyond the training corpus. Reinforcement learning (RL) avoids logit imitation by training on the student's own rollouts. However, on questions where every rollout fails - yielding zero advantage and being silently discarded - injecting a stronger teacher's response into the policy gradient breaks the on-policy assumption and induces drift. We introduce Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization (ZPPO), inspired by Vygotsky's zone of proximal development. ZPPO keeps the teacher inside the prompt rather than the policy gradient. On hard questions, where the student's mean rollout accuracy is below half, ZPPO constructs two reformulated prompts. A Binary Candidate-included Question (BCQ) pairs one correct teacher response with one incorrect student response as anonymized candidates the student uses as references. A Negative Candidate-included Question (NCQ) aggregates the student's wrong rollouts into a single prompt to surface their shared failure modes. A prompt replay buffer recirculates each hard question until it either graduates - the student's mean rollout accuracy on it reaches half or more - or is FIFO-evicted under finite capacity, amplifying BCQ and NCQ inside the student's current zone of proximal development. We post-train Qwen3.5 students at four scales (0.8B-9B) as vision-language models with a 27B teacher and evaluate them on a 31-benchmark su
- summary_zh: 知识蒸馏在小学生模型场景下易因模仿大教师logits而损害泛化，强化学习虽避免logit模仿，但在全部rollout失败的问题上会因零优势丢弃样本，而注入教师响应又会破坏on-policy假设。为此作者提出ZPPO，将教师置于提示而非策略梯度中：对学生平均rollout正确率低于一半的难题构造BCQ与NCQ两种重述提示，并用提示回放缓冲区分流式循环难题直至其毕业或被FIFO淘汰。作者以27B教师对四个规模（0.8B-9B）的Qwen3.5学生进行视觉语言模型后训练，在31个基准（16 VLM、10 LLM、5 Video）上ZPPO优于离策略/在策略蒸馏与GRPO，且最小规模增益最大。
- innovations_zh: 将教师信号放入提示而非策略梯度，保持on-policy假设; 用BCQ配对正确教师与错误学生响应作为匿名候选参考; 用NCQ聚合学生错误rollout以暴露共同失败模式; 提示回放缓冲区按毕业或FIFO淘汰循环难题
- limitations: 摘要仅给出整体优于基线的结论，未披露各基准的具体指标、方差或统计显著性，效果稳健性难以判断。方法依赖学生平均rollout正确率是否低于一半这一阈值来判定难题，阈值敏感性及对教师质量与规模的依赖未作说明。评测集中于Qwen3.5系列与单一27B教师，跨模型家族与跨任务的泛化性仍不确定。
- potential: 未来可探索难题判定阈值的自适应设定、缓冲区容量与淘汰策略的优化，以及将该提示式教师介入扩展到更多模型家族与模态。若能与课程学习或自博弈结合，或可形成更通用的近端发展区训练框架，长期看有望降低小模型后训练对大规模教师logits蒸馏的依赖。

### [14] Gradients Know What Outcomes Don't: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning with Gradient-Aligned Rewards  (id=2609.03342, 2026-09-04, rl=34, score=6.5)
- cats: 推理与数学, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models, yet its binary outcome reward cannot distinguish among correct trajectories. Existing dense reward alternatives, from surface heuristics to process reward models, either ignore the expert solutions already present in training corpora or require expensive offline annotation. We propose Gradient-Aligned Reward (GAR), which operates in the policy's own gradient space: truncated backpropagation through the output projection layer extracts a compact gradient vector for each rollout, and cosine similarity with an expert-anchor gradient yields a dense, reasoning-aware reward with less than 9% wall-clock overhead. We prove that this cosine admits a multiplicative decomposition into prediction-error and activation-pattern factors, providing a concrete characterization of what the alignment signal measures. On Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, GAR consistently improves over GRPO and other baselines on competition-level math benchmarks and transfers to GPQA Diamond and MMLU-Pro without domain-specific data. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LQgdwind/GAR.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对RLVR中二元结果奖励无法区分正确轨迹的问题，提出梯度对齐奖励（GAR），通过在策略自身的梯度空间中计算每个rollout与专家锚定梯度的余弦相似度，生成密集且推理感知的奖励，且开销低于9%。理论证明该余弦可分解为预测误差和激活模式因子，并在Qwen3-4B/8B上显著提升竞赛级数学基准表现，且能迁移到GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro。
- innovations_zh: 利用策略自身梯度空间构建密集奖励，无需额外标注或外部奖励模型; 通过截断反向传播提取紧凑梯度向量，计算开销低于9%; 理论上揭示余弦相似度的乘法分解，明确对齐信号的含义
- limitations: 摘要未提供在更大模型或更多任务上的验证，且仅报告了数学和部分科学基准，可能缺乏对通用推理任务的全面评估。此外，梯度对齐信号对超参数（如截断层、梯度计算方式）的敏感性未在摘要中说明。
- potential: 未来可探索GAR与过程奖励模型的结合，或将其扩展到多模态推理和长链推理场景。其低开销特性也使其有望应用于大规模模型训练，并可能推动基于梯度信号的奖励设计成为RLVR的通用组件。

### [15] Uncovering and Mitigating Aggregation-Induced Reward Hacking in Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.00213, 2026-09-02, rl=34, score=6.4)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 推理与数学
- ABS: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning of large language models increasingly adopts multiple reward dimensions, including verifiable rules, task-specific evaluators, and learned reward models, to provide richer supervision across diverse capabilities. These dimensions are commonly scalarized with fixed aggregation weights. We identify a failure mode in which aggregation itself induces reward hacking: static projection aliases qualitatively different reward profiles into a single scalar, steering optimization toward whichever dimensions are easiest, densest, or systematically favored by the reward signal. Over training, this traps the policy in suboptimal profiles and prevents convergence to better-balanced ones that would yield higher task performance. To address this, we propose Adaptive Multi-Reward Projection (AMRP), a lightweight online method that reallocates aggregation weights using three signals, relative shortfall, reward volatility, and recent progress, increasing pressure on lagging, unstable, or stagnant dimensions while relieving saturated ones. Across structured reasoning, citation-grounded generation, and open-ended alignment under GRPO, AMRP consistently improves reward-profile balance and downstream performance over fixed and dynamic weighting baselines; it also remains effective with GDPO and PPO, supporting compatibility across RL algorithms. Our code is available at https://github.com/yyhappier/AMRP.git.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对多奖励强化学习中固定聚合权重导致的奖励黑客问题，提出静态投影会使优化偏向易学或密集的奖励维度，陷入次优策略。作者提出自适应多奖励投影方法（AMRP），利用相对短缺、奖励波动性和近期进展三个信号动态调整聚合权重。实验表明，在GRPO、GDPO和PPO等多种算法下，AMRP能改善奖励分布平衡和下游性能，优于固定及动态加权基线。
- innovations_zh: 首次系统揭示多奖励聚合本身引发的奖励黑客现象; 提出基于三信号的轻量级在线权重重分配方法AMRP; 在多种RL算法和任务上验证了AMRP的通用有效性
- limitations: 摘要未提供AMRP在极端奖励冲突或奖励维度数量很大时的表现，适用范围可能有限。评测主要基于论文选定的任务和基线，缺乏对真实复杂场景中奖励信号噪声或不可靠性的讨论。此外，方法依赖三个启发式信号，其理论最优性未证明。
- potential: 未来可探索AMRP与更复杂奖励建模（如学习奖励权重）的结合，或扩展到多任务、多智能体场景。长期看，该工作可能推动自适应奖励聚合策略的标准化，并为理解奖励黑客的机制提供新线索，值得进一步理论分析和实证验证。

### [16] DE-Venus: A Data-Efficient RLVR Framework for Large Language Models  (id=2609.03324, 2026-09-04, rl=33, score=6.8)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves large language model reasoning, but its practical scaling is constrained by expensive on-policy rollouts and the cost of obtaining reliable targets at scale. Existing methods address sample selection, incomplete supervision, or noisy labels separately, often entangling supervision logic with distributed training and hindering controlled comparison and reuse. We present DE-Venus, a unified framework for data-efficient RLVR that treats supervision as evolving state across data preparation and policy optimization. It organizes this lifecycle into three modules: Active Data Selection allocates training and annotation budgets; Weak Supervision Construction derives learning signals from unlabeled examples; and Training-Time Supervision Refinement filters or corrects unreliable supervision. DE-Venus supports seven representative methods and a data-selection pipeline by expressing method-specific decisions as offline dataset transitions or online transformations of targets, rewards, batches, and advantages while preserving verl's distributed execution contracts. Across public benchmarks and three business scenarios, separate configurations preserve or improve model quality with only 10% of labels or as little as 13% of relevant data; selected business configurations also reduce observed convergence steps by 63%--75%. DE-Venus thus reduces annotation and training costs without sacrificing scalable RL execution.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出DE-Venus，一个面向大语言模型的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）的统一数据高效框架，将监督视为在数据准备和策略优化中演化的状态，并分为主动数据选择、弱监督构建和训练时监督精炼三个模块。该框架支持七种代表性方法和数据选择流水线，同时保持与verl分布式执行契约的兼容。在公共基准和三个业务场景中，仅用10%的标签或13%的相关数据即可保持或提升模型质量，并减少63%-75%的收敛步数。
- innovations_zh: 将RLVR中的监督视为跨数据准备和策略优化的演化状态，提出统一生命周期框架; 将方法特定决策表达为离线数据集转换或在线目标/奖励/批次/优势变换，兼容verl分布式执行; 支持七种代表性方法及数据选择流水线，实现模块化组合与受控比较
- limitations: 摘要未提供与现有方法在相同预算下的详细性能对比，也未说明框架在超大规模模型或复杂任务上的泛化性；业务场景的具体类型和评测指标未明确，可能影响结论的普适性；收敛步数的减少可能受具体配置影响，需更多独立验证。
- potential: 未来可扩展更多监督演化策略，如自适应预算分配或动态噪声校正；框架的模块化设计便于与新的RL算法或分布式后端集成，有望推动RLVR在更广泛任务和更大模型上的数据高效应用；长期价值在于建立标准化监督生命周期，促进可复现研究和跨场景迁移。

### [17] Anchoring What Matters: A Dual-Level Learning Framework for Visually-Grounded Multimodal Reasoning  (id=2609.18057, 2026-09-17, rl=33, score=6.5)
- cats: 推理与数学, 多模态, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capabilities of large vision-language models (LVLMs). However, standard on-policy RLVR algorithms face a critical optimization bottleneck in preserving and reinforcing visually grounded reasoning behaviors: valuable visually-grounded reasoning trajectories are discarded after a single update, while uniform token advantage allocation prevents the model from reinforcing critical perception or reasoning steps. To bridge this gap, we propose PIVOT, a dual-level learning framework that anchors policy optimization around informative visual reasoning signals. Specifically, PIVOT introduces a self-calibrated experience replay mechanism, which selectively collects and replays visually-grounded historical experiences as stable reference anchors for policy optimization. Building upon this, we further design a vision-guided advantage allocation mechanism to allocate additional vision-aware advantages to tokens based on their local visual support and impact on downstream reasoning. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that PIVOT achieves highly competitive performance in enhancing the multimodal reasoning capabilities of LVLMs.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注标准同策略RLVR算法在大型视觉语言模型推理中的优化瓶颈，即视觉接地的推理轨迹在单次更新后被丢弃，且统一的token优势分配无法强化关键感知或推理步骤。为此，作者提出PIVOT双层次学习框架，通过自校准经验回放机制选择性收集并重放视觉接地的历史经验作为稳定参考锚点，并设计视觉引导的优势分配机制，根据token的局部视觉支持和下游推理影响分配额外视觉感知优势。在多个基准上的实验表明，PIVOT在增强LVLM多模态推理能力方面取得了极具竞争力的性能。
- innovations_zh: 提出自校准经验回放机制，选择性重放视觉接地历史经验作为稳定参考锚点; 设计视觉引导优势分配机制，按局部视觉支持与推理影响分配视觉感知优势; 构建PIVOT双层次学习框架，锚定信息性视觉推理信号优化策略
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在多个基准上取得极具竞争力的性能，未给出具体指标、基准名称或消融实验细节，难以判断各机制的实际贡献。经验回放与优势分配引入额外计算和存储开销，其可扩展性与在不同LVLM架构上的通用性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证PIVOT在更广泛多模态任务和不同规模LVLM上的泛化能力，并探索经验回放策略与优势分配权重的自适应优化。长期看，该双层次锚定思路有望推广至其他需要保留关键推理轨迹的强化学习场景。

- ABS: Training embodied foundation models typically requires massive-scale datasets and extensive computational resources, yet often suffers from three critical limitations: (1) inefficient sample utilization due to low-informative samples; (2) imbalanced gradient contributions across heterogeneous tasks; and (3) severe credit assignment problem in long-horizon planning, where trajectory-level rewards indiscriminately penalize all tokens. To address these issues, we propose an efficient training paradigm that achieves state-of-the-art average performance through strategic data selection and hierarchical policy optimization. Our approach consists of three synergistic stages. First, Rejection Sampling-based Fine-Tuning (RSFT) filters out low-informative samples to establish robust behavioral priors while preventing distributional collapse. Second, Iterative Rejection GRPO (IR-GRPO) employs task-specific queues stratified by difficulty to keep datasets balanced across reinforcement learning iterations, coupled with a hybrid reward mechanism for precise cross-task feedback. Third, to enhance long-horizon task planning, we introduce Trie-GRPO, a novel reinforcement learning algorithm based on action prefix trees, which enables step-level advantage estimation. This resolves the credit assignment problem by isolating intermediate correct decisions from downstream errors, while effectively balancing exploration efficiency and depth compared to conventional search trees. As a result, EmbodiedMind achieves a state-of-the-art average performance of 70.02% across 18 benchmarks, and significa
- summary_zh: 该论文针对具身基础模型训练中样本利用率低、异构任务梯度贡献不平衡以及长时程规划中信用分配困难三大问题，提出了一种高效训练范式。方法包含三个阶段：基于拒绝采样的微调（RSFT）过滤低信息样本以建立行为先验，迭代拒绝GRPO（IR-GRPO）通过按难度分层的任务队列和混合奖励机制平衡跨任务反馈，以及基于动作前缀树的Trie-GRPO实现步级优势估计以解决信用分配问题。最终在18个基准上取得70.02%的平均性能，并在长时程任务规划准确率上显著优于其他具身基础模型。
- innovations_zh: 提出RSFT过滤低信息样本，建立稳健行为先验并防止分布崩溃; 设计IR-GRPO，用难度分层队列和混合奖励平衡跨任务梯度贡献; 提出Trie-GRPO，基于动作前缀树实现步级优势估计，解决信用分配
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了平均性能和长时程规划准确率，未提供各基准的详细结果、消融实验或计算成本对比。方法依赖拒绝采样和分层队列等设计，其在不同任务分布和规模下的泛化性尚不明确。此外，项目尚未发布，可复现性有待验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将前缀树强化学习扩展到更复杂的多模态决策和动态环境，并研究各阶段组件的通用性与可迁移性。若项目开源，有望促进具身智能训练范式的标准化和进一步优化。

- cats: 多模态, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: HopChain has shown on still images that multi-hop data synthesis improves vision-language reasoning, because long chain-of-thought reasoning exposes errors that compound across steps, while most data used for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) rarely demands a chain of visual evidence, so these weaknesses are likely to stay unexposed. We observe the same problem in video, where this framework has not yet been explored. We therefore build Video-HopChain, a dataset of 22,550 multi-hop video questions over 13,378 videos, together with a held-out benchmark of 1,000 questions. Each question chains three to six yes/no questions about moments in one video, and each yields one of two integers depending on its answer. The final answer is the sum of these integers, so an exact match on that sum gives the verifiable reward that RLVR needs. We first train Qwen3-VL-8B with GRPO on a standard video dataset, and a second stage on Video-HopChain then raises the mean over eight video understanding and reasoning benchmarks from 55.4 to 57.9 and improves every one of them. Training on such a dataset, however, exposes a known limitation of GRPO: its learning signal comes from the reward variance within a group, so hard questions whose rollouts are all incorrect and easy questions whose rollouts are all correct both leave the group with no gradient. To recover these groups at the same compute budget, we introduce Confidence-Gated Exploration (CGE). With 8 rollouts per question, CGE samples the first 4 as usual. If these 4 are either all correct or all incorrect, it samples th
- summary_zh: 该研究将图像领域的HopChain多跳数据合成思路扩展到视频推理，构建了包含22,550个多跳视频问题（覆盖13,378个视频）的Video-HopChain数据集及1,000题留出基准，每题串联三到六个关于同一视频片段的yes/no子问题，最终答案由子问题对应整数求和给出，从而为RLVR提供可验证奖励。作者先用GRPO在标准视频数据集上训练Qwen3-VL-8B，再用Video-HopChain进行第二阶段训练，使八个视频理解与推理基准的平均分从55.4提升至57.9且各项均有改善。针对GRPO在组内奖励方差为零时无梯度的问题，提出Confidence-Gated Exploration（CGE），在每题8次采样中若前4次全对或全错，则对后4次在推理区间内遮蔽策略最自信的token并排除其损失，最终将平均分进一步提升至59.3。
- innovations_zh: 构建22,550题多跳视频问答数据集及1,000题基准，以子问题整数求和提供可验证奖励; 将HopChain多跳数据合成框架从静态图像扩展至视频推理场景; 提出CGE，对全对或全错组遮蔽最自信token以恢复GRPO梯度信号
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在Qwen3-VL-8B单一模型和八个基准上的结果，方法的通用性尚不明确；CGE依赖“最自信token”这一启发式遮蔽策略，其在不同模型与任务上的稳定性未经验证。此外，多跳问题被限定为yes/no子问题加整数求和的固定形式，可能限制推理类型的多样性与答案空间的开放性。
- potential: 未来可探索将多跳可验证数据构造与CGE推广到更多视频模型、更大规模及开放式答案任务，并研究除最自信token外的其他梯度恢复策略。长期看，若多跳视频数据与置信门控探索能结合更丰富的推理结构，或可提升视频模型在复杂时序与因果推理上的可靠性。

### [20] BASIS: Batchwise Advantage Estimation from Single-Rollout Information Sharing for LLM Reasoning  (id=2605.27293, 2026-09-16, rl=32, score=6.6)
- cats: 推理与数学
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a standard recipe for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. Existing algorithms face a tradeoff between computational efficiency and sample efficiency in value estimation and policy learning. We introduce BASIS, a critic-free post-training algorithm designed to address this tradeoff. At each online training step, BASIS samples only one rollout per prompt, but leverages rich information across prompts in the entire batch to improve value function estimation. Our experiments demonstrate that BASIS reduces MSE in value function estimation by 69% compared to REINFORCE++, a representative single-rollout baseline, and achieves lower MSE with one rollout than group mean estimators with 8 rollouts. This improvement in value estimation translates to better policy optimization: using substantially less training time, BASIS achieves performance close to multi-rollout GRPO-type baselines and often outperforms single-rollout REINFORCE-type baselines.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对带可验证奖励的强化学习在提升大语言模型推理能力时，价值估计与策略学习中计算效率和样本效率难以兼顾的问题，提出了无需评论家的后训练算法BASIS。BASIS在每个在线训练步骤中仅对每个提示采样一次 rollout，但利用整个批次内跨提示的丰富信息来改进价值函数估计。实验表明，BASIS相比单 rollout 基线REINFORCE++将价值函数估计的MSE降低69%，且单 rollout 的MSE低于8次 rollout 的组均值估计器；在显著减少训练时间的情况下，其策略优化性能接近多 rollout 的GRPO类基线，并常优于单 rollout 的REINFORCE类基线。
- innovations_zh: 仅需每个提示单次 rollout，降低采样计算成本; 利用批次内跨提示信息共享改进价值估计; 无需评论家网络，简化训练流程; 以更低MSE实现接近多 rollout 基线的策略性能
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了MSE和相对性能，未说明具体任务、模型规模、评测基准及统计显著性，泛化性尚不明确。单 rollout 设定下跨提示信息共享的假设是否普遍成立、是否引入偏差，以及长期训练稳定性，均需更多证据。
- potential: 未来可探索将批次内信息共享扩展到更多样化的推理任务与模型规模，并研究其与多 rollout 方法的结合或自适应切换。若验证充分，该方法有望成为低资源条件下大语言模型推理后训练的实用方案，并推动更高效的价值估计研究。

### [21] How to Compress KV Cache in RL Post-Training? Shadow Mask Distillation for Memory-Efficient Alignment  (id=2605.06850, 2026-09-17, rl=32, score=6.1)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 微调与对齐, 推理与数学, 安全与可靠性, RAG 与知识增强
- ABS: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for unlocking the advanced reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), encompassing frameworks like RLHF and RLAIF. Regardless of the specific optimization algorithm (e.g., PPO, GRPO, or Online DPO), online RL inherently requires an exploratory trajectory generation (rollout) phase. However, for long-context reasoning tasks, this rollout phase imposes a severe ``memory wall'' due to the exorbitant Key-Value (KV) cache footprint. While applying KV cache compression during rollouts mitigates this memory overhead, it induces a critical off-policy bias. Although modern KV compression is often nearly lossless during standard inference, even minuscule approximation errors are drastically amplified by the inherent instability of RL optimization. Specifically, the sampler generates responses under a sparse context, whereas the learner updates parameters using the full, dense context. Existing statistical solutions, such as importance reweighting, struggle to correct this magnified bias, suffering from high gradient variance and severe sample inefficiency.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注强化学习后训练（如RLHF、RLAIF）中长上下文推理任务面临的KV缓存内存瓶颈问题。在线RL的rollout阶段因KV缓存占用过高形成“内存墙”，而现有KV压缩方法虽在标准推理中近乎无损，却会在RL优化中引入被放大的off-policy偏差，导致采样器使用稀疏上下文而学习器使用完整上下文。论文提出Shadow Mask Distillation方法，旨在实现内存高效的对齐训练，但摘要未给出具体实验设置与量化结果。
- innovations_zh: 指出KV压缩在RL rollout中引发被放大的off-policy偏差问题; 提出Shadow Mask Distillation以缓解稀疏采样与稠密学习间的偏差; 针对长上下文RL后训练的内存墙问题给出压缩对齐思路
- limitations: 摘要仅提出方法名称与问题动机，未提供Shadow Mask Distillation的具体算法细节、理论保证或实验验证。其有效性、可扩展性及在不同RL算法（PPO、GRPO、Online DPO）下的通用性尚不明确。此外，摘要未说明与现有KV压缩或重要性重加权方法的定量对比。
- potential: 未来可围绕Shadow Mask Distillation的算法设计、收敛性与偏差校正机制展开理论与实证研究。可探索其与不同RL优化器、长上下文长度及内存预算的适配性，并评估在真实对齐任务中的吞吐与效果权衡。若验证有效，该方法有望推动内存高效RL后训练的实用化。
