- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves language-model reasoning, but how these gains relate to inference-time decoding and search remains unclear. Does RL create reasoning the base model lacks, or shift the rollout distribution toward trajectories it can already reach but rarely samples? We study this behaviorally with a Unified Decoding Framework (UDF), which expresses token-level sampling, beam-like search, tree search, and sequence-level resampling as executable policies over a shared budgeted operating space, scored post hoc with pass@$k$, self-consistency, best-of-$N$, and first-finish success. Using paired Base/RL checkpoints from SimpleRL-Zoo, we ask whether an RL default-policy curve can be approximated by a structured path of Base operating points. On Math500, AIME, GPQA, and IFEval, the pass@$k$ recovery path follows a Budgeted Operating-Point Transition Rule (BOPTR), $N_{\mathrm{Base}} \approx αN_{\mathrm{RL}}^β$, with benchmark-conditioned exponents. On Qwen2.5-7B, BOPTR gives the lowest transfer error among the non-oracle rules we test, 3.41 pp (95% CI [2.32, 5.53]); a three-seed replication gives 3.07 $\pm$ 0.39 pp. The rule extends to ten models across four families (3.28 to 4.87 pp on checkpoints added after fitting), to four benchmarks it was never fitted on (5.03 pp vs. 4.44 pp in fit), and holds without an RL checkpoint for the target model (4.19 pp) or without RL supervision of any kind (5.08 pp). These results support a qualified internalized-search reading: under the recipe we test, much of the measured RL gain corresponds to a 
- summary_zh: 该研究通过统一解码框架（UDF）和预算操作点转换规则（BOPTR），探究了RLVR提升语言模型推理能力与推理时搜索之间的关系。基于SimpleRL-Zoo的配对检查点，在多个基准上发现RL的pass@k曲线可由基础模型的搜索路径近似，且遵循幂律规则。结果表明，RL的大部分增益可解释为采样效率向基础模型可达到的操作点转移，支持了内部化搜索的解释。
- innovations_zh: 提出统一解码框架，将多种解码策略统一为预算操作空间中的可执行策略; 发现预算操作点转换规则，量化基础模型与RL模型在pass@k上的关系; 通过跨模型、跨基准验证，支持RL增益源于采样效率变化的观点
- limitations: 研究仅基于特定配方（SimpleRL-Zoo）和有限模型/基准，结论的普适性受限。摘要未提供参数级证据，且主要依赖行为指标，无法完全排除其他解释。此外，BOPTR的拟合误差和置信区间表明规则存在一定不确定性。
- potential: 未来可探索BOPTR在不同训练配方、模型规模和任务类型中的适用性，并进一步结合内部机制分析验证内部化搜索假说。该框架有望发展为推理时资源分配的自适应策略，并推动对RL泛化能力的理论理解。

### [23] Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR  (id=2609.04108, 2026-09-04, rl=31, score=7.6)
- cats: 推理与数学, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to interfere.To provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.
- summary_zh: 该论文研究可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）与在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在推理大模型后训练中的结合方式。作者提出简单的两阶段方案（先OPD后RL），并在逻辑与数学推理基准上证明其优于纯OPD、纯RLVR及多种联合优化方法。通过pass@k、学习动态和参数更新分析，揭示OPD扩展覆盖而RL精炼支持，联合优化则相互干扰。最终给出实用建议：以OPD验证分数作为切换信号，且OPD比SFT更适合作为RL的冷启动。
- innovations_zh: 提出OPD-then-RL两阶段训练范式，超越复杂联合优化方法; 系统解释两阶段优势：OPD扩展覆盖，RL精炼支持，联合干扰; 发现OPD验证分数是切换阶段的关键信号，OPD优于SFT冷启动
- limitations: 摘要仅基于逻辑与数学推理基准，未涉及其他任务（如代码或对话）的泛化性。分析主要依赖pass@k等指标，对模型规模、数据分布等变量未提及，结论可能受限于特定设置。
- potential: 未来可探索更细粒度切换策略（如动态阈值）或自适应阶段长度。该两阶段框架或可推广至其他蒸馏与RL变体，长期价值在于为后训练流程设计提供理论指导与实用配方。

### [24] RTPO: Reverse-Turn Policy Optimization for Stabilizing Agentic RL Training  (id=2608.18682, 2026-08-20, rl=31, score=6.5)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Training multi-turn agentic workflows with reinforcement learning (RL) enables large language models to perform complex reasoning, use external tools, and conduct iterative search beyond single-turn settings. Yet multi-turn RL training remains highly unstable, often causing severe performance degradation as the number of turns increases. Through theoretical analysis, we identify three tightly coupled sources of instability: rollout-training context mismatch, weak turn-level credit assignment under sparse terminal rewards, and asynchronous policy drift when short and long trajectories are optimized under different policy versions. We show that these issues share a common structural origin in flattened trajectory optimization and address them through a unified reverse-turn formulation. We propose Reverse-Turn Policy Optimization (RTPO), which organizes multi-turn rollouts as sparse reverse trees and performs turn-level policy updates in temporal reverse order, aligning each decision with its downstream continuation. RTPO enables causally consistent turn-level credit assignment and on-policy continuation to control asynchronous drift. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that RTPO eliminates context mismatch and asynchronous drift under the proposed turn-level formulation, reduces credit bias, and converges to recursive optimality. Experiments on multi-turn agentic RL benchmarks show that RTPO improves upon trajectory- and turn-level baselines by 21.50% and 10.76%, respectively, highlighting its potential to support more stable training for tool-using agents.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对多轮智能体强化学习训练不稳定的问题，通过理论分析识别出三个紧密耦合的不稳定来源： rollout-训练上下文不匹配、稀疏终端奖励下的弱回合级信用分配，以及长短轨迹在不同策略版本下优化导致的异步策略漂移。作者提出反向回合策略优化（RTPO）方法，将多轮rollout组织为稀疏反向树，并按时间逆序进行回合级策略更新，以对齐每个决策与其后续延续。实验表明，RTPO在轨迹级和回合级基线上分别提升了21.50%和10.76%，验证了其稳定性优势。
- innovations_zh: 提出统一的反向回合公式，从结构上解决上下文不匹配、信用分配和异步漂移三个问题; 设计稀疏反向树与时间逆序回合级更新，实现因果一致的信用分配和在线延续控制
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体任务类型、模型规模或计算开销的细节，泛化性未知；实验仅基于基准测试，未讨论真实复杂环境中的表现；理论保证基于特定假设，实际应用中的偏差可能影响效果。
- potential: 未来可探索将RTPO扩展到更广泛的智能体架构（如多智能体协作）和更复杂的工具使用场景；其反向更新思想可能启发其他序列决策问题的稳定化方法，长期价值在于推动RL训练在动态环境中的可靠应用。

### [25] Reinforcement Learning with Decomposed Subtasks  (id=2609.27035, 2026-09-24, rl=30, score=6.8)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, RAG 与知识增强, 评测与基准
- ABS: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related policy-gradient methods for training language model agents collapse an entire multi-turn rollout into a single scalar trajectory reward before it enters the policy update. When the task composes distinct skills, especially under sparse and delayed environmental feedback, this collapsing is lossy: the optimizer must implicitly infer which competency drove the outcome and how that should change behavior. We argue the right primitive is not a better scalar but a decomposition: trajectory reward should be split along subtasks before it enters the policy update. We introduce Reinforcement Learning with Decomposed Subtasks (RLDS), whose core is Subtask-Decomposed Advantage Estimation (SDAE): a replacement for the scalar GRPO advantage that splits trajectory reward into per-subtask shares on a fixed taxonomy, computes a group-relative advantage per subtask, and distributes per-token credit by weighting each subtask's advantage by its importance, concentrating it around the step where a reflection marks that subtask's execution as consequential. We evaluate on four agentic benchmarks: FrozenLake (sparse grid navigation), HotpotQA (multi-hop QA, one retrieval tool), ScienceWorld (long-horizon embodied science), and DeepResearch (long-form research, four tools, composite rubric reward). Heterogeneity diagnostics emitted during training show where decomposition pays off - gains scale with subtask heterogeneity, largest on the high-heterogeneity tasks ScienceWorld (+11.5 points, paired-bootstrap 95% CI [+9.8, +13.3]) and FrozenLake 
- summary_zh: 该研究指出GRPO等策略梯度方法将多轮轨迹压缩为单一标量奖励，在任务由多种技能构成且反馈稀疏延迟时会造成信息损失。为此提出RLDS，其核心SDAE按固定分类法将轨迹奖励拆分为子任务份额，计算子任务级组相对优势，并按重要性将优势分配到标记该子任务执行的关键反思步骤附近。在四个智能体基准上评测，分解带来的增益随子任务异质性增大，ScienceWorld提升11.5分、FrozenLake提升9.8分，而HotpotQA和DeepResearch上增益在噪声范围内，ScienceWorld上每步墙钟时间还减少10.9%。
- innovations_zh: 提出按固定分类法将轨迹奖励拆分为子任务份额，替代单一标量优势; 设计子任务级组相对优势估计，并按重要性加权分配逐token信用; 利用反思步骤定位子任务执行关键位置，集中信用分配; 训练中输出异质性诊断，预测分解在哪些任务上有效
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了四个基准上的结果，且增益高度依赖子任务异质性，在HotpotQA和DeepResearch上未显著优于标量GRPO，说明方法适用范围有限。分解依赖固定分类法和反思标记，其标注成本、分类法迁移性及对反思质量的敏感性尚未在摘要中说明。
- potential: 未来可探索自动或自适应的子任务分类法构建，减少对固定分类法和人工反思标记的依赖。还可将子任务分解优势估计推广到更多异质性任务与多工具长程场景，并研究其与不同策略优化算法的结合，长期有望提升智能体在稀疏延迟奖励下的训练效率与可解释性。

### [26] EPIG-Tree: Compute-Optimal Branching for Gradient-Efficient Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.20004, 2026-09-18, rl=30, score=6.4)
- cats: 其他
- ABS: Reward-based reinforcement learning for language models, exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), collapses an entire stochastic trajectory into a single scalar reward. This is clean and scalable, but it explores and allocates reward inefficiently: a trajectory may contain many causal decisions, recovery attempts, and environment-randomness events, yet every token or action inherits one trajectory-level advantage. We study tree-based rollout construction as a compute-allocation problem for policy-gradient estimation. Our central claim is that branches should be placed not where the policy is merely uncertain, but where an additional branch most reduces uncertainty about the policy gradient per unit of compute. From a law-of-total-variance decomposition of the local policy-gradient random variable, we derive two allocation laws: new branches reduce decision uncertainty, while repeated suffix rollouts reduce continuation uncertainty. The resulting EPIG-Tree score allocates branches using the already computed rollouts. It estimates occupancy- and score-weighted value uncertainty, along with a suffix law $n_e \propto w_e \|\nabla_θ\log π(a_e|h_e)\| σ_e / \sqrt{c_e}$. Empirically, EPIG reduces gradient MSE in cloned-state control, winning in all nine dense continuous-control environments of a 13-environment sweep and recovering the reference gradient direction near-perfectly, and it improves frozen-LLM gradient calibration relative to entropy branching. In online single-turn math, tree-local credit beats flat GRPO, while branch placement is secondary to token-le
- summary_zh: 该论文将树状展开构造视为策略梯度估计中的计算分配问题，针对GRPO等基于奖励的强化学习将整条轨迹压缩为单一标量奖励、导致奖励探索与分配低效的问题，提出分支应放在每单位计算最能降低策略梯度不确定性的位置。作者基于局部策略梯度随机变量的全方差分解推导出两条分配律，并据此提出EPIG-Tree评分，利用已计算展开估计占用与得分加权的价值不确定性及后缀律。实验显示EPIG在克隆状态控制中降低梯度MSE并在13个环境中的9个稠密连续控制环境胜出，改善冻结LLM梯度校准，在在线多轮Wordle中取得最高最终胜率0.850，但在在线单轮数学中分支放置次于token级信用分配。
- innovations_zh: 将树状展开构造建模为策略梯度估计的计算分配问题; 基于全方差分解推导决策与延续两类不确定性分配律; 提出利用已计算展开的EPIG-Tree评分与后缀律; 在控制、LLM校准与多轮Wordle中验证梯度估计优势
- limitations: 摘要显示方法依赖局部策略梯度的全方差分解与已计算展开，其理论假设和实际近似误差未在摘要中说明。实验虽覆盖多类任务，但在线单轮数学中分支放置效果次于token级信用分配，说明其优势并非在所有场景中主导。此外，摘要未报告计算开销、超参数敏感性及与更多基线的大规模对比，外部有效性仍有限。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究EPIG-Tree与token级信用分配的结合，以在单轮与多轮任务中同时优化信用分配和分支放置。还可探索将该计算分配视角扩展到更大规模语言模型训练、更多有状态环境及不同策略梯度估计器，并分析其长期计算效率与理论保证。

### [27] FLARE: A Full-Lifecycle Dense Supervision Paradigm for Long-Horizon Coding Agents via Generative Reward Model  (id=2609.23808, 2026-09-23-补充, rl=29, score=7.2)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 推理优化与效率, 领域应用
- ABS: While test-time scaling enhances Large Language Model (LLM) agents in long-horizon software engineering (SWE), sparse binary rewards (Pass/Fail) create a severe credit assignment crisis and waste failed exploratory trajectories. Current trajectory optimization and scaling methods are costly and structurally limited, relying on heuristic state reuse without causal diagnosis or delayed scalar scoring without actionable online guidance. We propose FLARE (Full-Lifecycle Alignment and Reward Engine), a novel dense supervision paradigm driven by a lightweight Generative Reward Model (GRM). First, RADAR, an offline causal-aware diagnostic framework, extracts high-fidelity, hindsight-free supervision through causal-chain backtracking to distill a GRM providing real-time, step-level risk feedback. Second, FLARE uses this GRM to continuously optimize the agent across its entire lifecycle. During inference, FLARE acts as an Active Scaffold, autonomously intercepting high-risk generation steps for localized breakpoint re-execution, drastically reducing compute overhead. During post-training, the GRM's structured signals serve as process-supervised reranking scores for Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and step-level dense rewards for Reinforcement Learning (RL), mitigating policy collapse in sparse environments. Extensive evaluations show that FLARE establishes a new Pareto frontier across the agent lifecycle: FLARE (N=1) outperforms Global Rollout (N=5) with a 5x reduction in token consumption. Extending FLARE to training overcomes the sparse reward problem in long-horizon interactive task
- summary_zh: 针对长时程软件工程智能体中稀疏二值奖励导致的信用分配危机与失败轨迹浪费问题，本文提出FLARE全生命周期密集监督范式，其核心是轻量级生成式奖励模型（GRM）。离线阶段RADAR通过因果链回溯提取无后见之明的高保真监督信号以蒸馏GRM，在线阶段FLARE将GRM用作主动脚手架拦截高风险步骤进行局部重执行，训练阶段则用其结构化信号做SFT过程重排序与RL步级密集奖励。实验显示FLARE（N=1）以五分之一token消耗超越全局回放（N=5），SFT相对提升19.13%，RL稳定提升9.19%。
- innovations_zh: RADAR离线因果链回溯，提取无后见之明的高保真步级监督以蒸馏GRM; 推理时GRM作为主动脚手架，拦截高风险步骤做局部断点重执行以省算力; 训练时GRM结构化信号用于SFT过程重排序与RL步级密集奖励，缓解策略崩溃
- limitations: 摘要仅给出相对提升百分比，未说明基准模型、任务集合、评测规模与统计显著性，结论的泛化性难以判断。RADAR依赖因果链回溯，其在高噪声或因果结构不清晰任务中的可靠性未在摘要中讨论。此外，GRM本身可能引入偏差或奖励黑客风险，摘要未涉及相关分析。
- potential: 未来可探索GRM在更多长时程领域（如多智能体协作、工具链编排）的迁移，并研究因果诊断与在线反馈的联合优化。若能公开评测协议与失败案例分析，将有助于验证密集监督的鲁棒性并推动该范式成为长时程智能体训练的标准组件。

### [28] Information-Time Proximal Policy Optimization  (id=2609.24380, 2026-09-23-补充, rl=29, score=7.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: RLVR has substantially improved the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. However, existing methods typically parameterize temporal progression in the Markov Decision Process by token-by-token generation, despite the highly non-uniform information flow along autoregressive trajectories. In this paper, we propose InfoPPO, which reparameterizes temporal progression using information density rather than raw token count. This reparameterization induces a common state-dependent structure for both temporal credit propagation and policy updates. InfoPPO restores the effectiveness of non-trivial discounting in long-horizon reasoning, retaining effective-horizon contraction while avoiding excessive attenuation of terminal supervision over long token sequences. Moreover, the information-time policy-improvement analysis naturally leads to a state-dependent update constraint, which we implement through adaptive clipping. By adapting the clipping threshold at each token position to the information density of its corresponding state, this mechanism enables more targeted policy updates while preserving proximal control. Theoretically, we extend performance-difference and policy-improvement analyses to the information-time MDP, deriving a policy-improvement lower bound when policy changes are regulated by information density. We further connect the general information-time analysis to practical LLM policy optimization by relating state-wise information density to local policy movement, while also providing theoretical grounding for the adaptive update mechanism. Experiments on Qwen3 models demon
- summary_zh: 该论文针对RLVR提升大语言模型推理能力时，现有方法以逐token生成来参数化MDP时间推进、忽略了自回归轨迹中信息流高度不均匀的问题，提出InfoPPO。InfoPPO用信息密度而非原始token数重新参数化时间推进，使时间信用传播与策略更新共享状态依赖结构，并通过自适应裁剪实现状态依赖的更新约束。理论方面将性能差与策略改进分析扩展到信息时间MDP，实验在Qwen3模型和五个竞赛风格数学推理基准上取得稳定增益，并在非平凡折扣设置下保持稳定准确率与响应长度。
- innovations_zh: 以信息密度替代token数重新参数化MDP时间推进; 统一时间信用传播与策略更新的状态依赖结构; 基于信息密度的自适应裁剪实现状态依赖更新约束; 将性能差与策略改进分析扩展到信息时间MDP
- limitations: 摘要仅报告在Qwen3模型与五个数学推理基准上的结果，尚不清楚对其他模型、任务或模态的泛化性。信息密度的具体估计方式与计算开销未在摘要中说明，理论分析到实际LLM优化的连接也仅作概要性描述。
- potential: 未来可探索信息密度估计的稳健性与高效实现，并将信息时间参数化推广到更广泛的推理与智能体任务。若能与过程奖励、搜索或更长程规划结合，可能进一步提升LLM在复杂推理中的训练稳定性与上限。

### [29] Beyond Pairwise Feedback: Listwise Vision-Language Supervision for Preference-Based Reward Learning  (id=2608.25350, 2026-08-27, rl=29, score=6.9)
- cats: 多模态, Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as a powerful source of supervision for reinforcement learning, enabling agents to leverage rich semantic knowledge during training. Inspired by the success of preference-based reward learning (PbRL) in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), vision-language model generated image-based preferences provide an effective source for learning reward functions. This can be done by visually comparing two outcomes through the Bradley-Terry (BT) model. However, this pairwise formulation utilizes only two observations at a time, despite VLMs being capable of ranking multiple candidates. The Plackett-Luce (PL) formulation can shape a reward model with listwise rankings as opposed to pairwise preferences, allowing for a more suited use of a VLM based ranking. In this work, to our knowledge, we introduce the first framework that combines VLM-generated preferences with the Plackett-Luce model for reward learning. We evaluate our approach on Meta-World manipulation tasks and show that Plackett-Luce (PL) reward models can train robotic policies from VLM-generated rankings as effectively as pairwise Bradley-Terry, $K$-wise Bradley-Terry, and RL-VLM-F baselines. Across all environments, at least one PL ranking size ($K \in \{3,4,5\}$) consistently performs with or outperforms other methods in mean success rate. Unlike pairwise methods, which are restricted to $K=2$, PL supports different ranking sizes and can therefore be adapted to the environment and desired feedback format. Our best PL configuration achieves an 86% mean final success r
- summary_zh: 该研究提出首个将VLM生成的列表式偏好与Plackett-Luce模型结合用于奖励学习的框架，以替代传统的成对Bradley-Terry方法。在Meta-World操作任务上，PL奖励模型在平均成功率上与成对BT、K-wise BT及RL-VLM-F基线相当或更优，最佳配置达到86%的平均最终成功率，并在Drawer Open任务上匹配Oracle基线。结果表明，列表式VLM偏好监督是奖励学习的竞争性且灵活的方法。
- innovations_zh: 首次将VLM生成的列表式偏好与Plackett-Luce模型结合用于奖励学习; 支持不同排名大小K（3,4,5），可适应环境与反馈格式，优于固定K=2的成对方法
- limitations: 摘要仅基于Meta-World仿真任务，未涉及真实机器人或更复杂环境，泛化性未知。评测仅报告平均成功率，缺乏方差、统计显著性及不同K值选择策略的详细分析。此外，VLM生成排名的质量与可靠性未单独评估，可能影响奖励学习效果。
- potential: 未来可探索自适应选择排名大小K的策略，以及将列表式偏好应用于更广泛的多模态决策任务。长期来看，该方法有望替代成对偏好成为VLM监督奖励学习的标准范式，并可与更先进的VLM结合提升奖励质量。

### [30] PAC: Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning of LLMs  (id=2608.30528, 2026-09-01, rl=28, score=8.4)
- cats: 推理与数学
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) is used to improve the reasoning abilities of LLMs, while training data span heterogeneous tasks. However, most RL post-training pipelines rely on fixed or manually designed task mixtures, even though task usefulness changes as training progresses. Online curriculum methods often define learnability by update magnitude, ignoring whether the update translates into reward gains, which can misallocate rollout budget toward tasks with large but ineffective updates. We propose PAC, a Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for multi-task RL of LLMs that combines two task-level signals: advantage-derived learnability, which measures the magnitude of the policy update a task can induce, and recent reward gains, which show whether those updates have improved task performance. A Bayesian Thompson Sampling controller uses these signals to allocate rollouts across tasks during GRPO training. We evaluate PAC under two settings: a multi-level reasoning setting and a multi-domain reasoning setting. PAC improves sample efficiency and final performance: it reaches comparable validation scores with fewer rollout steps and achieves higher final averages than random sampling and advantage-based curriculum baselines in both settings. These results show that jointly tracking advantage signals and actual reward gains yields an effective online curriculum for LLM post-training.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型多任务强化学习后训练中固定任务混合和在线课程方法忽略奖励增益的问题，提出了一种进度增强优势课程（PAC）方法。PAC结合优势推导的可学习性和近期奖励增益，通过贝叶斯汤普森采样在GRPO训练中动态分配任务样本。在多层次和多领域推理设置下，PAC相比随机采样和基于优势的课程基线，提高了样本效率和最终性能。
- innovations_zh: 结合优势信号与奖励增益，更准确评估任务可学习性; 使用贝叶斯汤普森采样动态分配任务样本，提升课程效率
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体任务数量和类型细节，适用范围可能有限。评测仅基于验证分数和最终平均值，未涉及泛化性或计算开销。方法依赖奖励增益的稳定性，在奖励噪声较大时可能受影响。
- potential: 未来可探索将PAC扩展到更多任务类型和更大规模模型，结合更细粒度的奖励信号或离线评估。长期看，该方法有望成为自适应RL训练的标准组件，推动更高效的LLM后训练范式。

### [31] When Does Predictor-Based RL Align with Human Perception? A Study of Subjective Rewards in Codec-Based Speech Language Models  (id=2608.31035, 2026-09-01, rl=28, score=7.9)
- cats: 多模态, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Codec-based text-to-speech (TTS) models make language-model post-training applicable to speech generation, but it remains unclear when learned perceptual predictors can serve as reinforcement learning rewards without losing alignment with human listeners. We study this question with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using learned rewards for anime-like speaking style, naturalness, likability, and arousal. To prevent perceptual rewards from being optimized through transcript drift, we introduce a character error rate (CER) zone constraint and compare policy optimization with Best-of-$N$ reranking under the same reward gate. Across single-reward runs, each reward primarily improves its own target metric, showing that subjective predictors are not interchangeable quality surrogates. Multi-rater A/B tests further show uneven human transfer, while a reward-gap analysis separates average transfer from within-axis calibration: signed reward gaps significantly predict listener choices in the pooled analysis, whereas residual CER gaps do not, but per-axis calibration remains heterogeneous. Best-of-8 is a strong human-level baseline and is not clearly worse than GRPO perceptually, suggesting that GRPO should be viewed as amortizing reward-selected behavior into the policy rather than uniformly outperforming reranking. These results support analyzing subjective speech rewards as predictor-axis-base tuples and provide practical diagnostics for selecting rewards before multi-reward speech post-training.
- summary_zh: 本研究探讨基于编解码器的语音语言模型中，学习到的感知预测器何时能作为强化学习奖励并与人类感知对齐。通过GRPO优化动漫风格、自然度等主观奖励，并引入CER约束防止转录漂移，与Best-of-N重排序对比。结果显示各奖励主要提升自身目标，人类迁移不均，GRPO并非全面优于重排序，但可摊销奖励选择行为。
- innovations_zh: 引入CER区域约束防止感知奖励优化时转录漂移; 提出奖励差距分析分离平均迁移与轴内校准; 将主观奖励视为预测器-轴-基元组进行系统分析
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体数据集规模与评测细节，且仅基于单一编解码器模型，结论泛化性有限。多评分者A/B测试的异质性表明校准不均，但未深入解释原因。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更多语音属性与多奖励联合优化，探索自适应奖励加权或动态校准方法。长期价值在于建立主观奖励与人类感知对齐的理论框架，推动语音语言模型在交互式场景中的可控生成。

### [32] Rubric Dropout: A Simple Way to Mitigate Reward Hacking in Rubric-as-Reward RL  (id=2608.11669, 2026-08-13, rl=28, score=7.4)
- cats: 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性, 领域应用
- ABS: Reinforcement learning against rubrics, lists of criteria graded by an LLM judge, has become a standard way to post-train language models on tasks with no deterministic answer. The rubric, however, is a fixed proxy for quality, never a complete description of it, and a policy trained against it long enough will learn to exploit the difference. We measure this directly. Training Qwen3-8B with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on medical and science rubrics and grading out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks with both the training judge and a stronger gold judge, we find that the two scores diverge during training. The training judge's score keeps climbing while the gold judge's score peaks and then falls, by 3 points on HealthBench-Hard and by 22 points on ResearchQA. A judge with a fixed bias would shift the gold curve by a constant, not send it down while the training score rises, so the divergence is reward hacking, not judge noise. We propose Rubric Dropout, a one-line fix borrowed from neuron dropout. At every step, we randomly drop a subset of the rubric's criteria before computing the reward, so the policy never optimizes the same rubric twice. The dropped subset is shared across each rollout group, so GRPO's group-relative advantages stay comparable, and evaluation always uses the full rubric. Comparing no dropout against dropout at 30% and 50% on both benchmark pairs, dropout raises the OOD gold score at every matched checkpoint (+1 to +2 points on HealthBench-Hard, +6 to +7 points on ResearchQA), lowers the two hacking measures we track, and costs nothing in d
- summary_zh: 该论文针对基于评分标准的强化学习中奖励黑客问题，提出Rubric Dropout方法，即每步随机丢弃部分评分标准以计算奖励。实验表明，该方法在医疗和科学基准上提升了OOD金标分数，降低了奖励黑客指标，且不损害领域内性能。
- innovations_zh: 提出Rubric Dropout，随机丢弃评分标准子集，防止策略过度优化固定标准; 通过训练评判与金标评判分数发散直接量化奖励黑客现象
- limitations: 摘要仅基于两个基准对和Qwen3-8B模型，结论的普适性有限；未分析不同丢弃比例对训练稳定性的影响；未与更复杂的对抗性方法比较。
- potential: 未来可探索自适应丢弃策略或结合其他正则化方法，并验证在更大模型和更多任务上的效果；长期看，该方法可能成为标准RL训练中的默认组件。

### [33] PlanPO: Group Planning-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic LLMs  (id=2608.17289, 2026-08-19, rl=28, score=7.0)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Group-relative policy optimization has emerged as a key paradigm for training agentic large language models (LLMs) on multi-turn interactive tasks. However, most existing variants fail to distinguish advantages among successful trajectories even when these trajectories differ substantially in their interaction efficiency. For instance, circuitous successes are often assigned the identical outcome reward, causing advantage collapse and severe performance bottlenecks. To this end, we propose Group Planning-aware Policy Optimization (PlanPO), a simple yet effective RL method for learning generalizable planning abilities beyond task-specific high-quality behavior patterns. Specifically, PlanPO introduces coarse-to-fine advantage signals, which capture the relative differences in trajectory-level lengths and turn-level response lengths conditioned on successful trajectories sampled for the same task. Within the group-relative optimization structure, this enables agents to actively learn generalizable and deliberate behaviors spanning interaction planning and textual generation from high-quality rollouts, without degenerating into vanilla length minimization. Experimentally, PlanPO improves over GRPO by 27.2\% on average across the challenging multi-turn benchmarks ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, outperforming recent powerful baselines while incurring negligible additional training cost.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对多轮智能体大语言模型训练中，现有组相对策略优化方法无法区分成功轨迹间效率差异、导致优势坍缩和性能瓶颈的问题，提出了PlanPO方法。PlanPO通过引入从粗到细的优势信号，同时考虑轨迹长度和回合内响应长度，在组相对优化框架下鼓励智能体学习通用且审慎的规划行为。实验表明，PlanPO在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld等基准上平均比GRPO提升27.2%，且训练成本几乎不变。
- innovations_zh: 提出粗到细的优势信号，区分成功轨迹的交互效率差异; 在组相对优化中同时建模轨迹级和回合级长度，避免退化为简单长度最小化
- limitations: 摘要未提供在更多任务或真实场景下的泛化验证，且仅基于三个基准，可能不足以全面评估方法的普适性。此外，优势信号依赖长度作为效率指标，可能忽略其他质量维度，如正确性或用户满意度。
- potential: 未来可探索更丰富的优势信号（如结合任务完成质量或用户反馈），并扩展到更多样化的多轮交互任务。该方法也有望与更复杂的规划策略结合，进一步提升智能体的长期决策能力。

### [34] GCPO: Diagnosing and Constraining Subspace Geometry in Rollout RL for LLMs  (id=2608.11674, 2026-08-13, rl=28, score=6.9)
- cats: 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, 预训练与架构, 领域应用
- ABS: On-policy rollout methods such as GRPO are central to post-training of large language models, yet they frequently suffer from training instabilities, cross-task capability degradation, and response-length inflation. Although prior work has characterized the subspace geometry of aggregate updates, the stepwise variation of this geometry and its relationship to model performance remain unclear. We introduce Principal-Subspace Overlap, a dimension-corrected measure of individual rollout updates relative to the dominant singular subspaces of pretrained weights. Despite low average overlap, transient spikes often precede performance degradation. To address this, we propose GCPO (Geometrically Constrained Policy Optimization), which applies hard bilateral orthogonal projections to constrain updates to the complementary subspaces, preventing such excursions by construction. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and tool-use tasks on Qwen3-8B and GLM4-9B, GCPO consistently outperforms GRPO and recent variants, including DAPO and GSPO, improving over the base models and the strongest baseline by up to 27.69 and 2.37 points, respectively. Furthermore, GCPO preserves general capabilities, eliminates response-length inflation, and stabilizes policy entropy. Our findings provide a new diagnostic lens and a principled design perspective for stable reinforcement learning post-training.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型在策略梯度强化学习微调中出现的训练不稳定、跨任务能力下降和响应长度膨胀问题，提出了一种新的诊断指标——主子空间重叠度，用于衡量每次更新与预训练权重主奇异子空间的关系。基于该指标，作者设计了GCPO方法，通过硬性双侧正交投影将更新约束在互补子空间中，从而避免性能退化。在Qwen3-8B和GLM4-9B上的数学推理、代码生成和工具使用任务中，GCPO一致优于GRPO及其变体，最高提升27.69分，同时保持通用能力并消除响应长度膨胀。
- innovations_zh: 提出主子空间重叠度指标，用于诊断逐次更新与预训练权重的几何关系; 设计GCPO方法，通过硬性正交投影约束更新子空间，从构造上防止性能退化
- limitations: 摘要未提供详细的实验设置和消融分析，难以评估各组件贡献。评测任务和模型规模有限，可能无法完全反映在更大模型或更广泛任务上的表现。此外，硬性正交投影可能限制探索空间，其长期影响尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索自适应约束强度或动态调整投影方向，以平衡探索与稳定性。该诊断指标可推广到其他强化学习算法和模型架构，用于实时监控训练健康度。长期看，该方法有望成为大模型后训练的标准组件，促进更可靠、可解释的强化学习微调流程。

### [35] DEEPO: Dual-Entropy Enhanced Policy Optimization for Hallucination in MLLMs  (id=2609.28570, 2026-09-25, rl=28, score=6.7)
- cats: 安全与可靠性, 推理与数学, 多模态, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) is widely used to sharpen reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet its effect on hallucination is uneven. We trace this to two weak points in the \emph{correction chain} from reward to parameter update. At the rollout level, hard queries---those with high semantic entropy---frequently produce unanimously wrong sample groups, collapsing the group-relative advantage to zero exactly where hallucination risk is highest. At the optimization level, confident-but-wrong tokens are gradient-invisible: a categorical policy's expected score-gradient norm vanishes as its distribution sharpens, so the predictions that most need correction receive the weakest updates. We propose Dual-Entropy Enhanced Policy Optimization (DEEPO), a dual-stage enhancement combining signal variance regularization with gradient preconditioning: semantic-entropy-triggered expert prefixes inject grounded continuations on high-uncertainty queries, providing direct supervision and restoring advantage variance, while advantage-sign-aware Renyi preconditioning counteracts logit-level saturation so correction reaches confident errors in the operational confidence regime. Both branches improve over GRPO individually; their interaction is statistically significant on VideoMMMU---the most complex long-horizon task in our evaluation suite (+4.0$, 95\% CI [1.1, 6.9])---and additive elsewhere. DEEPO reduces hallucination while preserving accuracy and training stability.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注强化学习在提升多模态大模型推理时对幻觉改善不稳定的问题，将其归因于奖励到参数更新链条中的两个薄弱环节：高语义熵的困难查询常产生全错样本组，使组相对优势归零；以及自信但错误的token因分布尖锐化而梯度不可见。作者提出DEEPO，通过语义熵触发的专家前缀注入有依据的续写以恢复优势方差，并用优势符号感知的Renyi预处理对抗logit饱和。实验表明两个分支均优于GRPO，在VideoMMMU上交互效应显著（+4.0），并在其他任务上可加性提升，同时减少幻觉并保持准确率与训练稳定性。
- innovations_zh: 语义熵触发专家前缀，为高不确定查询注入有依据续写并恢复优势方差; 优势符号感知的Renyi预处理，缓解logit饱和以纠正自信错误; 双阶段增强分别优于GRPO，并在复杂长程任务上交互效应显著
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了有限评测套件上的结果，未说明模型规模、数据集覆盖范围与基线细节，外部有效性尚不明确。方法依赖语义熵触发与专家前缀，可能引入额外计算或对专家知识质量敏感。交互效应仅在VideoMMMU上显著，其他任务为可加性提升，泛化性仍需更多验证。
- potential: 未来可探索语义熵阈值与专家前缀策略的自适应设计，并将双阶段增强推广到更多模态与任务。长期看，若能与更广泛的强化学习算法结合并验证跨模型规模稳定性，有望成为缓解多模态幻觉的通用优化组件。

### [36] Hindsight-Anchored Policy Optimization: Learning Through Hindsight with Thompson Sampling-Inspired Adaptive Gating  (id=2603.11321, 2026-09-15, rl=27, score=9.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards improves reasoning in large language models, yet on-policy learning often suffers from cold-start challenges in sparse-reward settings. Recent mixed-policy approaches address this by combining off-policy teacher data with on-policy training. However, simply combining these introduce a persistent off-policy gradient mass that risks training collapse and instability. To address this challenge, we propose Hindsight-Anchored Policy Optimization (HAPO), a framework that allows teacher intervention to act as a temporary support. HAPO employs Beta-Binomial confidence gating, an adaptive gating mechanism that decides when to open the gate for teacher intervention. The intervention operates with Synthetic Success Injection, which replaces the group's lowest-reward rollout with a verified teacher trajectory. We also introduce adaptive threshold annealing, which gradually retracts the support and restores on-policy training within a finite horizon to mitigate persistent off-policy drift. We demonstrate that HAPO can be layered on top of existing mixed-policy methods in a generalizable manner. Across six math reasoning benchmarks and two model scales, HAPO improves the average accuracy of three major mixed-policy methods while maintaining training stability.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注可验证奖励强化学习在稀疏奖励场景下的冷启动问题，以及混合策略方法中离策略教师数据持续存在可能引发的训练崩溃与不稳定。作者提出 Hindsight-Anchored Policy Optimization（HAPO），通过 Beta-Binomial 置信门控自适应决定何时引入教师干预，并用合成成功注入将组内最低奖励轨迹替换为已验证的教师轨迹。HAPO 还引入自适应阈值退火，在有限时间内逐步撤回支持并恢复在策略训练。实验表明，HAPO 可叠加在三种主流混合策略方法上，在六个数学推理基准和两种模型规模上提升平均准确率并保持训练稳定。
- innovations_zh: 提出 Beta-Binomial 置信门控，自适应决定教师干预时机; 设计合成成功注入，用已验证教师轨迹替换最低奖励轨迹; 引入自适应阈值退火，有限时间内撤回支持以恢复在策略训练; 可通用地叠加在现有混合策略方法之上
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了六个数学推理基准和两种模型规模上的平均准确率与训练稳定性，未说明具体基准、模型、基线细节及统计显著性。方法依赖已验证教师轨迹和可验证奖励，在缺乏此类验证信号的开放域任务中适用性尚不明确。自适应门控与退火涉及额外超参数，其敏感性和调参成本未在摘要中讨论。
- potential: 未来可探索将 HAPO 的门控与退火机制推广到非数学推理或奖励不可完全验证的任务，并研究教师质量、门控策略与退火日程对稳定性和最终性能的影响。长期看，该思路可能推动更通用的混合策略强化学习框架，在有限教师支持下实现稳定、可持续的在策略能力提升。

### [37] Beyond the Stability-Exploration Dilemma: Environmental Regularization for LLM Policy Optimization  (id=2608.23311, 2026-08-25, rl=27, score=8.8)
- cats: 推理与数学, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Policy optimization (PO) for Large Language Models faces a stability--exploration trade-off, currently mediated by an action-side Policy-KL regularizer. This puts practitioners in a double bind: keeping Policy-KL constrains response behavior and consumes the action-side exploration budget, while dropping it leaves the optimization without an explicit drift control. We argue for an alternative that breaks the dilemma by moving regularization to the input side. As training progresses, the distribution over training queries induced by the current policy drifts unchecked from its pre-RL reference distribution. Concretely, Environment-Regularized Policy Optimization (ERPO) introduces a Query-KL (QKL) term that bounds this query distribution shift, together with a dataset-static reference-derived per-query weight that biases each per-query update toward queries typical under the reference. The QKL gradient flows strictly through the query likelihood; the response score function used by policy-gradient estimators does not appear in the QKL term, so QKL exerts no direct gradient pressure on the response distribution---exploration is preserved. ERPO plugs into GRPO/PPO/REINFORCE-style pipelines without additional forward passes. On six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ERPO replaces the standard Policy-KL regularizer while achieving effective control over query distribution drift, delivering stronger accuracy and substantially more stable behavior under high-temperature decoding and long-horizon training.Our source code are available at https://github.com/alibaba/ERPO
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型策略优化中稳定性与探索性的两难困境，提出将正则化从动作侧移至输入侧的环境正则化策略优化方法（ERPO）。ERPO通过引入查询KL散度（QKL）项约束查询分布漂移，并利用参考分布派生的逐查询权重调整更新，同时保持响应分布探索不受直接梯度压力。在六个数学推理基准上，ERPO替代标准Policy-KL正则化，有效控制查询漂移，提升准确率，并在高温解码和长时训练下表现更稳定。
- innovations_zh: 提出输入侧正则化（QKL）替代动作侧Policy-KL，打破稳定性-探索困境; QKL梯度仅作用于查询似然，不干扰响应探索，保持探索能力; 无需额外前向传播，可无缝集成到GRPO/PPO/REINFORCE等流程
- limitations: 摘要未提供与Policy-KL正则化的详细对比实验数据，如计算开销或超参数敏感性。评测仅基于数学推理任务，未涉及通用对话或生成多样性场景，泛化性有待验证。QKL对查询分布漂移的控制效果依赖于参考分布的质量，但摘要未讨论其构建方式。
- potential: 未来可探索QKL与其他正则化（如熵正则）的联合使用，或扩展到多模态LLM策略优化。长期看，输入侧正则化可能成为LLM对齐和RLHF的标准组件，尤其在长时训练和分布偏移场景下。代码已开源，便于社区复现和进一步研究。

### [38] SCOUT: Unlocking Enhanced Spatial Reasoning via Structured Chain-of-Thought and Multi-Objective Process Reward  (id=2608.12220, 2026-08-13, rl=27, score=7.3)
- cats: 推理与数学, 多模态, 评测与基准
- ABS: Existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibits a critical bottleneck in robust spatial reasoning. Recent reinforcement learning (RL) methods aim to close this gap with verifiable outcomes, yet they suffer from poor credit assignment across intermediate reasoning steps. Concurrently, structured reasoning approaches overlook the critical depth perception necessary for comprehensive 3D understanding. To address these challenges, we propose SCOUT (Structured Chain-Of-Thought Utilizing Process-Supervised RL Training). Specifically, we design a structured Chain-of-Thought (CoT) framework that explicitly models 3D environmental perception to ensure robust spatial understanding and reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a novel RL algorithm featuring multi-objective process rewards and a tailored advantage estimation method, facilitating fine-grained credit assignment across distinct segments of the reasoning trajectory. To support our framework, we develop SCOUT-24k, a structured spatial reasoning CoT dataset synthesized through a customized pipeline. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SCOUT-3B improves upon baseline models by 16.85% and 6.3% on general spatial benchmarks and complex spatial reasoning tasks respectively. Notably, our larger SCOUT-7B even outperforms GPT-4o by a margin of 4.28%. Moreover, despite being trained exclusively on single image, SCOUT-7B exhibits robust out-of-domain generalization to multi-image and video scenarios. These empirical results render SCOUT as a critical step towards next generation of spatially-aware VLMs.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出SCOUT框架，通过结构化思维链显式建模3D环境感知，并引入多目标过程奖励的强化学习算法，以解决视觉语言模型在空间推理中的不足。实验表明，SCOUT-3B在通用和复杂空间基准上分别提升16.85%和6.3%，SCOUT-7B超越GPT-4o达4.28%，且能泛化到多图像和视频场景。
- innovations_zh: 设计结构化CoT框架，显式建模3D感知，增强空间理解; 提出多目标过程奖励与优势估计方法，实现细粒度信用分配; 构建SCOUT-24k数据集，支持结构化空间推理训练
- limitations: 摘要未提供数据集规模、训练细节及评测基准的具体构成，难以全面评估方法普适性。性能提升主要基于基准测试，缺乏真实场景或用户研究验证。多图像和视频泛化仅定性描述，缺乏量化结果。
- potential: 未来可探索将结构化CoT扩展到更多模态（如3D点云）或更复杂空间任务。多目标过程奖励机制可进一步优化，应用于其他推理密集型领域。长期看，该方法有望推动VLM在机器人导航、AR等空间智能应用中的落地。

### [39] Efficient RLVR Scheduling via Graph-Structured Online Difficulty Estimation  (id=2608.17941, 2026-08-19, rl=27, score=7.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models but relies on costly rollout exploration. Assigning the same exploration budget to samples with different difficulty levels is inefficient: easy samples may receive redundant rollouts, whereas difficult but learnable samples may receive too little exploration. Existing adaptive schedulers address this mismatch through curriculum-based sample selection or non-uniform rollout allocation based on estimated sample difficulty. However, obtaining reliable online difficulty estimates remains challenging: dedicated probing adds substantial generation overhead, whereas history-based estimators face a cold start with no initial observations and stale feedback, and typically ignore relations among samples. To address these limitations, we propose a plug-and-play graph-based online difficulty estimator that shares rollout feedback across related samples and continuously updates their difficulty estimates, mitigating cold start and staleness without dedicated probing. Specifically, we first construct a difficulty-aware sample graph based on semantic and reasoning similarities. Based on this graph, we introduce latent difficulty states and use a Potts prior to encourage neighboring samples to share the same state. We then employ a state-level Beta-Binomial model to aggregate the rollout outcomes associated with each state. Finally, we use an online mean-field variational algorithm to continuously update the latent-state assignments and state-level difficulty as new feedback 
- summary_zh: 该论文针对强化学习中的可验证奖励（RLVR）在推理任务中因样本难度不同导致探索预算分配低效的问题，提出了一种基于图的在线难度估计方法。该方法通过构建难度感知的样本图，利用Potts先验和状态级Beta-Binomial模型聚合反馈，并采用在线平均场变分算法持续更新难度估计，从而无需额外探测即可实现自适应探索。实验表明，该框架在多个基座模型、RL调度器和基准上取得了更好的性能。
- innovations_zh: 提出基于图的在线难度估计器，共享相邻样本反馈，缓解冷启动和过时问题; 引入潜在难度状态和Potts先验，结合Beta-Binomial模型与在线变分推断，实现无探测的自适应探索
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体性能提升幅度或计算开销对比，难以评估实际收益。图构建依赖语义和推理相似性，可能对低质量表示敏感。实验仅覆盖特定基座模型和基准，泛化性需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索更高效的图更新机制或动态图结构，以应对大规模样本。该框架可扩展到其他RL场景或在线学习任务，长期价值在于推动难度感知的智能资源分配。

### [40] Boosting LLM Exploration via Weak-Model Guidance in RLVR  (id=2608.27420, 2026-08-28, rl=27, score=7.0)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly improves LLM reasoning but often causes a drop in policy entropy, leading to narrowed reasoning coverage and degraded pass@$k$ for large $k$. While existing methods mitigate this entropy collapse through algorithmic regularizations, cross-model non-parametric perturbation is also neglected. In this work, we propose a simple yet effective approach to preserve the generative diversity of LLMs during RLVR. Instead of relying solely on internal exploration, we force the target model to generate answers based on partial reasoning trajectories generated by a smaller, weaker language models. These unfamiliar prefixes effectively disrupt over-confidence and encourage the exploration of distinct reasoning paths. We empirically study the potential of outer prefixes, revealing the mechanism of the impact of distributional discrepancy to the exploration dynamics in RLVR training. Experiments across multiple mathematical benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms vanilla RLVR. Notably, the performance gain becomes increasingly pronounced as $k$ scales up, demonstrating a substantial expansion of reasoning coverage. Furthermore, our approach efficiently mitigates entropy collapse without requiring additional SFT, intricate reward designs, or complex prompting.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对RLVR训练中策略熵下降导致推理覆盖范围缩小的问题，提出利用更小更弱的语言模型生成部分推理轨迹作为外部前缀，强制目标模型基于这些不熟悉的前缀进行生成，从而扰动过度自信并促进探索。实验表明该方法在多个数学基准上优于标准RLVR，且随着k增大性能提升更明显，有效缓解熵坍缩，且无需额外SFT或复杂奖励设计。
- innovations_zh: 提出利用弱模型生成的部分推理轨迹作为外部前缀，扰动目标模型探索; 揭示分布差异对RLVR探索动态的影响机制; 无需额外SFT或复杂奖励设计，简单有效缓解熵坍缩
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验设置细节，如弱模型选择标准、前缀长度影响等，限制了方法的可复现性。评测仅基于数学基准，未涉及其他推理任务或真实世界场景，泛化性有待验证。此外，未讨论弱模型前缀可能引入的错误传播或计算开销。
- potential: 未来可探索自适应选择弱模型或前缀长度，以平衡探索与利用。该方法可与其他正则化技术结合，进一步提升RLVR的稳定性。长期看，跨模型引导可能成为多智能体协同推理的通用范式，推动LLM在复杂推理任务中的持续学习。

- ABS: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly relies on human teleoperation demonstrations, while reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse binary rewards faces an exploration challenge when successful trajectories are rarely sampled. We propose SynthDemo-RL, a teacher-student framework in which an automated teacher converts simulator-privileged state into successful manipulation trajectories, a VLA student is distilled from them by supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and PPO with binary task-success rewards refines the student. We study reward coverage, the fraction of tasks for which at least one success is observed under the fixed evaluation protocol, as a complement to the average success rate. On LIBERO-PRO, a public benchmark of perturbed LIBERO tasks for which no demonstrations exist, 27 of 57 scored tasks are at exactly 0% success for a pi_0.5 policy fine-tuned on the original LIBERO tasks. Direct PPO from this policy, under the same PPO recipe and the same RL compute as SynthDemo-RL's refinement stage, rescues 10 of these 27 tasks and leaves 17 at 0%. SynthDemo-RL, with 50 synthesized trajectories per task and no new human demonstrations, rescues all 27 and reaches average success rates of 97.8% and 97.1% on the Position and Task axes of LIBERO-PRO, respectively. On standard LIBERO, the same pipeline reaches 96.0% with no human demonstrations, within 1.7 points of pi_0.5 trained on 50 human demonstrations per task. We further validate the pipeline on RoboTwin 2.0 and verify that trajectories from a policy trained in a MuJoCo twin execute open-loop on a physical 
- summary_zh: 该研究针对VLA模型微调依赖人类遥操作演示、而稀疏二值奖励强化学习面临探索困难的问题，提出SynthDemo-RL教师-学生框架：自动教师将仿真器特权状态转化为成功操作轨迹，学生VLA通过监督微调蒸馏，再用二值任务成功奖励的PPO精炼。在LIBERO-PRO上，基线pi_0.5策略在57个评分任务中有27个成功率为0%，直接PPO仅挽救10个，而SynthDemo-RL每任务合成50条轨迹且无需新人类演示，挽救了全部27个任务，Position和Task轴平均成功率分别达97.8%和97.1%。在标准LIBERO上达到96.0%，与使用每任务50条人类演示训练的pi_0.5相差1.7个百分点，并在RoboTwin 2.0和MuJoCo孪生到物理机器人的开环执行上得到验证。
- innovations_zh: 提出教师-学生框架，用仿真器特权状态自动合成成功轨迹，替代人类演示; 将合成演示的SFT与二值奖励PPO结合，突破稀疏奖励下的零奖励障碍; 引入奖励覆盖率作为平均成功率的补充评估指标; 在无新人类演示下于LIBERO-PRO和标准LIBERO取得高成功率
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了LIBERO-PRO、标准LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0等仿真基准及MuJoCo孪生到物理机器人的开环验证，未涉及真实机器人闭环实验，实际部署效果不确定。方法依赖仿真器特权状态和自动教师，在无特权信息或仿真与现实差距大的场景中适用性可能受限。此外，摘要未说明合成轨迹质量、教师策略泛化性及PPO精炼的计算成本等细节。
- potential: 未来可探索将合成演示框架扩展到更多真实机器人任务和闭环控制场景，减少对仿真器特权状态的依赖。可研究自动教师生成轨迹的质量与多样性对RL精炼效果的影响，以及奖励覆盖率作为通用评估指标的推广价值。长期看，该方法有望降低VLA适应成本，推动无需人类演示的机器人技能学习。

### [42] DARS: Dual-Level Credit Assignment RL with Structured Reasoning for Instruction-Based Image Editing  (id=2608.20161, 2026-08-21, rl=27, score=6.7)
- cats: 多模态, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Instruction-based image editing uses a planner-renderer pipeline: a vision-language model (VLM) first converts the instruction into an edit plan, and a diffusion model then executes that plan. Training such systems with only final-image rewards is inefficient because a poor edit does not reveal whether additional optimization should place more emphasis on the planner or the renderer, and even planner-dominant cases remain difficult to localize within a free-form reasoning trace. We present DARS, a reinforcement learning framework for dual-level credit assignment in this two-stage setting. Across modules, multi-plan multi-render rollouts estimate between-plan and within-plan reward variability for soft module routing, while rollout mean rewards provide hardness estimates for an adaptive curriculum. Within the planner, a four-field structured reasoning output enables a prefix-gated reward and token-level advantage reweighting, turning outcome-level feedback into localized supervision. Experiments on five benchmarks show that DARS outperforms a Joint~RL baseline with the same backbone, data, reward model, and rollout budget, with the largest gains on reasoning-intensive edits.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对基于指令的图像编辑中规划器-渲染器两阶段系统仅用最终图像奖励训练效率低的问题，提出DARS框架，实现跨模块和规划器内部的双层信用分配。跨模块通过多规划多渲染 rollout 估计奖励变异性进行软模块路由，并用 rollout 均值奖励作为难度估计实现自适应课程；规划器内部采用四字段结构化推理输出，实现前缀门控奖励和 token 级优势重加权。在五个基准上，DARS 优于同骨干、数据、奖励模型和 rollout 预算的联合 RL 基线，尤其在推理密集型编辑上提升最大。
- innovations_zh: 提出双层信用分配框架，分别处理模块间和规划器内部的奖励归因问题; 利用多规划多渲染 rollout 估计奖励变异性，实现软模块路由和自适应课程; 设计四字段结构化推理输出，支持前缀门控奖励和 token 级优势重加权
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体性能数值或与更多基线对比，仅提及优于联合 RL 基线，优势幅度和统计显著性未知。实验基于五个基准，但未说明是否涵盖多样化的编辑类型和真实场景，泛化性有待验证。此外，方法依赖结构化推理输出，可能不适用于无明确推理结构的任务。
- potential: 未来可探索将双层信用分配扩展到更多模块化生成系统，如文本到视频或多轮对话。结构化推理输出可进一步与可解释性结合，提升编辑过程的可控性。长期看，该方法有望推动强化学习在复杂生成流水线中的更高效应用。

### [43] How to Train a Critic Stably and Efficiently  (id=2608.23566, 2026-08-25, rl=27, score=6.7)
- cats: 推理与数学
- ABS: Group-based reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO for large language models avoid training a critic by sampling multiple responses for each prompt. A reliable critic could instead estimate token-level advantages from one response, but standard critic-based training recipes are often unstable. We study this instability and develop \textbf{Best-Practice Critic Optimization (BPCO)}, a recipe that combines DPPO, value predictions bounded to the reward range, Monte Carlo value targets, unnormalized policy advantages, and length-adaptive generalized advantage estimation. Because the critic is used only during training, BPCO can also condition it on reward-defining information, such as a reference answer or grading rubric, that is hidden from the policy. Controlled experiments isolate the effect of each design choice. Across mathematical reasoning tasks with models ranging from 1.5B parameters to 30B-A3B mixtures of experts, BPCO improves a strong critic-based baseline consistently, and matches or exceeds a group-based baseline while sampling one response per prompt. The same recipe also improves learning with rubric-based rewards. These results show that a carefully designed critic provides a reliable alternative to group-relative advantage estimation. Code is available at https://github.com/QPHutu/golden_critic
- summary_zh: 该论文针对基于评论家（critic）的强化学习方法在大语言模型训练中不稳定的问题，提出了一种最佳实践评论家优化（BPCO）配方，结合了DPPO、值预测限制、蒙特卡洛目标、未归一化优势及长度自适应GAE。在数学推理任务中，从1.5B到30B-A3B混合专家模型上，BPCO一致优于强评论家基线，并达到或超过基于组采样的基线，同时每个提示仅采样一个响应。此外，该配方也适用于基于评分标准的奖励学习。
- innovations_zh: 提出BPCO配方，整合多种稳定训练技术，提升评论家训练稳定性; 利用评论家仅训练时可用，条件化奖励定义信息（如参考答案）; 通过控制实验分离各设计选择的影响，验证有效性
- limitations: 摘要未提供评测任务的具体多样性，可能仅限于数学推理领域。控制实验虽分离了各因素，但未报告计算成本或训练时间对比。此外，未讨论BPCO在非数学任务或更大规模模型上的泛化性。
- potential: 未来可探索将BPCO扩展到更多任务类型（如代码生成、对话）和更大模型。条件化评论家的思想可能启发新的奖励建模方法。长期来看，该配方有望成为标准RL训练流程的一部分，提升样本效率和稳定性。

- ABS: Acute poly-substance intoxication requires rapid, life-saving decisions under substantial uncertainty, as clinicians must rely on incomplete ingestion details and nonspecific symptoms. Effective diagnostic reasoning in this chaotic environment requires fusing unstructured, non-medical narratives (e.g. paramedic scene descriptions and unreliable patient self-reports or known histories), with structured medical data like vital signs. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show potential for processing such heterogeneous inputs, they struggle in this setting, often underperforming simple baselines that rely solely on patient histories. To address this, we present DeToxR (Decision-support for Toxicology with Reasoning), the first adaptation of Reinforcement Learning (RL) to emergency toxicology. We design a robust data-fusion engine for multi-label prediction across 14 substance classes based on an LLM finetuned with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We optimize the model's reasoning directly using a clinical performance reward. By formulating a multi-label agreement metric as the reward signal, the model is explicitly penalized for missing co-ingested substances and hallucinating absent poisons. Our model significantly outperforms its unadapted base LLM counterpart and supervised baselines. Furthermore, in a preliminary clinical validation study, the model indicates a clinical advantage by achieving higher micro-F1 (0.644 vs 0.473) and recall in identifying the correct poisons. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-aligned LLMs to synthesize unstructured pre-clini
- summary_zh: 该研究针对急性混合物质中毒场景下临床决策高度不确定的问题，提出需融合非结构化院前叙述与结构化生命体征等医疗数据。作者构建了DeToxR，首次将强化学习引入急诊毒理学，基于经GRPO微调的LLM实现14类物质的多标签预测，并以临床性能奖励直接优化推理。模型在评测中显著优于未适配的基座LLM和监督基线，初步临床验证中micro-F1达0.644（对比0.473）且召回更高。
- innovations_zh: 首次将强化学习适配到急诊毒理学决策支持场景; 设计面向14类物质的多标签数据融合引擎; 用GRPO微调LLM并以临床性能奖励优化推理; 以多标签一致性指标为奖励惩罚漏检与幻觉
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了初步临床验证，样本规模、验证设计与外部泛化性未说明，结论稳健性有待检验。评测聚焦14类物质与micro-F1、召回等指标，对更细粒度毒物、罕见类别及真实部署中的安全性、可解释性尚缺乏证据。
- potential: 未来可扩大临床验证规模与多中心外部验证，检验模型在不同人群与院前数据质量下的稳健性。还可探索奖励设计与不确定性校准、人机协同流程的结合，长期有望推动强化学习对齐LLM在急诊等高危场景的可靠决策支持。

### [45] LEGO-RL: Harness-Native Reinforcement Learning for Coding Agents  (id=2608.17393, 2026-08-19, rl=27, score=6.4)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 多模态, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Reinforcement learning for coding agents increasingly relies on long-running agent harnesses to manage tool integration, repository contexts, and execution feedback. However, the native execution environments of these harnesses are inherently misaligned with policy-gradient training: environmental crashes and reward hacking corrupt outcome signals, while train-inference discrepancies decouple rollout behavior from policy updates. To address this, we present LEGO-RL, a framework that bridges native coding-agent harnesses with scalable policy-gradient optimization without modifying their internal control flow. LEGO-RL is built upon three pillars: (1) faithful optimization via in-process LLM proxying that captures raw generation streams for token-level alignment and robust trainer-side log-probability recomputation, even under harness-side compaction or re-serialization; (2) reliable execution via scalable sandbox orchestration featuring image caching and stage-wise defenses to mitigate reward hacking; and (3) observable training through an integrated plugin that automates validation and monitoring, paired with a Live UI for granular trajectory diagnostics. We evaluate LEGO-RL by training the sparse MoE model Qwen3.5-35B-A3B with GSPO across three native coding-agent harnesses. LEGO-RL improves Qwen3.5-35B-A3B across OpenHands SDK (64.0% to 70.4%), Claude Code (62.4% to 68.2%), and OpenCode (57.2% to 66.6%) on SWE-bench Verified, while maintaining a rollout-training probability correlation above 0.99.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出LEGO-RL框架，旨在解决编码智能体强化学习中原生执行环境与策略梯度训练不匹配的问题，包括环境崩溃、奖励黑客和训练-推理不一致。框架通过进程内LLM代理实现忠实优化，通过沙箱编排确保可靠执行，并通过集成插件实现可观测训练。在Qwen3.5-35B-A3B模型上使用GSPO算法，在三个原生编码智能体框架上均提升了SWE-bench Verified性能，且保持训练-回滚概率相关性高于0.99。
- innovations_zh: 提出进程内LLM代理，实现token级对齐和鲁棒的log-prob重计算; 设计可扩展沙箱编排，包含镜像缓存和分阶段防御机制; 集成自动化验证与实时UI，提供细粒度轨迹诊断
- limitations: 摘要仅基于SWE-bench Verified单一基准评估，缺乏其他任务或真实场景的泛化验证。未报告计算成本、训练时间或资源消耗，难以评估实际部署可行性。奖励黑客防御的有效性仅通过性能提升间接体现，缺乏消融实验或失败案例分析。
- potential: 未来可探索将LEGO-RL扩展到更多编码智能体框架和更大规模模型，验证其通用性。可进一步研究其防御机制在不同奖励黑客攻击下的鲁棒性，并优化沙箱编排以降低资源开销。长期来看，该框架有望成为编码智能体强化学习训练的标准基础设施，推动更复杂智能体任务的可靠训练。

### [46] TIGPO: Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents  (id=2609.03383, 2026-09-04, rl=27, score=6.3)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用
- ABS: Graph-based policy optimization improves credit assignment for long-horizon LLM agents by organizing rollout trajectories into state-transition graphs. However, existing methods construct graphs independently within each policy update, discarding transitions discovered by earlier policies and limiting advantage estimation to small, batch-local rollout groups. We propose \emph{Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization} (TIGPO), which extends graph-based credit assignment across policy updates. TIGPO maintains a persistent transition graph for each task, allowing valid transitions discovered by different policy versions to jointly determine credit for current rollouts. To actively reconnect current exploration with historical experience, TIGPO allocates a fixed rollout budget between Exploration slots for ordinary task sampling and Revisit slots for delayed reattempts of previously explored tasks. For each revisit, TIGPO pairs the current rollout group with its corresponding earlier Exploration group to construct a cross-temporal reference. The enlarged reference is designed to stabilize relative advantage estimation under small rollout groups, while comparison on the same task directly captures policy improvement across training stages. Historical transitions and scores serve only as structural and detached statistical references and are never replayed in the policy loss. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that TIGPO consistently outperforms prior group-based and graph-based policy optimization methods.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出TIGPO方法，用于改进长周期LLM智能体的图基策略优化。现有方法在每个策略更新中独立构建图，丢弃早期策略发现的转换，限制优势估计。TIGPO维护每个任务的持久转换图，跨策略更新共享历史经验，并分配探索与重访预算以连接当前与历史数据。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，TIGPO优于现有组基和图基方法。
- innovations_zh: 跨策略更新维护持久转换图，实现历史经验复用; 引入探索与重访槽位机制，主动连接当前与历史探索; 通过跨时间参考组稳定小批量下的相对优势估计
- limitations: 摘要未提供详细实验设置与消融分析，难以评估各组件独立贡献。评测仅基于ALFWorld和WebShop两个环境，泛化性需更多任务验证。未讨论计算开销与内存增长问题，持久图可能带来额外资源消耗。
- potential: 未来可探索动态图剪枝或压缩策略以降低存储成本，并扩展至更多交互式任务。该方法或可结合离线强化学习，进一步利用历史数据提升样本效率。长期看，跨时间信用分配有望推动LLM智能体在真实世界长程决策中的应用。

### [47] Learn What's Left, Not What's Mastered: Saturation Aware Advantage Reweighting for Multi-Reward Policy Optimization  (id=2608.16072, 2026-08-18, rl=26, score=7.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) with group-relative advantages has become the de facto standard for post-training language model reasoners. However, when optimizing multiple reward objectives, existing methods typically scalarize the reward vector with a fixed weighted sum before group-wise standardization. We show that this design leads to two fundamental problems: rollouts with distinct reward profiles can receive identical advantages, and all objectives are optimized with fixed relative weights regardless of their current level of saturation. As a result, training continues to allocate gradient budget to already-solved objectives instead of focusing on those with greater remaining headroom. We introduce \textbf{Saturation Aware Advantage Reweighting for Multi-Reward Policy Optimization} (SA-MRPO), which standardizes each reward objective independently and adaptively discounts its contribution according to a batch-level estimate of objective saturation. This dynamically reallocates optimization effort toward under-optimized objectives while empirically maintaining performance on those that are already well satisfied. We further show that saturation-aware reweighting can reverse the sign of an update, rather than merely rescale its magnitude. Across mathematical reasoning with two- and three-objective reward combinations, SA-MRPO improves the harder correctness objective over GDPO in 12 of 15 benchmark comparisons, with gains of up to $5\%$ on AIME24. On adaptive reasoning it improves accuracy on all five benchmarks, by $3.8\%$ on average and up to $9.2 \%$ on AMC23, and on co
- summary_zh: 该论文针对多奖励策略优化中固定加权求和与组内标准化导致的问题，提出饱和感知优势重加权方法（SA-MRPO），独立标准化每个奖励目标并根据批次级饱和程度自适应调整其贡献。实验表明，在数学推理、自适应推理和代码生成等任务上，SA-MRPO在多数基准上优于GDPO，同时保持已满足目标性能。

- potential: 未来可探索更细粒度的饱和估计（如基于状态或轨迹），并扩展到更多奖励类型和复杂任务。长期看，该方法有望成为多目标RL的标准组件，推动更高效的奖励优化策略。

### [48] VERPO: Verified Evidence Regularized Policy Optimization  (id=2609.06100, 2026-09-23, rl=26, score=7.0)

- ABS: Verifiable rewards improve language models through reliable task-level feedback, but methods based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) apply a sequence-level advantage uniformly across all tokens. This coarse credit assignment reinforces or penalizes entire responses without identifying which local decisions to preserve, reinforce, or revise. Conversely, evidence-conditioned self-distillation provides denser token-level supervision, yet teacher imitation can transfer stylistic artifacts and miscalibrated confidence that destabilize training when misaligned with task success. We introduce VERPO, which converts evidence-conditioned guidance into reward-aligned token-level credit assignment while retaining the outcome objective. VERPO decomposes teacher guidance into an evidence-free reference term and signed, evidence-induced corrections at each token. A stopped controller combines selective acceptance, token-wise localization, and cost-aware scaling by balancing alignment with the local GRPO update direction against Fisher movement cost. Furthermore, we introduce Fisher Evidence Contrast (FEC), which attenuates nuisance shifts along an estimated evidence-presence direction through a regularized projection. Across five scientific reasoning and tool-use tasks, VERPO prevents optimization collapse and consistently achieves the highest multi-task average across model backbones, yielding marked improvements particularly on smaller models over strong baselines. Qualitative diagnostics confirm that token acceptance selectively targets reasoning bottlenecks consistent with 
- summary_zh: 现有基于GRPO的可验证奖励方法在序列级别统一分配优势，无法识别哪些局部决策应保留、强化或修正；而证据条件自蒸馏虽提供更密集的token级监督，却可能迁移风格伪影和误校准置信度。VERPO将证据条件指导转化为与奖励对齐的token级信用分配，同时保留结果目标，通过分解教师指导为无证据参考项和带符号的证据诱导修正项，并利用停止控制器结合选择性接受、token定位和成本感知缩放。在五个科学推理和工具使用任务上，VERPO防止了优化崩溃，并在不同模型骨干上持续取得最高多任务平均成绩，尤其在小模型上提升显著。
- innovations_zh: 将证据条件指导分解为无证据参考项与带符号证据诱导修正项，实现token级信用分配; 设计停止控制器，结合选择性接受、token定位与基于Fisher移动成本的缩放; 提出Fisher证据对比（FEC），通过正则化投影衰减证据存在方向上的干扰偏移

- potential: 未来可探索将VERPO扩展至更广泛的开放域生成、多轮对话及多模态任务，验证其跨领域有效性。可进一步研究停止控制器的自适应简化与FEC方向估计的在线更新，降低计算与调参负担。长期看，该token级信用分配思路有望与过程奖励模型、可解释性分析结合，推动更可靠的语言模型后训练范式。

### [49] SocialRL: Refining LLMs' Social Intelligence through Multi-turn Reinforcement Learning and Reward Design  (id=2609.09764, 2026-09-10, rl=26, score=6.8)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Social intelligence enables agents to read social context, infer intent, and adapt over sustained dialogue. As language models become autonomous collaborators, it is central to building effective and trustworthy human-AI interaction. Existing reinforcement learning methods optimize single-turn utterances and sparse outcome rewards, producing short-sighted policies that struggle to manage goal-relationship tensions across multi-turn interactions. We propose SocialRL, a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework addressing both challenges. First, we apply multi-turn reinforcement learning using PPO that propagates delayed outcome rewards back to each turn, enabling long-horizon planning. Second, we design six process reward dimensions capturing the goal-relationship trade-off, including goal advancement, relational attunement, contextual coherence, etc. A reward model dynamically generates fine-grained scoring criteria for each dimension, while a stage-aware weight schedule prioritizes relationship-building in early turns, goal advancement mid-way, and balanced closure late. Across multiple social-dialogue benchmarks, SocialRL improves Goal Achievement by an average of 9.2 percentage points over the corresponding Base models. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of SocialRL across synthetic and real social scenes, as well as standard and challenging social scenarios.
- summary_zh: 该研究关注语言模型在多轮社交对话中难以兼顾目标推进与关系维护的问题，指出现有强化学习方法多优化单轮话语且依赖稀疏结果奖励，导致策略短视。作者提出SocialRL多轮强化学习框架，采用PPO将延迟的结果奖励回传到每一轮以支持长程规划，并设计六个过程奖励维度刻画目标与关系的权衡，由奖励模型动态生成细粒度评分标准，同时用阶段感知权重调度在不同对话阶段侧重关系建立、目标推进与平衡收尾。在多个社交对话基准上，SocialRL相比对应基座模型平均提升目标达成率9.2个百分点，覆盖合成与真实社交场景。
- innovations_zh: 用PPO进行多轮强化学习，将延迟结果奖励回传至各轮以支持长程规划; 设计六个过程奖励维度，刻画目标推进与关系维护之间的权衡; 奖励模型动态生成各维度细粒度评分标准，提升奖励的适应性; 引入阶段感知权重调度，按对话阶段侧重关系、目标与平衡收尾
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了目标达成率的平均提升，未说明关系维护、上下文连贯等其他维度的具体效果，也未披露基准规模、基座模型类型与统计显著性。过程奖励维度与阶段权重调度涉及较多设计选择，其泛化性、对奖励模型质量的依赖以及在不同文化或任务场景下的适用边界尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可进一步检验该方法在更广泛真实社交场景与跨文化对话中的稳健性，并探索过程奖励维度与权重调度的自动化设计以减少人工先验。长期看，若能与更丰富的社交能力评测体系结合，有望推动语言模型在协作、谈判与长期陪伴等任务中实现更平衡的目标与关系管理。

### [50] Bellman Policy Optimization  (id=2609.15987, 2026-09-15, rl=26, score=6.8)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). We introduce Bellman Policy Optimization (BPO), a critic-free method derived from Policy Mirror Descent (PMD). For autoregressive generation with terminal rewards, BPO uses the Bellman equations to reformulate PMD as a trajectory-level objective. The reformulation avoids estimating state values at intermediate states. We prove that it has the same unique optimal solution as the original PMD objective. We derive the practical BPO loss by approximating this objective. Its mismatch-correction weight is a smoothed ratio of complementary token probabilities. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of BPO.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对大语言模型推理能力的提升。作者提出 Bellman Policy Optimization（BPO），一种从策略镜像下降（PMD）推导出的无评论家方法。针对带终端奖励的自回归生成，BPO 利用贝尔曼方程将 PMD 重构为轨迹级目标，从而避免估计中间状态的价值。作者证明该重构与原 PMD 目标具有相同的唯一最优解，并通过近似该目标导出实际损失，其失配修正权重是互补 token 概率的平滑比值。在数学推理基准上的实验表明 BPO 有效。
- innovations_zh: 从策略镜像下降推导出无评论家的 BPO 方法; 用贝尔曼方程将 PMD 重构为轨迹级目标，避免估计中间状态价值; 证明重构目标与原 PMD 目标具有相同唯一最优解; 失配修正权重采用互补 token 概率的平滑比值
- limitations: 摘要仅报告在数学推理基准上的实验，未说明其他任务或模型规模上的泛化性。方法依赖终端奖励与可验证奖励设定，在更一般的奖励场景下是否适用尚不明确。此外，摘要未给出与基线方法的量化对比或消融分析，实际收益幅度难以判断。
- potential: 未来可探索 BPO 在数学推理之外任务上的适用性，以及在不同模型规模和奖励形式下的表现。可进一步研究其与评论家方法的计算效率与稳定性权衡，并分析失配修正权重的理论性质。若验证充分，该方法有望成为 RLVR 中轻量且理论可解释的优化选择。

- ABS: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has been promising in single-turn LLM fine-tuning. However, long-horizon agentic reasoning introduces increasingly branching interactions and sparse rewards, exposing several limitations of RL: its heavyweight backpropagation-based training stack makes it impractical to fine-tune larger LLMs, and longer-horizon trajectories make credit assignment in RL substantially harder. This paper argues that evolution strategies (ES) can be a better choice for fine-tuning long-horizon LLM agents. Compared with agentic RL, ES offers three key advantages: 1) Model Scalability: ES enables full-parameter optimization with only minimal, inference-level GPU memory, making it possible to fine-tune large LLMs. 2) Flexibility: its lightweight, black-box feedback interface makes ES fine-tuning easy to compose with prompt-space evolution (e.g., skill optimization & test-time compute); and 3) Long-Horizon Scalability: ES performs trajectory-level parameter attribution without decomposing rewards across horizons, yielding better scalability than Agentic RL as the horizon length grows. Based on this insight, we propose Agentic ESOpt, a full-parameter agentic fine-tuning framework tailored to flexible parameter--context co-evolution. At each step, Agentic ESOpt samples perturbations around the current LLM parameters, evaluates the resulting agents with rewards, and applies an online reward-weighted update. To improve the exploration--adaptation trade-off, Agentic ESOpt further introduces a cosine decay schedule of the perturbation scale $σ$. On WebArena-Lite, full-paramete
- summary_zh: 该论文针对长视野智能体微调中强化学习（RL）在计算成本和信用分配上的局限，提出使用进化策略（ES）作为替代方案。作者提出Agentic ESOpt框架，实现全参数微调且仅需推理级GPU内存，并支持参数与提示空间的协同进化。在WebArena-Lite上，对Qwen-3.5-27B进行全参数优化，相比无技能基线提升了6.69%；在测试时自动启发式设计中，36个设置中有28个优于匹配基线。
