### [52] PGPO: Potential-Guided Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic Tasks  (id=2609.02236, 2026-09-03, rl=26, score=6.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用
- ABS: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective paradigm for LLM post-training, but in multi-turn agentic tasks with sparse terminal rewards, it often provides coarse credit for intermediate actions. To obtain more fine-grained credit assignment, recent work such as GiGPO introduces step-level advantages for intermediate actions. However, these step-level signals still rely on the final outcome of each individual trajectory. As a result, actions within failed trajectories can remain poorly differentiated, so effective actions can receive the same unfavorable credit as erroneous ones. In this work, we propose Potential-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO) for multi-turn agentic tasks. PGPO estimates empirical state potentials from anchor-state-group return statistics within each rollout group. It then derives action advantages from potential differences between adjacent states, enabling cross-trajectory credit propagation. This provides finer-grained step-level credit assignment, especially within failed trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show strong overall performance relative to recent group-based RL methods. Further analysis provides evidence that PGPO yields more informative failure-side credit signals with negligible training overhead.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对多轮智能体任务中稀疏终端奖励导致的信用分配粗糙问题，提出了一种名为PGPO的强化学习方法。PGPO通过从锚点状态组的回报统计中估计经验状态势，并利用相邻状态间的势差推导动作优势，从而实现跨轨迹的信用传播，为失败轨迹内的中间动作提供更细粒度的信用信号。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，PGPO相对于近期基于组的RL方法取得了较强的整体性能，且训练开销可忽略不计。
- innovations_zh: 提出基于状态势差的跨轨迹信用传播机制，改善失败轨迹内的动作区分度; 利用锚点状态组回报统计估计经验势，无需额外模型或复杂计算
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了ALFWorld和WebShop两个环境的结果，未涉及更广泛或更复杂的任务，泛化性有待验证。此外，方法依赖组内回报统计，可能对组大小或回报分布敏感，且未与更多基线或真实场景对比，性能优势的稳健性尚需更多证据。
- potential: 未来可探索将PGPO扩展到更多类型的多轮任务（如工具使用、对话决策），并研究势函数估计的自适应策略。其低开销特性使其有望成为LLM后训练的标准组件，长期价值在于推动更精细、更高效的智能体强化学习范式。

### [53] Towards Bridging the Gap Between Offline and Iterative Alignment via Preference Distillation  (id=2609.06893, 2026-09-09, rl=25, score=9.3)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Direct preference optimization DPO is a promising offline approach for aligning large language models (LLMs) due to its simplicity, computational efficiency, and implicit modeling of human preferences. Interestingly, iterative extensions of DPO have achieved stronger performance on academic benchmarks, raising two key questions: (i) Why do iterative methods generally outperform offline ones? (ii) Can their advantages be incorporated into offline alignment? To answer the first question, our controlled experiments reveal that the explicit preference model, additionally introduced in the iterative procedure, is a key factor behind its superiority over offline methods. This insight leads us to answer the second question affirmatively and propose Distilled Preference Probability Policy Optimization (DP3O), an effective and efficient offline alignment algorithm. DP3O first learns an explicit preference model using a helper class of LLMs and then distills its knowledge into policy optimization. Theoretically, we show that explicit preference modeling admits better estimation error control than implicit formulations, and that DP3O achieves a tighter generalization bound than hard-label DPO through variance reduction. Empirically, we evaluate DP3O on a wide range of chat-based and downstream tasks and show that it outperforms state-of-the-art offline methods, achieves performance comparable to iterative DPO, and reduces training time by about $42\%$, demonstrating both its effectiveness and efficiency.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对直接偏好优化（DPO）等离线对齐方法在性能上不如迭代方法的问题，通过控制实验发现迭代方法中显式偏好模型是关键优势来源，并提出DP3O算法：先利用辅助LLM学习显式偏好模型，再将其知识蒸馏到策略优化中。理论上证明了显式偏好模型在估计误差控制上更优，且DP3O通过方差缩减获得更紧的泛化界。实验表明DP3O在多种对话和下游任务上优于现有离线方法，性能接近迭代DPO，并减少约42%训练时间。
- innovations_zh: 通过控制实验揭示显式偏好模型是迭代方法优于离线方法的关键因素; 提出DP3O算法，将显式偏好模型知识蒸馏到策略优化中，实现高效离线对齐; 理论上证明DP3O具有更紧的泛化界和更好的估计误差控制
- limitations: 摘要未提供DP3O在极端偏好分布或复杂多轮交互场景下的详细表现，且实验基准可能未覆盖所有实际应用。理论分析基于特定假设，其泛化界在实际复杂模型中的适用性需进一步验证。此外，辅助LLM的选择和蒸馏过程可能引入额外计算开销，摘要未明确其整体资源消耗。
- potential: 未来可探索将DP3O扩展到在线或动态偏好场景，以及结合更高效的偏好模型学习方式。其理论框架可能启发其他离线对齐算法的改进，长期来看有助于实现更接近人类价值观且计算高效的LLM对齐。

### [54] ANO: Robust Policy Optimization via Bounded, Redescending Gain Fields  (id=2605.02320, 2026-09-24, rl=25, score=6.9)
- cats: 微调与对齐
- ABS: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) dominates reinforcement learning and LLM alignment, yet its hard-clipping mechanism and unconstrained alternatives (e.g., SPO) sit at two extremes of a stability-efficiency dilemma. We argue that this dilemma is best understood dynamically: a surrogate objective is a feedback law on the probability ratio, and its clipping/penalty shape defines a gain field that drives the update dynamics. PPO's clip induces a dead zone (zero feedback outside the trust region), leaving the policy to drift open-loop under momentum; SPO's quadratic penalty induces an unbounded, linearly growing gain that stiffens the dynamics and destabilizes under aggressive step sizes. Guided by this view, we derive Anchored Neighborhood Optimization (ANO), which designs the gain field directly: a $C^\infty$ shaping kernel that anchors the identity map at $r{=}1$, peaks exactly at a prescribed trust-region boundary $1{+}ε$, bounds the push on severely off-policy samples by a tunable $κ_{+}$, and exerts a bounded, redescending pull of tunable depth $κ_{-}$ on extreme outliers. The three hyperparameters have decoupled roles, and all internal constants are solved in closed form. Empirically, ANO ranks first on both Atari (40 games) and MuJoCo in IQM and Median of normalized scores. While the runner-up differs across domains (PAPO on Atari, SPO on MuJoCo), ANO is the only method consistently at the top. Under a learning-rate stress test ($3\times10^{-4}\!\to\!10^{-3}$), ANO degrades by only $0.9\%$ whereas PPO collapses by $54.5\%$, and the stressed ANO still outperforms PPO an
- summary_zh: 该研究针对PPO硬裁剪与SPO无界惩罚在稳定性与效率之间的两难问题，提出将代理目标视为概率比上的反馈律，其裁剪或惩罚形状定义了驱动更新动态的增益场。基于此视角，作者推导出ANO方法，直接设计增益场：采用C∞整形核，在r=1处锚定恒等映射，在信任域边界1+ε处精确达到峰值，对严重离策略样本施加可调上界κ+的推动，并对极端离群值施加有界、可调深度κ-的再下降拉力。实验表明，ANO在Atari 40款游戏和MuJoCo的IQM与归一化分数中位数上均排名第一，且在学习率压力测试下仅退化0.9%，而PPO崩溃54.5%。
- innovations_zh: 将代理目标解释为概率比上的反馈律，提出增益场视角统一理解PPO与SPO的稳定性-效率困境; 设计C∞整形核直接构造增益场，在信任域边界精确峰值并锚定恒等映射; 对严重离策略样本和极端离群值分别施加有界推动与有界再下降拉力，超参数角色解耦且内部常数闭式求解
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了Atari和MuJoCo上的结果，未涉及LLM对齐等更广泛场景，泛化性有待验证。理论分析主要基于增益场视角的定性论证，缺乏收敛性等正式理论保证。此外，超参数虽角色解耦，但实际调参成本与敏感性未在摘要中说明。
- potential: 未来可将ANO拓展至LLM对齐等大规模场景，验证其在不同任务分布下的鲁棒性。可进一步从动力系统或控制理论角度分析增益场设计的理论性质，如收敛性与稳定性边界。长期看，增益场视角可能催生更多直接设计反馈律的策略优化方法。

### [55] Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences  (id=2609.04066, 2026-09-04, rl=24, score=8.8)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对RLHF中奖励模型学习样本效率低的问题，提出了一种名为PreferenceEKF的主动偏好学习方法。该方法将主动偏好学习视为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，利用扩展卡尔曼滤波器在低维参数子空间中进行推理，以高效追踪奖励模型的不确定性。在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上的实验表明，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，且学习到的奖励模型能带来有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。
- innovations_zh: 将主动偏好学习建模为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，实现奖励模型不确定性的持续更新。; 在低维参数子空间中使用扩展卡尔曼滤波，避免全参数空间的后验推断，显著提升计算效率。; 支持从神经网络参数中高效采样，用于计算主动学习的采集函数，增强可扩展性。
- limitations: 摘要未提供与现有非贝叶斯主动学习方法的详细对比，因此其相对优势的普适性尚不明确。实验仅基于D4RL和V-D4RL基准，未涉及更广泛或更复杂的真实任务，泛化能力有待验证。此外，低维子空间的选择和扩展卡尔曼滤波的近似误差可能影响极端非线性场景下的不确定性估计精度。
- potential: 未来可探索自适应子空间选择或更先进的滤波方法以提升近似精度，并扩展到更多样化的RLHF任务和真实人类反馈场景。该方法有望与离线强化学习、多任务学习结合，形成更高效的奖励建模工具，长期价值在于推动贝叶斯方法在偏好学习中的实用化。

### [56] MileGPO: Milestone Inference with Local Evidence for Graph-Based Policy Optimization of Long-Horizon LLM Agents  (id=2608.19803, 2026-08-21, rl=24, score=7.2)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用
- ABS: Credit assignment is challenging in long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning, where supervision often comes only from final rewards. Existing methods refine trajectory-level signals into step-level credits through step grouping or graph-based advantage estimation, but can overlook meaningful intermediate milestones. We propose MileGPO (Milestone Inference with Local Evidence for Graph-Based Policy Optimization), which derives process-level credit from grouped on-policy rollouts through three designs. Milestone Discovery identifies candidate milestones on successful rollouts and recurring traps on failed ones. Reliability-Calibrated Shaping (RCS) weights these candidates by outcome-based confidence, strengthening reliable milestones and traps while down-weighting uncertain ones. Progress-Contrastive Calibration (PCC) further tests whether a candidate reflects local progress and whether its incoming ansition outperforms observed alternatives from the same state.MileGPO requires neither auxiliary models nor additional environment interaction. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show state-of-the-art performance and a small in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap on ALFWorld. Ablations and credit diagnostics indicate that reliability weighting, local progress, and same-state branch evidence complement milestone discovery and resolve ambiguous intermediate credit.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对长程智能体强化学习中的信用分配难题，提出MileGPO方法，通过里程碑发现、可靠性校准塑形和进度对比校准三种设计，从分组在线轨迹中提取过程级信用。实验在ALFWorld和WebShop上取得最先进性能，并在ALFWorld上表现出较小的分布内到分布外差距。消融实验和信用诊断验证了各组件对解决模糊中间信用的有效性。
- innovations_zh: 提出里程碑发现机制，从成功轨迹中识别关键节点，从失败轨迹中识别重复陷阱; 设计可靠性校准塑形，基于结果置信度加权候选里程碑，抑制不确定信号; 引入进度对比校准，通过同状态分支对比验证候选是否反映局部进展
- limitations: 摘要未提供方法在更复杂或更长任务上的验证，且实验环境仅限ALFWorld和WebShop，泛化性未知。此外，里程碑发现依赖成功轨迹，可能受轨迹多样性影响，且未讨论计算开销或超参数敏感性。
- potential: 未来可探索将MileGPO扩展到更多真实世界任务，结合多模态或分层结构提升里程碑发现能力。长期价值在于推动无需密集奖励的智能体学习，为自动化任务规划提供更稳健的信用分配框架。

### [57] IterSynth: Rethinking Deep Search Agents via Role-Decoupled Iterative Synthesis  (id=2609.29444, 2026-09-25, rl=24, score=7.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Deep search requires LLM agents to decompose complex queries, search for evidence, and synthesize grounded answers, yet existing ReAct-style agents suffer from two limitations: role coupling, where one policy must handle planning, evidence use, and synthesis; and context accumulation, where growing search histories introduce noise and obscure useful information. To address these issues, we propose IterSynth, a role-decoupled and summary-based paradigm that alternates between a Planner for identifying information needs and a Synthesizer for integrating evidence into an evolving summary state. This design separates planning from synthesis while using the summary as the persistent state of search, reducing both capability coupling and context noise. To train IterSynth effectively, we further introduce Role-Decoupled Policy Optimization (RDPO) for reinforcement learning, which combines terminal outcome rewards with turn-level rubric evaluations and computes role-specific advantages for more precise credit assignment. Experiments on five long-horizon deep-search benchmarks such as BrowseComp and Xbench-DS show that IterSynth-8B achieves an average score of 50.7, surpassing the strongest prior $\leq$8B agent by +4.2\%. Moreover, IterSynth serves as a model-agnostic prompting paradigm, delivering substantial zero-shot gains over ReAct and similar prompting paradigms on frontier proprietary models.
- summary_zh: 针对深度搜索中ReAct式智能体存在的角色耦合与上下文累积两大问题，本文提出IterSynth，一种角色解耦且基于摘要的范式，让规划器与合成器交替工作，并以演化摘要作为持久搜索状态。为有效训练该范式，作者进一步提出角色解耦策略优化（RDPO），结合终端结果奖励与轮级评分标准评估，并计算角色特定优势以实现更精确的信用分配。在BrowseComp、Xbench-DS等五个长时程深度搜索基准上，IterSynth-8B平均得分50.7，超过最强≤8B智能体4.2%，同时作为模型无关的提示范式在前沿专有模型上相较ReAct等取得显著零样本提升。
- innovations_zh: 提出角色解耦与基于摘要的迭代合成范式，分离规划与合成并减少上下文噪声; 设计RDPO强化学习方法，融合终端结果奖励与轮级评分并计算角色特定优势; 验证该范式可作为模型无关提示策略，在专有模型上获得零样本增益
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了平均得分和相对提升，未披露各基准的具体表现、方差或统计显著性，评测细节有限。角色解耦与摘要机制可能引入额外推理开销或对摘要质量敏感，但摘要未讨论效率与鲁棒性。此外，实验主要围绕≤8B模型和若干专有模型，更广泛模型规模与任务类型的泛化性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可进一步探索角色解耦范式在更多多步推理与工具使用任务中的迁移，并研究摘要状态的自适应压缩与更新策略以平衡信息保留与噪声控制。RDPO的轮级评分与角色优势计算也可扩展到其他多角色协作智能体训练场景，长期有望推动深度搜索智能体向更可解释、可训练的方向发展。

### [58] DCRL: Decoupling and Coupling Reinforcement Learning via Policy-Reward Manifold Alignment  (id=2609.27572, 2026-09-24, rl=24, score=6.9)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing reward systems, such as rule-based and reward-model-based, often exhibit issues such as unstable optimization and reward hacking. In this work, we revisit the general reasoning of LLMs from a geometric perspective, conceptualizing it as a coupled manifold composed of three interdependent sub-manifolds: logical deduction, evaluation, and representation. Based on this perspective, response generation in RL can be interpreted as a decoupling process from the evaluation manifold, while reward estimation corresponds to a decoupling process from the logical deduction manifold. The limitations of rule-based and reward-model RL systems can be geometrically interpreted as the mismatch of policy-reward manifolds during RL process. To address the aforementioned misalignment, we propose Decoupling and Coupling Reinforcement Learning (DCRL) framework, which incorporates two key components: (1) a syllogistic logic-based prompt evolution mechanism that dynamically refines reward rubrics to enhance the expressiveness of the reward manifold; and (2) a policy-reward re-coupling mechanism that jointly updates the reward and policy models, ensuring consistent evaluation and mitigating manifold mismatch during training. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments across multiple reasoning domains demonstrate that DCRL consistently outperforms both rule-based and reward-model baselines. Notably, a Qwen3-4B model trained under DCRL surpasses a Qw
- summary_zh: 该论文针对现有基于规则和奖励模型的强化学习系统在提升大语言模型推理能力时存在优化不稳定和奖励黑客等问题，从几何视角将大语言模型的一般推理视为由逻辑演绎、评估和表示三个子流形构成的耦合流形。基于此，作者提出解耦与耦合强化学习框架，包含基于三段论逻辑的提示演化机制以动态优化奖励评分标准，以及策略-奖励重耦合机制以联合更新奖励和策略模型。理论分析和多领域实验表明，DCRL持续优于规则和奖励模型基线，且Qwen3-4B模型经DCRL训练后超越Qwen3-32B基线并接近Qwen3-235B性能。
- innovations_zh: 从几何流形视角重新解释大语言模型推理与强化学习中的策略-奖励不匹配问题; 提出基于三段论逻辑的提示演化机制，动态优化奖励评分标准以增强奖励流形表达力; 设计策略-奖励重耦合机制，联合更新奖励与策略模型以缓解训练中的流形不匹配
- limitations: 摘要仅提供高层描述，未给出具体实验设置、数据集、评测指标和消融分析，难以判断各组件贡献及泛化边界。理论分析的具体假设和适用范围未说明，几何视角的严谨性和可验证性有待全文确认。此外，方法依赖提示演化和联合更新，可能带来额外计算开销，但摘要未涉及效率讨论。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证该几何流形视角在更广泛任务和模型规模上的适用性，探索更高效的策略-奖励联合优化策略。可研究提示演化机制与重耦合机制的独立贡献及协同方式，并拓展至多模态推理或交互式学习场景，长期有望为强化学习与推理的几何理论融合提供基础。

### [59] Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards: Symbolic Backpropagation for Post-Training LLMs  (id=2608.28421, 2026-08-31, rl=24, score=6.7)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Post training a language model to reason means updating its weights. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning both place the acquired capability inside the model where it cannot be inspected cannot be checked step by step and cannot be moved to another model. We argue that for tasks whose intermediate steps admit verification, reasoning is better placed outside the base models weights as an explicit program composed from deterministic and neural primitives. We introduce PLVR (Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards): a post training method that learns such programs directly from input-output examples. Its mechanism is symbolic backpropagation: each program layer carries a typed ontology a loss is computed at the output against ground truth and required input ontologies are propagated backward by type inference over primitive signatures: an analogue of the chain rule in which credit assignment is a derivation rather than an estimate. Where RLVR verifies a terminal outcome, PLVRs reward is a per step contract verdict dense over program structure. On LiveCodeBench v6 and Tau2Bench, 30B base models with PLVR outperform RL at matched budget by 27.8 points on average and frontier models an order of magnitude larger by 13.6 points. A single primitive library serves two benchmarks, so the marginal cost of a new task is 100 examples of program search and no new finetuning data. Replacing the loss guided search with uniform sampling over the same type admissible space at equal budget collapses the median program from 65.6 to 17.5, identifying the backward pass rather than th
- summary_zh: 该论文提出PLVR方法，将推理能力从模型权重中移出，表示为显式程序，并通过符号反向传播进行后训练。方法在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B模型优于同预算的RL和更大规模的前沿模型。实验表明，符号反向传播是优势来源，且新任务仅需100个示例和少量搜索。
- innovations_zh: 将推理表示为显式程序，而非模型权重，实现可验证的逐步推理; 提出符号反向传播，通过类型推断进行逐层信用分配，替代传统梯度估计
- limitations: 摘要未提供方法在更广泛任务（如非可验证中间步骤）上的表现，适用范围可能有限。评测仅基于两个基准，且未与更大规模模型在同等数据条件下对比，泛化性需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将PLVR应用于更多领域，如科学推理或复杂规划，并扩展原始库的多样性。其符号反向传播思想可能启发新的可解释AI训练范式，长期价值在于推动推理过程的外部化与模块化。

### [60] A Verifier-Guided Explainable Reasoning Framework with Gold-Anchored QLoRA, Task-Aware Mixture-of-Experts, and Group-Relative RLVR  (id=2609.05221, 2026-09-07, rl=24, score=6.7)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 预训练与架构, 领域应用
- ABS: Large language models (LLMs) show strong reasoning ability, but their explanations can remain inconsistent, weakly grounded, or difficult to verify. We propose a verifier-guided explainable reasoning framework for transparent educational question answering that combines gold-anchored QLoRA, task-aware symbolic routing, and group-relative RLVR. Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct is first adapted with field-weighted QLoRA supervision anchored to authoritative answers. A lightweight router then assigns logic problems to a FOL/Z3 verifier and physics problems to a formula- and unit aware symbolic solver. Verifier feedback is further used to support candidate evaluation, self-revision, and reward construction during RLVR. Candidate responses are evaluated along three complementary dimensions: P1 for answer correctness, P2 for evidence or unit consistency, and P3 for reasoning depth and explainability. At inference, gold-free self-consistency aggregates multiple candidate responses before an optional question-only physics verifier performs conservative system-level correction. On 438 held-out examples, RLVR increases P3 from 50.68% to 72.20%, while hybrid P1 remains approximately stable at 55.94%. Self-consistency improves model only P1 from 48.86% to 50.23%, with symbolic verification providing the remaining hybrid gain. These results indicate that RLVR primarily strengthens explicit reasoning structure, while symbolic verification complements the neural policy by improving answer reliability at the system level.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出了一种验证器引导的可解释推理框架，用于透明教育问答，结合了金锚定QLoRA、任务感知的符号路由和分组相对RLVR。在438个保留样本上，RLVR将推理深度指标P3从50.68%提升至72.20%，而混合答案正确率P1稳定在55.94%。自一致性仅小幅提升模型P1，符号验证在系统层面补充了神经策略，提高了答案可靠性。

- limitations: 摘要仅基于438个保留样本，规模较小且领域限于逻辑和物理，泛化性未知。混合P1提升有限，且符号验证依赖特定求解器，可能难以扩展到更开放或非形式化的问题。

### [61] LEEPS: Latent-Guided Explore-Exploit Prompt Sampling for Efficient RLVR in Large Language Models  (id=2607.28077, 2026-09-17, rl=24, score=6.5)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but prompt groups with identical rollout rewards consume generation budget without effective learning signals. Pre-rollout prompt selection can reduce this waste by screening prompts before rollout generation. However, existing pre-rollout methods struggle to balance exploitation and exploration: repeatedly exploiting historically informative prompts can narrow training coverage, whereas broader exploration can lower the fraction of informative prompts. To address these limitations, we introduce LEEPS, a Latent-Guided Explore--Exploit Prompt Sampler that adaptively balances the reuse of previously observed informative prompts with continued exploration of uncertain ones. LEEPS partitions candidates into exploit and explore portfolios and adaptively allocates rollout budget according to their recent non-trivial ratios. It further uses representation-space neighbors and historical rollout outcomes to prioritize uncertain prompts likely to yield non-zero reward variance, thereby making exploration more targeted without additional rollouts. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, LEEPS achieves the highest average score at both model scales, with relative gains of 2.6\% and 3.7\% over the strongest baseline for Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and 7B, respectively, and generally improves faster during the training process. It also achieves the highest average score across the three evaluated OOD general-reasoning benchmarks at both model scales and adds only about 2 second
- summary_zh: 该论文针对RLVR中相同rollout奖励的提示组浪费生成预算、缺乏有效学习信号的问题，提出预rollout提示选择方法LEEPS。LEEPS将候选提示划分为利用与探索两个组合，依据近期非平凡比率自适应分配rollout预算，并利用表示空间邻居和历史rollout结果优先选择可能产生非零奖励方差的不确定提示。在六个数学推理基准上，LEEPS在两个模型规模均取得最高平均分，相对最强基线分别提升2.6%和3.7%，并在三个OOD通用推理基准上同样最高，每训练步仅增加约2秒在线采样开销。
- innovations_zh: 将提示选择分为利用与探索组合，按近期非平凡比率自适应分配rollout预算; 利用表示空间邻居和历史rollout结果定位可能产生非零奖励方差的不确定提示; 无需额外rollout即可实现更 targeted 的探索，降低无效生成开销
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了六个数学推理基准和三个OOD通用推理基准的结果，未说明其他任务类型或更大模型规模上的泛化性。评测主要依赖平均分和相对增益，未披露方差、显著性检验或与更多预rollout方法的全面对比。此外，约2秒每训练步的在线采样开销在大规模训练中的累积影响尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索将LEEPS的自适应预算分配与不确定性优先策略扩展到数学推理之外的更多RLVR任务，如代码生成、多跳问答等。可进一步研究表示空间邻居构建方式与历史结果利用的鲁棒性，以及在不同模型规模和训练阶段下的长期收益。若能与更高效的rollout生成或课程学习结合，可能进一步降低训练成本并提升推理能力。

Two normative frameworks are instantiated and evaluated: a Utilitarian framework minimizing total casualties and a Kan- tian framework enforcing course maintenance as a categori- cal imperative. The EDH is trained via Proximal Policy Op- timization (PPO) [Schulman et al., 2017] against a Bradley- Terry reward model [Bradley and Terry, 1952] learned from pairwise human preference annotations over 200 collision- imminent scenarios. Results reveal an asymmetry in the learnability of normative ethical frameworks under human su- pervision. The Kantian condition, which reduces to a con- stant prediction task under the codebook, serves as a pipeline control: it confirms training stability and rules out infrastruc- ture failure as an explanation for the utilitarian result. The Utilitarian agent learned something more unsettling: human raters rewarded self-sacrifice over casualty minimization, and the model learned that preference faithfully. This divergence between what humans prescribe in theory and what
- summary_zh: 本文提出伦理决策头（EDH），一种基于深度强化学习的框架，通过将伦理推理编码为可微分的奖励信号，使自动驾驶车辆在碰撞迫近场景中学习符合道德规范的驾驶行为。研究实例化了功利主义（最小化伤亡）和康德主义（保持车道作为绝对命令）两种规范伦理框架，并使用基于人类成对偏好标注的Bradley-Terry奖励模型，通过PPO算法训练。结果显示，康德主义条件作为管道控制验证了训练稳定性，而功利主义智能体学习到人类评分者更偏好自我牺牲而非伤亡最小化，揭示了人类在理论上规定的伦理与实践奖励之间的分歧。

- potential: 未来可扩展至更多伦理框架（如美德伦理）和更丰富的驾驶场景，并探索如何调和人类理论规范与实际奖励之间的冲突，以设计更符合社会期望的自动驾驶伦理决策系统。

- ABS: Agent trajectories record what an agent does and what happens next. Yet standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies loss only to agent-authored action tokens, using environment observations as context but not as prediction targets. We ask whether this convention provides the best initialization for subsequent reinforcement learning. We introduce ActObs, which also supervises the observation tokens already present in each trajectory. Although deployed agents never generate observations, learning to predict them encourages the policy to model action consequences without adding data, parameters, sequence tokens, or forward passes. The methods perform similarly after SFT but diverge after GRPO. On Qwen3-4B, GRPO from ActObs achieves higher pass@k at every evaluated sampling budget than its action-only counterpart on Terminal-Bench 2.0. On Qwen3-8B, it trades some pass@1 reliability for higher pass@k (+3.4 pp at pass@16) and solves more distinct tasks. The advantage extends to cross-domain code editing on aider-polyglot (+4.2 pp at pass@1 at 4B), whose tasks are unseen during SFT and RL. ActObs retains more entropy during RL while requiring less policy movement, leaving the final policy closer to its SFT initialization. Our analysis traces this difference to SFT: action and observation gradients rapidly become orthogonal, while action-only training leaves a large residual observation gradient and degrades environment prediction below the base model. Joint supervision prevents this one-sided specialization, preserving consequence prediction and preparing the policy for downstre
- summary_zh: 该研究质疑标准SFT仅对动作token计算损失、将环境观测仅作上下文而不作为预测目标的惯例，提出ActObs方法，同时监督轨迹中已有的观测token。实验显示两种方法在SFT后表现相近，但在GRPO后出现分化：在Qwen3-4B的Terminal-Bench 2.0上，ActObs在各采样预算下pass@k更高；在Qwen3-8B上以部分pass@1可靠性换取更高pass@k（pass@16提升3.4个百分点）并解决更多不同任务。优势还扩展到SFT与RL均未见过的aider-polyglot跨域代码编辑任务（4B上pass@1提升4.2个百分点），且ActObs在RL中保留更高熵、所需策略移动更少，最终策略更接近SFT初始化。
- innovations_zh: 对轨迹中已有观测token施加监督，无需新增数据、参数、序列token或前向传播; 揭示动作与观测梯度在SFT中迅速正交，仅动作训练会残留大观测梯度并损害环境预测; 联合监督防止单侧特化，保留后果预测能力并为下游RL探索做准备
- limitations: 结论主要基于Qwen3-4B与Qwen3-8B及Terminal-Bench 2.0、aider-polyglot等特定基准，跨模型规模与任务类型的普适性尚不明确。摘要未报告与更多SFT变体或RL算法的对比，也未说明观测监督在观测空间庞大或噪声较高时是否同样有效。此外，pass@1与pass@k之间的权衡在8B上表现明显，实际部署中如何取舍缺乏指导。
- potential: 未来可探索观测监督在不同模态、不同RL算法及更大模型上的泛化性，并研究观测预测目标的设计（如加权、选择性监督）以缓解pass@1与pass@k的权衡。长期看，该方向可能推动SFT从“模仿动作”转向“建模环境动态”，为构建更具探索性与样本效率的智能体提供基础。

### [64] GTA-RAG: Graph-Trajectory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning  (id=2608.22479, 2026-08-25, rl=23, score=9.6)
- cats: RAG 与知识增强, 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enables LLMs to access external knowledge for answering knowledge-intensive questions. For complex multi-hop questions, multi-turn retrieval-augmented reasoning extends RAG into an iterative process that repeatedly searches for and integrates evidence across documents. However, existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches for agentic RAG are typically optimized with final-answer rewards, which provide sparse supervision and overlook whether the model actually retrieves the required evidence chain. We present \textsc{GTA-RAG}, a graph-trajectory-augmented RL framework for multi-turn retrieval-augmented reasoning. From an entity--document graph, we sample connected document paths, synthesize multi-hop QA trajectories, and validate them with the deployed retriever to obtain executable trajectory-level supervision. We then optimize the retrieval policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and a trajectory-guided reward that encourages both accurate answers and acquisition of target evidence documents, followed by answer-reward training on natural QA instances. Experiments on three multi-hop and two simple QA benchmarks show that \method{} consistently outperforms RL-based RAG baselines with both Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-7B backbones, while substantially improving evidence-chain coverage. Our code is available at https://github.com/cjcj46262/GTA-RAG.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出GTA-RAG框架，针对多轮检索增强推理中强化学习奖励稀疏、忽视证据链获取的问题。方法从实体-文档图采样路径并合成多跳QA轨迹，用检索器验证后提供轨迹级监督，结合GRPO和轨迹引导奖励优化检索策略。在三个多跳和两个简单QA基准上，使用Qwen2.5-3B/7B均优于RL基线，并显著提升证据链覆盖率。
- innovations_zh: 利用实体-文档图采样路径合成多跳QA轨迹，提供可执行的轨迹级监督; 设计轨迹引导奖励，同时鼓励答案准确性和目标证据文档获取; 结合GRPO与轨迹奖励，再在自然QA实例上训练答案奖励
- limitations: 摘要未提供详细消融实验或计算开销分析，轨迹合成依赖图质量可能限制泛化。评测仅基于QA基准，未涉及开放域或动态知识场景，且未报告失败案例或鲁棒性分析。
- potential: 未来可探索将轨迹监督扩展到更复杂推理任务或跨模态检索，结合在线学习适应动态知识库。长期价值在于推动RL与图结构结合，实现更可解释、证据驱动的检索推理系统。

- ABS: Long-horizon agentic tasks require large language models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve, integrate, and maintain dispersed information across multi-turn interactions, but preserving all interaction histories leads to a continuously growing working context. Recent proactive context management methods allow models to edit their own working context with specialized tools, yet they still face three key limitations: (1) a limited toolset restricted to search, deletion, and summarization, with no support for global planning, long-term memory, and adaptive compression; (2) inefficient exploration that treats context management actions uniformly despite their heterogeneous impacts on final outcomes; and (3) coarse-grained credit assignment that assigns the final trajectory-level reward to all intermediate context editing actions during RL. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ContextPilot, a proactive context management framework for long-horizon agentic reasoning. Our approach systematically augments the toolset with planning, long-term memory, and soft context offloading tools. We further propose an RL method tailored for context management, which uses context and entropy variation to identify critical editing decisions for branch sampling and estimates action-level advantages from all branched trajectories that pass through the corresponding context editing action. - ABS: Large reasoning models can produce correct yet unnecessarily long reasoning traces. Existing methods improve reasoning efficiency with trajectory-level objectives or local token- and step-level signals, but rarely model inter-step semantic dependencies. This limits their ability to distinguish redundant steps from those that support later deductions, making it harder to shorten reasoning without sacrificing accuracy. We introduce RECAP (REdundancy-aware Credit Assignment via Propagation), which addresses this limitation by assigning credit where it is due based on both a step's downstream role in the reasoning structure and its contribution to solving the problem correctly. We define structural responsibility to capture the step's downstream role by measuring how strongly later reasoning depends on it, using credit propagated backward from the final-answer node through an outcome-independent, LLM-annotated semantic dependency graph. However, a step can have high structural responsibility yet steer the reasoning away from the correct solution. RECAP therefore introduces step efficacy to measure answer-directed progress through changes in gold-answer log-likelihood as each step is added. Together, these signals reshape rollout-level GRPO advantages into step-specific updates. RECAP requires neither a separately trained process reward model nor preconstructed concise trajectories. Across two 7B models and four mathematical reasoning benchmarks, RECAP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen2.5-Math-7B, it improves pass@1 by 2.0-3.7 percentage points while reducing r
- summary_zh: 大型推理模型常生成正确但冗长的推理链，现有方法多依赖轨迹级目标或局部信号，难以建模步骤间语义依赖，因而无法区分冗余步骤与支撑后续推导的步骤。本文提出RECAP，通过LLM标注的语义依赖图从最终答案节点反向传播信用，定义结构责任，并结合基于金答案对数似然变化的步骤有效性，将rollout级GRPO优势重塑为步骤级更新。在两个7B模型和四个数学推理基准上，RECAP改善了准确率与效率的权衡，在Qwen2.5-Math-7B上pass@1提升2.0-3.7个百分点，同时推理token减少8%-31%。

### [67] Connect the Dots: Training LLMs for Long-Lifecycle Agents with Cross-Domain Generalization Via Reinforcement Learning  (id=2606.20002, 2026-09-23-补充, rl=23, score=7.0)

- ABS: This work presents a general framework for training large language models (LLMs) to "Connect the Dots" (CoD), a meta-capability required by long-lifecycle agents: as an LLM-based AI agent gets deployed in an environment, it solves a long sequence of tasks while continuously exploring the environment, learning from its own experiences, and iteratively self-updating its context about the environment, thereby achieving progressively better performance on future tasks conditioned on the updated context. Major components of the CoD framework include: (1) algorithm design and infrastructure for end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) with long rollout sequences interleaving solve-task and update-context episodes; (2) tasks and environments for incentivizing and eliciting the targeted meta-capability in LLMs during training, as well as for faithfully measuring progress during evaluation. We present proof-of-concept implementations of the CoD framework, including a GRPO-style RL algorithm with fine-grained credit assignment, as well as tasks and environments tailored to the targeted meta-capability (rather than domain-specific LLM capabilities or standard task-by-task RL). Empirical results with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.6-27B validate the efficacy of end-to-end RL training in the CoD setting, and demonstrate the potential for out-of-distribution generalization -- within the training domains, across different domains, and from CoD to Ralph-loop settings -- of the elicited meta-capability. Our investigation of CoD connects several lines of prior work, and opens up new opportunities for adva
- summary_zh: 该工作提出训练大语言模型具备“Connect the Dots”（CoD）元能力的通用框架，以支撑长生命周期智能体在环境中持续探索、从经验中学习并迭代更新上下文。框架包含端到端强化学习算法与基础设施，以及用于激发和评估该元能力的任务与环境。作者实现了带细粒度信用分配的GRPO风格RL算法，并用Qwen3-8B和Qwen3.6-27B验证了端到端RL训练的有效性，展示了在训练域内、跨域以及从CoD到Ralph-loop设置的分布外泛化潜力。
- innovations_zh: 提出CoD元能力框架，面向长生命周期智能体的持续自更新; 设计交错求解与上下文更新长序列的端到端RL算法与基础设施; 采用细粒度信用分配的GRPO风格RL算法进行训练; 构建激发与评估CoD元能力的任务环境，并验证跨域泛化
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了概念验证结果，未给出具体评测指标、任务规模或基线对比，难以判断方法的实际增益与稳定性。泛化验证限于训练域内、跨域及Ralph-loop设置，尚未覆盖更开放或真实世界的长周期场景。此外，框架对基础设施和长序列RL训练的要求可能带来较高计算成本。
- potential: 未来可进一步扩展至更多样、更长期的真实环境，检验CoD元能力的持续性与鲁棒性。可探索更高效的信用分配与训练策略，降低长序列RL的开销。若元能力可稳定迁移，将推动LLM智能体在自主探索、持续学习与跨域适应方面的发展。

### [68] Rethinking Critic Learning in PPO: Understanding and Mitigating Value Flattening  (id=2609.18708, 2026-09-17, rl=23, score=6.7)
- cats: 评测与基准
- ABS: In reinforcement learning for large language models, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) commonly uses a critic to estimate state values and reduce the variance of policy updates. However, we uncover a systematic failure mode in PPO critics, which we call Value Flattening: state values, estimated from multiple Monte Carlo continuations, change sharply across intermediate states while critic predictions remain comparatively flat. We further observe this phenomenon in a controlled FrozenLake environment and find that it becomes more pronounced as the state space grows. Our theoretical and empirical analyses relate Value Flattening to an implicit variance penalty in the critic loss and redundant updates from temporally correlated states with similar gradients. Motivated by these findings, we introduce SParse Proximal Policy Optimization (SP$^3$O), which applies the value loss to only a few well-separated states in each response to mitigate both effects. Experiments on Qwen3-Base show that SP$^3$O with only three states supervised per response can mitigate Value Flattening and consistently improve the learned policy across model sizes and evaluation suites. Together, our results identify Value Flattening as an important yet overlooked failure mode of critic learning in standard PPO and show that a simple sparse supervision strategy can mitigate it.
- summary_zh: 该研究指出PPO中critic存在一种系统性失效模式，称为Value Flattening，即由多条蒙特卡洛延续估计的状态值在中间状态间剧烈变化，而critic预测却相对平坦。作者在FrozenLake环境中验证该现象，并发现其随状态空间增大而更明显，将其归因于critic损失中的隐式方差惩罚和时序相关状态的冗余更新。为此提出SP3O，仅对每个回复中少量且间隔良好的状态施加价值损失，在Qwen3-Base上以每回复三个状态监督即可缓解Value Flattening并稳定提升策略表现。
- innovations_zh: 识别并命名PPO critic中的Value Flattening失效模式; 从隐式方差惩罚与冗余更新角度解释其成因; 提出SP3O，仅稀疏监督少量间隔状态的价值损失
- limitations: 摘要仅给出Qwen3-Base上的实验，未说明是否覆盖其他模型家族或更大规模模型，泛化性仍不确定。Value Flattening的度量方式、理论分析与实验细节在摘要中未展开，且SP3O仅监督三个状态虽简单，但最优稀疏程度与状态选择策略可能依赖任务。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究Value Flattening在不同RL算法与任务中的普遍性，并探索自适应选择监督状态的方法。长期看，若能将稀疏价值监督与更稳健的critic目标结合，可能提升大语言模型强化学习的稳定性与可扩展性。

### [69] Le Critique: Privileged Value Functions for LLM Reinforcement Learning  (id=2608.16739, 2026-08-18, rl=23, score=6.6)
- cats: 推理与数学
- ABS: Reinforcement learning algorithms for Large Language Models (LLMs) are largely distinguished by their variance reduction strategy. Group-relative methods like GRPO reduce gradient variance by sampling multiple rollouts per prompt, but provide only sequence-level credit. Training is also blocked by straggler rollouts, reducing throughput and increasing off-policyness. Learned value functions theoretically address both problems, providing token-level advantages without requiring large groups. However, additional infrastructure engineering challenges combined with the practical success of critic-free methods have made it difficult to justify their inclusion in RL pipelines. We propose two complementary strategies to improve the performance of value function RL: 1) Privileged Value Functions (PVF) which provide an elegant mechanism to inject additional task-relevant token-level signal without biasing the policy objective; 2) TETHER, a baseline that adaptively interpolates between group-relative and value baselines depending on the value function accuracy. Across several reasoning tasks, both strategies consistently improve over the standard value function baseline, and are competitive with or outperform mean-baseline GRPO.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型强化学习中的方差缩减问题，提出两种互补策略：特权价值函数（PVF）和自适应基线TETHER。PVF通过注入任务相关的token级信号而不偏置策略目标，TETHER则根据价值函数准确性在组相对基线与价值基线间自适应插值。在多个推理任务上，两种策略均优于标准价值函数基线，并与均值基线GRPO相当或更优。
- innovations_zh: 提出特权价值函数（PVF），在不偏置策略目标的前提下注入额外token级任务信号; 设计TETHER基线，根据价值函数准确性自适应混合组相对与价值基线
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体任务类型、数据集规模及计算开销的详细分析，限制了泛化性判断。评测仅基于推理任务，未覆盖对话、生成等场景，且未报告与GRPO的统计显著性差异。
- potential: 未来可探索PVF在不同任务族（如代码生成、多轮对话）中的适用性，并研究TETHER自适应机制的理论性质。长期来看，该工作可能推动价值函数在LLM RL中的重新普及，促进更高效、更稳定的训练范式。

- ABS: Enabling human stakeholders to specify reward functions that lead to their desired outcomes is a key challenge in deploying reinforcement learning agents. Preference-based methods such as online RLHF can reduce the burden of manual reward design, but they require repeatedly training policies, sampling trajectories from the real world, and eliciting feedback, making them impractical in settings where environment interaction is computationally expensive or unsafe. We introduce Experience-Free Autonomous Reward Specification (EARS), a method for learning reward functions from preferences without environment interaction. Our approach uses a structured LLM-mediated process to construct a small set of expressive reward features from a task description and the environment observation space, then strategically samples imagined trajectories in this feature space and learns feature weights from preferences over the imagined trajectory pairs. We evaluate on three long-horizon domains: pandemic lockdown regulation design, insulin administration for diabetes patients, and autonomous vehicle control on a highway. We compare EARS to baselines that also enable reward specification without environment interaction--namely, methods that directly prompt an LLM to generate a reward function. When learning from either ground-truth preference labels or preferences labeled by a LLM, EARS designs reward functions that are more aligned with the ground truth reward function that produced the preferences or LLM context than these baselines. These results suggest that preference-based reward specificat
- summary_zh: 该研究关注如何让人类利益相关者指定能实现其目标的奖励函数，指出现有基于偏好的在线RLHF方法需要反复训练策略、采样真实轨迹并获取反馈，在环境交互昂贵或不安全时不可行。作者提出EARS方法，通过LLM介导的结构化过程从任务描述和观测空间构建奖励特征，在特征空间中采样想象轨迹，并从想象轨迹对的偏好中学习特征权重，从而无需环境交互即可学习奖励函数。在疫情封锁调控、糖尿病患者胰岛素管理和高速公路自动驾驶三个长时域任务上，与直接提示LLM生成奖励函数的基线相比，EARS在基于真实偏好标签或LLM标注偏好学习时，设计的奖励函数更接近产生偏好的真实奖励函数或LLM上下文。
- innovations_zh: 无需环境交互，仅从偏好学习奖励函数; 用LLM构建奖励特征并采样想象轨迹; 在特征空间学习权重，避免真实轨迹采样
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了三个长时域域上的结果，未说明方法在更广泛任务、不同观测空间或复杂偏好结构下的泛化能力。评测主要比较与真实奖励函数或LLM上下文的一致性，未涉及最终策略性能、样本效率或对LLM质量的敏感性。此外，想象轨迹采样与特征构建依赖LLM介导过程，其可靠性和偏差尚未在摘要中讨论。
- potential: 未来可探索将EARS扩展到更多真实世界高风险决策场景，并研究其与离线强化学习、模型基强化学习的结合。可进一步分析LLM生成特征的质量、偏好标注噪声的影响以及最终策略性能，从而推动无需环境交互的奖励设计在安全关键领域中的长期应用。

### [71] Beyond Forgetting: Diagnosing and Harnessing Shared Reasoning in Continual RLVR  (id=2608.18574, 2026-09-25, rl=23, score=5.3)
- cats: 推理与数学, 多模态
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) commonly post-trains reasoning models on multiple tasks, while rerunning multitask RLVR (MTRL) as new tasks are added makes capability expansion costly. We therefore study continual RLVR, which updates the existing model as each task arrives. The central question is whether a model updated this way can perform as well as a jointly trained model. To answer this question, we introduce Continual Reasoning Gym, a continual-RLVR environment that organizes text and visual reasoning tasks into five task sequences. In this setting, we identify two key observations: Sequential RLVR exhibits modest forgetting, yet its final performance remains below that of MTRL. To understand the latter, we decompose final performance and show that forgetting accounts for only part of the gap. To explain the former, we identify shared reasoning: transferable reasoning structure allows training on one task to support others on average. We therefore introduce Continual Prompt Replay (CPR), which harnesses shared reasoning to improve learning on the arriving and future tasks by replaying previous-task prompts and regenerating their responses with the current policy. On average, only CPR reaches MTRL-level performance.
- summary_zh: 论文研究持续式RLVR（continual RLVR），即在每个任务到来时更新已有模型，核心问题是其能否达到多任务联合训练（MTRL）的水平。作者提出Continual Reasoning Gym环境，将文本与视觉推理任务组织为五条任务序列。实验发现顺序RLVR存在适度遗忘，但最终性能仍低于MTRL，且遗忘只能解释部分差距；同时识别出可迁移的共享推理结构。为此提出Continual Prompt Replay（CPR），通过回放旧任务提示并用当前策略重新生成回答，平均而言仅CPR达到MTRL级性能。
- innovations_zh: 提出Continual Reasoning Gym持续RLVR环境，含五条文本与视觉推理任务序列; 识别共享推理：可迁移推理结构使任务间训练相互支持; 提出CPR，回放旧任务提示并用当前策略重生成回答以利用共享推理
- limitations: 摘要仅给出平均结果，未说明各任务序列的方差、CPR相对MTRL的具体差距及计算开销。评测局限于自建的Continual Reasoning Gym五条序列，向其他任务域或更大模型的泛化性未知。共享推理的机制分析较初步，CPR中提示回放与重生成的各自贡献未拆解。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更多模态与任务类型，验证共享推理的普适性，并探索更高效的提示回放策略以降低重生成成本。长期看，若能将共享推理显式建模，或可推动持续RLVR在真实动态任务流中接近甚至超越联合训练，减少重复训练开销。

### [72] GMTS: Gradient Magnitude-based Token Selection Improves RLVR Training for LLM Reasoning  (id=2608.30632, 2026-09-01, rl=22, score=8.2)
- cats: 推理与数学
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL), particularly RL with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), has recently emerged as a central paradigm for enhancing large language models' (LLMs) reasoning abilities, demonstrating remarkable effectiveness across reasoning tasks. Recent studies suggest that high-entropy tokens play an exceptionally important role in model training, since training with only the highest 20% entropy tokens yields significant performance gains. However, why such high-entropy tokens are beneficial remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we find that although high-entropy tokens within one answer tend to correlate with large gradient magnitude, entropy alone fails to consistently reflect token importance across different answers, considering the variations in the answer-level reward signals. Based on this observation, we introduce the Gradient Magnitude-based Token Selection (GMTS) method to quantify token importance, which leverages the entropy-gradient connection to approximate gradient-magnitude rankings for token selection. We find that training on the top 20% tokens ranked by GMTS consistently outperforms entropy-based token selection across three reasoning domains and various model sizes, suggesting that GMTS provides a more fine-grained estimate of token contribution for RLVR training.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对RLVR训练中高熵token选择的有效性提出质疑，发现熵在不同答案间不能一致反映token重要性。基于熵与梯度幅度的关联，提出GMTS方法近似估计token梯度幅度排名，并选择前20%的token进行训练。实验表明，GMTS在三个推理领域和多种模型规模上均优于基于熵的token选择方法，提供了更细粒度的token贡献估计。
- innovations_zh: 首次揭示熵与梯度幅度在答案间的不一致关系，指出熵作为token重要性指标的局限性; 提出基于梯度幅度排名的GMTS方法，利用熵-梯度关联近似计算token重要性，无需额外计算梯度; 在多个推理任务和模型规模上验证GMTS优于熵基线，提供更细粒度的token贡献估计
- limitations: 摘要未提供GMTS在不同训练阶段或不同奖励信号下的稳定性分析，适用范围可能有限。评测主要基于三个推理领域，未涉及更广泛任务或真实世界场景，泛化性需进一步验证。此外，GMTS依赖熵-梯度近似，其近似误差对最终性能的影响未明确讨论。
- potential: 未来可探索GMTS与其他token选择策略（如基于损失或不确定性）的结合，或将其扩展到多模态推理任务。长期来看，梯度信息驱动的token选择可能成为RLVR训练的标准组件，助力更高效的大模型推理优化。

### [73] Breaking Failure Cascades: Step-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Medical Multimodal Reasoning  (id=2606.31825, 2026-09-25, rl=22, score=7.6)
- cats: 推理与数学, 多模态, 领域应用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Recent multimodal large language models have shown great promise in clinical image reasoning, but existing post-training pipelines remain predominantly outcome-centric, relying on final answer correctness or sequence-level preferences. This suffers from sparse credit assignment, making it difficult to optimize the reasoning process essential for clinical applications. Our analysis reveals that cascading errors from early-stage reasoning failures are a leading cause of incorrect predictions in medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmarks. Motivated by this, we propose Medical Reasoning-aware Policy Optimization (MRPO), an RL algorithm that incorporates step-wise process rewards. When the final answer is incorrect, MRPO assigns exponentially larger penalties to tokens in earlier invalid reasoning steps, breaking failure cascades without compromising successful paths. Across four multimodal LLM backbones, MRPO consistently outperforms standard GRPO and a recent RL baseline, and on Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking even surpasses substantially larger medical MLLMs such as HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B by 4.59 points. Moreover, MRPO reduces early-stage reasoning failures from 58.6% to 13.4%, showing that targeted mitigation of cascading failures improves both reasoning quality and final answer accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/dmis-lab/MRPO
- summary_zh: 该研究针对医疗多模态大模型后训练以最终答案正确性为导向、导致稀疏信用分配的问题，发现早期推理失败引发的级联错误是医疗VQA预测错误的主要原因。为此提出MRPO强化学习算法，在最终答案错误时对早期无效推理步骤中的token施加指数级更大惩罚，从而打断失败级联且不损害成功路径。在四个多模态LLM骨干上，MRPO持续优于标准GRPO和近期RL基线，在Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking上超过HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B达4.59分，并将早期推理失败率从58.6%降至13.4%。
- innovations_zh: 引入步骤级过程奖励，缓解最终答案导向的稀疏信用分配问题; 对早期无效推理步骤token施加指数级更大惩罚以打断失败级联; 在不损害成功推理路径的前提下提升推理质量与答案准确率
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了医疗VQA基准上的结果，未说明在其他临床任务或真实临床场景中的泛化性。评测主要基于答案准确率和早期推理失败率，缺乏对推理过程质量更细粒度的评估。此外，指数惩罚的具体设计、超参数敏感性及与不同骨干的适配成本未在摘要中说明。
- potential: 未来可探索将步骤感知奖励扩展到更多临床推理任务与多轮交互场景，并研究更细粒度的过程监督信号。长期看，若能在真实临床决策中验证其减少级联错误的效果，将有助于提升医疗多模态系统的可靠性与可解释性。

The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. ### [75] Pairwise Ranking Outperforms Single-Action RL for Offline Explanation Selection: A Practical Lesson  (id=2608.18531, 2026-09-23-补充, rl=22, score=7.0)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Industrial explainable-recommendation systems built on LLMs incur a substantial serving cost: each request triggers an LLM generation, with latency in the hundreds of milliseconds and cost that scales linearly with traffic. We separate generation from selection: explanations are produced ahead of time as a frozen candidate pool (six prompt styles, two commodity LLMs), and a small CPU-resident selector picks one at request time. The stack needs no GPU and returns in under 100 ms. Our primary benchmark is a 2,958-pair XRec Google Local subset, evaluating six offline-pool selectors (LambdaRank, PPO, GRPO, DPO, teacher-student distillation) and three KG-path selectors (random walks, edge-disjoint enumeration, MMR-reranked paths). A 300-pair MovieLens-1M split with Claude-Sonnet-4.5 references serves as an internal cross-dataset check, since no public benchmark exists for this setting. All variants use the same BERTScore-F1 protocol as XRec and G-Refer, averaged across five seeds. LambdaRank reaches F1 = 0.500 on Google Local, exceeding both G-Refer and XRec, and F1 = 0.329 on the MovieLens-1M check. With seed variance below 0.003 F1, the ordering is reliable: pairwise learning-to-rank outperforms single-action RL (PPO, GRPO, DPO), which use only one labelled candidate per rollout, leaving K-1 labels unused. The KG-path family targets a different objective: all three variants reach USR = 1.000 on Google Local and 0.997-1.000 on MovieLens-1M, since per-request path grounding yields a unique output per query, avoiding template-collapse failures affecting cached-LLM outputs. A gene
- summary_zh: 该研究针对基于LLM的可解释推荐系统服务成本高、延迟大的问题，将解释生成与选择分离：预先用六种提示风格和两个商用LLM构建冻结候选池，请求时由CPU常驻的小型选择器在100毫秒内完成选择，无需GPU。在2958对XRec Google Local子集上评测六种离线池选择器与三种KG路径选择器，并以300对MovieLens-1M作为跨数据集校验，统一采用BERTScore-F1协议、五次种子平均。结果显示LambdaRank在Google Local上F1达0.500，超过G-Refer和XRec，在MovieLens-1M上为0.329，种子方差低于0.003，成对学习排序优于单动作RL（PPO、GRPO、DPO）；KG路径族因逐请求路径接地可达USR=1.000，避免模板坍缩。
- innovations_zh: 将LLM解释生成与选择解耦，用冻结候选池加CPU选择器实现无GPU、百毫秒内响应; 发现成对学习排序优于单动作RL，因后者每次rollout仅用一个标注候选、浪费K-1个标签; KG路径选择器以逐请求路径接地实现唯一输出，规避缓存LLM输出的模板坍缩问题; 验证选择器与生成器可独立评估，更换生成器仅小幅影响F1且保持选择器排序
- limitations: 评测主要依赖2958对的XRec Google Local子集，MovieLens-1M仅300对且使用Claude-Sonnet-4.5参考，规模有限且缺乏公开基准，结论的泛化性有待验证。绝对F1依赖生成器，且KG路径族以USR为指标、与F1目标不同，跨族比较的公平性受限。此外，摘要未报告与在线A/B或真实用户满意度相关的验证。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更大规模、多领域、多语言的公开基准，并引入在线A/B与用户研究验证离线指标与真实体验的一致性。可进一步探索成对排序与KG路径选择的融合、生成器与选择器的联合优化，以及将冻结候选池范式推广到其他LLM密集型推荐与解释场景，长期有望形成低成本、可独立迭代的工业解释服务架构。

### [76] GSAR: Goal-State-Anchor Rewards for Mobile GUI Agents with Self-Evolving Data Synthesis  (id=2608.22847, 2026-08-25, rl=22, score=6.8)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 多模态, 评测与基准
- ABS: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) based GUI agents stand to benefit significantly from online reinforcement learning (RL). However, their training is bottlenecked by two fundamental issues: current data synthesis methods for GUI Agents rely on specific environments and struggle to generate diverse data, while existing evaluators either suffer from limited scalability or provide inaccurate and unreliable reward signals. To overcome these challenges, we introduce GSAR (Goal-State-Anchor Reward), a RL reward framework that supports scalable task generation and delivers reliable reward signals for stable and efficient policy optimization. Our approach features self-evolving data synthesis, which produces multiple environments through task execution and generates diverse tasks and goal states. Complementing this, a state-anchor mechanism automatically annotates task-relevant UI elements in successful goal states as reference anchors. During RL training, these reference anchors provide accurate, scalable reward signals that substantially enhance efficiency. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves over 90% accuracy on offline trajectory verification and performs closest to rule-based methods. Furthermore, agents trained using our reward framework exhibit strong performance on both AndroidWorld and our constructed benchmark, establishing a scalable approach for GUI agent training.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对基于视觉语言模型的GUI智能体在在线强化学习训练中面临的数据合成依赖特定环境、多样性不足以及奖励信号不准确或不可扩展的问题，提出了GSAR（目标状态锚定奖励）框架。该框架通过自进化数据合成生成多样化的任务与目标状态，并利用状态锚定机制自动标注成功目标状态中的相关UI元素作为参考锚点，在强化学习训练中提供准确且可扩展的奖励信号。实验表明，该框架在离线轨迹验证上准确率超过90%，性能接近基于规则的方法，且训练出的智能体在AndroidWorld和自建基准上表现强劲。
- innovations_zh: 提出自进化数据合成方法，通过任务执行生成多环境与多样化任务及目标状态; 设计状态锚定机制，自动标注成功目标状态中的UI元素作为参考锚点，提供准确可扩展的奖励信号
- limitations: 摘要未提供与现有数据合成或奖励方法在更多任务类型上的对比细节，且评估主要基于AndroidWorld和自建基准，可能缺乏跨平台泛化验证。此外，自进化数据合成和状态锚定机制在复杂或动态UI环境中的鲁棒性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索将GSAR扩展到更多移动或桌面平台，并研究其与离线预训练或人类反馈结合的可能性。长期来看，该框架有望成为GUI智能体通用训练范式，推动自主智能体在真实软件环境中的部署。

- ABS: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed as autonomous SOC analysts, but two limitations make them unreliable at enterprise scale: a finite context window cannot hold a multi-thousand-host authentication graph, and free-form generation offers no guarantee that a recommended containment action is consistent with the topology it operates on. We present Sentinel-RL, an agentic-SOC architecture that decouples topological reasoning from semantic reasoning: a heterogeneous graph attention encoder summarizes the live authentication subgraph into a fixed-dimensional state, a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy maps this state to a constrained set of investigative actions, and an LLM agent loop is restricted to consuming the policy's recommendations and producing analyst-readable narratives gated by a critic. We instantiate the system on the LANL Comprehensive, Multi-Source Cyber-Security Events dataset and the Indiana University Quartz HPC cluster, reporting four results: (i) a two-phase CREATE ingestion pattern loads a 24M-edge authentication subgraph into Neo4j in 14.2 minutes on a single 32-core node, roughly 24x faster than the canonical MERGE-based pipeline; (ii) a sliding-window alert engine reliably trips a 25-event/10-second threshold in <=2.5 s across 50 trials; (iii) PPO training over 200 iterations converges to a mean episodic return of 8.74+/-0.31, with held-out precision of 0.91 and recall of 0.87 on labeled red-team events; and (iv) the integrated containment loop completes a full detect-investigate-recommend-human-approve cycle in a median of
- summary_zh: 该论文提出Sentinel-RL架构，用于安全运营中心（SOC）的自主分析，将拓扑推理与语义推理解耦：图注意力编码器将认证子图压缩为固定维度状态，PPO策略生成受限调查动作，LLM代理仅负责生成可读叙述并受评论家门控。在LANL和Quartz数据集上，系统实现了快速图加载、可靠告警触发、PPO收敛（精确率0.91，召回率0.87）以及端到端检测-调查-推荐-人工批准循环中位耗时6.3秒。
- innovations_zh: 将拓扑推理从LLM代理中解耦，通过图编码器和PPO策略保证动作与拓扑一致; 提出两阶段CREATE图加载模式，比MERGE管道快约24倍; 集成可复用的工程模式（热节点死锁规避）和HPC部署模式（锚点节点共置）
- limitations: 摘要未提供与现有LLM代理基线或人工分析师的对比，难以评估相对优势。评测仅基于两个特定数据集（LANL和Quartz），可能缺乏跨域泛化证据。PPO训练和评估依赖标注的红队事件，其标注质量和覆盖范围可能影响结果可靠性。
- potential: 未来可探索将Sentinel-RL扩展到更多样化的网络拓扑和攻击模式，并研究其与多代理协作或主动学习结合的可能性。长期看，该架构有望成为SOC中混合推理（符号+语义）的标准范式，并推动可解释、可审计的自主安全决策系统发展。

### [78] PoEM: Predicting RL Outcomes from Existing Policies  (id=2609.30226, 2026-09-25, rl=22, score=6.7)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 多模态
- ABS: Foundation models are post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to maximize specific rewards, such as human alignment, correctness, or instruction following. This post-training process is computationally intensive, sometimes unstable, and has to be run from scratch every time the reward model changes or when we want to combine multiple rewards. We hence ask: given a new reward function, is it possible to predict the RL outcomes without actually running RL on it? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing PoEM, a framework to predict the outputs of RL on a new reward function using a set of models already post-trained on other rewards. First, we show that if the new reward function can be written as a linear combination of existing ones, then the new policy in log-space can be written as a linear combination of the existing log-policies. Surprisingly, even in cases where the rewards are not linearly connected, we observe that often log-policies from RL training span an approximately low-rank subspace across rewards. To our benefit, the weighting coefficients for this combination can be estimated using only the reward or basis policy outputs on the samples. We turn these observations into an algorithm that takes post-trained models and a new reward function, and approximates the target RL policy without actually running any additional RL training. We experimentally validate our approach across synthetic and real rewards, spanning both text and image modalities.
- summary_zh: 该论文研究能否在不实际运行强化学习的情况下，预测新奖励函数下的RL训练结果。作者提出PoEM框架，利用已针对其他奖励后训练好的模型来近似新奖励下的策略。其核心观察是：若新奖励可表示为已有奖励的线性组合，则新策略在对数空间也可表示为已有对数策略的线性组合；即使奖励非线性相关，不同奖励下的对数策略也常近似张成低秩子空间。组合权重可仅用样本上的奖励或基策略输出估计，并在文本和图像模态的合成与真实奖励上进行了实验验证。
- innovations_zh: 提出PoEM，用已有后训练模型预测新奖励下的RL策略，免去重新训练; 发现新奖励为已有奖励线性组合时，对数策略也呈线性组合关系; 观察到不同奖励的RL对数策略常近似张成低秩子空间; 仅用样本上的奖励或基策略输出即可估计组合权重
- limitations: 摘要仅给出高层观察与实验验证，未说明预测精度、失败条件与理论保证的边界。低秩子空间与线性组合假设在更复杂、非线性的真实奖励下是否普遍成立仍不确定。实验虽覆盖文本和图像，但未披露规模、基线对比与计算开销等细节。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究非线性奖励下的策略预测、低秩结构的理论刻画以及权重估计的鲁棒性。若能与奖励建模和多目标对齐结合，可能形成无需反复RL的后训练新范式，长期价值在于降低对齐与多奖励优化的计算门槛。

### [79] DMRL: Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning for Skill Optimization in Advertising Recommendation  (id=2609.02170, 2026-09-03, rl=22, score=6.6)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准, 多模态, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Advertising recommendation requires continuously tuning complex system parameters while balancing commercial returns and user experience. Recent work has introduced large language models (LLMs) with skill documents to assist this labor-intensive process, but skill optimization remains largely prompt-driven, lacking a principled mechanism to attribute rewards to specific document edits. To address this limitation, we propose Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning (DMRL), a skill self-evolution framework that models skill document optimization as a sequence of structured editing actions. In DMRL, an upper-level agent performs controlled document edits, while a frozen lower-level task agent evaluates their effects through A/B testing. To address credit assignment and long-term outcomes, we introduce two key components: (1) Dual-Relative Policy Optimization (DRPO), a post-training policy optimization method for robust and risk-aware advantage estimation; and (2) Long-term Reward Predictor (LRP), which estimates long-term outcomes by modeling population heterogeneity with disentangled representation learning and cross-attention transfer. DMRL was deployed on a large-scale short-video ads platform and extensive empirical evaluation shows that DMRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across key advertising metrics
- summary_zh: 该论文提出文档介导强化学习（DMRL）框架，将广告推荐中的技能文档优化建模为结构化编辑动作序列，以解决奖励归因问题。框架包含上层编辑代理和冻结的下层任务代理，通过A/B测试评估效果，并引入双相对策略优化（DRPO）和长期奖励预测器（LRP）分别处理信用分配与长期结果估计。在大规模短视频广告平台上的实验表明，DMRL在关键广告指标上优于现有基线。
- innovations_zh: 提出文档介导的强化学习框架，将技能优化转化为结构化编辑动作序列，实现可归因的奖励分配。; 设计双相对策略优化方法，结合风险感知优势估计，提升策略训练的鲁棒性。; 引入长期奖励预测器，通过解耦表示学习和交叉注意力迁移建模用户异质性，预测长期效果。
- limitations: 摘要未提供离线评测或消融实验细节，难以判断各组件（DRPO、LRP）的独立贡献。长期奖励预测器的准确性依赖于用户异质性建模，但未说明在冷启动或数据稀疏情况下的表现。实验仅基于单一平台，可能限制结论的通用性。
- potential: 未来可探索将DMRL扩展到更多类型的推荐任务（如电商、新闻）和更复杂的文档结构，并研究跨域迁移能力。长期奖励预测器可结合在线学习持续更新，以应对动态用户偏好。此外，该方法或可启发其他领域（如对话系统、内容生成）中基于文档的强化学习研究。

### [80] Taming CoT Obfuscation in VLMs: From Mechanistic Evidence to Activation Enforcement  (id=2609.24243, 2026-09-23-补充, rl=22, score=6.5)
- cats: 多模态, 评测与基准, 推理与数学
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) improves reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs) but can induce chain-of-thought (CoT) obfuscation: an operational, non-intentional outcome where task reward or accuracy rises while traces become less grounded and monitorable. Prior work largely documents this decay behaviorally, leaving its representation-level correlates and actionable controls unclear. We find that template- and ground-associated activations become less separable during RL; matched interventions support the contribution of selected features to monitorability degradation. Guided by this evidence, we propose Targeted Anti-obfuscation with Mechanistic Enforcement (TAME), which uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to combine behavioral feedback with targeted suppression of template-associated activations during RL. Its asymmetric constraint penalizes template activations only above their pre-RL baseline, anchoring the localized features while behavioral feedback promotes grounded refinements. Across VIRL-39k, SPA-VL, and two model families, TAME improves CoT monitorability by up to 30.9 and 16.7 percentage points over Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), respectively. Blinded human evaluation finds higher human monitorability on both datasets, and two held-out monitor families reproduce the monitorability gains. Task accuracy changes are small and mixed, and general-capability benchmarks show task-specific trade-offs. These results provide a path from behavioral monitoring to representation-level oversight for more auditable RL-trained multimodal systems.
- summary_zh: 该研究关注强化学习提升视觉语言模型推理能力时出现的思维链混淆现象，即任务奖励或准确率上升但推理轨迹的可监控性下降。作者发现强化学习过程中模板相关激活与接地相关激活的可分离性降低，并通过匹配干预支持这些特征对可监控性退化的贡献。基于此提出TAME方法，利用稀疏自编码器在强化学习中对模板相关激活进行非对称抑制，同时保留行为反馈以促进接地推理。在多个数据集和模型家族上，TAME相比GRPO将思维链可监控性分别提升最多30.9和16.7个百分点，盲评人类评估和两个留出监控器家族均复现了增益，任务准确率变化较小且混合，通用能力基准显示任务特定权衡。
- innovations_zh: 发现RL中模板与接地激活可分离性下降，并建立其与可监控性退化的因果联系; 提出TAME，用稀疏自编码器结合行为反馈与模板激活非对称抑制; 非对称约束仅惩罚高于RL前基线的模板激活，锚定局部特征; 在多个数据集和模型家族上验证可监控性提升，并经人类与留出监控器复现
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了可监控性提升和任务准确率的小幅混合变化，未说明任务准确率是否在统计上显著或是否在某些任务上下降。通用能力基准显示任务特定权衡，表明方法可能不适用于所有场景。此外，评估主要基于特定数据集和模型家族，跨更多任务和模型的泛化性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可进一步探索模板激活与接地激活的细粒度区分，以及非对称约束强度对可监控性与任务性能的权衡。可将TAME扩展到更多强化学习算法和模态，并研究其与不同监控器家族的兼容性。长期看，表示级监督有望成为训练可审计多模态推理系统的通用范式。

### [81] Neuro-symbolic PRM: Enhancing Scientific Reasoning via Structured Traces and Symbolic Verification  (id=2608.26329, 2026-08-28, rl=22, score=6.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, 领域应用
- ABS: While tool-augmented Large Language Models have significantly improved multi-step reasoning in quantitative STEM tasks, a critical residual failure mode remains: intermediate reasoning steps that are syntactically well-formed, mathematically executable, and unit-consistent, yet contextually ungrounded. Current approaches either rely on formal verifiers that cannot assess semantic intent, or burden Process Reward Models (PRMs) with the dual task of checking both arithmetic and logic. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework that cleanly decouples reasoning into two formal dimensions: Symbolic Validity ($V$) and Semantic Groundedness ($G$). We guarantee $V$ by construction using a deterministic symbolic verifier acting as a hard filter. To assess $G$, we train a PRM conditionally on the verifier-accepted manifold. To train this PRM efficiently, we introduce Counterfactual Symbolic Perturbation (CSP), a novel data synthesis strategy that algorithmically generates constraint-preserving hard negatives (steps that perfectly pass the verifier but are logically flawed). At inference, we deploy a verifier-first constrained search that guarantees execution consistency for verifier-covered operations while relying on the PRM solely to rank semantic grounding. By targeting the exact residual error class of strong tool-using LLMs, our method significantly improves reasoning reliability without the sprawling heuristics of prior frameworks.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对工具增强大语言模型在定量STEM推理中，中间步骤虽语法正确、可执行且单位一致但缺乏语义依据的失败模式，提出神经符号框架，将推理解耦为符号有效性和语义扎根性两个维度。通过确定性符号验证器保证符号有效性，并训练条件于验证器接受流形的过程奖励模型（PRM）评估语义扎根性，同时引入反事实符号扰动（CSP）生成硬负样本。推理时采用验证器优先的约束搜索，确保执行一致性，仅用PRM对语义扎根性排序，从而显著提升推理可靠性。
- innovations_zh: 将推理解耦为符号有效性与语义扎根性，分别用验证器和PRM处理; 提出CSP数据合成策略，生成通过验证但逻辑错误的硬负样本; 采用验证器优先的约束搜索，减少启发式规则依赖
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验数据集和基线对比，效果提升幅度未知；方法依赖符号验证器覆盖范围，对未覆盖操作可能失效；语义扎根性评估仍依赖PRM，其泛化能力未明确。
- potential: 未来可扩展CSP到更多领域和复杂逻辑约束，探索与更强大验证器结合；长期看，该框架可能成为可解释、可验证推理系统的基础，促进LLM在科学发现中的可靠应用。

### [82] TRACE: Training Reasoning Agents for Causal Exploration with Synthesized Rewards  (id=2609.10315, 2026-09-10, rl=22, score=6.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has advanced language-model reasoning in domains such as mathematics and code, where objective answers are inexpensive to check. Diagnostic reasoning over complex data lacks this advantage: establishing the true cause of an anomaly often requires costly expert investigation and may remain ambiguous after the fact. We ask whether this asymmetry of verification can instead be engineered. We sample an intervention, inject it into a controlled simulator, and generate the observations it would produce. The hidden intervention provides an oracle label and objective reward, while the agent must still investigate noisy, confounded, and distributed evidence. We instantiate this approach in TRACE, a digital-advertising diagnostic environment with 12 root causes and fine-grained segment attribution. Agents investigate each episode using Python and SQL and must identify both the root cause and, when applicable, the affected segment assignment. On a held-out 235-episode test set, the strongest prompted baseline, Claude Opus 5, reaches 0.686 FullAttr@1. Supervised fine-tuning raises Qwen3.5-35B-A3B from 0.159 to 0.637, and subsequent RL with synthesized rewards reaches 0.757, outperforming all evaluated prompted baselines, including frontier closed-source models and a prompted Qwen3.5-122B-A10B model. The resulting policy also uses substantially fewer tool calls than the prompted 35B base. These results provide evidence that access to a scalable, objective training signal can be a more important constraint than model scale alone. More
- summary_zh: 该研究针对诊断推理缺乏可验证奖励的问题，提出通过模拟器采样干预并生成观测，从而构造客观的oracle标签与奖励信号。作者构建了TRACE数字广告诊断环境，包含12种根因和细粒度细分归因，智能体需用Python和SQL调查并识别根因及受影响细分。在235个测试集上，监督微调将Qwen3.5-35B-A3B从0.159提升至0.637，后续合成奖励强化学习达到0.757，超过所有评估的提示基线，且工具调用次数显著减少。
- innovations_zh: 用模拟器注入干预生成观测，为诊断推理构造可验证的oracle奖励; 构建TRACE数字广告诊断环境，含12种根因与细粒度细分归因; 结合SFT与合成奖励RL，使35B模型超越前沿闭源提示基线
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了TRACE这一数字广告模拟环境的结果，结论向真实诊断场景的迁移性尚不明确。评测依赖模拟器生成的oracle标签，模拟保真度与真实因果结构的差距可能影响策略的泛化。此外，未说明与更多基线或消融实验的对比细节。
- potential: 未来可将模拟验证框架扩展到医疗、运维、金融等诊断领域，并研究模拟到现实的迁移与鲁棒性。长期看，若能降低对高保真模拟器的依赖，该方法有望成为复杂因果推理任务中可扩展强化学习的重要范式。

### [83] Eliciting Self-Verification in Multimodal Reasoning Agents with Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.08025, 2026-09-09, rl=21, score=9.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 多模态, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reasoning agents increasingly rely on external tools such as web search to answer complex queries. Reinforcement learning (RL) finetuning algorithms such as GRPO have improved long-form reasoning in text-only language models, particularly for coding and mathematics. Reliable tool use in multimodal agents, however, remains challenging because models must interpret text and images while integrating noisy retrieved evidence, often under sparse outcome-level supervision without explicit verification signals. We present Self-Verification via Reinforcement Learning (SVRL), an RL-only finetuning framework that trains multimodal agents to verify and filter retrieved evidence within their own reasoning traces, reducing reliance on external verifiers at inference time. SVRL also introduces a search-aware penalty that discourages unnecessary tool calls and a query-diversity reward that encourages diverse, well-formed search queries, providing fine-grained feedback on when and what to search. Finetuning Qwen-2.5-VL-7B with SVRL on only 5{,}000 visual question answering examples yields consistent gains in multi-hop VQA generalization and tool efficiency across benchmarks. Overall, SVRL narrows the gap between compact agents and much larger proprietary models while requiring substantially lower training and inference cost.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对多模态智能体在推理中依赖外部工具（如网络搜索）时，因噪声证据和稀疏结果监督而难以可靠使用工具的问题，提出了一种仅基于强化学习的微调框架SVRL。SVRL训练模型在自身推理轨迹中验证和过滤检索证据，并引入搜索感知惩罚和查询多样性奖励，以优化搜索时机与查询质量。在仅用5000个视觉问答样本微调Qwen-2.5-VL-7B后，该方法在多跳VQA泛化和工具效率上取得一致提升，缩小了与大型专有模型的差距，同时显著降低了训练和推理成本。
- innovations_zh: 提出SVRL框架，通过RL训练模型在推理轨迹内自我验证和过滤检索证据，减少推理时对外部验证器的依赖。; 引入搜索感知惩罚和查询多样性奖励，提供关于何时搜索及如何搜索的细粒度反馈，提升工具调用效率。
- limitations: 摘要未提供在非视觉问答任务或更广泛多模态场景上的验证，泛化性尚不明确。评测仅基于基准数据集，未涉及真实复杂环境中的噪声和动态性。此外，训练样本规模较小（5000例），其在不同数据分布下的稳定性有待进一步考察。
- potential: 未来可探索将SVRL扩展到更多任务类型（如多模态对话、具身智能）和更大规模模型，并研究其与在线搜索、知识库等动态工具的深度整合。长期看，该框架有望推动轻量级智能体在资源受限场景下的自主推理与工具使用，成为多模态RL训练的重要范式。

### [84] EAGER: Enhancing Generative Event Extraction via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards  (id=2609.29230, 2026-09-25, rl=21, score=8.7)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: End-to-end event extraction remains challenging for large language models as it requires simultaneous identification of event triggers, classification of event types, and extraction of schema-grounded argument spans. We present EAGER, a reinforcement learning framework for generative event extraction that combines fine-grained verifiable rewards with Schema-Contrastive Advantage Estimation to alleviate advantage collapse under sparse binary rewards. Our reward design explicitly targets structural validity, extraction accuracy, groundedness, coverage, over-generation, and span precision. Experiments across seven benchmark datasets show that EAGER consistently outperforms prompting, supervised fine-tuning, and prior reinforcement learning baselines, achieving a substantial improvement over the strongest prior method. Results demonstrate that task-aligned verifiable rewards and contrastive advantage estimation substantially improve structured extraction.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型在端到端事件抽取中需同时识别触发词、分类事件类型并抽取基于模式的论元跨度这一难题，提出了EAGER强化学习框架。EAGER结合细粒度可验证奖励与模式对比优势估计，以缓解稀疏二值奖励下的优势坍缩问题。其奖励设计显式覆盖结构有效性、抽取准确性、接地性、覆盖率、过度生成和跨度精度。在七个基准数据集上，EAGER持续优于提示、监督微调及先前强化学习基线，并较最强先前方法取得显著提升。
- innovations_zh: 提出EAGER强化学习框架用于生成式事件抽取; 设计覆盖结构、准确性、接地性等多维可验证奖励; 引入模式对比优势估计缓解稀疏奖励优势坍缩
- limitations: 摘要仅报告在七个基准数据集上的整体优势，未说明各数据集的具体表现、统计显著性及错误类型分析。奖励设计涉及多个维度，但摘要未交代各奖励项的权重设定、消融实验及对超参数的敏感性。此外，未讨论跨领域、跨模式或低资源场景下的泛化能力与计算成本。
- potential: 未来可进一步探索可验证奖励在更复杂事件模式与跨领域场景中的适应性，并研究奖励权重自动调节或与人类反馈结合的方式。长期看，若能在更多结构化抽取任务中验证其泛化性，该框架有望为生成式信息抽取提供可复用的强化学习优化范式。
