- ABS: Compact instruction-following rerankers are attractive for deployment, but conventional distillation pipelines typically train students by offline imitation of teacher outputs on a fixed set of examples, constraining supervision to the teacher's observed ranking space. We revisit reranker distillation through the lens of reinforcement learning. We propose a two-stage framework combining off-policy teacher optimization with on-policy student distillation. In Stage 1, a 4B teacher reranker is strengthened with off-policy GRPO using LLM-judge feedback on 88K instruction-following examples. In Stage 2, a compact 1B student samples rankings from its own policy and receives soft teacher-derived rewards on those rankings, coupling student exploration with knowledge transfer. Our strongest gains appear under distribution shift. On MAIR-11, the original 11-subset, 869-query evaluation, the proposed student reaches 0.7670 nDCG@6, outperforming offline listwise KD by +4.6 points. Controlled comparisons against offline pairwise RankNet KD and on-policy GKD show that neither changing the offline distillation objective nor moving teacher-distribution matching on-policy reproduces the performance of reward-based on-policy distillation over student-sampled rankings. The advantage persists on MAIR-Full: across all 126 tasks and 9,356 queries, the proposed method obtains the highest task-macro point estimates among the evaluated distillation variants, reaching 0.6808 nDCG@6 and 0.7865 MRR@6. It also exceeds two released 7B RL-trained rerankers on the comparable MAIR-11 evaluation, while the 
- summary_zh: 该论文针对指令跟随重排序器的知识蒸馏问题，提出了一种两阶段框架：先用离策略GRPO优化4B教师模型，再让1B学生模型基于自身采样排序并接收教师奖励进行在策略蒸馏。实验表明该方法在分布偏移场景下显著优于离线列表式蒸馏和在线GKD，并在MAIR-11和MAIR-Full基准上取得最佳性能，同时保持高效推理。
- innovations_zh: 将重排序器蒸馏重新定义为强化学习问题，结合离策略教师优化与在策略学生蒸馏; 学生从自身策略采样排序并接收教师奖励，实现探索与知识迁移的耦合; 在多个学生骨干和大型基准上验证了方法的泛化性与效率优势
- limitations: 摘要未提供方法在非分布偏移场景下的详细对比，且主要依赖nDCG和MRR等单点指标，缺乏对推理延迟、训练成本及不同奖励噪声的敏感性分析。此外，教师奖励的可靠性及在更小或更大模型上的适用性仍需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将教师奖励替换为更可扩展的合成反馈或在线人类反馈，以降低对LLM-judge的依赖；同时可研究在更多任务类型和语言上验证该框架，并进一步优化学生采样策略以提升样本效率。长期看，该RL蒸馏范式有望推广至其他生成式排序或检索增强系统。

### [86] Co-RL: Unsupervised Reasoning Emerges from Diverse Cohort in Multi-agent RL  (id=2608.17253, 2026-08-19, rl=21, score=7.9)
- cats: 推理与数学, 多模态, Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for improving reasoning in language and vision-language models, yet its strongest successes still depend heavily on ground-truth supervision (e.g., verifiable reward). Such annotations are costly to obtain and become increasingly scarce as reasoning capabilities advance beyond what humans can reliably evaluate. Self-rewarding RL reduces this dependence by enabling models to derive reward signals from their own completions. However, training solely on self-generated feedback can reinforce existing biases and suboptimal behaviors, reduce response diversity, and ultimately lead to homogenized responses and training collapse. In this work, we show that unsupervised reasoning can emerge through cooperative multi-agent training. We introduce Co-RL, a framework in which multiple decoupled models, sharing no parameters, are simultaneously optimized through RL using rewards derived from their peers. We further show that increasing cohort diversity, through heterogeneous model families, sizes, and rephrased training samples, reduces the correlated errors that drive self-reinforcing feedback loops. This diversity consistently improves reasoning performance, maintains behavioral diversity, and mitigates training collapse. Across text-only and multimodal domains, Co-RL consistently outperforms the base models and prior label-free approaches, while matching or surpassing supervised methods, without access to any ground-truth labels. Concretely, Co-RL yields average gains of 3.0-8.6% across seven text-only benchmarks for LLMs 
- summary_zh: 该论文提出Co-RL框架，通过多智能体强化学习实现无监督推理，多个不共享参数的模型利用同伴奖励进行协同训练，以克服自奖励机制中的偏差和训练崩溃问题。实验表明，增加队列多样性（如异构模型、重述样本）能提升推理性能并保持行为多样性，在文本和多模态基准上均优于无标签方法，甚至接近或超越有监督方法。
- innovations_zh: 提出多智能体协同强化学习框架，利用同伴奖励替代自奖励，避免自我反馈偏差。; 通过异构模型和重述样本增加队列多样性，减少相关错误，缓解训练崩溃。; 在无真实标签条件下，跨文本和多模态任务实现与有监督方法相当的性能。
- limitations: 摘要未提供详细的实验设置和消融分析，难以评估多样性因素的具体贡献。评测基准集中于特定文本和多模态任务，泛化性尚需验证。未讨论计算开销和训练稳定性等实际部署问题。
- potential: 未来可探索更大队列规模、动态多样性调整策略，以及将Co-RL扩展到更多模态和复杂推理任务。长期看，该方法有望成为无监督RL训练的基础范式，减少对人工标注的依赖，推动通用智能体的自主进化。

### [87] BoostAPR: Boosting Automated Program Repair via Execution-Grounded Reinforcement Learning with Dual Reward Models  (id=2605.09134, 2026-09-21, rl=21, score=7.7)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning for program repair is hindered by sparse execution feedback and coarse sequence-level rewards that obscure which edits actually fix bugs. We present BoostAPR, a three-stage framework addressing these challenges: (1) supervised fine-tuning on execution-verified demonstrations with reasoning traces, (2) training dual reward models--a sequence-level assessor and a line-level credit allocator--from execution outcomes, and (3) PPO optimization where the line-level model redistributes rewards to critical edit regions. This line-level credit assignment operates at an intermediate granularity naturally suited to code changes. Trained on SWE-Gym and evaluated on four benchmarks, BoostAPR achieves 40.7% on SWE-bench Verified (+22.9pp over base model), 24.8% on Defects4J (Python-to-Java transfer), 84.5% on HumanEval-Java, and 95.0% on QuixBugs, achieving competitive results among open-source models with strong cross-language generalization.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对自动程序修复中强化学习面临的执行反馈稀疏、序列级奖励过于粗糙的问题，提出了BoostAPR三阶段框架。方法包括：在经执行验证的带推理轨迹的演示上进行监督微调，基于执行结果训练序列级评估器和行级信用分配器两个奖励模型，以及在PPO优化中利用行级模型将奖励重新分配到关键编辑区域。在SWE-Gym上训练后，BoostAPR在SWE-bench Verified上达到40.7%（较基座模型提升22.9个百分点），在Defects4J上为24.8%，在HumanEval-Java上为84.5%，在QuixBugs上为95.0%，在开源模型中具有竞争力并展现出较强的跨语言泛化能力。
- innovations_zh: 提出行级信用分配，以介于序列与令牌之间的粒度定位关键编辑区域; 设计双奖励模型：序列级评估器与行级信用分配器，均从执行结果训练; 构建三阶段框架，融合执行验证演示的监督微调与PPO优化; 在多个基准上实现强跨语言泛化，包括Python到Java的迁移
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了基准上的通过率结果，未说明与同类方法的统计显著性、消融实验或计算成本，难以判断各阶段与双奖励模型的独立贡献。评测集中于SWE-bench Verified、Defects4J、HumanEval-Java和QuixBugs，跨语言迁移仅体现为Python到Java，对其他语言与真实仓库级修复的泛化性仍不确定。此外，行级信用分配依赖执行结果训练奖励模型，在执行反馈本身稀疏或不可靠的场景下可能受限。
- potential: 未来可探索将行级信用分配扩展到更细粒度（如语句或令牌级）或更粗粒度（如块级），并研究在更少执行反馈下的奖励模型训练策略。跨语言泛化结果提示该方法可能推广到更多编程语言与多语言混合修复场景，长期有望降低自动程序修复对大规模标注与密集执行反馈的依赖。

### [88] VerTox: Verifiable Reward-Guided Corpus Poisoning Against Neural Ranking Models  (id=2609.01325, 2026-09-02, rl=21, score=7.4)
- cats: RAG 与知识增强, 安全与可靠性, 微调与对齐, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Neural ranking models have become core components of modern information retrieval systems and important building blocks of AI systems such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines. However, their robustness remains insufficiently understood in the presence of large language models (LLMs), which can generate fluent and deceptive content at scale. This work investigates the vulnerability of neural ranking models to corpus poisoning attacks, in which an adversary injects a small number of maliciously crafted documents into the corpus to distort ranking behavior. We propose VerTox, the first framework to formulate corpus poisoning as a verifiable reward-guided reinforcement learning (RLVR) problem. By explicitly coupling ranking distortion with factual corruption through specialized reward shaping, we fine-tune compact LLMs into adversarial generators. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves near-perfect attack success rates, producing adversarial documents that frequently rank higher than target documents across major neural ranking architectures, as well as a proprietary commercial embedding model. The generated adversarial documents are fluent and exhibit low perplexity, making them difficult to detect. Furthermore, by explicitly encouraging factual corruption, our adversarial documents significantly degrade the performance of a downstream RAG application.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对神经排序模型在大型语言模型生成的欺骗性内容面前的鲁棒性问题，提出了一种名为VerTox的语料投毒攻击框架。VerTox将语料投毒形式化为可验证的奖励引导强化学习问题，通过专门的奖励塑造将排序扭曲与事实破坏相结合，微调小型语言模型生成对抗性文档。实验表明该方法在多种神经排序架构及商业嵌入模型上实现了接近完美的攻击成功率，生成的文档流畅且困惑度低，难以检测，并能显著降低下游RAG应用的性能。
- innovations_zh: 首次将语料投毒建模为可验证的奖励引导强化学习（RLVR）问题，实现攻击的自动化生成。; 通过奖励塑造成对耦合排序扭曲与事实破坏，使生成的对抗文档同时影响排序和RAG性能。; 生成的对抗文档流畅且低困惑度，具有强隐蔽性，且攻击成功率接近完美。
- limitations: 摘要未提供实验数据集的具体规模与多样性，可能限制攻击效果的泛化性结论。攻击成功率的评测主要基于排序位置，未涉及真实用户查询的复杂性与语义多样性。未讨论防御措施或攻击检测的对抗性场景，因此实际威胁程度可能因环境而异。
- potential: 未来可探索将VerTox扩展到更多类型的检索模型（如交叉编码器、多模态检索）及真实动态语料库。该框架可反向用于开发鲁棒性训练或投毒检测方法，推动可验证的防御机制研究。长期看，RLVR与事实破坏的结合可能为生成内容的安全评估提供新工具，但需警惕其被滥用于恶意攻击。

### [89] Salesforce Koa: An Enterprise Language Model for Agentic Tool Use  (id=2609.15066, 2026-09-15, rl=21, score=7.4)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: We present Salesforce Koa, an enterprise language model built by post-training the open-weight Nemotron-3-Super-120B foundation model with reinforcement learning using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Salesforce Koa is trained on public and synthetically generated data, with no customer data, to improve tool use and agentic capabilities while preserving strong general-purpose performance. Its distinctive component is a simulation-to-reward pipeline that expands workflow specifications into persona-conditioned multi-turn tasks with task-resolution rewards grounded in successful tool use for data-dependent requests. For enterprise domains, these specifications are written in Agent Script, Salesforce's declarative language for building Agentforce agents; for public tool-use domains, we synthesize the workflow structure directly. The same simulation and grounded-reward machinery drives GRPO across both. Across public tool-use, agentic-reasoning, and enterprise Customer Relationship Management (CRM) benchmarks, Salesforce Koa improves over its open-weight base, with the clearest gains on multi-turn tool use, and surpasses a strong proprietary baseline while remaining below the strongest frontier models. These results show that specification-driven reinforcement learning is a practical path to specializing open-weight foundation models for enterprise agentic tasks.
- summary_zh: 该工作提出 Salesforce Koa，一个面向企业场景的语言模型，通过在开放权重基座 Nemotron-3-Super-120B 上使用 GRPO 强化学习进行后训练得到。其训练数据来自公开与合成数据，不含客户数据，目标是提升工具使用与智能体能力，同时保持通用性能。其核心是仿真到奖励的流水线，将工作流规范扩展为带角色设定的多轮任务，并以成功工具使用为依据构造任务解决奖励，企业域规范用 Agent Script 编写，公开工具域则直接合成工作流结构。在公开工具使用、智能体推理和企业 CRM 基准上，Koa 优于其开放权重基座，多轮工具使用提升最明显，超过一个强专有基线，但仍低于最强前沿模型。
- innovations_zh: 提出仿真到奖励流水线，将工作流规范扩展为角色化多轮任务并构造基于工具使用成功的奖励; 企业域用 Agent Script 声明式语言编写规范，公开工具域直接合成工作流结构; 同一套仿真与接地奖励机制同时驱动两类域的 GRPO 训练; 在不使用客户数据的前提下，用公开与合成数据后训练开放权重模型
- limitations: 摘要未披露具体基准、评测规模、消融实验与统计显著性，因此提升幅度与稳定性难以独立判断。方法依赖工作流规范与仿真奖励的设计质量，其在真实企业流程中的覆盖度与泛化性尚不明确。结果仍低于最强前沿模型，说明该专门化路径在绝对能力上存在上限。
- potential: 未来可探索更自动化的规范生成与奖励设计，降低对人工编写 Agent Script 的依赖，并扩展到更多企业域与更长程的多轮任务。若能与真实企业工具生态更紧密结合，同时保持无客户数据的合规优势，该路径有望成为企业智能体模型持续迭代的通用范式。

### [90] GVPO++: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training and On-Policy Distillation  (id=2609.21432, 2026-09-21, rl=21, score=7.4)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 推理与数学
- ABS: Post-training plays a pivotal role in enhancing the reasoning capabilities and task-specific expertise of large language models (LLMs). Despite recent advances in post-training methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), their practical deployment remains impeded by training instability arising from the reliance on importance sampling. We introduce Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO), a novel post-training method that integrates the analytical solution of KL-constrained reward maximization into its gradient weighting scheme. This formulation provides an intuitive interpretation: GVPO's gradient corresponds to the mean squared error between the central distance of implicit rewards and that of actual rewards. GVPO offers two key advantages: (1) it guarantees a unique optimal solution, exactly to the KL-constrained reward maximization objective, and (2) it enables flexible sampling distributions without requiring importance sampling. Beyond general post-training, we show that GVPO naturally extends to on-policy distillation (OPD). Furthermore, GVPO enables the optimization of a broad family of extended OPD objectives, providing a principled foundation for diverse objective design. By unifying theoretical guarantees with practical adaptability, GVPO establishes a new paradigm for reliable and versatile LLM post-training and on-policy distillation.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型后训练中GRPO等方法因依赖重要性采样而导致训练不稳定的问题，提出了GVPO方法。GVPO将KL约束奖励最大化的解析解融入梯度加权方案，其梯度可解释为隐式奖励中心距离与实际奖励中心距离之间的均方误差。GVPO保证唯一最优解精确对应KL约束奖励最大化目标，并支持灵活采样分布而无需重要性采样，同时可自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏及其扩展目标族。
- innovations_zh: 将KL约束奖励最大化解析解融入梯度加权，梯度等价于奖励中心距离均方误差; 无需重要性采样即可支持灵活采样分布，缓解训练不稳定问题; 保证唯一最优解精确对应KL约束奖励最大化目标; 自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏并支持更广泛的扩展目标优化
- limitations: 摘要仅给出理论性质与框架描述，未提供实验设置、基准任务或与GRPO等方法的定量对比，实际效果与稳定性优势尚不明确。其唯一最优解与无需重要性采样的性质依赖KL约束奖励最大化假设，在复杂真实后训练场景中的适用性仍需验证。
- potential: 未来可围绕GVPO在推理任务与任务特定专家化中的实证验证展开，并探索其扩展OPD目标族的具体设计与选择准则。若理论优势能在多样场景中兑现，该方法可能成为后训练与在线策略蒸馏的统一优化基础，并推动更稳定的LLM训练流程。

### [91] Trident : How to Break Deep Reinforcement Learning Cyber Defenses (Agentic)  (id=2608.04317, 2026-09-25, rl=21, score=7.4)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准, 预训练与架构, 推理与数学, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Autonomous cyber defense systems based on Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) have attracted significant research attention, yet remain evaluated almost exclusively against static, heuristic red agents, leaving their robustness against adaptive threats critically understudied. Meanwhile, recent advances in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) have improved LLM reasoning, but their integration into cybersecurity remains elusive due to the absence of suitable benchmark environments and interaction datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce Trident, an agentic LLM red teaming framework comprising three components: a dynamic benchmark with isolated sandbox servers spanning CybORG CAGE 4 and CyberWheel, a dataset comprises over 13,000 high-fidelity red-blue interaction trajectories for RLVR, and a ``Code-as-Policy'' RLVR agentic architecture Trident Agentic). The latter reformulates red agent training as a contextual bandit via a tripartite Log Summarizer--Planner--Coder design, where a trainable Planner generates complete attack strategies from compressed execution logs, which a frozen Coder translates into executable Python policies deployed against live DRL defenders. Empirical evaluations reveal a fundamental brittleness in existing defenses: with a single trainable 7B planner, Trident reduces blue agent defensive performance by an average of 522% compared to static red agent baselines while autonomously discovering emergent behaviors such as decoy avoidance and adaptive state prioritization that static heuristics entirely fail to uncover.
- summary_zh: 现有基于深度强化学习的自主网络防御系统几乎只针对静态启发式红方智能体进行评估，其面对自适应威胁的鲁棒性研究严重不足。为此，作者提出 Trident 智能体式 LLM 红队框架，包含覆盖 CybORG CAGE 4 与 CyberWheel 的隔离沙箱动态基准、超过 13000 条高保真红蓝交互轨迹数据集，以及将红方训练重构为上下文赌博机的 Code-as-Policy RLVR 架构。实验表明，仅用一个可训练的 7B 规划器，Trident 就使蓝方防御性能平均下降 522%，并自主发现诱饵规避与自适应状态优先级等静态启发式无法揭示的行为。
- innovations_zh: 提出 Trident 智能体式 LLM 红队框架，用于攻击 DRL 网络防御; 构建覆盖 CybORG CAGE 4 与 CyberWheel 的隔离沙箱动态基准; 发布超 13000 条高保真红蓝交互轨迹的 RLVR 数据集; 设计 Log Summarizer–Planner–Coder 的 Code-as-Policy 架构
- limitations: 评估主要基于摘要所述的两个基准环境，结论向真实网络环境的泛化性尚不明确。性能下降 522% 的具体度量方式与统计细节未在摘要中说明，且仅使用单一 7B 规划器，规模与消融验证有限。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更多样、更接近真实的网络攻防环境，并探索更大规模或多种 LLM 规划器的效果。该框架有望成为评估与加固 DRL 防御系统的标准化红队工具，并推动攻防双方协同演化研究。

### [92] MATCH: Model-Aware Tool Learning with Curriculum Scheduling and Hierarchically Gated Rewards  (id=2609.20082, 2026-09-18, rl=21, score=7.2)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用
- ABS: Tool learning enables large language models (LLMs) to use external tools for tasks beyond parametric knowledge. Reinforcement learning can optimize tool-call behavior from feedback, but current methods still face two problems: fixed-threshold curricula can become misaligned with the policy's evolving capability boundary, and additive rewards can leak argument-level credit when the predicted tool is wrong. To address these problems, we propose MATCH, a closed-loop framework for model-aware tool learning with curriculum scheduling and hierarchically gated rewards. Model-Aware Curriculum Learning (MACL) maintains reward-derived sample difficulty that co-evolves with the policy, and each epoch selects samples near the current capability boundary together with a top-k pool of harder cases. Hierarchical Tool-call Gated Reward (HTGR) scores tool name, argument key, and argument value as a gated chain, granting credit at each level only when prerequisites hold. The same HTGR rewards drive both GRPO updates and MACL's difficulty refresh, closing the loop between policy optimization and sample scheduling. On API-Bank and BFCL V3, MATCH reaches 72.19% and 62.87% overall accuracy, outperforming the main supervised and RL-based baselines. Backbone experiments further show consistent improvements across four backbones from two model families.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对工具学习中强化学习面临的两个问题：固定阈值课程与策略能力边界不匹配，以及加性奖励在工具预测错误时泄露参数级信用。作者提出MATCH闭环框架，包含模型感知课程学习（MACL）和分层工具调用门控奖励（HTGR）。MACL利用奖励驱动的样本难度与策略共同演化，每轮选择能力边界附近样本及更难样本池；HTGR按工具名、参数键、参数值门控链评分，仅在前置条件满足时授予信用。在API-Bank和BFCL V3上，MATCH分别达到72.19%和62.87%的总体准确率，优于主要监督和RL基线，并在两个模型家族的四个骨干上表现一致提升。
- innovations_zh: 提出MACL，用奖励驱动难度与策略共同演化，动态选择能力边界样本; 提出HTGR，按工具名、参数键、参数值门控链评分，避免错误工具下的信用泄露; HTGR奖励同时驱动GRPO更新和MACL难度刷新，形成策略优化与样本调度闭环
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了API-Bank和BFCL V3两个基准的总体准确率，未提供消融实验、训练成本或超参数敏感性分析，难以判断各模块的独立贡献。方法依赖奖励驱动的难度估计和门控链设计，在更开放或工具调用步骤更长的场景下是否同样有效尚不明确。此外，未说明与基线比较的统计显著性和具体基线配置。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证MATCH在更多工具学习基准、多轮交互和跨领域任务中的泛化能力，并分析MACL与HTGR各自的贡献及计算开销。长期看，模型感知课程与分层门控奖励的结合可能为LLM智能体训练提供更通用的信用分配和样本调度范式。

The framework first establishes a strong foundation through large-scale multimodal supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Building on this SFT foundation, we propose Interleaved Distillation and Reinforcement Learning (IDRL), a novel post-training paradigm that tightly integrates on-policy distillation and reinforcement learning within a single training loop. By alternating between the two objectives, rather than optimizing either in isolation or combining them in a static joint loss, IDRL enables more effective knowledge transfer, greater optimization stability, and more precise credit assignment for long-horizon agentic trajectories, leading to stronger performance while mitigating common capability trade-offs. At both model scales, the framework produces two specialized variants: Pistis-Thinking, designed to strengthen deep multimodal reasoning, and Pistis-Agentic, which additionally incorporates agentic trajectory data to support long-horizon planning, iterative reasoning, and tool use. Pistis-Agentic is particularly strong in multimodal search. Both scales outperform their corresponding base models. Beyond model-parameter optimization, we further introduce Pistis-Auto-Harnessing (PAH), a system-level method that automatically improves the agent's inference harness through iterative optimization. Experiments demonstrate that PAH enhances the model performance witho
- summary_zh: 该报告提出Pistis模型家族，包含基于Qwen3.6和Qwen3.5构建的27B与9B参数多模态大语言模型，采用通用可扩展的后训练框架。框架先通过大规模多模态监督微调建立基础，再提出交错蒸馏与强化学习（IDRL）范式，在单一训练循环中交替进行在策略蒸馏与强化学习。该框架产出Pistis-Thinking与Pistis-Agentic两种变体，后者在多模态搜索上表现突出，两个规模均优于对应基座模型，并进一步提出Pistis-Auto-Harnessing（PAH）在系统层面自动改进智能体推理框架。
- innovations_zh: 提出IDRL范式，在单一训练循环中交替进行在策略蒸馏与强化学习; 通过交替优化而非静态联合损失，提升知识迁移与优化稳定性; 面向长程智能体轨迹实现更精确的信用分配，缓解能力权衡; 提出PAH系统级方法，不更新参数即可自动改进推理框架
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了模型优于对应基座模型，未给出具体基准、评测指标或与同类模型的对比数据，性能优势缺乏可验证细节。IDRL与PAH的有效性依赖特定训练框架与数据配置，其在不同模型规模或任务域上的泛化性尚不明确。此外，PAH声称不增加交互预算，但摘要未说明其优化成本与收敛条件。

### [94] TRCA: Transition-wise Rubric Credit Assignment for Long-horizon LLM Agents  (id=2608.16156, 2026-08-18, rl=21, score=6.8)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents are typically optimized with sparse terminal outcomes, making fine-grained credit assignment across multi-step interactions difficult. Existing approaches either rely on process evaluators, which incur annotation and inference costs, or derive step-level credit from successful trajectories. However, successful trajectories are extremely scarce during early-stage reinforcement learning, substantially weakening anchor-based methods. We propose Transition-wise Rubric Credit Assignment (TRCA), which derives step-level supervision directly from action-induced transitions without learned evaluators or successful anchors. TRCA evaluates each transition using Evidence, Execution, and Invalidity rubrics to capture task-relevant information acquisition, valid task execution, and invalid or regressive behavior. From these judgments, Foundational Rubric Reward measures local transition quality, while Breakthrough Rubric Reward tracks newly covered Evidence and Execution conditions to reward incremental task progress. Combined with terminal outcomes, these signals produce fine-grained step-level advantages for policy optimization. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and seven search-augmented question-answering benchmarks show consistent improvements over the evaluated baselines. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, TRCA improves the WebShop score by 6.0%-12.6%; with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, it improves the average SearchQA score by 1.9%-18.3%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of transition-wise rubric credit assignment for long-horizon tasks wit
- summary_zh: 该论文针对长时程LLM智能体在稀疏终端奖励下难以进行细粒度信用分配的问题，提出了一种基于转换的评分信用分配方法（TRCA）。TRCA无需学习型评估器或成功轨迹锚点，直接利用动作引发的转换，通过证据、执行和无效性三个评分标准生成步骤级奖励，并结合终端结果计算优势。在ALFWorld、WebShop及七个搜索增强问答基准上的实验表明，该方法在多个模型上均优于基线，显著提升了任务得分。
- innovations_zh: 提出无需学习评估器或成功锚点的转换级信用分配方法，降低标注与推理成本。; 设计证据、执行、无效性三维评分标准，细粒度捕捉任务进展与无效行为。; 结合基础与突破性奖励，动态追踪新覆盖条件，增强长时程任务优化信号。
- limitations: 摘要未提供与过程评估器方法的直接成本对比，实际效率优势需进一步验证。实验仅基于Qwen2.5系列模型，未说明在其他架构上的适用性。评测基准集中于文本交互任务，对多模态或物理环境的长时程任务效果未知。
- potential: 未来可探索将TRCA扩展到更多模型架构和复杂环境，如机器人控制或多智能体协作。其评分标准可进一步自适应学习，减少人工设计依赖。长期看，该方法有望成为长时程智能体强化学习的通用组件，推动更高效、可解释的信用分配研究。

### [95] Beyond Verified Answers: Solver-Informed Self-Distillation for Bootstrapping Operations Research Language Models  (id=2609.09957, 2026-09-11, rl=21, score=6.8)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Modern large language models (LLMs) can translate natural-language descriptions into operations research (OR) formulations. Post-training techniques including reinforcement learning and on-policy self-distillation have further improved this capability. However, three limitations remain in training LLMs for OR formulations. First, training commonly relies on synthetic formulations validated by human experts or stronger models, constraining scalable supervision. Second, credit assignment is either coarse or costly: outcome rewards score an entire trajectory without locating the responsible modeling decision, whereas process-level supervision requires an additional evaluator. Third, privileged self-distillation can induce style mismatch by using solver context unavailable at deployment. We find that a model can improve from solver-artifact feedback generated by its own rollouts, making self-distillation a practical, evaluator-free source of dense supervision. Therefore, we propose SOLID: Solver-Informed On-Policy LearnIng through Self-Distillation, a novel framework for self-improving OR language models without verified answers or external evaluators. SOLID executes candidate programs from multiple rollouts, clusters their objectives, and selects a majority-group artifact as a pseudo-reference. The model then performs updates using group-relative advantages and dense self-supervision signals. Across multiple OR benchmarks, SOLID improves solution accuracy for both general-purpose and OR-tuned models over outcome-only group-relative training. These results show that solver arti
- summary_zh: 该论文关注运筹学（OR）建模中LLM训练面临的三类限制：依赖人工或强模型验证的合成数据、信用分配粗糙或昂贵、以及特权自蒸馏带来的部署风格不匹配。作者提出SOLID框架，利用模型自身多次 rollout 生成的求解器工件，通过聚类目标并选取多数组工件作为伪参考，结合组相对优势和稠密自监督信号进行更新，无需已验证答案或外部评估器。在多个OR基准上，SOLID相比仅使用结果奖励的组相对训练提升了通用模型和OR微调模型的求解准确率。
- innovations_zh: 利用自身rollout的求解器工件作为伪参考，无需验证答案或外部评估器; 通过聚类目标并选多数组工件，实现无评估器的稠密自监督; 结合组相对优势与稠密自监督信号进行策略更新
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在多个OR基准上的准确率提升，未说明基准的具体规模、难度分布或与更强基线的全面对比。方法依赖模型自身rollout能产生可聚类的求解器工件，若初始模型能力不足或问题类型不适用，伪参考质量可能受限。此外，未讨论计算开销、聚类稳定性及部署时风格匹配的实际影响。

### [96] Agent Lightning v1.0: Towards Harnessed Agentic RL  (id=2608.17528, 2026-08-19, rl=21, score=6.7)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Modern agents operate inside agent harnesses that manage tools, context, and control flow, making the harness a critical part of the agent system. Our original Agent Lightning introduced a disaggregated architecture that connects arbitrary agents to RL training through an LLM endpoint proxy, an approach later adopted by frameworks such as verl Uni-Agent, AReaL 2.0, slime, and Polar. We refer to this paradigm as harnessed agentic RL, where the deploy-time harness directly participates in model post-training. Harnessed agentic RL differs fundamentally from traditional agentic RL: the harness, rather than the training engine, owns the environment interaction loop, while the trainer observes only sequences of LLM request-response pairs. This introduces challenges in retokenization, sample merging, advantage calculation, loss normalization, and backend scheduling, which can substantially affect training stability and effectiveness. We present Agent Lightning v1.0, a lightweight framework for harnessed agentic RL implemented in approximately 3,500 lines of code. It supports arbitrary agent harnesses and serves as a practical testbed for studying these challenges. We evaluate it on instruction-following, search, and coding agents, and provide a complete reproducible pipeline for coding-agent RL. Using only 6K training examples and modest compute, RL improves Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified from 41.8% to 56.4%, a 14.6-point absolute gain. We release the complete workflow and training scripts to facilitate reproducible research on harnessed agentic RL.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出了一种名为“受控智能体强化学习”的新范式，其中部署时的智能体框架直接参与模型后训练，而非由训练引擎控制环境交互。作者介绍了Agent Lightning v1.0，一个约3500行代码的轻量级框架，支持任意智能体框架，并作为研究该范式下挑战的测试平台。在指令跟随、搜索和编码智能体上进行了评估，其中编码智能体仅用6K训练样本，将Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上的性能从41.8%提升至56.4%。
- innovations_zh: 提出受控智能体RL范式，框架参与后训练; 轻量级框架支持任意智能体框架; 公开完整可复现的编码智能体RL流程
- limitations: 摘要仅基于单一模型和编码任务展示主要结果，泛化性未充分验证。评测集中于SWE-bench Verified，可能未覆盖更广泛的实际应用场景。此外，框架的稳定性和效率在更大规模训练中的表现尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索将受控智能体RL扩展到更多任务类型和更大模型，并研究框架与训练引擎的协同优化。该范式有望成为智能体后训练的标准方法，推动可复现、可扩展的智能体RL研究。

### [97] From LLM-Generated Specifications to Learned Quadruped Locomotion  (id=2609.07111, 2026-09-09, rl=21, score=6.7)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Quadruped robot locomotion policies are often trained using reinforcement learning, which in turn relies heavily on hand-crafted reward functions. Designing reward functions requires substantial manual engineering, and it is often unclear which local rewards will induce the desired global behavior. Shaped rewards from formal specifications in languages like Signal Temporal Logic (STL) can make rewards more interpretable, but writing STL specifications itself still requires domain expertise. We study whether large language models (LLMs) can fill this gap by generating Parametric Signal Temporal Logic (PSTL) specifications that are subsequently used for policy learning. Given a natural language locomotion objective and a constrained specification grammar, GPT-5.5 and Qwen 3.6 independently propose STL templates for command tracking, safety, and gait structure. We instantiate the parameters of the generated PSTL templates using expert trajectories and retain only specifications that are consistent with demonstrated expert behavior. The resulting specifications are then transformed into smooth, finite-history reward functions and used to train a quadruped locomotion policy with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in MuJoCo XLA (MJX). We evaluate both \emph{gait-aware} and \emph{gait-agnostic} settings. The former specifies walking-trot, trot, and bound regimes, while the latter allows contact patterns to emerge from the task objective. We compare against hand-engineered rewards, Text2Reward-style LLM-generated reward code, and an expert-switching oracle. Gait-aware Qwen 3.6 spec
- summary_zh: 该研究探索利用大语言模型（LLM）自动生成参数化信号时序逻辑（PSTL）规范，以替代手工设计的奖励函数，用于四足机器人运动策略的训练。研究中，GPT-5.5和Qwen 3.6根据自然语言目标生成STL模板，并通过专家轨迹实例化参数，仅保留与专家行为一致的规范，再将其转化为平滑奖励函数，使用PPO在MuJoCo中训练策略。实验对比了步态感知与步态无关设置，结果显示步态感知的Qwen 3.6规范在0.3-2.1 m/s速度下实现100%存活率和命令成功率，而Text2Reward在高速下完全失败。
- innovations_zh: 首次将LLM生成的PSTL规范用于四足机器人运动策略学习，替代手工奖励设计。; 提出基于专家轨迹的规范参数实例化与一致性筛选方法，确保规范可行性。; 在步态感知与步态无关两种设置下系统评估，展示LLM规范在高速场景的显著优势。
- limitations: 摘要未提供GPT-5.5与Qwen 3.6在低速或复杂地形下的性能对比，且仅基于MuJoCo仿真，未涉及真实机器人部署。规范生成依赖受限语法和专家轨迹，可能限制其泛化性；评测指标仅包含存活率、命令成功率和步态匹配，未报告能耗、平滑性等运动质量指标。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更复杂的步态（如跳跃、奔跑）和动态环境，结合在线规范调整或自适应参数。长期价值在于建立LLM与形式化验证的闭环，实现从自然语言到可验证控制策略的自动化流水线，并可能推广至其他机器人形态或多智能体系统。

### [98] Learning Generalizable Behaviors for Terminal Agents  (id=2608.22631, 2026-09-25, rl=21, score=6.5)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Terminal agents are a compelling application of large language models (LLMs), with the potential to integrate deeply into users' daily workflows. Reinforcement learning (RL) is a key technique for improving their capabilities, making scalable training environments a central challenge. Since public real-user interaction data are scarce, synthetic environments provide a practical alternative, but often suffer from domain gaps and limited fidelity, leading to poor generalization. Existing work mainly scales the quantity and diversity of synthetic environments, while reward-signal quality and the mechanisms governing generalization remain under-explored. We study how RL improves terminal agents and propose the Agentic Compositional Generalization hypothesis: rather than teaching new domain-specific skills from scratch, RL primarily shapes high-level decision-making behaviors that compose and route low-level skills acquired during pre-training and supervised fine-tuning (SFT). This account is consistent with our empirical results and suggests that verifier quality, which determines which behaviors are reinforced, is more important than simply increasing environment quantity or diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose River, a simple training recipe that improves reward quality by filtering low-quality environments and augmenting outcome rewards with process-level behavior regularization. Using this recipe, our RL-trained agent achieves the best performance among evaluated open-source RL-trained 8B models across four terminal-agent benchmarks. River also generalizes acros
- summary_zh: 该研究关注终端智能体的强化学习训练，指出合成环境存在域差距与保真度不足导致泛化差，而现有工作多关注环境数量与多样性，忽视奖励信号质量与泛化机制。作者提出“智能体组合泛化”假设：RL主要塑造高层决策行为，以组合和路由预训练与SFT获得的低层技能，而非从头学习新领域技能。基于此提出River训练配方，通过过滤低质量环境和过程级行为正则增强结果奖励，在四个终端智能体基准上取得开源8B RL模型最佳表现，且跨模型家族、规模、智能体框架和RL目标泛化，用不到30%的TMax环境使2B至27B模型平均提升106%和30%。
- innovations_zh: 提出智能体组合泛化假设，强调RL塑造高层决策而非学新技能; 指出验证器质量比环境数量与多样性更关键; 提出River配方，过滤低质量环境并加入过程级行为正则; 用不到30%环境实现显著RL增益并跨多维度泛化
- limitations: 摘要仅提供高层假设与结果，未披露具体实验设置、基准细节与消融分析，假设的普适性仍需更多证据；方法依赖验证器质量与过程级正则设计，其在不同任务与真实环境中的迁移效果尚不确定；评测限于终端智能体基准与开源8B模型，结论向更大规模或非终端场景推广需谨慎。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证组合泛化假设在更广泛智能体任务与模型规模上的适用性，探索更鲁棒的验证器与过程奖励设计，并将River配方扩展到多模态、长程交互与真实用户环境，长期有望降低对大规模合成环境的依赖，推动终端智能体在真实工作流中的可靠部署。

### [99] Is Next-Chunk Reasoning RL Really Better than SFT? Revisiting Training Strategies under no-CoT Data  (id=2608.23256, 2026-08-25, rl=21, score=6.4)
- cats: 推理与数学, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Recent work proposes next-chunk reasoning RL for leveraging no-CoT data---corpora such as worked solutions and textbook derivations that contain reasoning-rich content but lack explicit chain-of-thought annotations. The method trains a model to generate implicit reasoning traces and rewards them by their ability to predict the next chunk of text. While promising, existing evaluations primarily compare against conventional SFT baselines, leaving open whether the gains come from the RL formulation itself or from more effectively exposing the model to no-CoT data. We address this question with a controlled study of next-chunk reasoning RL and a simple but previously overlooked alternative: Mixed SFT, a single supervised fine-tuning stage that jointly trains on no-CoT and long-CoT data. Despite its simplicity, Mixed SFT achieves a clearly higher post-RLVR performance ceiling than next-chunk reasoning RL while requiring over 60 times less training compute. The advantage is consistent across in-domain mathematical reasoning and out-of-domain reasoning tasks. Moreover, we show that higher pre-RLVR accuracy does not necessarily translate into higher post-RLVR accuracy, highlighting the need to evaluate no-CoT training strategies in the context of the full post-training pipeline.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对无CoT数据下的next-chunk reasoning RL方法，通过对照实验比较其与简单混合SFT（同时训练无CoT和长CoT数据）的效果。结果显示，混合SFT在RLVR后的性能上限明显更高，且训练计算量减少60倍以上，在数学推理和域外任务上均表现一致。此外，研究发现RLVR前的高准确率并不必然带来RLVR后的高准确率，强调需在全流程中评估训练策略。
- innovations_zh: 提出混合SFT作为简单但被忽视的基线，统一训练无CoT和长CoT数据; 揭示next-chunk RL的增益可能源于数据暴露而非RL本身，通过受控实验验证; 发现pre-RLVR准确率与post-RLVR准确率非正相关，强调全流程评估
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验设置（如模型规模、数据集大小），可能影响结论的普适性。评测主要基于数学推理和部分域外任务，未覆盖更广泛任务类型。此外，混合SFT的优势是否在更大模型或不同RLVR配置下保持尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索混合SFT与其他数据混合比例、课程学习等变体，进一步提升性能。该研究也提示需开发更全面的评估框架，考虑RLVR前后多阶段指标。长期看，简单高效的方法可能成为无CoT数据训练的标准基线，推动实际应用。

### [100] From Interests to Semantic IDs: Retrieval-Grounded Credit Assignment for Generative Recommendation  (id=2609.29983, 2026-09-25, rl=21, score=6.3)
- cats: RAG 与知识增强, 推理与数学, 推理优化与效率, 多模态
- ABS: Semantic IDs (SIDs) encode each catalog item as a short token sequence, enabling generative recommenders to predict the next item autoregressively. Reasoning-enhanced variants, an increasingly common extension, first generate a textual trace and then decode a next-item SID by beam search. Such recommenders are commonly trained with group-relative policy optimization under an exact-match SID reward, which is sparse in large catalogs. Two failure modes follow. When all rollouts in a group miss the target, the group yields zero advantage and no learning signal. Rollouts sharing the same SID reward receive identical advantages, however much their traces differ. In both cases the reward reflects only the decoded SID, never the reasoning that produced it. This creates a credit-assignment gap. We address this gap with retrieval-grounded query attribution. Each trace is structured into a history summary, a set of interest hypotheses, and a final SID. A frozen retriever executes every hypothesis as a catalog query, so that each hypothesis becomes independently verifiable rather than judged only through the final SID. A rollout is rewarded when any of its queries retrieves the target within the \mbox{top-$K$}, and per-query hit indicators localize that reward to individual hypotheses. Credit is thus assigned at the span level: only hypotheses that individually hit receive positive retrieval advantage, while the retrieval channel never updates the final SID span. Rollouts that share a SID reward can therefore receive different updates. Across experiments on three Amazon Reviews datase
- summary_zh: 该论文针对生成式推荐中语义ID（SID）奖励稀疏导致的信用分配问题展开研究。作者指出，在基于组相对策略优化和精确匹配SID奖励的训练下，存在组内全部未命中导致零优势、以及相同SID奖励获得相同优势而忽略推理轨迹差异两种失败模式。为此，论文提出检索接地的查询归因方法，将推理轨迹结构化为历史摘要、兴趣假设和最终SID，并用冻结检索器逐一执行兴趣假设，以top-K命中指标将奖励定位到具体假设片段。在三个Amazon Reviews数据集上，该方法在SID推荐上取得一致提升，且在Video Games上通过oracle分析展示了兴趣条件SID解码的潜力。
- innovations_zh: 将推理轨迹结构化为历史摘要、兴趣假设与最终SID，实现可验证的中间表示; 用冻结检索器执行每个兴趣假设，以top-K命中实现片段级信用分配; 检索通道仅更新兴趣假设片段，不更新最终SID片段，避免奖励混淆; 使相同SID奖励的rollout可获得不同更新，缓解组内零优势问题
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了三个Amazon Reviews数据集上的实验，未说明跨领域或大规模目录下的泛化能力。方法依赖冻结检索器的质量与top-K命中定义，检索器偏差可能影响信用分配效果。此外，oracle分析仅针对Video Games数据集，兴趣条件SID解码的潜力尚未在完整系统中验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将检索接地的信用分配扩展到更多领域和更大规模目录，并研究检索器质量对训练稳定性的影响。兴趣条件SID解码在oracle分析中展现的召回与排序提升，可进一步整合到实际推理流程中。长期看，该思路有望推动生成式推荐中推理轨迹与检索验证的深度融合。

### [101] Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning  (id=2609.04194, 2026-09-04, rl=20, score=8.9)
- cats: 推理与数学, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.
- summary_zh: 该研究探讨了思维链推理文本的可读性是否等同于可解释性，通过将推理步骤的重要性定义为优势（即包含该步骤对期望奖励的影响），并使用蒙特卡洛 rollout 估计。研究发现，即使强大的 LLM 法官能识别高优势步骤，其表现仍远低于噪声上限；微调步骤级批评者虽能改善错误回答的识别，但对正确回答仍存在差距。这表明推理步骤的重要性仅能从文本中部分恢复，警示不应将思维链的可读性视为可解释性。
- innovations_zh: 提出用优势（advantage）操作化推理步骤重要性，基于蒙特卡洛 rollout 估计。; 系统比较 LLM 法官与噪声上限，揭示文本对步骤重要性的有限编码能力。
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验设置和数据集细节，限制了结果泛化性的判断；优势估计依赖蒙特卡洛 rollout，可能受采样偏差影响；评测仅基于 LLM 判断，未涉及人类评估或跨模型泛化。
- potential: 未来可探索更有效的步骤重要性提取方法，或结合因果干预与文本特征；长期价值在于引导可解释性研究超越表面可读性，发展基于功能角色的评估框架，并改进过程监督的可靠性。

### [102] One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL  (id=2608.12253, 2026-08-13, rl=20, score=7.4)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to
- summary_zh: 该论文指出，在人类-AI交互的多智能体强化学习中，使用单一大型语言模型模拟用户会导致系统泛化失败，即模拟器坍缩现象。作者从理论上形式化了该问题，并提出推理时的Verbalized Sampling和训练时的Co-Training两种互补方案。在三个多轮基准上，两种方法分别将保留成功率提升至9%和14%，人类研究也验证了其有效性。
- innovations_zh: 提出并形式化模拟器坍缩问题，揭示单一模拟器导致策略过拟合的机制; 提出推理时Verbalized Sampling，通过口头化分布采样减少模式坍缩; 提出训练时Co-Training，联合优化策略与模拟器群体以提升泛化; 开源SCOPE框架，支持群体协同训练的多智能体RL研究
- limitations: 摘要未提供理论形式化的具体细节和假设条件，可能限制其普适性。评测基准和人类研究的具体规模、场景多样性未明确，可能影响结论的广泛适用性。
- potential: 未来可探索更高效的模拟器群体生成方法，以及将Co-Training扩展到更复杂、更开放的任务中。该工作可能启发对训练环境多样性理论的研究，并推动多智能体RL在真实部署中的鲁棒性提升。

### [103] TelecomGPT-R1: Unified Post-Training for Reasoning Across Heterogeneous Telecom Tasks  (id=2609.25356, 2026-09-23, rl=20, score=7.3)

- ABS: Large language models (LLMs) offer great potential to automate a broad range of telecom engineering tasks by reasoning over standards, network configurations, mathematical models, source code, and operational logs. However, existing telecom LLMs struggle to reliably reason across these diverse tasks and data types. General-purpose LLMs often lack reliable grounding in telecom-specific knowledge, while telecom-specialized models are typically developed for narrower task families and exhibit limited multi-task performance. To fill this gap, we introduce TelecomGPT-R1, a family of open source unified telecom reasoning models structured around four complementary axes: protocol, knowledge, modeling, and fault. We first develop an axis-aware data generation framework that refines coarse public telecom artifacts into verified question-answer pairs and high quality chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories, yielding a training corpus containing 104,880 examples. Building on this corpus, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) instills telecom knowledge and evidence-grounded reasoning patterns to overcome the cold start barrier for reinforcement learning (RL). We then apply dynamic sampling policy optimization (DAPO) with task-routed rubric rewards to keep RL updates informative and stable across heterogeneous telecom reasoning tasks. These rewards decompose axis-specific CoT traces into verifiable reasoning units and combine grounded dense process credit with outcome correctness, allowing RL to learn generalizable problem solving behaviors from verifiable telecom evidence.
