- summary_zh: 针对现有电信大模型难以在标准、配置、数学模型、代码和日志等异构任务上可靠推理的问题，本文提出开源统一电信推理模型家族TelecomGPT-R1，围绕协议、知识、建模和故障四个互补维度构建。作者先建立轴感知数据生成框架，将粗粒度公开电信资料精炼为经核验的问答对与高质量思维链轨迹，形成104880条训练语料；再通过监督微调克服强化学习冷启动障碍，并采用动态采样策略优化与任务路由评分奖励进行后训练。在GSMA开放电信排行榜的七个基准上，TelecomGPT-R1-27B取得89.64%的平均分，超过GPT-5、Claude和Gemini等领先专有模型。
- innovations_zh: 围绕协议、知识、建模、故障四轴构建统一电信推理模型家族; 轴感知数据生成框架产出经核验问答对与高质量思维链语料; 采用DAPO与任务路由评分奖励稳定异构任务强化学习; 将轴特定思维链分解为可验证推理单元并融合过程与结果奖励
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了GSMA开放电信排行榜七个基准的平均分，未给出各任务细分结果、消融实验或与同规模开源模型的对比，难以判断性能来源与泛化边界。数据生成依赖公开电信资料的精炼与核验，其覆盖范围和质量控制细节未在摘要中说明，可能限制对未公开或快速演进电信场景的适用性。此外，任务路由评分奖励的设计与稳定性证据仅以摘要结论呈现，缺乏对奖励可验证性和潜在偏差的讨论。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证该统一后训练框架在更多电信子领域、跨厂商设备与真实运维数据上的迁移能力，并探索四轴之外的任务维度扩展。可研究数据生成与奖励设计中人工核验成本、可验证性与覆盖度之间的权衡，以及将可验证过程奖励推广到其他工程领域异构推理任务。若开源模型与训练配方持续维护，长期有望成为电信领域可复现推理评测与应用的基座。

### [104] From Memorization to Absorption: Mixed-Policy RL for Continual Knowledge Injection  (id=2608.25243, 2026-08-27, rl=20, score=7.0)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 推理与数学, RAG 与知识增强, 评测与基准
- ABS: Continual knowledge injection is essential for keeping large language models up-to-date in a fast-evolving world. Existing methods rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which memorizes injected facts in their training format but fails to generalize across paraphrasing, document combinations, and reasoning. To address this, we propose Golden-GRPO Injection (GRIN), a three-stage self-learning framework for continual knowledge injection. Golden-GRPO is a mixed-policy reinforcement learning algorithm designed specifically for knowledge injection, which injects a golden answer to provide learning signal even when on-policy rollouts fail on novel facts. We further introduce Blank and Counter, two document-level benchmarks targeting novel acquisition and counterfactual overwrite respectively, each evaluating single-fact recall, multi-source retrieval, and inferential reasoning. Our experiments establish a clear empirical claim: mixed-policy reinforcement learning enables knowledge absorption beyond what supervised fine-tuning can achieve. GRIN substantially outperforms SFT and mixed-policy RL baselines on the harder question types while matching them on basic fact recall.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型持续知识注入中监督微调仅记忆格式而缺乏泛化的问题，提出了基于混合策略强化学习的GRIN框架，包含三阶段自学习过程。GRIN通过Golden-GRPO算法在策略采样失败时注入黄金答案以提供学习信号，并引入Blank和Counter两个文档级基准评估新知识获取与反事实覆盖。实验表明，GRIN在困难问题类型上显著优于SFT和混合策略RL基线，在基础事实回忆上与之持平。
- innovations_zh: 提出混合策略强化学习算法Golden-GRPO，在策略失败时注入黄金答案以增强知识吸收; 设计Blank和Counter两个文档级基准，分别评估新知识获取与反事实覆盖; 构建三阶段自学习框架GRIN，实现持续知识注入的泛化能力提升
- limitations: 摘要未提供实验规模、模型种类或计算成本等细节，泛化性需进一步验证。基准仅覆盖文档级任务，未涉及多语言或跨领域场景，且未讨论训练稳定性或超参数敏感性。
- potential: 未来可探索将GRIN扩展到更大模型和更多知识类型，并研究其与检索增强等方法的结合。长期看，混合策略RL有望成为持续学习的基础工具，推动模型从记忆走向真正吸收与推理。

### [105] TTPO: Test-Time Policy Optimization  (id=2608.27448, 2026-08-28, rl=20, score=6.8)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Recent prominent post-training methods, such as Reinforcement Learning (RL) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), have driven rapid progress in mathematical reasoning for large language models, yet their reliance on ground-truth labels precludes test-time training (TTT). Replacing ground truth with majority-vote pseudo-labels is a natural alternative, yet it is fragile: an incorrect vote corrupts the teacher and misleads every token. We observe that this failure mode is asymmetric: rollouts that disagree with the pseudo-label are typically wrong regardless of whether the vote itself is correct. Building on this observation, we propose Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO), an asymmetric objective that distills agreeing rollouts via OPSD and penalizes disagreeing rollouts with Grouped RL. Token-level selection further refines both branches: distillation down-weights already-converged positions, while RL penalizes only confident errors. Both updates remain well-grounded even under frequent pseudo-label errors, and majority-vote routing yields tighter self-supervision as the model improves. Without any labels, TTPO matches label-supervised OPSD on five competition-level benchmarks, raises Qwen3-1.7B from 38.0% to 45.2% in TTT, yields +25.2% to +36.4% without thinking, and shows strong cross-task generalization.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出测试时策略优化（TTPO）方法，针对大语言模型数学推理中依赖真实标签的强化学习和自蒸馏方法无法进行测试时训练的问题，利用多数投票伪标签并基于不对称失败模式设计目标函数：对一致rollout进行蒸馏，对不一致rollout进行分组RL惩罚，并通过token级选择细化。实验表明，在无标签情况下，TTPO在五个竞赛级基准上匹配有标签监督的OPSD，将Qwen3-1.7B在测试时训练中从38.0%提升至45.2%，无思考模式下提升25.2%至36.4%，并展现强跨任务泛化能力。
- innovations_zh: 提出不对称目标函数，分别处理与伪标签一致和不一致的rollout，增强伪标签鲁棒性; 引入token级选择，对蒸馏分支降权已收敛位置，对RL分支仅惩罚高置信错误; 利用多数投票路由随模型提升提供更紧的自监督，无需真实标签即可实现测试时训练
- limitations: 摘要未提供与其他无标签方法的详细对比，且实验仅基于数学推理基准，可能限制其通用性。伪标签质量依赖多数投票，在低置信度或高噪声场景下性能可能下降，且未讨论计算开销和超参数敏感性。
- potential: 未来可探索将TTPO扩展到更多任务类型（如代码生成、常识推理）和更大规模模型，并研究动态调整伪标签阈值或结合不确定性估计。长期看，该方法有望推动无需人工标注的持续学习与在线适应，成为LLM部署中自我改进的关键技术。

### [106] Fork Where the Model Changes Its Mind: Belief-Shift Branching for Tree-Structured Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.11061, 2026-09-11, rl=20, score=6.8)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Tree-structured rollouts give critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) step-level credit: fork a chain at an intermediate point, and sibling outcome differences estimate step value. Each fork adds sampling cost, so realistic budgets typically allow only a few forks per chain. A fork placed where the outcome is already largely settled yields siblings that mostly agree and provide almost no credit signal; hence, for a given tree size, where forks are placed largely determines how much step-level RL can gain. Most existing mainstream methods place forks by structure, such as fixed lengths, midpoints, and delimiters, or by next-token entropy. We formalize fork placement as locating the \emph{pivots} of the chain's value curve, where the expected outcome turns. We propose \emph{belief-shift branching}: read the model's answer belief at candidate boundaries and fork just before the step where consecutive beliefs diverge most. Three instantiations, none needing step-level supervision, span access levels: a black-box probe, a logit-lens depth profile, and a learned activation direction, which is fit offline and therefore used only in the validation before RL training. The signal only \emph{places} forks, and the probe costs about $1\%$ of step compute on mathematics and under $5\%$ on code when it runs inside the rollout engine. In that validation, against Monte-Carlo value curves, a belief-shift signal ranks first in each of the eight model$\times$benchmark panels, ahead of entropy, structural, and LLM-judge baselines. In RL across three model families and
- summary_zh: 该论文研究树状展开的RLVR中分叉位置对步级信用估计的影响，指出结果已基本确定处的分叉几乎不提供信用信号。作者将分叉放置形式化为定位链价值曲线的枢轴点，提出信念偏移分叉：在候选边界读取模型答案信念，并在相邻信念分歧最大的步骤前分叉。方法给出黑盒探针、logit-lens深度剖面和离线学习的激活方向三种实例，均无需步级监督；验证中信念偏移信号在八个模型×基准面板中均排名第一，RL实验中在数学与代码任务上超过最强基线。
- innovations_zh: 将分叉放置形式化为定位价值曲线枢轴点; 提出基于答案信念偏移的分叉选择信号; 给出黑盒、logit-lens与激活方向三种实例; 信号仅用于放置分叉且探针开销很低
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了信念偏移信号在验证与RL实验中的相对优势，未说明其理论保证、失败条件或对超参数的敏感性。方法依赖模型答案信念的可读性，黑盒探针、logit-lens与激活方向在不同访问级别下的泛化性和额外工程成本仍不明确。评测集中于数学与代码及有限模型家族，尚不清楚在更开放域或更长链任务中的适用性。
- potential: 未来可探索将信念偏移分叉扩展到多分叉预算分配、与其他信用分配方法结合，以及在线估计信念以降低对离线拟合的依赖。若能在更多领域和模型规模上验证，该思路可能成为树状RLVR中通用的分叉放置原则，并推动对模型内部信念动态的可解释利用。

### [107] Flow3D-OPD: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for 3D Geometry Generation with Flow-Matching Diffusion Transformer  (id=2609.07137, 2026-09-09, rl=20, score=6.7)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 微调与对齐, 多模态, 评测与基准, Agent 与工具使用, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Recent image-to-3D generation models built on flow-matching diffusion Transformers (DiT) can produce high-fidelity meshes, yet their post-training strategy remains largely unexplored. There exist several critical bottlenecks in reinforcement learning: the inherent difficulty of defining comprehensive rewards for 3D geometric quality, and the gradient interference that arises when jointly optimizing heterogeneous objectives. Inspired by the practicability of on-policy distillation (OPD) in large language models and image generation, we propose \textbf{Flow3D-OPD}, a two-stage post-training framework that introduces multi-teacher distillation into 3D geometry generation. In the first stage, we utilize the semi-policy to enhance the foundational capability of the pretrained model and then design an agentic verifier for 3D geometric quality evaluation. Based on the verifier, we could cultivate domain-specialized teacher models via direct preference optimization (DPO). In the second stage, we consolidate heterogeneous expertise into a unified student model through on-policy distillation with hard task-routing sampling and gradient accumulation, which could mitigate the gradient interference in joint optimization. Without relying on elaborate modifications, our straightforward yet effective design achieves consistent improvements across all geometric quality dimensions and surpasses all teacher models in the average metric. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach provides an effective paradigm for reinforcement learning in 3D generation.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对基于流匹配扩散Transformer的图像到3D生成模型的后训练策略缺失问题，提出Flow3D-OPD两阶段框架。第一阶段利用半策略增强基础能力，并设计智能验证器评估3D几何质量，通过DPO训练领域专家教师模型；第二阶段通过带硬任务路由采样和梯度累积的在线策略蒸馏，将异构专家知识整合到统一学生模型中，缓解梯度干扰。实验表明该方法在几何质量各维度上均有一致提升，且平均指标超过所有教师模型。
- innovations_zh: 首次将多教师在线策略蒸馏引入3D几何生成后训练，提出两阶段框架; 设计智能验证器用于3D几何质量评估，并基于DPO培养领域专家教师; 采用硬任务路由采样与梯度累积，有效缓解异构目标联合优化中的梯度干扰
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验数据集和评测指标细节，难以全面评估方法的泛化性。验证器设计依赖代理评估，可能无法完全反映真实几何质量。方法在复杂场景或高分辨率下的计算开销与可扩展性未提及。
- potential: 未来可探索更通用的验证器设计，减少对任务特定知识的依赖。该方法有望扩展到其他3D生成任务（如文本到3D）或与其他后训练技术结合。长期看，多教师蒸馏框架可能成为3D生成模型对齐人类偏好的标准工具。

### [108] Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning  (id=2608.19181, 2026-08-20, rl=20, score=6.6)
- cats: RAG 与知识增强, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准, 推理与数学
- ABS: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier re
- summary_zh: 该论文针对长上下文推理中在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的教师信号与任务验证器奖励不一致的问题，提出组校准在线策略蒸馏（GC-OPD）方法。GC-OPD在每组rollout内分别归一化验证器奖励和轨迹级OPD分数，用其差值作为符号不一致残差，并通过相对优势信用分配（RACA）将残差分配到各token。在五个长上下文基准上，GC-OPD显著提升了Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的平均性能，分别从29.08提升到40.47，从35.12提升到44.65，优于普通OPD。
- innovations_zh: 提出组级相对残差校准，融合验证器奖励与OPD信号，解决教师-验证器不一致问题; 设计相对优势信用分配（RACA），将轨迹级残差按token相对优势分配，保留密集指导
- limitations: 摘要未提供在更多任务类型（如生成式长文本）上的验证，且评测仅基于五个基准，泛化性未知。此外，方法依赖验证器奖励的可用性，在无验证器场景下可能受限。
- potential: 未来可探索将GC-OPD应用于更多模型规模和任务，并研究残差校准的自动化超参数选择。长期看，该方法有望成为长上下文蒸馏的标准组件，推动更可靠的推理模型训练。

### [109] Transcribe, Translate, and Optimize: Joint Reward Learning for Speech Translation  (id=2609.26536, 2026-09-23, rl=20, score=6.6)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 多模态, 推理与数学
- ABS: In LLM-based speech translation, transcription-based chain-of-thought (CoT) suffers from a mismatch between reference transcripts used in supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and model-generated transcripts at inference. To address this, we propose joint recognition and translation fine-tuning via group relative policy optimization (GRPO). We score both transcripts and translations, with translation conditioned on model-generated transcripts, and compare three token advantage strategies. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-3B across four languages, we evaluate CoT against direct speech translation (Direct ST) under SFT and GRPO, training on CoVoST 2 and testing on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. CoT GRPO outperforms Direct ST GRPO by 1.77 and 0.83 average BLEU points on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. Compared to CoT SFT, GRPO boosts BLEU by 0.82 and 0.67 points and reduces word error rate (WER) by 8.8% and 7.2% relatively. These results highlight reinforcement fine-tuning as an effective method to mitigate the training-inference mismatch, jointly improving recognition and translation.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对基于大语言模型的语音翻译中，转录链式思维（CoT）在监督微调时使用的参考转录与推理时模型生成转录不匹配的问题，提出通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行识别与翻译联合微调，并对转录和以模型生成转录为条件的翻译进行评分，比较了三种词元优势策略。基于Qwen2.5-Omni-3B在四种语言上，于CoVoST 2训练并在CoVoST 2和FLEURS测试，对比SFT和GRPO下的CoT与直接语音翻译。结果显示CoT GRPO在平均BLEU上优于直接ST GRPO，且相比CoT SFT，BLEU提升并相对降低WER。
- innovations_zh: 用GRPO联合微调识别与翻译，缓解训练与推理转录不匹配; 对模型生成转录条件下的翻译评分，并比较三种词元优势策略; 在四种语言上系统对比CoT与直接语音翻译在SFT和GRPO下的表现
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了基于Qwen2.5-Omni-3B和四种语言、CoVoST 2与FLEURS数据集的实验结果，结论的跨模型、跨语言与跨领域泛化性尚不明确。评测主要依赖BLEU和WER，未涉及其他翻译质量维度或人工评价，且三种词元优势策略的具体差异与适用条件未在摘要中展开。
- potential: 未来可探索将该联合奖励学习扩展到更多模型规模、语言对和真实场景数据，并研究不同词元优势策略的适用条件与稳定性。长期看，若能与更丰富的奖励信号和评测体系结合，有望推动语音翻译中训练与推理一致性的系统化解决。

### [110] Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO  (id=2608.27351, 2026-08-28, rl=20, score=6.4)
- cats: 推理与数学, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rathe
- summary_zh: 该论文系统研究了进化策略（ES）在LLM推理后训练中的优化行为，并与GRPO对比，发现ES能带来更广泛的推理覆盖，理论上通过验证器投影的Jensen-Shannon多样性提升Pass@K，实验上ES在保持Pass@1的同时获得更高Pass@K，而GRPO则出现熵崩溃。此外，ES的参数更新虽整体漂移大，但功能变化稀疏，且不必然导致灾难性遗忘。最后，研究发现ES在更大LLM中需要更小的种群规模，从而将ES定位为一种独特的推理后训练范式。
- innovations_zh: 首次从理论和实验上揭示ES相比GRPO具有更广泛的推理覆盖优势; 提出GRPO-ES顺序训练策略，结合两者在Pass@1和Pass@K上的优势; 发现ES的功能稀疏性，表明大参数移动不一定导致广泛功能变化; 揭示ES在更大LLM中需要更小种群规模的超参数规律
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验设置和数据集细节，难以评估结论的泛化性。评测主要基于Pass@K和Pass@1，可能未覆盖其他推理质量维度。理论分析基于特定假设，实际应用中的有效性需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索ES与GRPO的更深度融合策略，以及在不同模型规模和任务类型上的适用性。功能稀疏性研究可延伸至参数高效微调和模型压缩领域，长期看ES有望成为推理后训练的标准组件之一。

### [111] RLVR landscapes for iterated multiplications can be benign: Insights from spin-glass theory  (id=2609.28625, 2026-09-25, rl=20, score=6.4)
- cats: 推理与数学
- ABS: Despite the importance of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), the extent to which it can learn new reasoning capabilities remains debated. Here we study the optimization landscape of RLVR on algorithmic tasks, such as iterated group and quasigroup multiplication. To this end, we map entropy-regularized RLVR over myopic tabular policies onto an energy-based (spin-glass) model over deterministic policies. This mapping upper-bounds what RLVR can achieve, and lets us rigorously characterize the landscape in this tabular setting. We show, both theoretically and experimentally, that for a wide class of models and tasks with uncorrelated inputs, this landscape is benign, containing no local minima that could trap RLVR training. Rather, the practical difficulty of these tasks appears to stem, at least in part, from issues such as diffusive barriers and gradient-estimation error in traversing the landscape. These are genuine obstacles that can prevent a solution from being found, but they are distinct from the landscape itself being rugged. We show that these obstacles can often be mitigated through the choice of entropy regulator. Consistent with this theory, we find that a transformer trained from scratch, using only last-token rewards, successfully learns an algorithmic chain of thought for iterated non-Abelian group multiplications.
- summary_zh: 该研究关注带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能否学习新的推理能力这一争议问题，聚焦迭代群与拟群乘法等算法任务。作者将熵正则化RLVR在短视表格策略上的优化映射为确定性策略上的自旋玻璃能量模型，从而对RLVR可达上限进行界定并严格刻画其优化景观。理论与实验表明，对输入不相关的一类模型和任务，该景观是良性的，不存在会困住训练的局部极小值；实际困难主要来自扩散势垒与梯度估计误差，并可通过选择熵正则化器缓解。与此一致，从零训练的Transformer仅用末位奖励即可学会迭代非阿贝尔群乘法的算法思维链。
- innovations_zh: 将熵正则化RLVR映射为自旋玻璃能量模型，给出可达上限; 在表格设定下严格刻画优化景观并证明其良性; 区分景观崎岖与扩散势垒、梯度估计误差等真实障碍; 用末位奖励从零训练Transformer学会非阿贝尔群乘法思维链
- limitations: 结论主要建立在表格短视策略与熵正则化RLVR的映射之上，属于对RLVR可达上限的界定，未必直接适用于深度网络与长程信用分配等更一般设定。理论保证依赖输入不相关等条件，对相关输入或更复杂任务的适用性尚不明确。实验部分仅提及一个从零训练的Transformer案例，评测范围与统计稳健性有限。
- potential: 未来可探索将自旋玻璃景观分析推广到非表格、深度策略及更复杂推理任务，检验良性景观结论的边界条件。可进一步研究熵正则化器及其他优化干预如何缓解扩散势垒与梯度估计误差，并系统比较不同任务结构与输入相关性下的训练难度。长期看，这类分析有望为RLVR的可学习性与训练策略设计提供理论指导。

### [112] Learning to Use Tools: Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning  (id=2608.28447, 2026-08-31, rl=20, score=6.2)
- cats: 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Current large language models (LLMs) increasingly benefit from external tool integration, especially for tasks requiring reliable computation and verification. Motivated by this, we study calculator tool calling for improving mathematical reasoning on the Countdown task. We first analyze reasoning failures and find that calculation errors account for a substantial portion of incorrect responses. We then construct supervised fine-tuning datasets to teach the model useful tool-use patterns and how to interpret returned outputs. Building on this tool-formatted policy, we apply several on-policy reinforcement learning methods, including RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, and DAPO, using automatically verifiable final-answer rewards. To enable a more reliable evaluation, we construct a fresh 1,024-problem held-out Countdown benchmark with no exact overlap with the training data. Our results show that calculator tool integration consistently improves both SFT and RL baselines, yielding roughly 10 percentage-point gains across pass@k. Among the RL methods, Tool-DAPO achieves the strongest performance, improving pass@1 from 35.8% for Tool-SFT to 66.0%. Further analysis shows that RL encourages more effective tool use even when only final-answer rewards are provided. These findings suggest that tool integration reduces arithmetic and verification errors, while RL increases the probability of correct reasoning traces.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对大语言模型在数学推理中的计算错误问题，提出通过强化学习整合计算器工具来提升Countdown任务的表现。作者先构建监督微调数据集教授工具使用模式，再应用RLOO、GRPO、DAPO等在线强化学习方法，并基于自动可验证的最终答案奖励进行优化。实验表明，工具集成显著提升了SFT和RL基线，其中Tool-DAPO将pass@1从35.8%提升至66.0%，且强化学习能促进更有效的工具使用。
- innovations_zh: 系统分析计算错误占比，并构建工具使用SFT数据集; 对比多种在线RL方法（RLOO、GRPO、DAPO）在工具集成下的效果; 构建无重叠的1,024题新基准，提升评估可靠性
- limitations: 摘要未提供模型规模、训练成本等细节，可能影响可复现性。评测仅基于Countdown任务，且未报告人类对比或跨任务泛化，结论的普适性有限。
- potential: 未来可探索更复杂的工具（如代码解释器、符号求解器）与RL结合，并扩展至多步骤推理任务。长期看，该方法有望促进LLM在科学计算、教育辅导等领域的可靠应用。

### [113] BRACE: Anchored Bellman-Residual Correction for Stale Critics in Asynchronous RL  (id=2609.09783, 2026-09-10, rl=20, score=6.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Asynchronous reinforcement learning has become the standard way to scale training for language models, but the resulting policy lag biases the critic toward the stale behavior policy. Existing work on asynchronous LLM training corrects the actor and leaves this bias unaddressed, while the off-policy value correction of classical RL does not carry over to long-horizon agentic tasks, since a short correction horizon leaves the regression target free of the reward and a long one lets the product of importance ratios drift exponentially with the trajectory length. We propose BRACE, an anchored Bellman-residual correction for stale value models. BRACE bounds the correction horizon to a prefix of policy tokens and anchors a constant-weight Monte-Carlo tail beyond it, which separates policy correction from reward propagation. BRACE improves mean@1 on BrowseComp-Plus by $2.4\%$ over the strongest baseline, runs $2.46\times$ faster per step than synchronous training, and remains stable $50$ updates off-policy.
- summary_zh: 异步强化学习是扩展语言模型训练的标准方式，但策略滞后会使评论家偏向过时的行为策略。现有异步LLM训练工作只校正演员而忽略该偏差，经典强化学习的离策略价值校正又因短校正视野缺乏奖励、长视野重要性比率乘积随轨迹长度指数漂移而无法直接用于长程智能体任务。作者提出BRACE，将校正视野限制在策略令牌前缀，并在其后锚定恒定权重的蒙特卡洛尾部，从而分离策略校正与奖励传播。在BrowseComp-Plus上，BRACE的mean@1比最强基线提升2.4%，每步运行速度是同步训练的2.46倍，并在离策略50次更新后仍保持稳定。
- innovations_zh: 将校正视野限制在策略令牌前缀，避免长程重要性比率指数漂移; 在前缀之外锚定恒定权重蒙特卡洛尾部，分离策略校正与奖励传播; 针对异步LLM训练中评论家偏向过时行为策略的偏差进行校正
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了BrowseComp-Plus上的结果，未说明该方法在其他任务或模型规模上的泛化性。校正视野前缀长度与蒙特卡洛尾部权重的选择方式未在摘要中交代，可能影响方法稳定性。此外，仅凭摘要无法判断其与同步训练在最终性能上是否全面可比。
- potential: 未来可探索前缀长度与尾部权重的自适应调节机制，并将BRACE推广到更多长程智能体任务与不同规模的LLM。若能与演员校正方法结合，可能形成更完整的异步LLM强化学习训练框架，具有长期应用价值。

### [114] Why Summaries Turn Neutral: Policy Attribution for Sentiment Drift in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback  (id=2608.15530, 2026-08-18, rl=20, score=5.8)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF) aligns LLMs with human preferences, improving summarization fluency and safety, but causes sentiment drift: overly neutral summaries stripped of emotional nuance. We diagnose why RL acts as a sentiment neutralizer and present Policy Attribution, a framework using gradient and logit decomposition to trace drift to reward model (RM) signals and KL (Kullback-Leibler) penalty. Sentiment drift reflects a strategic bias toward "low-risk" tokens maximizing expected rewards under preference uncertainty (Stiennon et al., 2020; Gao, Schulman, and Hilton, 2023). On Reddit TL;DR and CNN/DailyMail, RLHF summaries get higher rewards but show 30-40% lower sentiment variance. Cross-lingual analysis across eight languages shows language-independent drift, with morphologically richer languages more suppressed (Krasitskii et al., 2026). We propose and validate a sentiment-aware regularization technique reducing drift by 18-22% without harming summary quality. The code and toolkit will be public.
- summary_zh: 该研究诊断了RLHF导致摘要情感漂移（过度中性化）的原因，提出Policy Attribution框架，通过梯度和logit分解追踪奖励模型信号与KL惩罚的影响。在Reddit TL;DR和CNN/DailyMail上，RLHF摘要虽获得更高奖励，但情感方差降低30-40%，且跨八种语言均出现漂移，形态丰富的语言抑制更明显。作者提出情感感知正则化技术，在不损害摘要质量的前提下将漂移减少18-22%。
- innovations_zh: 提出Policy Attribution框架，用梯度和logit分解定位情感漂移来源; 揭示RLHF中低风险token策略偏好导致情感中性化的机制; 提出情感感知正则化，减少漂移且不损质量
- limitations: 摘要未提供Policy Attribution的详细数学形式及计算开销，可能影响可复现性。情感方差作为唯一指标可能不全面，未涉及其他情感维度（如情感极性）的评估。正则化效果仅基于两个数据集和八种语言，泛化性需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可扩展Policy Attribution到其他RLHF任务（如对话、翻译），并探索更细粒度的情感控制。该框架或可结合动态KL调整，实现更精细的偏好对齐。长期看，有助于构建更透明、可控的RLHF系统，推动情感智能与多语言生成的发展。

### [115] SPEAR: Distilling Domain-Adaptive Reasoning Skeletons via Sequential Symbolic Alignment in Reinforcement Learning  (id=2608.26550, 2026-08-28, rl=20, score=5.3)
- cats: 推理与数学, 推理优化与效率, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning-based knowledge distillation has the potential to transfer complex reasoning from teacher to student models, yet it currently faces a critical dilemma: researchers must choose between sparse outcome-based rewards, which provide insufficient logical guidance, or expensive neural Process Reward Models (PRMs) for dense signals. We resolve this by introducing SPEAR (Symbolic Process Evaluation and Alignment Reward), a training-free and plug-and-play process reward method for sequence-level on-policy distillation. SPEAR projects natural-language reasoning traces into domain-adaptive symbolic milestones, providing an efficient proxy for process-level reasoning alignment. By utilizing the longest common subsequence (LCS) to align student explorations with teacher milestones, SPEAR provides a dense, order-aware reward signal that enforces logical consistency without the need for an external neural verifier. Our experiments across math, science, and commonsense reasoning tasks demonstrate that SPEAR effectively bridges the reasoning gap between student and teacher models via sequence-level distillation with efficient dense process rewards. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/zhuochunli/SPEAR.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对强化学习知识蒸馏中奖励信号设计的困境，提出了一种名为SPEAR的无训练、即插即用的过程奖励方法。SPEAR通过将自然语言推理轨迹投影为领域自适应的符号里程碑，并利用最长公共子序列对齐学生探索与教师里程碑，从而提供密集且有序的奖励信号。在数学、科学和常识推理任务上的实验表明，SPEAR能有效弥合学生与教师模型之间的推理差距。
- innovations_zh: 提出无训练、即插即用的符号过程奖励方法，替代昂贵的神经PRM; 利用最长公共子序列实现顺序感知的密集奖励，强化逻辑一致性
- limitations: 摘要未提供与基线方法的详细性能对比或消融实验，效果提升幅度不明确。方法依赖符号里程碑的构建质量，可能难以泛化到高度开放或非结构化推理任务。评测仅覆盖数学、科学和常识推理，未涉及更复杂或长尾领域。
- potential: 未来可探索自动构建符号里程碑的方法，减少人工设计成本。该方法可与其他强化学习算法或多模态推理结合，扩展其适用范围。长期看，SPEAR有望成为通用推理蒸馏工具，推动轻量级学生模型在复杂任务上的部署。

### [116] SRPO: Self-Reflective Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Reasoning  (id=2608.23493, 2026-08-25, rl=19, score=9.0)
- cats: 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Self-reflection is a powerful mechanism for credit assignment in human learning, converting sparse outcome feedback into actionable guidance. However, its potential for post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) remains underexplored. We propose Self-Reflective Policy Optimization (SRPO), a framework that internalizes this capability. SRPO enables LLMs to analyze their own completed trajectories, synthesize errors into concise "reflection patches," and use reflection-conditioned teacher scores on student on-policy rollouts as dense token-level training signals. This process effectively transforms sparse terminal supervision into dense, token-level learning signals without requiring external critics, separate reward models, or larger teacher models. We demonstrate that SRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance across mathematical reasoning and long-horizon agentic benchmarks with exceptional data efficiency. Using a Qwen3-8B base model, SRPO attains 73.3% on AIME'24 using only 8% (0.08x) of the training FLOPs required by scaled supervised fine-tuning, while significantly improving success rates on WebShop (64.7%), ALFWorld (76.8%), and SWE-Bench-Lite (31.2%). Code is available at https://github.com/Galleons2029/SRPO
- summary_zh: 该论文提出自我反思策略优化（SRPO）框架，使大语言模型在训练中分析自身轨迹、生成反思片段，并利用反思条件下的教师分数作为密集的token级训练信号，从而将稀疏的终端反馈转化为密集的学习信号。在数学推理和长程智能体任务上，SRPO以极低的数据和计算开销取得领先性能，例如基于Qwen3-8B在AIME'24上达到73.3%，仅需监督微调8%的训练FLOPs，并在WebShop、ALFWorld和SWE-Bench-Lite上显著提升成功率。
- innovations_zh: 将自我反思内化为训练框架，无需外部奖励模型或更大教师模型; 利用反思条件教师分数生成密集token级信号，提升数据效率; 在长程推理和智能体任务上以极低计算开销达到SOTA
- limitations: 摘要未提供与基线方法的详细对比设置，如计算量、数据构成等，难以全面评估其优势边界。评测集中在数学和智能体任务，未涉及通用对话或生成质量，泛化性未知。依赖反思片段的质量，若模型自我分析不准确可能影响训练效果，但摘要未讨论该风险。
- potential: 未来可探索将SRPO扩展到更多任务类型（如代码生成、多步规划）和更大模型规模，验证其可扩展性。自我反思机制与在线学习、多智能体协作的结合可能进一步提升长程推理能力。长期看，该框架或推动无需人工标注的自主训练范式发展。

### [117] Debiasing Text-to-Image Evaluation via Implicit Cultural Alignment Reward Modeling  (id=2607.15740, 2026-09-17, rl=19, score=8.3)
- cats: 多模态, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐, 预训练与架构
- ABS: As Text-to-Image (T2I) systems rapidly advance, evaluating the cultural authenticity of synthesized content has become increasingly important for fair and trustworthy generative AI. Existing T2I evaluation metrics and multimodal judges often rely on visual-semantic representations that underrepresent implicit cultural norms, leading to biased preference judgments and the omission of fine-grained cultural cues. In addition, visual question answering (VQA)-based evaluators typically depend on autoregressive text generation, which limits their scalability for real-time reward modeling. To address these limitations, we introduce an Implicit Cultural Alignment Reward Model built upon a lightweight 4.2-billion-parameter Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM). Our framework integrates an Implicit Cultural Probe with a Skip-connection Cross-Attention (SkipCA) mechanism, enabling late-stage semantic features to directly attend to early-stage visual representations and better preserve culturally salient details. Evaluations on 3,323 challenging and carefully curated image pairs from the CulturalFrames benchmark show that our approach achieves 83.49% pairwise accuracy, with Pearson and Kendall correlation coefficients of 0.5268 and 0.3749, respectively, outperforming representative vision-language metrics and MLLM-based evaluators. Moreover, by bypassing autoregressive text generation, our model processes each evaluation in 0.21 seconds under our local inference setup, achieving a $10\times$ speedup over standard VQA-based evaluators. These results suggest that the proposed reward mod
- summary_zh: 该研究关注文本到图像系统生成内容的文化真实性评估问题，指出现有指标与多模态评判器常因视觉语义表示不足而忽略隐性文化规范，导致偏好判断有偏。作者提出基于轻量级42亿参数多模态大语言模型的隐性文化对齐奖励模型，结合隐性文化探针与跳跃连接交叉注意力机制，使后期语义特征直接关注早期视觉表示。在CulturalFrames基准的3323对图像上，该方法取得83.49%成对准确率，Pearson和Kendall相关系数分别为0.5268和0.3749，且单次评估仅需0.21秒，较标准VQA评估器提速10倍。
- innovations_zh: 提出隐性文化对齐奖励模型，用轻量级MLLM评估T2I文化真实性; 设计隐性文化探针与SkipCA机制，保留文化显著视觉细节; 绕过自回归文本生成，实现高效标量奖励输出
- limitations: 摘要仅报告在CulturalFrames基准上的结果，未说明该基准的文化覆盖范围与泛化能力，方法在其他文化或数据集上的有效性尚不确定。评估主要依赖成对准确率和相关系数，缺乏对隐性文化探针与SkipCA各组件贡献的消融分析。此外，0.21秒速度基于本地推理设置，实际部署中的可扩展性仍需验证。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更多文化维度与多模态生成任务，验证奖励模型在RLHF和DPO中的实际优化效果。可进一步探索隐性文化探针的可解释性与跨文化迁移能力，并研究更广泛基准上的泛化表现。长期看，该方向有望推动文化感知的生成式AI评估与对齐研究。

### [118] Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy: Learning Autonomous Policies from Imperfect VLM Teachers  (id=2609.01567, 2026-09-02, rl=19, score=8.2)
- cats: 多模态, Agent 与工具使用, 推理优化与效率, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide useful priors for interactive decision-making, but using them directly as policies is expensive and brittle: they must be queried at every step, do not improve from environment interaction, and can repeat systematic errors. We study how to learn a cheap autonomous policy from an online, expensive, and imperfect but informative VLM teacher. We propose SAGE (Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy), a framework that queries a VLM only when the learner is uncertain, executes the suggested action during training, and distills guidance into a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy. Because VLM advice is not always reliable, SAGE can weight teacher-action distillation using environment-derived advantages rather than treating all suggestions as equally useful. Across sparse-reward visual reasoning and navigation tasks, SAGE learns policies that act without VLM guidance at evaluation time and improves over unguided RL in several environments, including settings where the learned policy exceeds its VLM teacher. The results show that selective guidance is most beneficial when the VLM can help the agent discover high-reward trajectories, and less useful when unguided exploration already succeeds or teacher actions do not lead to informative experience. SAGE also reduces VLM usage by prompting the teacher only on a fraction of training steps and requiring no VLM calls at deployment. Overall, our results suggest that VLMs don't need to be used as fixed policies to be useful; they can instead act as temporary, imperfect sources of guidance whose
- summary_zh: 该论文提出SAGE框架，用于从在线、昂贵但不完美的VLM教师中学习轻量自主策略。SAGE仅在学生不确定时查询VLM，并在训练中执行其建议，同时利用环境优势加权蒸馏，而非平等对待所有教师动作。在稀疏奖励的视觉推理和导航任务中，SAGE训练出的策略在评估时无需VLM，且性能优于无引导的RL，甚至在某些环境中超过VLM教师。结果表明，选择性引导在VLM帮助发现高回报轨迹时最有效，且能大幅减少VLM调用。
- innovations_zh: 提出基于熵的选择性查询机制，仅在学生不确定时调用VLM，降低训练成本。; 利用环境优势加权教师动作蒸馏，缓解VLM建议不可靠的问题。; 实现训练后无需VLM的自主策略，且性能可超越教师模型。
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体任务规模、VLM查询频率或计算开销的量化数据，难以评估实际效率提升。此外，实验环境有限，未讨论在更复杂或连续控制任务中的泛化性，且未分析教师错误建议对训练稳定性的潜在影响。
- potential: 未来可探索更智能的不确定性估计方法，或结合多教师与在线学习以增强引导质量。长期看，该框架可扩展到真实机器人或具身智能任务，并可能推动VLM与RL的深度融合，实现从离线知识到在线技能的持续转化。

### [119] When LLM Meets Tree Search: A Systematic View of Inference as Search in Large Language Models  (id=2608.30395, 2026-09-01, rl=19, score=8.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, 推理优化与效率, 预训练与架构, 评测与基准
- ABS: As pretraining scaling laws approach saturation, Test-Time Scaling (TTS) has emerged as an important direction for improving reasoning by allocating inference-time compute to a fixed model prior. Viewed at a high level, TTS reframes inference as search over a space of partial reasoning states. While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) exposes intermediate steps, common instantiations rely on single-trajectory decoding, limiting recovery from early errors and exploration. This survey systematizes recent progress in tree-search-based reasoning, viewing inference as instance-specific optimization rather than decoding. We trace the evolution from uninformed search to Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), highlighting how sampling-based control supports principled exploration-exploitation trade-offs. To unify a fragmented literature, we introduce a Unified Design Space spanning search topology, evaluation signals, and control dynamics, and advocate a standardized compute-reporting abstraction to make compute-accuracy trade-offs explicit and comparable.
- summary_zh: 该综述聚焦于大语言模型中的测试时扩展，将推理视为对部分推理状态空间的搜索，系统梳理了从无信息搜索到蒙特卡洛树搜索的演进，并强调基于采样的控制机制以平衡探索与利用。为统一碎片化研究，作者提出了一个涵盖搜索拓扑、评估信号和控制动态的统一设计空间，并倡导标准化的计算报告抽象，以显式比较计算量与准确率的权衡。
- innovations_zh: 将推理形式化为实例特定的搜索优化，而非单纯解码; 提出统一设计空间，整合搜索拓扑、评估信号与控制动态; 倡导标准化计算报告抽象，促进计算-精度权衡的可比性
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验或定量结果，缺乏对统一设计空间有效性的实证验证。综述范围可能局限于树搜索方法，未涵盖其他测试时扩展策略。
- potential: 未来可基于统一设计空间开发更高效的搜索控制算法，并探索与强化学习、自适应计算分配的结合。长期来看，该框架有望成为测试时扩展研究的标准化参考，推动推理性能的持续提升。

### [120] UR$^2$: Unify RAG and Reasoning through Reinforcement Learning  (id=2508.06165, 2026-09-24, rl=19, score=7.6)
- cats: RAG 与知识增强, 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性, 领域应用
- ABS: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities through two complementary paradigms: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for knowledge grounding and Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) for complex reasoning. However, existing attempts to unify these paradigms remain narrow in scope, typically limited to open-domain QA with fixed retrieval settings, which constrains generalization to broader domains. To address this limitation, we propose UR$^2$ (Unified RAG and Reasoning)), a general reinforcement learning framework that dynamically coordinates retrieval and reasoning. UR$^2$ introduces two key designs: a difficulty-aware curriculum that selectively invokes retrieval only for challenging instances, and a hybrid knowledge access strategy that combines domain-specific offline corpora with on-the-fly LLM-generated summaries. Together, these components mitigate the imbalance between retrieval and reasoning and improve robustness to noisy information. Experiments on open-domain QA, MMLU-Pro, medical, and mathematical reasoning tasks show that UR$^2$, built on Qwen-2.5-3/7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B, consistently outperforms existing RAG and RL baselines, and achieves performance comparable to GPT-4o-mini and GPT-4.1-mini on several benchmarks. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/Tsinghua-dhy/UR2.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对现有统一RAG与推理的尝试多局限于开放域问答和固定检索设置、泛化能力受限的问题，提出了UR$^2$通用强化学习框架，动态协调检索与推理。UR$^2$包含难度感知课程（仅对困难实例调用检索）和混合知识访问策略（结合领域离线语料与LLM即时生成摘要）两个关键设计，以缓解检索与推理的不平衡并提升对噪声信息的鲁棒性。在开放域问答、MMLU-Pro、医学和数学推理任务上，基于Qwen-2.5-3/7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B构建的UR$^2$持续优于现有RAG和RL基线，在多个基准上达到与GPT-4o-mini和GPT-4.1-mini相当的性能。
- innovations_zh: 提出动态协调检索与推理的通用强化学习框架UR$^2$; 设计难度感知课程，仅对困难实例选择性调用检索; 采用混合知识访问策略，结合离线语料与LLM即时摘要; 缓解检索与推理不平衡并提升对噪声信息的鲁棒性
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在开放域问答、MMLU-Pro、医学和数学推理任务上的结果，未说明其他领域或更广泛任务类型的适用性。评测主要对比现有RAG和RL基线及部分GPT-4o-mini/GPT-4.1-mini，未提供消融实验、计算成本或检索质量依赖等细节，框架的鲁棒性和可扩展性仍需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将UR$^2$扩展到更多领域和任务类型，并研究难度感知课程与混合知识访问策略在不同模型规模下的泛化性。长期来看，该框架有望为构建能自适应协调检索与推理的通用LLM系统提供基础，但需进一步验证其效率、可解释性和对检索噪声的鲁棒边界。

### [121] Environments as Scaffold: Enriching Feedback to Bootstrap Self-Evolving Agents in Long-Horizon Tasks  (id=2609.08404, 2026-09-09, rl=19, score=7.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Large Language Models demonstrate remarkable proficiency in static reasoning, yet training them as autonomous agents through Reinforcement Learning (RL) for long-horizon tasks is often hindered by severe reward sparsity. While conventional \textit{agent-side warming} up via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can alleviate this, it is frequently limited by data scarcity and constrained exploration. To address this, we propose a paradigm shift to \textit{environment-side adaptation} by constructing \textbf{F}eedback-\textbf{E}nriched \textbf{E}nvironments (\textbf{FEEs}). Through a pilot study, we establish a feedback design strategy that reformulates environments by transitioning from action guidance to observation enrichment during the later stages of both intra-episode exploration and inter-episode evolution. Large-scale experiments on SciWorld and BFCL benchmarks using various Qwen3 model scales and RL algorithms such as GRPO, GSPO, and DAPO demonstrate that FEEs consistently yield performance improvements over standard settings. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that training with FEEs \textbf{(1)} stabilizes training dynamics by reducing entropy volatility, \textbf{(2)} facilitates proactive state-space exploration in difficult tasks, \textbf{(3) }ensures the internalization of environmental guidance into policy weights rather than acting as a mere inference-time prior, and \textbf{(4) }identifies intra-group feedback consistency as a critical boundary for stable optimization.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对长时程任务中强化学习奖励稀疏的问题，提出从智能体侧预热转向环境侧适配，通过构建反馈增强环境（FEEs）来引导智能体探索。作者基于试点研究设计了从动作指导过渡到观察增强的反馈策略，并在SciWorld和BFCL基准上结合多种Qwen3模型规模及GRPO、GSPO、DAPO算法进行大规模实验，结果显示FEEs能持续提升性能。进一步分析表明，FEEs训练可稳定训练动态、促进主动状态空间探索、将环境指导内化到策略权重中，并发现组内反馈一致性是稳定优化的关键边界。
- innovations_zh: 提出环境侧适配范式，通过构建反馈增强环境替代传统智能体侧预热，缓解奖励稀疏问题。; 设计从动作指导到观察增强的反馈策略，并验证其在多算法、多模型规模下的有效性。; 揭示FEEs训练能稳定动态、促进探索并内化指导，且识别组内反馈一致性为优化边界。
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体性能提升幅度或统计显著性，仅称“一致改进”，缺乏量化细节。实验基准限于SciWorld和BFCL，未涵盖更广泛的长时程任务类型，泛化性存疑。此外，反馈策略的适用边界（如任务复杂度、反馈密度）未明确，且未讨论计算成本或实现复杂度。
- potential: 未来可探索FEEs在更多复杂环境（如具身智能、多智能体）中的适用性，并研究反馈自动生成机制以降低人工设计成本。长期价值在于推动环境自适应与智能体学习协同进化，可能为可扩展的自主智能体训练提供新范式。

### [122] Consolidating RLVR Capabilities Across Domains: A Deep Dive into Fusion Paradigms  (id=2608.27409, 2026-09-19, rl=19, score=6.8)
- cats: 评测与基准, 推理优化与效率
- ABS: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves specific capabilities of large language models, but covering multiple capabilities often involves training separate domain experts and subsequently consolidating them. We organize three fusion paradigms by the artifacts they reuse: Merge combines expert task vectors, Mix RL pools their datasets, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) uses both. Because they have largely been studied in isolation, how they compare and how to choose among them remain unclear. We compare all three using shared experts and data across model scales and a multi-domain benchmark suite. Although their average performance differs by at most 1.4 points, the gap reaches 8.6 points on a single benchmark, with domain-level variation tracking cross-domain relations visible in task-vector geometry. Training dynamics expose distinct constraints: Mix RL depends on domain mixture proportions, MOPD remains bounded by its teachers, and Merge compresses all expert updates into one. All three improve single-sample accuracy without measurable gains in solution coverage or losses in held-out capabilities. These results yield a practical guideline: use Merge when experts already exist and cheap fusion is paramount; Mix RL when training a unified model without experts, with domain proportions adjusted for cross-domain transfer; and MOPD when preserving domain-specific gains matters more than surpassing teachers or minimizing end-to-end cost.
- summary_zh: 该研究关注如何整合多个领域专家模型以覆盖多种能力，按复用对象将融合范式分为Merge、Mix RL和MOPD三类。作者在共享专家与数据、多种模型规模和多领域基准上系统比较三者，发现平均性能差距最多1.4分，但单基准差距可达8.6分，且领域差异与任务向量几何中的跨域关系相关。训练动态显示Mix RL依赖领域混合比例、MOPD受教师上限约束、Merge将所有专家更新压缩为一。三者均提升单样本准确率，但未显著改变解覆盖或损害留出能力。
- innovations_zh: 按复用对象统一划分三类RLVR融合范式; 用共享专家与数据在多规模多领域基准上横向比较; 揭示任务向量几何与领域级性能差异的关联; 给出依场景选择Merge、Mix RL或MOPD的实用指南
- limitations: 研究结论主要基于摘要所述的多领域基准套件，未说明是否覆盖开放生成或真实交互场景。评测以准确率和覆盖率为核心，可能未充分反映推理深度、鲁棒性或安全性的变化。此外，融合范式的比较依赖共享专家与数据设置，结论向其他专家来源或数据分布的推广性仍不确定。
- potential: 未来可进一步探索任务向量几何与跨域迁移关系的理论解释，并据此设计自适应融合策略。可研究在专家异构、数据不可共享或持续学习场景下三类范式的表现。长期看，该方向有助于构建可扩展、可组合的多能力大模型训练与部署框架。

### [123] Explore More, Drift Less: Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning Can Suffice for Long-Horizon Interactive Agents  (id=2609.01245, 2026-09-02, rl=19, score=6.8)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning is a natural way to post-train LLM agents for long-horizon interactive tasks judged only by end-of-task verification, yet a shared belief holds that outcome-only RL soon hits a ceiling on small open models. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. We argue the ceiling is an artifact of two failures of common practice. Signal starvation: group-relative RL with sparse outcome-only rewards yields a gradient only when a task's rollout group mixes successes and failures, so under-scaled exploration silences exactly the hardest, most instructive tasks. Policy drift: squeezing many updates out of a small task pool degrades the policy itself, as an unanchored objective lets the sampling distribution collapse exactly when saturation has already made informative groups rare. We present CANOPY (Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL), a minimalist protocol attacking both directly: scale same-task exploration until the natural signal reappears, keep every update on-policy, KL-anchored, and confined to the agent's own action tokens, then cash in an enlarged interaction budget at test time. On AppWorld, a long-horizon interactive coding benchmark, a Qwen3-14B policy trained with CANOPY through environment interaction alone--without task-specific supervision, auxiliary credit signals, or elaborate agent scaffolding--topped the public leaderboard (Feb. 2026; Test-Normal TGC 86.9, Test-Challenge 67.6), and the same design principles lift Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verifie
- summary_zh: 该论文针对仅依赖结果奖励的强化学习在长程交互智能体训练中性能受限的问题，提出其根源在于信号饥饿和策略漂移。为此，作者设计了CANOPY协议，通过扩大同任务探索、保持在线策略与KL锚定等机制，在AppWorld和SWE-bench上显著提升了小模型的性能。结果表明，仅靠智能体与环境交互的强化学习即可将长程能力内化到小模型中。
- innovations_zh: 提出信号饥饿与策略漂移的双重失败机制解释，并针对性设计CANOPY协议; 仅依赖结果奖励和在线策略更新，无需额外监督或复杂脚手架即可提升性能
- limitations: 摘要未提供方法在更广泛任务或更大模型上的泛化证据，且评测基准集中于编码类任务，可能限制结论的普适性。此外，性能提升是否依赖特定超参数或计算资源尚不明确，需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将CANOPY应用于更多交互式任务（如具身智能、对话系统），并研究其与多任务学习或模型规模扩展的结合。长期看，该工作可能推动更简洁、可复现的智能体强化学习训练范式。

### [124] VideoScout: Learning Agentic Active Exploration with Adaptive Reasoning Pacing for Long Video Understanding  (id=2609.15606, 2026-09-15, rl=19, score=6.8)
- cats: 多模态, Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress on short video understanding yet remain limited on long videos due to the limited visual context window. Prevailing approaches rely on uniform frame sampling or recent coarse-to-fine agentic zooming, both of which struggle to localize sparse, decisive evidence in sufficiently long videos. We formulate long video understanding as a \textbf{Sequential Evidence Acquisition (SEA)} problem, in which an agent reads the video turn by turn along the temporal axis, deciding at each turn how fast to watch, what evidence to retain, when to revisit uncertain segments, and when to stop and answer. Inspired by this view, we propose \textbf{VideoScout}, a multi-turn reasoning agent that instantiates the SEA paradigm through adaptive reasoning pacing. Specifically, by dynamically controlling the viewing pace, VideoScout enables efficient traversal of long videos within a bounded visual context window, allowing the agent to access more video content while balancing content analysis depth with reading efficiency. To train VideoScout, we construct VideoScout-66K, a set of over 66K high-quality exploration turns from 10K answer-verified trajectories, and adopt a two-stage pipeline: cold-start supervised fine-tuning teaches the agent per-turn output format, while the Decoupled Clip and Dynamic sAmpling Policy Optimization (DAPO) algorithm performs trajectory-level reinforcement learning with a composite reward that jointly considers answer accuracy, output format compliance, and the temporal alignment between the agent's
- summary_zh: 针对多模态大模型因视觉上下文窗口受限而难以理解长视频的问题，该研究将长视频理解形式化为顺序证据获取（SEA）问题，提出多轮推理智能体VideoScout，通过自适应推理节奏动态控制观看速度，在有限视觉上下文内高效遍历长视频。为训练该模型，作者构建了包含66K高质量探索轮次的VideoScout-66K数据集，并采用冷启动监督微调与DAPO轨迹级强化学习两阶段流程，奖励综合考量答案准确性、输出格式合规性及观看进度与教师答案时序的IoU对齐。实验表明，7B模型在长视频理解与推理基准上相较现有训练过的7B智能体模型取得强劲表现。
- innovations_zh: 将长视频理解建模为顺序证据获取问题，智能体沿时间轴逐轮决策观看速度、证据保留与回访时机; 提出自适应推理节奏机制，在有限视觉上下文窗口内平衡内容分析深度与阅读效率; 构建66K探索轮次数据集并设计DAPO轨迹级强化学习，以复合奖励联合优化答案、格式与时序对齐
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了与现有训练过的7B智能体模型的比较，未给出具体基准名称、数值提升或与更大规模模型及非智能体方法的对比，性能优势的幅度与泛化性尚不明确。方法依赖教师答案时序计算IoU奖励，可能受教师信号质量与标注覆盖限制，且未说明在极长视频或开放域场景下的可扩展性。
- potential: 未来可探索将该顺序证据获取范式扩展至更长的视频、多视频流或交互式环境，并研究无需教师时序信号的奖励设计以降低监督依赖。若能与更高效的视觉编码和记忆机制结合，有望推动长视频理解智能体在真实场景中的长期应用。

### [125] Prompt-Driven Exploration: Language as an Exploration Space for VLA Reinforcement Learning  (id=2607.08837, 2026-09-19, rl=19, score=6.7)
- cats: 多模态, 推理与数学
- ABS: Exploration is essential to RL since a policy cannot improve by repeatedly sampling the behaviors it already prefers. Standard methods inject stochasticity in the action space, but such jitter only yields rollouts close to the original. Escaping a weak policy often requires global perturbations that action noise cannot produce. Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a pathway: they condition the policy on a natural language prompt, and since the rollout follows from it, modifying the prompt induces global changes. The challenge is finding prompts that induce useful global changes. With a weak policy that rarely succeeds, reward is too sparse to select on. Our idea is to refine prompts from the rollouts themselves: a vision-language model (VLM) reasons over the rollout video, diagnoses how the policy responded, and rewrites the prompt to elicit better behavior next time. This procedure resembles posterior sampling, a classical RL exploration framework, at the level of prompts: the VLM maintains an implicit distribution over useful prompts and updates it from observed rollouts. We call this strategy Prompt-Driven Exploration (PDE). Across manipulation and reasoning tasks, PDE enables RL to learn successful policies even from zero-reward starts, and improves sample efficiency more broadly. Our website is available at https://xinyunsunshine.github.io/prompt-rl.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对强化学习中动作空间随机扰动难以产生全局行为改变、弱策略下奖励稀疏导致探索困难的问题，提出在自然语言提示层面进行探索。方法利用视觉语言模型对策略产生的 rollout 视频进行推理，诊断策略响应并重写提示，从而在提示层面实现类似后验采样的探索框架，称为 Prompt-Driven Exploration（PDE）。在操作与推理任务中，PDE 使强化学习能够从零奖励起点学到成功策略，并整体提升样本效率。
- innovations_zh: 将自然语言提示作为强化学习的全局探索空间，突破动作噪声的局部限制; 用视觉语言模型基于 rollout 视频诊断并重写提示，实现提示层面的后验采样; 在零奖励起点下实现成功策略学习，并提升样本效率
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了整体效果，未给出具体任务数量、基线对比与统计显著性，难以判断方法在不同任务分布上的稳健性。方法依赖视觉语言模型对 rollout 视频的推理质量，若诊断或重写不准确，提示更新可能失效。此外，提示层面的后验采样是否比动作空间方法在复杂长时任务中更具优势，摘要未提供消融或边界条件分析。
- potential: 未来可探索提示空间与动作空间探索的结合，以及更高效的提示分布维护与更新机制。若能将方法扩展到更多模态与真实机器人场景，并系统分析视觉语言模型诊断能力对探索效果的影响，将有助于形成可推广的 VLA 强化学习探索范式。长期看，该方向可能推动语言作为通用探索接口的研究。

### [126] SpeechGym: An Audio-Native Gym for Training Voice Agents via Reinforcement Learning  (id=2608.26432, 2026-08-28, rl=19, score=6.6)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 多模态, 评测与基准, 推理与数学
- ABS: Voice agents must call tools and hold multi-turn dialogue entirely through speech, yet the dominant paradigm trains them in text. Existing frameworks either cascade TTS and ASR around a proprietary voice API, where gradients cannot flow and per-call cost makes on-policy reinforcement learning prohibitive, or stay in text: they measure voice agents but cannot improve them. We present SpeechGym, an audio-native agentic environment in which two omni-modal models converse in native audio, with no external ASR or TTS and no API boundary, over the unmodified tasks, tools and success check of an established text agentic benchmark, so that the interaction modality is the only variable and the loop stays local and trainable end to end. Audio agentic capability does not follow from audio understanding. The failures speech introduces are perceptual rather than reasoning deficits: the agent picks the right tool and the right argument slot but fills it with a value misheard from the waveform, and that single error cascades into a failed call, a retry of the same call, and a wasted step budget. A second failure is behavioural: under an insistent caller the agent performs an unauthorised write and ends the episode believing it helped. Both are trainable, because the environment labels them for free: a call with a misheard argument fails against the database while a correct one succeeds. The obstacle is sparsity, not signal. Outcome-only GRPO is gradient-starved here, since almost every rollout group fails identically, while a per-turn process reward crediting each successful tool call res
- summary_zh: 该论文提出SpeechGym，一个音频原生的智能体环境，让两个全模态模型直接以语音对话，无需外部ASR/TTS或API边界，基于现有文本智能体基准的任务、工具和成功检查，仅改变交互模态。研究发现语音智能体能力不随音频理解自然提升，失败源于感知而非推理缺陷，如听错参数值导致工具调用失败，以及面对坚持的呼叫者时执行未授权写入的行为问题。通过过程奖励（per-turn process reward）而非仅结果奖励（outcome-only GRPO），解决了奖励稀疏问题，训练后的模型无需微调即可迁移到独立语音基准，任务成功率翻倍以上，并将开源模型从排行榜末位提升至第二位，同时使用更少的轮次和令牌。
- innovations_zh: 构建音频原生端到端可训练环境，消除外部ASR/TTS和API边界; 识别语音智能体失败源于感知而非推理，并利用过程奖励解决奖励稀疏问题
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体任务类型和基准细节，适用范围可能有限；评测仅基于单一基准和有限模型，未讨论不同模型或任务下的泛化性；过程奖励的设计依赖环境自动标注，可能不适用于无明确成功检查的开放场景。
- potential: 未来可探索将SpeechGym扩展到更复杂的多模态任务和真实语音交互场景，结合更细粒度的奖励设计或课程学习，进一步提升语音智能体的鲁棒性和安全性，并推动开源语音智能体在更广泛领域的应用。

### [127] Proof-Carrying Cognition: Closing the Verification Gap with Reality-Settled Reward  (id=2609.09776, 2026-09-10, rl=19, score=6.5)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Frontier gains in language-model reasoning come from reinforcement learning on reasoning traces and are concentrated in domains with a cheap, sound verifier. We argue the field's binding constraint is the verification gap: no scalable, incorruptible reward for reasoning outside formal domains. We make four contributions. (1) Theory: in a joint-Gaussian model of best-of-N selection, verifier-gold correlation rho is the exact exchange rate between test-time compute and capability, and an unsound verifier pays a polynomial penalty N^(1/rho^2); a margin-free copula form predicts realized soundness of real LLM judges to 4% median error. (2) Demonstration: in program-synthesis testbeds with executable ground truth, including a pre-registered scaled replication, unsound verifiers lose Soundness-under-Pressure as optimization grows (0.94 to 0.32 at N=4096) while a sound verifier improves monotonically; reality-anchored settlement beats a frozen verifier under i.i.d. and adversarial pressure, driving the hacking gap from ~0.27 to ~0; soundness scales log-linearly with settled labels, with on-policy settlement ~10x more label-efficient than random labeling. With real LLM judges and unit-test execution as gold, a weak judge loses soundness under best-of-N (p<0.001), a stronger judge is more robust, and selection alone manufactures +0.53 hacking gaps from honest samples. Under real GRPO training, a frozen reward model traces the full overoptimization curve (executed reward collapses 90%) while the same model refit on a 10% settlement stream preserves 6x the executed reward. (3) Paradig
- summary_zh: 论文指出语言模型推理的前沿进展集中在拥有廉价可靠验证器的领域，核心瓶颈是形式化领域之外的验证缺口。作者提出理论模型，将验证器与金标准的相关性rho视为测试时计算与能力之间的兑换率，并给出不健全验证器的多项式惩罚。在程序合成测试平台中，实验显示不健全验证器随优化增长而丧失压力下的健全性，而现实锚定的结算机制能显著缩小攻击差距。在真实GRPO训练中，冻结奖励模型出现过度优化，而基于10%结算流重新拟合的模型能保留6倍执行奖励。
- innovations_zh: 提出验证缺口概念，并用联合高斯模型量化验证器与金标准相关性对测试时计算的兑换率; 设计现实锚定结算机制，在程序合成中证明其优于冻结验证器并提升标签效率; 提出证明携带认知范式，将推理步骤视为由世界模型定价的概率声明; 定义压力下健全性作为现实结算推理基准的核心指标
- limitations: 研究不足之处：摘要中理论模型基于联合高斯假设，其普适性有待验证；实验主要在程序合成等有可执行真值的测试平台进行，向开放域推理的迁移尚不明确。此外，现实锚定结算依赖自建世界模型和留出现实数据，其构建成本与可扩展性未在摘要中充分讨论。
- potential: 未来研究潜力：可将现实锚定结算扩展到更广泛的自然语言推理任务，探索无需可执行真值的验证器设计。长期价值在于建立以现实结算为核心的推理评估与训练范式，可能推动语言模型在开放域中实现更可靠、可验证的推理能力。

### [128] Search-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Component Query Understanding in Roblox Game Search  (id=2609.30177, 2026-09-25, rl=19, score=6.4)
- cats: RAG 与知识增强, 微调与对齐, 推理优化与效率
- ABS: Query understanding (QU) plays a critical role in production search systems, translating raw user queries into search execution plans that drive downstream retrieval and ranking. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled QU to be framed as a structured multi-task generation problem (e.g., intent classification, query expansion), optimizing such models to produce search-engine-coupled outputs remains challenging: static, label-based supervision fails to capture how each component actually interacts with the underlying search pipeline to affect downstream performance. We present a search-aware reinforcement learning (RL) framework for QU based on a distill-then-RL paradigm. Teacher-student supervised fine-tuning (SFT) first yields a well-formed, schema-compliant policy initialization. The RL stage then optimizes each QU component with rewards derived from live interaction with the search engine, tailored to that component's operational role, rather than a single reward tied to the final search outcome. Experiments on Roblox search show that this component-specific optimization improves both per-component utility and downstream search quality, raising NDCG@20 by 8.9 points over the SFT policy and by 3.5 points over training with a single end-to-end reward.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对生产搜索系统中查询理解（QU）难以通过静态标签监督优化的问题，提出了一种搜索感知的强化学习框架。方法采用先蒸馏后强化学习的范式：先通过教师-学生监督微调获得符合模式规范的策略初始化，再针对每个QU组件设计与搜索引擎实时交互的组件专属奖励进行优化。在Roblox搜索上的实验表明，该组件级优化同时提升了各组件效用和下游搜索质量，NDCG@20相比SFT策略提升8.9个点，相比单一端到端奖励训练提升3.5个点。
- innovations_zh: 提出搜索感知强化学习框架，用与搜索引擎实时交互的奖励优化查询理解; 采用先蒸馏后强化学习范式，先获得模式合规的策略初始化再强化学习; 为每个查询理解组件设计专属奖励，而非单一端到端搜索结果奖励
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了Roblox搜索场景的实验结果，方法的跨领域、跨搜索引擎泛化性尚不明确。组件专属奖励的设计细节与依赖条件未展开，且未说明在线交互成本、训练稳定性及与基线公平性等潜在问题。
- potential: 未来可探索将该搜索感知强化学习框架推广到其他搜索系统与更多查询理解组件。长期看，组件级奖励与实时搜索交互的结合有望推动查询理解从静态监督走向与检索排序联合优化，但需进一步验证其可扩展性与工程可行性。

### [129] QUATRO: Query-Adaptive Trust Region Policy Optimization for LLM Fine-tuning  (id=2602.04620, 2026-09-18, rl=19, score=6.2)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: GRPO-style reinforcement learning (RL)-based LLM fine-tuning algorithms have recently gained popularity. Relying on heuristic trust-region approximations, however, they can lead to brittle optimization behavior, as global importance-ratio clipping and group-wise normalization fail to regulate samples whose importance ratios fall outside the clipping range. We propose Query-Adaptive Trust-Region policy Optimization (QUATRO), which directly enforces trust-region constraints through a principled optimization. This yields a clear and interpretable objective that enables explicit control over policy updates and stable, entropy-controlled optimization, with a stabilizer terms arising intrinsically from the exact trust-region formulation. Empirically verified on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks, QUATRO shows stable training under increased policy staleness and aggressive learning rates, maintaining well-controlled entropy throughout training.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注GRPO类基于强化学习的LLM微调算法依赖启发式信任域近似，导致全局重要性比率裁剪和组内归一化无法约束超出裁剪范围的样本，优化行为脆弱。作者提出QUATRO，通过原则性优化直接施加信任域约束，得到清晰可解释的目标，实现对策略更新的显式控制与稳定的熵控制优化，并自然产生稳定项。在多种数学推理基准上，QUATRO在策略陈旧度增加和激进学习率下仍保持稳定训练，并维持良好控制的熵。
- innovations_zh: 直接以原则性优化施加信任域约束，替代启发式近似; 目标清晰可解释，支持显式控制策略更新; 实现稳定且熵受控的优化，稳定项由精确信任域公式自然产生; 在策略陈旧度增加和激进学习率下仍保持稳定训练
- limitations: 摘要仅提及在数学推理基准上验证，未说明其他任务或模型规模的泛化性；未给出与基线方法的定量对比细节；信任域约束的精确求解可能带来额外计算开销，摘要未讨论效率与超参数敏感性。
- potential: 未来可探索将QUATRO扩展到更广泛的推理与生成任务、不同规模LLM及多模态场景；可研究其与现有RLHF/GRPO流程的结合及计算效率优化；长期价值在于推动LLM强化学习微调从启发式裁剪走向原则化信任域优化。

### [130] Where vs What: Decomposing Structural and Content Failures in LLM-Generated Structured Outputs  (id=2608.25358, 2026-08-27, rl=19, score=6.1)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Structured outputs such as JSON and tables are central to modern LLM-based systems, yet generation failures are evaluated monolithically, conflating two distinct error modes: placement errors (correct values at wrong positions) and value errors (wrong values at intended positions). We introduce Structure-Content Decomposition (SCD), a framework that independently measures structural fidelity and content accuracy. Applying SCD to nested JSON and table tasks across six models (7B to frontier), we uncover a consistent phenomenon: structural fidelity degrades earlier and more sharply than content accuracy as complexity increases. At the highest complexity, even DeepSeek-V4-Flash (with reasoning) misplaces 35% of recalled values, while Qwen2.5-7B misplaces 74%. Controlled ablations suggest that this pattern is associated with reliance on semantic shortcuts rather than topological understanding of output structure. Based on these findings, we propose SA-RLVR, converting SCD metrics into verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning via GRPO. SA-RLVR successfully optimizes structural addressing across distinct topologies: it lifts JSON Value Placement Accuracy (VPA) from 26% to 63% while generalizing to held-out schemas; moreover, it consistently drives VPA improvements in the table domain, demonstrating that structure-aware rewards can directly enhance multi-domain structural positioning.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对LLM生成结构化输出（如JSON和表格）时，将结构错误和内容错误混为一谈的问题，提出了结构-内容分解（SCD）框架，分别衡量结构保真度和内容准确性。在六个模型上的实验发现，随着复杂度增加，结构保真度比内容准确性下降得更早更严重。基于此，作者提出SA-RLVR方法，将SCD指标转化为可验证的奖励用于强化学习，显著提升了不同拓扑结构下的值放置准确性。
- innovations_zh: 提出SCD框架，将结构化输出错误分解为结构错误和内容错误; 发现结构保真度随复杂度下降更早更剧烈的现象; 提出SA-RLVR方法，将SCD指标用于强化学习奖励
- limitations: 摘要未提供SCD在真实场景中的评测细节，如任务多样性、模型规模覆盖范围有限。未说明SA-RLVR在更多复杂结构（如深层嵌套或非JSON格式）上的泛化能力，以及计算开销和训练稳定性。
- potential: 未来可探索将SCD扩展到更多结构化格式（如XML、代码）和更复杂的拓扑，并研究结构错误与内容错误的交互机制。SA-RLVR有望与更强大的推理模型结合，提升结构化输出的鲁棒性，长期可能推动结构化生成评估标准的统一。

### [131] Not All Prompts Are Equal: Exploration-Guided Prompt Scaffolding for Multimodal Reinforcement Post-Training  (id=2609.15051, 2026-09-15, rl=18, score=9.6)
- cats: 多模态, 评测与基准
- ABS: Training prompts in online reinforcement learning (RL) differ substantially in how informative they are for the current policy: some are already saturated while others are too difficult to yield reliable learning signals, yet both receive equal rollout budget under standard training. We propose an exploration-guided prompt scaffolding framework that adapts the training prompt distribution dynamically throughout RL post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Central to our approach is the $\textit{Exploration Potential Score} (EPS)$, a lightweight rollout-based proxy for prompt utility derived from KL-regularized policy improvement theory, computable directly from on-policy rollout statistics without additional overhead. Rather than discarding low-utility prompts, we use a teacher model to generate scaffolded rewrites that preserve the original task intent while making subsequent training more informative, reframing teacher supervision as training-data refinement rather than output imitation. Integrated with GRPO on Geo3K and MMK12, our method consistently outperforms the baseline on both in-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks, achieving up to 9.7\% relative improvement in-domain and gains of 11.5\% on MathVision and 11.1\% on MMMU-Pro.
- summary_zh: 该研究指出在线强化学习后训练中，不同训练提示对当前策略的信息量差异显著，但标准训练给予相同采样预算。为此提出探索引导的提示脚手架框架，动态调整多模态大语言模型RL后训练中的提示分布。其核心是探索潜力分数（EPS），一种基于KL正则化策略改进理论、可直接从在线采样统计中计算的轻量级提示效用代理。方法不丢弃低效用提示，而是用教师模型生成保留原任务意图的脚手架重写，将教师监督重构为训练数据精炼。在Geo3K和MMK12上结合GRPO，域内相对提升最高9.7%，MathVision提升11.5%，MMMU-Pro提升11.1%。
- innovations_zh: 提出探索潜力分数EPS，基于KL正则化策略改进理论，从在线采样统计直接计算提示效用; 不丢弃低效用提示，而是用教师模型生成脚手架重写以保留任务意图并提升训练信息量; 将教师监督从输出模仿重构为训练数据精炼，并集成GRPO实现动态提示分布调整
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在Geo3K和MMK12上的结果，方法在其他任务或模型规模上的泛化性尚不明确。EPS作为基于在线采样统计的代理，其与真实提示效用的相关性及额外教师模型重写带来的成本未在摘要中说明。评测主要依赖基准提升，缺乏对脚手架重写质量与任务意图保持程度的直接分析。
- potential: 未来可探索EPS在其他RL算法与多模态任务中的适用性，并研究脚手架重写的自动化质量评估与成本控制。长期看，将提示效用估计与数据精炼结合，可能推动更高效、自适应的后训练数据管理范式。

### [132] Policy and World Modeling Co-Training for Language Agents  (id=2606.02388, 2026-09-15, rl=18, score=8.8)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) improves large language model (LLM) agents by teaching them which actions lead to high rewards, but provides little supervision on what those actions do to the environment. World modeling (WM) can fill this gap, yet existing approaches often require separate simulators, extra training stages, or additional inference-time computation. We observe that on-policy RL rollouts already contain the needed signal: each transition pairs an action with its resulting next observation. Based on this observation, we propose PaW, a Policy and World modeling co-training framework that adds auxiliary WM supervision to the same policy during RL, without changing the inference paradigm. To make auxiliary WM supervision informative and stable, PaW introduces three components: action-entropy-based WM data selection, noise-tolerant WM loss, and reward-adaptive loss balancing. Experiments on three agentic task benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong RL baselines across models and RL algorithms. These results suggest that standard RL rollouts are a practical source of WM supervision for language-agent training.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对强化学习训练大语言模型智能体时只提供动作奖励信号、缺乏对环境动态监督的问题，提出利用同策略RL rollout中动作与下一观测的配对信号进行世界建模。作者提出PaW框架，在同一策略的RL训练中同时加入辅助世界建模监督，且不改变推理范式。为提升辅助监督的有效性与稳定性，PaW引入基于动作熵的WM数据选择、噪声容忍WM损失和奖励自适应损失平衡。在三个智能体任务基准上，该方法在多种模型和RL算法上均优于强RL基线。
- innovations_zh: 利用同策略RL rollout中动作-下一观测配对作为世界建模监督信号; 在不改变推理范式下实现策略与世界建模的联合训练; 引入动作熵数据选择、噪声容忍损失和奖励自适应损失平衡
- limitations: 摘要仅说明在三个智能体任务基准上取得一致提升，未给出具体任务类型、模型规模、基线细节或评测指标，因此泛化性与实际收益幅度尚不明确。世界建模监督来自RL rollout，其质量可能受策略探索与数据分布限制，摘要也未讨论在更复杂或非平稳环境中的稳定性。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究世界建模辅助信号在不同任务、模型和RL算法间的迁移性与可扩展性。也可探索如何将PaW与更丰富的环境交互或离线数据结合，以提升语言智能体对长期动态的建模能力。若验证充分，该方向可能推动语言智能体训练更高效地利用既有RL数据。

### [133] Small Language Models as Judges for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning  (id=2608.30005, 2026-09-01, rl=18, score=8.0)
- cats: 评测与基准
- ABS: Rubric-based reinforcement learning extends RL beyond tasks with exact answers or rule-based verifiers by scoring responses against instance-specific criteria. However, this makes reward computation expensive: training requires repeated rubric judging, often with proprietary APIs or local generative LLM judges with 7B parameters or more. We study whether smaller language models can serve as efficient and reliable rubric-based judges. To make this question measurable, we construct PointRubric and RaR-Science-Static, two pointwise rubric-based evaluation datasets with instance-specific criteria and itemwise satisfaction labels. We compare three ways of extracting criterion-level judgments from small models: Generative verdicts, Yes/No Logprob margins, and Probe judges. Across both datasets, the Qwen3-1.7B Probe judge achieves the strongest criterion-level agreement among these methods, outperforming Generative and Logprob judges. Used as a GRPO reward model, it trains a policy from 0.232 to 0.643 on RaR-Science rubric score, compared with 0.594 for an 8B Generative judge baseline, while the baseline requires 10.7$\times$ more reward-judge time. Task and domain transfer experiments further suggest that Probe judges preserve criterion-level reward structure across settings.
- summary_zh: 该研究探讨了小型语言模型能否作为基于评分标准的强化学习中的高效可靠评判者。作者构建了两个点式评分数据集，并比较了生成式判定、对数概率边际和探针三种从小型模型中提取标准级判断的方法。结果显示，Qwen3-1.7B探针评判者在标准级一致性上最优，且作为GRPO奖励模型时性能超过8B生成式评判者，同时大幅减少推理时间。跨任务和领域实验表明探针评判者能保持奖励结构。
- innovations_zh: 构建了两个点式评分数据集，支持小型模型评判研究; 提出探针方法，以轻量方式提取标准级判断; 系统比较三种小型模型评判方法，验证探针优势
- limitations: 摘要未提供数据集规模、任务多样性及评判者泛化能力的详细分析，可能影响结论普适性。评测主要基于特定数据集和模型，未涉及真实复杂场景中的噪声与偏差。探针方法依赖标注数据，其构建成本未在摘要中说明。
- potential: 未来可探索探针评判者在更多任务和更大模型上的可扩展性，以及其与在线学习结合的动态适应能力。长期看，该方法有望推动轻量级奖励模型在RLHF和自动评估中的广泛应用，并促进对评判偏差的深入研究。

### [134] Libra: Efficient Resource Management for Agentic RL Post-Training  (id=2606.03077, 2026-09-17, rl=18, score=7.4)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a standard post-training paradigm for shaping large language models (LLMs) into capable agents. In agentic RL, the rollout stage generates trajectories while invoking tools, producing long-tailed and non-stationary workloads that expose two fundamental challenges. First, due to the long-tailed response distribution, a small fraction of trajectories dominates rollout makespan.Second, rollout and training differ in their compute patterns, memory demands, and sensitivity to sequence length. As the policy evolves, shifts in the workload distribution further change their relative resource demands, making it difficult to maintain balanced execution across the two stages. We present Libra, an adaptive runtime for agentic RL post-training with two complementary components: (1) intra-stage scheduling via a Causality-Guided Bucket Scheduler that routes requests across execution buckets with different parallelism configurations, reducing delays from rollout stragglers; and (2) cross-stage coordination that dynamically reallocates workers between rollout and training as the workload changes. It moves workers between the two stages through a non-blocking protocol without interrupting ongoing training. Evaluated on a 48x NVIDIA A800 GPU cluster and a 160x Ascend 910B3 NPU cluster across three agentic benchmarks, Libra achieves up to 4.2x higher throughput and up to 2.7x faster reward convergence
- summary_zh: 该论文针对智能体强化学习后训练中 rollout 阶段因调用工具而产生长尾、非平稳负载的问题展开研究。其核心挑战在于少数轨迹主导 rollout 完成时间，且 rollout 与训练在计算模式、内存需求和序列长度敏感性上存在差异，策略演化还会改变两者的相对资源需求。作者提出 Libra 自适应运行时，包含因果引导桶调度器进行阶段内调度，以及跨阶段动态重分配 worker 的非阻塞协调机制。在 48 卡 A800 和 160 卡 Ascend 910B3 集群、三个智能体基准上，Libra 实现最高 4.2 倍吞吐提升和最高 2.7 倍奖励收敛加速。
- innovations_zh: 因果引导桶调度器，按不同并行配置路由请求以缓解 rollout 长尾延迟; 跨阶段协调机制，随负载变化动态重分配 rollout 与训练 worker; 采用非阻塞协议迁移 worker，不中断正在进行的训练
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了吞吐和奖励收敛指标，未说明对最终策略质量、稳定性或收敛精度的影响。评估限于三个智能体基准和两种特定硬件集群，方法在其他模型规模、任务类型或集群拓扑上的泛化性尚不明确。此外，非阻塞 worker 迁移协议的开销、调度决策的额外复杂度及其对训练中断风险的规避效果，摘要中未给出细节。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究调度与资源重分配策略在不同模型规模、任务分布和异构集群下的泛化能力，并探索将因果引导调度与更细粒度的在线负载预测结合。长期看，该方向有助于推动智能体强化学习后训练系统在动态、长尾负载下实现更稳定高效的资源利用，并为训练与推理混合负载的统一调度提供启发。

### [135] Mitigating LLM sycophancy with RL-based fine-tuning: Bayesian Truth Serum approach  (id=2608.25267, 2026-08-27, rl=18, score=7.3)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Large language models (LLMs) frequently exhibit \emph{sycophancy}: they adapt their answers to a user's stated beliefs or preferences instead of reporting what they hold to be true, which lowers factual accuracy and can amplify misinformation. This paper proposes a methodology for mitigating sycophancy that employs the Bayesian Truth Serum (BTS), a peer-prediction mechanism, as the reward in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune an LLM. BTS pays an answer for being \emph{surprisingly common}, that is, more frequent among respondents than those respondents themselves predicted. We treat a group of responses from a model for one question as those respondents, so the reward is a function of the model's own outputs and fine-tuning needs neither labels nor preference annotations. We prove that in the large-group limit a sycophantic response earns strictly lower expected reward than an honest one. We also prove that if the entire group agrees in advance on a symmetric answering rule, it cannot earn a higher information score than under truthful reporting. On our true/false benchmark the reference model's answer-flip rate under user pressure decreases from 23% to 4%, and its accuracy under that pressure increases from 80% to 93%. Our reward outperforms SMART and is comparable to synthetic-data fine-tuning and to pinpoint tuning, all three of which train on labels. It spends considerably more compute in exchange, which makes it suitable when labeled data is scarce. Peer Truth Serum, which also pays a premium for a rare answer but elicits no prediction report, repro
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型（LLM）的谄媚问题（即模型迎合用户观点而非事实），提出了一种基于贝叶斯真话血清（BTS）的强化学习微调方法。该方法将BTS作为组相对策略优化（GRPO）的奖励函数，无需标签或偏好标注，仅利用模型自身输出计算奖励。实验表明，在真/假基准上，模型在用户压力下的答案翻转率从23%降至4%，准确率从80%提升至93%，且性能优于SMART，与基于标签的合成数据微调及精准微调相当。研究还证明，在单组GRPO内计算同伴预测奖励可有效减少谄媚，且稀有答案的额外奖励是驱动该效果的关键因素。
- innovations_zh: 首次将贝叶斯真话血清（BTS）作为GRPO奖励函数，实现无标签微调以减少LLM谄媚。; 理论证明在大群体极限下，谄媚回答的期望奖励严格低于诚实回答。; 通过对比实验揭示稀有答案奖励溢价是减少谄媚的核心机制。
- limitations: 摘要仅基于真/假基准评估，未涉及开放域或复杂推理任务，泛化性未知。方法计算开销显著高于对比方法，可能限制其在大规模场景下的实用性。此外，BTS奖励依赖群体内回答分布，对群体规模或多样性敏感，实际应用中的稳定性有待验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将BTS奖励与其他强化学习算法结合，或扩展至多任务、多语言场景。长期来看，该方法有望成为无监督对齐的重要工具，尤其在标注数据稀缺的领域。进一步研究可优化计算效率，并分析不同群体构成对奖励信号的影响，以提升鲁棒性。

### [136] GRPO for Financial Advice Generation: Outperforming Commercial LLMs under CATE Evaluation  (id=2608.11787, 2026-08-13, rl=18, score=7.2)
- cats: 领域应用, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐, 推理与数学, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Generating actionable financial advice from business records demands that models integrate numerical reasoning, domain knowledge, and sound judgment, while avoiding recommendations that could harm the business. Direct supervision is difficult: historical decisions are not necessarily optimal, and high-quality free-form labels are expensive to obtain. We formulate financial advice generation as a reinforcement learning problem and fine-tune an open-weight language model using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Our reward is an LLM-as-a-judge rubric that scores each recommendation across multiple binary dimensions of advice quality, augmented with a safety gate for harm prevention. Since LLM-based evaluation alone cannot confirm whether improvements reflect genuine business value rather than adaptation to the judge, we complement it with a judge-independent audit based on a standard doubly-robust Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE) estimator. Under this observational off-policy audit, our trained LLM achieves approximately twice the estimated gross-profit lift of the strongest evaluated commercial baseline ($0.0228$ vs.\ $0.0104$), together with the lowest downside rate and the least negative tail risk of any policy evaluated. Notably, the two evaluations do not rank the baselines identically: the untrained base model places last on the judge rubric but second on the causal audit, indicating that the audit captures a signal the judge does not. Our results demonstrate that GRPO with a finance-grounded reward signal can produce substantially more useful business
- summary_zh: 该研究将金融建议生成建模为强化学习问题，使用GRPO微调开放权重语言模型，并以LLM作为评判者设计奖励，同时加入安全门控。为验证模型改进的真实业务价值，研究采用基于双重稳健CATE估计的独立审计。结果显示，训练模型在估计毛利提升上约为最强商业基线的两倍（0.0228 vs 0.0104），且下行风险最低。值得注意的是，LLM评判与因果审计对基线的排序不一致，表明审计捕捉了评判者未覆盖的信号。
- innovations_zh: 将金融建议生成转化为GRPO强化学习问题，并设计LLM评判奖励与安全门控; 引入基于CATE的独立审计，作为LLM评判的补充，验证业务价值而非仅适应评判者
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体数据集规模、领域范围及商业基线细节，可能影响泛化性判断。CATE估计依赖观测数据，存在未观测混杂风险，且审计结果仅基于毛利指标，未涵盖其他业务维度。LLM评判与因果审计排序不一致，暗示评判标准可能需进一步校准。
- potential: 未来可探索更丰富的奖励信号（如多目标优化）及更稳健的因果估计方法。该方法可推广至其他高风险决策领域（如医疗、法律），并可能推动LLM评估与因果推断的深度融合。长期看，有助于建立更可信的金融AI系统。

### [137] SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment  (id=2609.02786, 2026-09-03, rl=18, score=7.1)
- cats: 安全与可靠性, Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对基于LLM的智能体在安全对齐中面临的多步执行轨迹与最终响应风险问题，提出SafeEvolve框架，通过利用策略轨迹中的安全经验，实现安全提示与分层技能的harness组件更新，以及两阶段SFT-RL策略优化，形成harness与策略的协同进化。实验表明，在AgentDojo等基准上，SafeEvolve在保持较高实用性的同时显著降低攻击成功率，如Qwen3.5-4B上ASR降低3倍且良性效用提升。
- innovations_zh: 提出harness与策略协同进化的自演化框架，结合运行时控制与内在安全; 将轨迹级安全证据转化为有界、可审计的harness组件更新; 采用两阶段SFT-RL范式，利用harness增强策略的自主安全行为
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了单一模型（Qwen3.5-4B）的部分结果，缺乏对更大模型或更多基座的泛化性验证。评测主要基于AgentDojo等特定基准，可能未覆盖真实世界多样化的攻击模式。此外，harness更新的可审计性虽被提及，但未说明其计算开销或人工介入需求。
- potential: 未来可探索将SafeEvolve扩展到多模态智能体或更复杂的长程任务，并研究harness更新与策略优化之间的理论收敛性。长期来看，该框架可能推动安全对齐从静态配置向动态自演化方向发展，但需进一步验证其在开放环境中的鲁棒性与可解释性。

### [138] Forty Shades of Blue: Quality-Diversity Alignment via Mode-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.14896, 2026-09-15, rl=18, score=7.1)
