- limitations: 实验主要基于可精确求解的小规模博弈和精确最佳响应，向大规模复杂博弈的推广性尚不明确。固定离流形参考会产生可利用度代价，边界目标存在欠冲，且选择鲁棒性权衡在对抗最佳响应时退化。
- potential: 未来可探索将锚定参考方法扩展到近似最佳响应和大规模博弈，并研究自适应参考更新以缓解离流形代价。长期看，该方法可能为多均衡环境下的策略对齐与可控均衡选择提供通用设计原则。

### [174] Rollout Efficiency in Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning Large Language Models: A Taxonomy and Future Directions  (id=2609.25463, 2026-09-23, rl=16, score=6.4)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning enables large language models to solve mathematical, coding, and other multi-step tasks, but shifts a substantial portion of the training cost to rollout, where trajectories are generated for policy updates. Efficient rollout mechanisms are therefore essential to reduce this cost while maintaining the freshness, consistency, and statistical validity of training data. This survey provides a systematic taxonomy of recent research on rollout efficiency for reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning, classifying existing approaches from both mechanism and bottleneck perspectives. Based on this taxonomy, we analyze how different technique families address distinct sources of rollout inefficiency, examine opportunities and potential conflicts for combining them, identify gaps in the evaluation and reporting of efficiency gains, and discuss open challenges and future research directions.
- summary_zh: 该综述聚焦于面向推理的大语言模型强化学习中 rollout 阶段的效率问题，指出 rollout 已成为训练成本的主要来源。作者从机制和瓶颈两个视角对现有研究进行系统分类，构建了 rollout 效率技术的分类体系。基于该分类，分析了不同技术族如何应对 rollout 低效的不同来源，并探讨了技术组合的机会与潜在冲突。同时指出了效率增益评估与报告中的不足，并讨论了开放挑战与未来方向。
- innovations_zh: 从机制与瓶颈双视角构建rollout效率技术分类体系; 分析不同技术族针对的rollout低效来源及组合冲突; 识别效率增益评估与报告中的缺口
- limitations: 作为综述，其贡献限于对现有文献的归纳与分类，未提出新的算法或实验验证。摘要未说明分类体系的覆盖范围与筛选标准，也未给出效率增益的定量比较。此外，评估缺口的具体表现和严重程度在摘要中未展开。
- potential: 未来可基于该分类体系开展实证研究，验证不同技术组合的实际效率增益与冲突。长期价值在于推动 rollout 效率评估标准的建立，并启发面向推理强化学习的高效训练系统设计。

### [175] HCGRec: Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation with Semantic IDs  (id=2608.11980, 2026-08-13, rl=16, score=6.3)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Semantic-ID generative recommenders represent each item as a short sequence of discrete semantic tokens and predict the next item by autoregressively generating this token sequence. This paradigm enables a unified generation interface for item IDs, histories, and item text, but it also creates a structured optimization bottleneck during reward-based post-training: when an early semantic token enters the wrong branch of the item-token space, finite rollout groups rarely reach the ground-truth item, so group-relative optimization receives identical zero rewards and produces no useful advantage. We propose Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation (HCGRec), a semantic-ID generative recommendation framework that recovers learning signal for such hard training instances. HCGRec diagnoses each instance with checkpoint rollouts and supplies a minimal target-prefix hint only when the current generator cannot reach the correct item. The model then generates the unhinted suffix under the hinted semantic branch, turning zero-reward groups into informative comparisons over item-token completions. Hinting also changes token identity: hinted prefix tokens are oracle-provided item context, while unhinted suffix tokens are sampled generation actions. We therefore introduce hint-aware credit decomposition, using supervised learning to preserve item-semantic and prefix-structure alignment for hinted tokens and GRPO to optimize the sampled suffix. Experiments on sequential recommendation benchmarks show that HCGRec substantially improves over supervised fine-tuning and vanilla reward-based p
- summary_zh: 该论文针对语义ID生成式推荐系统中奖励后训练阶段的结构性优化瓶颈，即早期语义token错误导致组相对优化获得零奖励且无有效优势的问题，提出了提示条件生成推荐框架HCGRec。HCGRec通过检查点回滚诊断困难实例，并仅在必要时提供最小目标前缀提示，使模型生成未提示的后缀部分，从而将零奖励组转化为信息丰富的比较。同时引入提示感知的信用分解，对提示token使用监督学习保持语义对齐，对未提示token使用GRPO优化。实验表明，HCGRec显著优于监督微调和普通奖励后训练，并将零优势训练样本从超过70%降至20%以下。
- innovations_zh: 提出提示条件生成机制，动态提供最小目标前缀以恢复困难样本的学习信号; 设计提示感知信用分解，区分提示token与生成token的优化方式
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体数据集规模和领域多样性，可能影响泛化性结论；仅基于顺序推荐基准，未涉及其他推荐场景；未讨论提示长度选择对性能的敏感性及计算开销。
- potential: 未来可探索自适应提示策略以减少人工干预，并将HCGRec扩展到更多生成式任务（如对话推荐）；长期看，该方法有望推动生成式推荐在复杂交互场景中的实际应用。

### [176] Information-Gain Rewards over Diversity-Pruned Tests: GT-Anchored Verifier Co-Training for Reliable Code Generation  (id=2609.21208, 2026-09-19, rl=16, score=6.2)
- cats: 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性, 领域应用
- ABS: Self-play methods that co-train a single language model as both coder and test author promise to move code-generation RL beyond fixed test suites, but they suffer from two coupled pathologies: permissiveness collapse, where pass-rate rewards are maximised by trivial, non-discriminative tests, and concentration bias, where i.i.d. sampled tests cluster on modal inputs and inflate estimator variance. We introduce CoVer (Co-trained Coder and Verifier), a single-policy GRPO framework that addresses both failure modes. First, an information-gain (IG) reward scores each self-generated test by the mutual information between its pass/fail vector and a graded, ground-truth-anchored correctness signal y [0, 1] m, gated by the sign of their covariance so that only positively discriminative tests receive reward. Second, a three-stage diversity-aware selection step prunes a candidate pool to a behaviourally non-redundant suite (invalidity, input-string, execution-profile filtering), raising the effective sample size of the IG estimator at fixed execution budget. On five benchmarks (LiveBench, MBPP, LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, Code-Forces), CoVer raises one-shot pass@1 by +5.8 points at 7B and +7.1 points at 14B over the Qwen2.5-Instruct backbone, and achieves the highest macro-average among all compared methods at both scales. As a drop-in backbone inside the CodeT ranking pipeline, CoVer-7B adds +3.5 points, demonstrating the dual benefit of co-training for both generation and selection.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对自博弈式代码生成中“单一模型兼任编码者与测试作者”存在的两个问题：宽容性崩塌（通过无区分度的测试刷高通过率）和集中偏差（独立同分布采样测试聚集于常见输入、增大估计方差）。作者提出 CoVer，一个单策略 GRPO 框架，用信息增益奖励（以测试通过/失败向量与真值锚定正确性信号之间的互信息打分，并用协方差符号门控）和三级多样性感知筛选（无效性、输入字符串、执行剖面过滤）来提升测试的有效性与估计效率。在五个基准上，CoVer 在 7B 和 14B 规模分别比 Qwen2.5-Instruct 提升 one-shot pass@1 达 +5.8 和 +7.1 点，并在两种规模下取得最高宏平均；作为 CodeT 排序流程的骨干还可额外提升 +3.5 点。
- innovations_zh: 提出信息增益奖励，用测试通过/失败向量与真值锚定正确性信号的互信息打分，并以协方差符号门控只奖励正向区分性测试; 设计三级多样性感知筛选，按无效性、输入字符串、执行剖面过滤候选池，得到行为非冗余测试套件; 在固定执行预算下提升信息增益估计器的有效样本量，缓解集中偏差与估计方差; 将 CoVer 作为即插即用骨干用于 CodeT 排序流程，同时受益于生成与选择
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了基于 Qwen2.5-Instruct 骨干、7B 与 14B 规模在五个基准上的结果，未说明方法在更小或更大模型、其他代码生成任务或非代码领域的泛化性。信息增益奖励依赖“真值锚定正确性信号”，其获取方式与成本未在摘要中说明，可能限制在缺乏可靠标注场景中的适用性。此外，摘要未给出与固定测试套件基线在测试执行预算、训练开销上的公平性对比细节，多样性筛选三阶段的具体阈值与消融也未见披露。
- potential: 未来可探索将信息增益奖励与多样性筛选推广到更广泛的程序合成、形式验证或需要自动评估的生成任务，并研究在真值信号稀疏或昂贵时如何构造可靠的锚定信号。长期看，若能与执行预算自适应分配、跨模型规模迁移结合，有望形成不依赖固定测试套件的可持续自博弈训练范式，降低对人工测试维护的依赖。

### [177] Which Forms of Caregiver Feedback Support Grammar Learning? A Reinforcement-Learning Study of Child-Like Language Models  (id=2609.08576, 2026-09-09, rl=16, score=6.1)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Social interaction is central to children's language learning, but the effects of different forms of caregiver feedback are difficult to isolate in naturalistic data. We use child-like language models as controlled learners to test which forms of feedback support grammatical development. Small GPT-2-style models are pretrained on child-directed language from CHILDES, then fine-tuned with reinforcement learning using reward models trained to capture four feedback types: communicative feedback, structural alignment, semantic contingency, and affective feedback. Reward fine-tuning yields limited gains on minimal-pair evaluations, but clearer effects in free generation. Structural alignment produces the strongest improvements in grammaticality, providing a novel, plausible mechanistic account of how this feedback can support grammar learning. Communicative feedback yields more moderate gains. In contrast, semantic contingency and affective feedback do not improve grammaticality, although further analyses suggest that they may support other aspects of language learning beyond grammar. These results suggest that different forms of caregiver feedback make complementary contributions to language learning.
- summary_zh: 本研究使用类儿童语言模型作为受控学习者，通过强化学习微调，系统评估四种看护者反馈形式（交际反馈、结构对齐、语义连贯、情感反馈）对语法学习的影响。结果显示结构对齐对语法正确性提升最显著，交际反馈效果中等，而语义连贯和情感反馈未改善语法，但可能支持其他语言学习方面。研究基于CHILDES语料预训练GPT-2风格模型，并通过最小对评估和自由生成任务进行测试。
- innovations_zh: 首次用强化学习在类儿童模型中分离测试不同反馈形式的语法效应; 发现结构对齐反馈对语法学习有最强促进作用，提供机制解释; 区分了不同反馈类型对语法与其他语言方面的互补贡献
- limitations: 摘要未提供模型规模、训练细节及评测的具体统计显著性，限制了结果的可量化评估。最小对评估增益有限，可能表明语法学习效果依赖任务类型，且模型与真实儿童学习机制存在差异，结论外推需谨慎。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更丰富的反馈组合和交互场景，探索反馈对词汇、语用等其他语言层面的长期影响。该框架有望用于模拟不同养育风格，为语言发展研究和人机交互教育系统提供理论指导。

### [178] Navigating Sparse Evidence: Agentic Visual RAG via Explicit Context Selection and Consolidation  (id=2609.15800, 2026-09-15, rl=16, score=6.1)
- cats: 多模态, 推理与数学, Agent 与工具使用, RAG 与知识增强, 推理优化与效率
- ABS: Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (VRAG) empowers models to navigate and answer queries about visually rich documents by retrieving relevant page images as visual evidence and reasoning over their content. However, effectively utilizing this visual evidence is usually impeded by two main challenges. First, answer-relevant evidence is sparse and may be concentrated in a small region of one page or dispersed across multiple pages. Second, existing agentic methods often generate answers based on raw exploration trajectories or compressed textual memories rather than an explicitly organized set of supporting images, making answers susceptible to exploration noise and obscuring the evidence-backed reasoning trace. We argue that the bottleneck lies not only in evidence discovery but also in its preservation and organization before answer generation. We propose SCoRE (Selection and Consolidation for Robust Evidence), a unified agent loop for explicit evidence selection and consolidation. During exploration, SCoRE retains only query-relevant observations and their source pointers in a maintained textual ledger, preserving earlier evidence while keeping the visual context bounded. At termination, it reloads the referenced original images and consolidates the visual evidence for answering, arranging it into a logical sequence. This decouples final reasoning from exploratory trial-and-error while ensuring strict visual grounding via indexed claim-to-image linkages. To enable end-to-end optimization of this unified rollout, our training paradigm combines filtered cold-start traject
- summary_zh: 该论文针对视觉检索增强生成中证据稀疏且分散、现有智能体方法基于原始探索轨迹或压缩文本记忆生成答案而易受噪声干扰的问题，提出SCoRE统一智能体循环。SCoRE在探索时仅保留查询相关观察及其源指针于文本账本中，终止时重载原始图像并整合为逻辑序列的视觉证据，实现显式证据选择与整合。训练范式结合过滤冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知强化学习，奖励促进证据覆盖、整合紧凑性和答案正确性。
- innovations_zh: 提出显式证据选择与整合的智能体循环，维护文本账本保留相关观察及源指针; 终止时重载原始图像并整合为逻辑序列，解耦最终推理与探索试错; 通过索引化声明到图像链接确保严格视觉 grounding; 训练结合冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知强化学习，奖励覆盖、紧凑与正确性
- limitations: 摘要未提供实验数据或与基线方法的定量比较，无法判断实际性能提升幅度。方法依赖文本账本和源指针的维护，可能引入额外开销或选择偏差；强化学习奖励设计对证据覆盖和紧凑性的权衡效果尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索更高效的证据选择与整合策略，降低文本账本维护成本，并验证方法在更多视觉文档类型和查询复杂度下的泛化能力。长期价值在于推动视觉RAG从探索驱动转向证据组织驱动，提升可解释性和可靠性。

### [179] Mint-Agent: Introducing Finance-Native Agentic Foundation Models  (id=2608.16386, 2026-08-18, rl=15, score=7.6)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 领域应用, 推理优化与效率, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: Financial agents must do more than recall domain knowledge: they must be both reliable, executing precise operations over grounded evidence, and executive, sustaining long-horizon research whose conclusions remain auditable. We present Mint-Agent, a family of finance-native agentic models designed around these two scales of financial intelligence. Mint-Agent is built upon three pillars: data, harness, and algorithm. Our data engine constructs clean, specialized tasks for atomic financial capabilities and long-horizon agentic execution from real-world financial sources. MintHarness enables stable interaction with open-ended environments and maintains auditable evidence trails across extended research trajectories. Our training recipe combines SFT, critical-step OPD, and RLVR to develop separate financial reasoning and agentic execution experts, which are then unified through model merging and multi-teacher on-policy distillation into compact, general-purpose financial agents. This pipeline yields two flagship models, Mint-Cu (9B) and Mint-Ag (27B). Across professional financial benchmarks, our models demonstrate two defining strengths: (1) Reliability: Mint-Ag achieves 98.33% on RFC-Bench, surpassing GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude-Opus-4.8 by 3.66 and 3.00 points; and (2) Executability: Mint-Cu reaches 69.86% on FinSearchComp T2, outperforming Agents-A1-35B and Nex-N2-mini by 22.83 and 12.78 points, while Mint-Ag achieves 76.00% and 60.49% on FinanceAgentBench v1.1 and v2, respectively. These results establish a path toward trustworthy financial intelligence in which domain expertis
- summary_zh: 该论文提出Mint-Agent，一个金融原生智能体模型家族，旨在同时实现可靠性和可执行性。模型基于数据、框架和算法三大支柱构建，通过SFT、关键步骤OPD和RLVR训练，并融合模型合并与多教师策略蒸馏，最终推出Mint-Cu（9B）和Mint-Ag（27B）两个模型。在多个金融基准上，Mint-Ag在RFC-Bench上达到98.33%，Mint-Cu在FinSearchComp T2上达到69.86%，均超越对比模型。
- innovations_zh: 提出金融原生智能体模型，兼顾原子金融能力与长程执行; 构建MintHarness框架，支持开放环境交互并保留可审计证据链; 采用SFT、关键步骤OPD与RLVR结合的训练方案，并融合多教师蒸馏
- limitations: 摘要未提供模型在真实金融场景中的部署验证，仅依赖基准测试。评测基准可能未覆盖所有金融任务类型，且未提及计算资源、训练数据规模等细节，限制了可复现性评估。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更多金融子领域（如风险管理、投资组合优化），并探索更高效的模型压缩方法。其可审计证据链设计有望推广至其他高合规要求领域，长期价值在于推动智能体从任务执行向可信决策演进。

### [180] AgenticRag-R1: Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Stack Memory for Multi-Step Reasoning, Retrieval and Memorizing  (id=2608.29622, 2026-09-01, rl=15, score=7.6)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, RAG 与知识增强, 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models (LLMs), yet existing RAG systems often struggle with complex, multi-step reasoning that requires adaptive retrieval and continuous revision of intermediate contexts. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based agentic RAG methods partially alleviate this issue, but typically rely on coarse-grained action spaces and trajectory-level rewards, resulting in weak reward assignment and a bias toward short-horizon, stereotyped reasoning template. To address, we propose AgenticRag-R1, a RL framework that deeply integrates reasoning, retrieval, and memory via a memory stack and fine-grained action space, supported by hierarchical action-aware rewards and an information-aware trajectory rejection strategy to enable effective long-horizon learning. Experiments across a diverse set of multi-hop, open-domain, and agentic reasoning benchmarks, spanning multiple backbone model sizes, demonstrate that AgenticRag-R1 consistently outperforms strong baselines. Moreover, AgenticRag-R1 learns more robust, interpretable, and memory-aware reasoning behaviors, highlighting the effect of fine-grained action modeling and information-aware optimization for long-horizon reasoning. Our code is anonymous available at https://github.com/jiangxinke/Harness-RL/tree/AgenticRAG-R1-Whitebox.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出AgenticRag-R1，一种基于强化学习的RAG框架，通过记忆栈和细粒度动作空间整合推理、检索与记忆，并采用分层动作感知奖励和信息感知轨迹拒绝策略，以解决现有方法在长程多步推理中的奖励分配弱和短视模板化问题。实验在多个多跳、开放域和智能体推理基准上，跨多种骨干模型规模，显示该方法持续优于强基线，并学习到更稳健、可解释和记忆感知的推理行为。
- innovations_zh: 引入记忆栈结构，实现推理、检索与记忆的深度整合; 设计细粒度动作空间与分层动作感知奖励，改善长程学习; 提出信息感知轨迹拒绝策略，提升长程推理有效性
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体实验数值和基线细节，难以评估改进幅度和统计显著性。评测主要基于公开基准，未涉及真实应用场景或部署成本，且代码匿名可能影响可复现性验证。
- potential: 未来可探索将记忆栈与更复杂的外部知识库交互结合，或扩展到多模态推理场景。该框架的奖励设计和轨迹拒绝策略可能推广到其他长程决策任务，具有长期价值。

### [181] An Agentic Retrobiosynthesis Framework with Learned Frontier Selection  (id=2608.30702, 2026-09-01, rl=15, score=7.3)
- cats: 微调与对齐, Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准, 领域应用
- ABS: Large language models are increasingly used as agents for multistep retrosynthesis, raising the question of how much their search policy contributes independently of the underlying reaction model. We investigate this question in a biological setting through rule-based retrobiosynthesis: a deterministic biochemical engine generates the same validated transitions for every method, searching for routes that terminate in metabolites available to an \emph{Escherichia coli} chassis, while the policy only selects which frontier molecule to expand next. Prompted and LoRA-tuned Qwen2.5-7B policies use a strict choice-only interface. The fine-tuned policy reaches $65\pm1$\% solve rate at 10 expansions on LASER versus 59\% for MCTS, and at 200 expansions reaches $78\pm1$\% versus 75\% on LASER, $88\pm3$\% versus 80\% on the RetroPath RL Golden benchmark, and $63\pm2$\% versus 45\% on the BioNavi-NP benchmark. Fine-tuning also consistently outperforms direct prompting. These results show that route-supervised frontier selection can improve budgeted search without altering biochemical generation, although performance remains dependent on frontier construction and reaction ranking.
- summary_zh: 该研究探讨了在基于规则的逆生物合成中，大语言模型作为搜索策略的独立贡献。通过严格的选择接口，对比了提示和LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-7B策略与MCTS在多个基准上的表现。微调策略在10次扩展时达到65%的解决率，优于MCTS的59%，并在200次扩展时在多个基准上持续领先。结果表明，路线监督的前沿选择能有效提升预算受限的搜索性能，且不改变生化生成过程。
- innovations_zh: 提出严格选择接口，隔离策略与反应模型贡献; 将LoRA微调应用于逆生物合成前沿选择; 在多个生物合成基准上系统对比策略性能
- limitations: 摘要未提供模型规模、训练数据细节及计算成本，限制了可复现性。性能仍依赖前沿构建和反应排序，且评测仅基于规则引擎，未涉及真实实验验证。
- potential: 未来可探索更复杂的前沿构建方法，结合反应排序学习以进一步提升性能。长期来看，该框架可扩展至其他逆合成任务，并可能整合实验反馈实现闭环优化。

### [182] MLREF: Efficient Module Reuse for Reward Design in Reinforcement Learning via Large Language Models  (id=2608.18827, 2026-08-20, rl=15, score=7.2)
- cats: 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Reward function design remains a bottleneck in reinforcement learning. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled automated reward generation, existing methods generate and revise reward functions as monolithic programs, making it difficult to reliably preserve and reuse effective components discovered in earlier iterations, leading to unstable performance across iterations. To address this, we propose Module Level Reward Evolution Framework (MLREF). At the core of MLREF is a module pool, a persistent repository of reusable reward components. MLREF treats the module pool as the primary optimization object: the pool evolves across iterations by accumulating successful modules, refining underperforming ones, and reusing proven components; while reward functions are constructed as linear combinations of modules drawn from this pool. To drive this evolution, MLREF integrates three mechanisms: reflection-based refinement, hybrid credit assignment, and a merge strategy with rollback, which together improve the effectiveness and robustness of reward optimization. Experiments on 17 tasks show that MLREF outperforms strong baselines by 25.2% in locomotion and 6.6% in manipulation, with more stable optimization dynamics.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对强化学习中奖励函数设计这一瓶颈，提出了一种基于大语言模型的模块级奖励进化框架（MLREF）。MLREF通过维护一个可复用的奖励模块池，将奖励函数构建为模块的线性组合，并利用反思细化、混合信用分配和带回滚的合并策略驱动模块池进化。在17个任务上的实验表明，MLREF在运动控制和操作任务上分别比强基线提升25.2%和6.6%，且优化动态更稳定。
- innovations_zh: 提出模块级奖励进化框架，将优化对象从整体奖励函数转为可复用的模块池; 设计混合信用分配与带回滚的合并策略，提升奖励优化的鲁棒性; 利用大语言模型实现模块的自动生成、细化与复用，减少人工设计成本
- limitations: 摘要未提供任务的具体类型和多样性细节，可能限制结论的普适性。评测仅基于17个任务，未涉及真实世界或高维复杂环境，性能提升的泛化性有待验证。此外，模块池的初始构建和进化过程依赖LLM，其计算成本和不确定性未在摘要中说明。
- potential: 未来可探索模块池的跨任务迁移，以提升奖励设计的通用性。结合更细粒度的信用分配或自适应模块组合策略，可能进一步提高优化效率。长期来看，该框架有望成为自动化奖励设计的基础工具，并扩展到多智能体或分层强化学习场景。

### [183] WeightBridge: An Efficient Weight Transfer Library for Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.25442, 2026-09-23, rl=15, score=7.2)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用
- ABS: Weight transfer - the propagation of updated parameters from trainers to rollout generators - is becoming an important performance bottleneck in reinforcement learning (RL) systems for LLMs. The central challenge is supporting the diverse trainer and rollout layouts and synchronization requirements of modern RL workloads without sacrificing efficiency. Existing solutions are efficient under some configurations but perform poorly or lack support under others. We present WeightBridge, a flexible, efficient weight-transfer library designed to deliver high performance across diverse RL configurations. WeightBridge first automatically extracts the correspondence between trainer and rollout weight layouts, then plans and executes redundancy-free and load-balanced weight transfer. It exposes a small, general API while coordinating workers across diverse synchronization modes. Across configurations spanning different models, parallelization layouts, and synchronization modes, WeightBridge reduces average GPU stall time by up to 42$\times$ over the state-of-the-art open-source RL framework and achieves high performance in all settings. A coding agent was able to integrate WeightBridge into two different RL frameworks without manual guidance, demonstrating the generality and ease of use of its APIs.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注大语言模型强化学习系统中，训练器到采样生成器的参数更新传播（权重传输）正成为性能瓶颈，核心挑战是在不牺牲效率的前提下支持多样的训练器与采样布局及同步需求。现有方案仅在部分配置下高效，其他情况下性能差或缺乏支持。作者提出WeightBridge库，自动提取训练器与采样权重布局的对应关系，规划并执行无冗余、负载均衡的权重传输，并暴露小而通用的API以协调多种同步模式下的工作进程。实验表明，在跨模型、并行布局和同步模式的配置中，其平均GPU停顿时间相比最先进开源RL框架最多降低42倍，且编码智能体无需人工指导即可将其集成到两个不同RL框架中。
- innovations_zh: 自动提取训练器与采样权重布局的对应关系; 规划并执行无冗余且负载均衡的权重传输; 提供小而通用的API以协调多种同步模式; 编码智能体可无人工指导集成到不同框架
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了相对单一最先进开源RL框架的GPU停顿时间加速比，未说明绝对性能、其他基线或端到端训练吞吐等指标。其通用性验证限于两个RL框架的集成，未覆盖更广泛的工业级或异构部署场景。此外，自动布局提取与传输规划在极端动态或非规则布局下的开销与鲁棒性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索将WeightBridge扩展到更异构的硬件拓扑、动态并行策略及多训练器场景，并研究其与通信压缩、异步更新等机制的协同。长期看，通用权重传输层有望成为LLM强化学习系统的基础组件，降低框架适配成本并提升整体训练效率。

### [184] HarmTrace: Anchor-Calibrated Decoupled Optimization for Fine-Grained Target Identification in Harmful Memes  (id=2608.16622, 2026-08-18, rl=15, score=7.1)
- cats: 多模态, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Multimodal harmful meme detection is typically formulated as image--text harmfulness classification. A model may correctly predict harmfulness while misidentifying the attacked target or its supporting evidence. We therefore extend harmful meme detection with fine-grained target identification, asking what type of target is attacked, who is targeted, and where the target appears in the meme. The model predicts harmfulness for every meme and, for harmful memes, outputs the target category, target entity, textual mention, and visual region. To support this task, we introduce Meme3W, which unifies multiple public harmful meme datasets and provides human-verified annotations for harmful instances. We further introduce Joint Record Accuracy (JRA), a strict record-level metric requiring the harmfulness label and all target-identification fields to be jointly correct. Experiments with representative multimodal large language models reveal a substantial gap between harmfulness accuracy and JRA. To narrow this gap, we propose HarmTrace, an anchor-calibrated decoupled optimization framework. HarmTrace strengthens target-entity supervision through entity-aware supervised fine-tuning. It then applies Conditional Target-identification Policy Optimization (CTPO) to decouple harmfulness and target-identification advantages, restricting target-identification optimization to label-correct responses for harmful examples. CTPO uses a Virtual Positive Anchor (VPA) as a fully correct reference for target-identification advantage normalization. HarmTrace improves both JRA and harmfulness accurac
- summary_zh: 该论文针对有害模因检测中目标识别不精确的问题，提出了细粒度目标识别任务，要求模型同时预测危害性、目标类别、目标实体、文本提及和视觉区域。为此，作者构建了统一数据集Meme3W并引入严格指标JRA，发现现有模型在JRA上表现不佳。提出的HarmTrace框架通过实体感知微调和条件目标识别策略优化，显著提升了JRA和危害性准确率，例如在Qwen3-VL-8B上JRA从17.58%提升至52.51%。
- innovations_zh: 提出细粒度目标识别任务，扩展了有害模因检测的范畴; 构建统一数据集Meme3W并引入严格记录级指标JRA; 设计锚定校准的解耦优化框架HarmTrace，含CTPO和VPA机制
- limitations: 摘要未提供多数据集上的详细性能对比，泛化性有待验证。JRA指标要求所有字段完全正确，可能过于严格，且未讨论字段间依赖关系。实验仅基于特定几个MLLM骨干，未覆盖更多模型类型。
- potential: 未来可探索更细粒度的目标属性（如攻击意图、情感强度）或跨模态对齐的改进。HarmTrace框架可迁移至其他多模态理解任务，如视觉问答中的指代消解。长期看，该方向有助于构建更可解释、更可靠的多模态内容审核系统。

### [185] One Policy, Any Budget: Internalizing Budget-Aware Search via Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.00813, 2026-09-02, rl=15, score=7.0)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: While reinforcement learning has enabled LLM-based search agents to invoke external tools, existing methods train under fixed budgets and cannot adapt when constraints vary at deployment. We propose AnySearch, a framework that enables a single policy to perform budget-aware search under any budget constraint through a training scaffold and curriculum reinforcement learning. In the first phase, we train the agent with explicit budget state injection and structured reasoning prompts that guide efficient allocation under linearly decaying budgets. In the second phase, the scaffold is removed and the agent learns to operate autonomously under adaptively sampled budget constraints, matching inference conditions. Both phases are optimized with a composite reward that couples answer accuracy with budget efficiency through absolute and relative signals, where an adaptive weight amplifies the efficiency signal for high-accuracy queries and attenuates it for low-accuracy ones. Extensive experiments on seven general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method outperforms baselines across all budget scales, generalizes to unseen constraints beyond the training range, and achieves superior tool productivity without excessive token overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/xwsun01/AnySearch.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出AnySearch框架，通过训练脚手架和课程强化学习，使单个策略能在任意预算约束下进行预算感知的搜索。方法分两阶段：先注入显式预算状态和结构化推理提示，再移除脚手架让代理在自适应采样的预算约束下自主操作。复合奖励结合准确率与预算效率，并自适应调整权重。在七个QA基准上，该方法在所有预算尺度上优于基线，并能泛化到未见过的约束。
- innovations_zh: 提出单一策略适应任意预算约束的框架，无需针对不同预算重新训练。; 采用两阶段课程学习，从脚手架训练过渡到自主操作，匹配推理条件。; 设计复合奖励，通过绝对和相对信号耦合准确率与效率，并自适应加权。
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体性能数值，无法评估改进幅度。评测仅基于QA基准，可能未覆盖其他任务类型（如对话、代码生成）。训练过程依赖课程采样和复合奖励设计，其超参数敏感性未讨论。
- potential: 未来可探索将预算感知能力扩展到更复杂的多步骤推理和真实世界工具调用场景。该框架的课程学习与奖励加权机制可迁移至其他资源受限的决策任务，如时间或计算预算约束。长期看，有望推动自适应资源分配在通用代理中的标准化。

### [186] VISA: Agentic Self-Evolving Data Synthesis for Multimodal Instruction Following  (id=2608.26013, 2026-08-27, rl=15, score=6.8)
- cats: 多模态, Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Multimodal instruction-following models require training data that is accurate, diverse, verifiable, and challenging. Existing synthesis pipelines typically follow a one-pass generate-and-filter paradigm, discarding feedback from failed samples, verifier outcomes, and target-model errors. We present VISA (Visual Instruction Synthesis Agent), an agentic framework that reformulates multimodal instruction synthesis as a self-evolving loop. At each round, VISA analyzes an image to filter incompatible constraints and discover new verifiable ones, samples diversity- and difficulty-aware constraint sets from persistent memory, generates candidate instructions, and verifies the resulting samples with executable tools and structured large language model judges. Failed samples trigger diagnostic-guided recovery, while accepted samples are probed against the target model to estimate difficulty. The resulting verifier signals and target-model failure profiles are written back to memory, allowing subsequent rounds to adaptively expand the constraint space, reduce template repetition, and focus on unresolved model weaknesses. The same verifier contracts further provide reward signals for reinforcement learning without a separately trained reward model. Experiments on MM-IFEval show that VISA consistently improves multimodal instruction following over strong baselines, while preserving general multimodal capability across seven public benchmarks.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出VISA框架，将多模态指令合成重构为自进化循环，通过分析图像、过滤约束、生成候选指令并验证，利用失败样本和模型错误反馈迭代优化。实验表明VISA在MM-IFEval上持续提升多模态指令跟随性能，同时保持七个公共基准上的通用能力。
- innovations_zh: 将指令合成从单次生成-过滤改为自进化循环，利用失败反馈和验证器信号; 引入持久记忆机制，动态扩展约束空间并聚焦模型弱点; 验证器信号直接用于强化学习，无需单独训练奖励模型
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体性能数值，仅提及在MM-IFEval上的提升，缺乏与其他方法的量化对比细节。评测基准和任务范围有限，未讨论在更广泛多模态场景或不同模型架构下的泛化性。
- potential: 未来可探索将自进化循环扩展到更多模态和复杂推理任务，并研究记忆机制的可解释性与可迁移性。长期看，该框架有望成为自动化数据合成与模型持续学习的基础组件。

### [187] Qwen-Planner-Agent: A Closed-Loop AI-for-AI Framework for Real-World Mobile Planner Agents  (id=2609.29892, 2026-09-25, rl=15, score=6.8)
