- ABS: The rapid progression of large language models is extending AI from passive content generation into the active workflows of engineering and scientific discovery. This shift raises a compelling question: can AI be both the object of development and an active participant in building next-generation AI systems? We explore this question by building Qwen-Planner-Agent within a closed-loop AI-for-AI framework for scalable development and iterative improvement. Mobile planning offers a demanding test of this approach: complex, long-horizon tasks challenge agent reliability, while costly real-device interaction limits development scalability. The framework connects data production, model training, and deployment through a shared action-feedback-verification contract. (i) AI for Data builds a human-gated agentic data flywheel in which specialized agents construct tasks, collect interaction trajectories, curate and balance training data, and use training feedback to guide subsequent data generation. (ii) AI for Training combines a supervised planning cold start with hybrid-environment online agentic reinforcement learning, where we introduce Competence-Aware Reward-and-Advantage Engineering (CARE) to reduce reasoning and tool-use costs while preserving task performance. (iii) AI drives model--harness co-evolution through an execution-evidence-driven loop that orchestrates memory, skills, and tools at runtime and feeds structured action feedback and preserved failure traces back into coordinated model and harness adaptation. Qwen-Planner-Agent achieves the best overall performance amo
- summary_zh: 该研究探讨AI能否既作为开发对象又作为构建下一代AI系统的参与者，提出闭环AI-for-AI框架并构建Qwen-Planner-Agent。框架通过共享的动作-反馈-验证契约连接数据生产、模型训练与部署，包含AI for Data的数据飞轮、AI for Training的冷启动与在线强化学习（含CARE方法）以及AI驱动的模型-工具协同演化。Qwen-Planner-Agent在MobilePA-Bench上取得最佳整体性能，并在非移动智能体基准上有所提升且基本保持通用能力。
- innovations_zh: 提出闭环AI-for-AI框架，以共享动作-反馈-验证契约连接数据、训练与部署; 构建人控智能体数据飞轮，用训练反馈指导后续数据生成; 引入CARE方法，在保持任务性能的同时降低推理与工具使用成本; 实现执行证据驱动的模型-工具协同演化，整合记忆、技能与工具
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了在MobilePA-Bench及部分非移动基准上的结果，未提供具体数值、统计显著性或消融细节，难以全面评估各组件贡献。框架依赖真实设备交互与人工门控数据飞轮，可能限制其可扩展性与通用性，且未讨论失败案例或安全风险。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证该闭环框架在更多移动场景与非移动领域的泛化能力，探索减少人工门控与真实设备依赖的替代方案。长期看，若模型-工具协同演化与CARE方法可迁移，或能推动AI系统自主迭代研发范式的成熟。

### [188] Beyond Token-Local Imitation: Reward-Compatible Temporal Credit Assignment for On-Policy Distillation  (id=2609.16937, 2026-09-16, rl=15, score=6.7)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 推理与数学
- ABS: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective approach for large language model post-training, yet existing objectives face a trade-off between objective fidelity and optimization stability. Token-level OPD provides stable but local supervision, whereas sequence-level OPD captures future credit at the cost of horizon-dependent variance. We establish a unified temporal-credit view of these formulations, showing that practical token-level OPD can be interpreted as a temporal approximation to the sequence-level reverse-KL gradient. Building on this connection, we propose $γ$OPD, which uses discounted temporal credit assignment to balance long-horizon supervision and optimization stability, while admitting a horizon-independent variance bound. We further develop a reward-compatible bounded mixing (RBM) mechanism for $γ\mathrm{OPD}$ that balances verifiable outcome feedback with the discounted OPD advantage to move beyond purely teacher-dependent optimization. Experiments on mathematical and code reasoning demonstrate consistent improvements over existing OPD methods across vanilla, size-mismatched, and multi-teacher distillation settings.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中目标保真度与优化稳定性之间的权衡问题：词元级OPD监督稳定但局部，序列级OPD能捕捉未来信用但方差随视野增长。作者从时间信用分配的统一视角出发，将实用的词元级OPD解释为序列级反向KL梯度的时间近似，并据此提出γOPD，用折扣时间信用分配平衡长视野监督与优化稳定性，且具有与视野无关的方差界。作者还提出奖励兼容的有界混合（RBM）机制，将可验证结果反馈与折扣OPD优势结合，减少对教师信号的纯依赖。在数学与代码推理任务上，该方法在原始、规模不匹配和多教师蒸馏设置中均取得一致改进。
- innovations_zh: 建立统一时间信用视角，将词元级OPD解释为序列级反向KL梯度的时间近似; 提出γOPD，用折扣时间信用分配平衡长视野监督与优化稳定性; 给出与视野无关的方差界，提升优化稳定性分析; 提出RBM机制，融合可验证结果反馈与折扣OPD优势
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了数学与代码推理任务上的结果，未说明在其他领域或更广泛任务上的泛化能力。方法涉及折扣时间信用分配与有界混合机制，可能引入额外超参数，摘要未说明其敏感性或调参成本。此外，摘要未提供与基线方法的计算开销、训练效率或理论假设的详细对比，实际可扩展性仍需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索折扣因子与混合机制在不同任务、模型规模和教师数量下的自适应设置，以降低调参负担。可将时间信用分配视角推广到其他序列生成后训练目标，如强化学习或偏好优化。若能与可验证奖励和在线反馈更紧密结合，长期有望提升蒸馏方法在复杂推理任务中的自主优化能力。

### [189] Rufus-Air: An Open LLM Post-Training Recipe  (id=2609.29421, 2026-09-25, rl=15, score=6.7)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 推理优化与效率, 推理与数学
- ABS: Rufus-Air is an open and reproducible post-training recipe on GLM-4.5-Air-Base (106B-A12B), organized as a serial pipeline of eight stages: SFT, Reasoning RL, Coding RL, Instruction-Following RL, General Agent, Coding Agent, Search Agent, and RLHF. We document the data, reward design, infrastructure, stage order, and stagewise results needed to reproduce the recipe. Stages progress from basic to advanced capabilities and from hard, verifiable rewards to softer judge-based signals. Training builds on open-source components and public data, much of it used as released, without new human annotation or an in-house distillation teacher. Our main findings are that (i) diverse, high-quality SFT establishes a strong capability floor; (ii) difficulty filtering keeps RL prompts within a productive learning range; (iii) reward reliability provides a practical principle for ordering stages; and (iv) infrastructure and engineering choices are part of the recipe, not just an implementation detail. Rufus-Air improves over the official GLM-4.5-Air post-trained release and is competitive with similarly sized open models.
- summary_zh: 该工作提出了 Rufus-Air，一个在 GLM-4.5-Air-Base（106B-A12B）上开放且可复现的后训练配方，由 SFT、推理 RL、编码 RL、指令遵循 RL、通用智能体、编码智能体、搜索智能体和 RLHF 八个阶段串联组成。作者记录了数据、奖励设计、基础设施、阶段顺序和各阶段结果，并强调训练基于开源组件与公开数据，无需新的人工标注或自研蒸馏教师。主要发现包括：多样高质量 SFT 奠定能力下限，难度过滤使 RL 提示保持在有效学习区间，奖励可靠性为阶段排序提供实践原则，基础设施与工程选择也是配方的一部分。Rufus-Air 优于官方 GLM-4.5-Air 后训练版本，并与同规模开放模型具有竞争力。
- innovations_zh: 提出八阶段串联的开放可复现后训练配方，覆盖从基础到高级能力; 奖励从硬可验证信号逐步过渡到较软的评判信号并据此排序阶段; 无需新人工标注或自研蒸馏教师，基于开源组件与公开数据完成训练; 强调基础设施与工程选择是配方组成部分而非实现细节
- limitations: 摘要仅给出总体结论，未披露各阶段的具体数据规模、奖励设计细节与超参数，复现难度仍不确定。评测仅称优于官方后训练版本并与同规模开放模型竞争，未说明具体基准、评测方式与统计显著性。配方绑定 GLM-4.5-Air-Base 与特定开源组件，向其他基座或规模迁移的有效性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证该八阶段配方在不同基座模型与参数规模上的可迁移性，并探索阶段顺序与奖励可靠性的更一般化原则。若能公开更细粒度的数据、奖励与工程配置，将提升复现性并推动后训练流程的标准化与持续迭代。

### [190] Policy Iteration with Human Feedback: Bringing Post-Training RL to In-context Learning  (id=2608.16831, 2026-08-18, rl=15, score=6.5)
- cats: 预训练与架构, Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 领域应用
- ABS: Generative pretraining established reusable task representations; later work on language-based task conditioning and in-context learning showed that a fixed model could adapt its behavior from instructions and demonstrations. Policy Iteration with Human Feedback (PIHF) builds on this development and the recurrent evaluate-and-improve structure of generalized policy iteration. PIHF uses a pretrained language model as its execution substrate and moves persistent revision to a versioned natural-language policy and tool set. A language-model critic and clinical expert review complete-panel reasoning and tool-use trajectories to localize recurrent failures and form candidate revisions; the expert may reinterpret the evidence and retains authority over admission and rollback, while Recall@1 and Recall@5 validate outcomes after candidate execution. Across cumulative ablations and ultra-rare-disease benchmarks, a PIHF-derived policy improved Recall@1 in one proprietary executor and three open-weight executors spanning 3 to 49 billion active parameters. Gains were 32.7 percentage points for GPT-5.4 and 31.1 points for Qwen3.6-35B, a difference of 1.7 points. These results support the feasibility of using pretrained language models as fixed-weight execution substrates for expert-guided policy development in rare-disease diagnosis.
- summary_zh: 该研究提出策略迭代与人类反馈（PIHF）方法，将后训练强化学习引入上下文学习，利用预训练语言模型作为固定权重执行主体，通过语言模型批评者和临床专家审查推理轨迹，迭代更新自然语言策略和工具集。在罕见病诊断基准上，PIHF派生的策略在多个执行器上显著提升Recall@1，如GPT-5.4提升32.7个百分点，Qwen3.6-35B提升31.1个百分点。结果表明，固定权重的预训练语言模型可支持专家引导的策略开发。
- innovations_zh: 将策略迭代与人类反馈结合，实现上下文学习中的持久策略修订; 使用版本化自然语言策略和工具集，支持可回滚的专家审核机制; 在多个开源和专有执行器上验证跨模型泛化性，提升罕见病诊断性能
- limitations: 摘要未提供评测数据集规模、基线对比细节及统计显著性检验，结果稳健性存疑。仅报告Recall@1和Recall@5，缺乏精确率、F1等指标，可能掩盖误诊风险。专家审核依赖人工，可能限制可扩展性。
- potential: 未来可探索自动化专家反馈生成，减少人工干预，并扩展到其他领域（如法律、金融）。长期看，该框架或推动固定权重模型通过外部策略持续进化，降低训练成本，提升模型适应性和可靠性。

### [191] STAGE: Controlled Objective Admission for Multi-Preference LLM Alignment  (id=2608.16553, 2026-08-18, rl=15, score=6.5)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Multi-preference alignment is often framed as scalarization: combine reward dimensions, then optimize. This leaves a temporal decision underspecified: when should each preference dimension enter policy optimization? We propose \methodname, a stability-guided active-set controller for controlled objective admission. \methodname starts from a small active set, retains admitted objectives, and expands when reward-deviation gates indicate low recent deviation or a patience budget is exhausted. A probing phase estimates a hard-to-easy order, and adaptive weighting emphasizes underperforming active dimensions. Automatic evaluations with 15 training preferences and 16 held-out benchmark columns show that \methodname obtains higher averages than simultaneous scalarization and shared-budget adapted baselines. Component ablations and expansion dynamics further support cumulative retention, gated admission, and probing-derived ordering as useful design choices in this setting. These results position objective-entry timing as a concrete control variable in reward-vector RLHF.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出STAGE方法，用于多偏好对齐中的目标准入控制，通过稳定性引导的主动集控制器决定各偏好维度何时进入策略优化。STAGE从小的主动集开始，基于奖励偏差门控和耐心预算扩展目标，并通过探测阶段估计难易顺序和自适应加权。在15个训练偏好和16个保留基准上的自动评估显示，STAGE优于同时标量化和共享预算基线，消融实验支持其设计选择。
- innovations_zh: 提出目标准入时间控制，将偏好维度进入策略优化的时机作为显式控制变量; 设计稳定性引导的主动集控制器，结合门控和耐心预算动态扩展目标; 引入探测阶段估计难易顺序，并采用自适应加权强调表现不佳的维度
- limitations: 摘要仅基于自动评估，未提及人工评测或真实应用场景，泛化性未知。方法依赖多个超参数（如门控阈值、耐心预算），其敏感性未在摘要中讨论。此外，评估基准为特定列，可能未覆盖所有偏好类型。
- potential: 未来可探索将STAGE扩展到更复杂偏好结构或在线学习场景，并研究目标进入顺序与最终性能的理论联系。长期看，该控制变量可能与其他对齐技术（如DPO、RLHF变体）结合，提升多目标优化效率。

### [192] Turn-level Multiscale Density Ratio Estimation for LLM Agents  (id=2609.16760, 2026-09-16, rl=15, score=6.5)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 微调与对齐, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: With the rapid development of Large language model (LLM), agent systems enhanced by LLMs show huge potential in being able to deal with complex tasks, especially involving multi-step thinking or interaction with tools. For applying LLM techniques with a well-designed agent paradigm, post-training of LLM in multiple agent scenarios is necessary to achieve better performance. Among the variable post-training techniques, alignment methods such as PPO, DPO, DIL, and GRPO become popular because many papers show a significant positive impact on the model's performance by punishing negative samples while keeping acceptable training complexity. However, most alignment methods address simple single-turn tasks, and there remains room for improvement for complex multi-turn tasks. We propose Turn-level Multiscale Density Ratio Estimation (tlm-DRE), which assigns different weights on corresponding turns and proposes asymmetric token-level training based on the positive-negative space gaps across multiple turns of tasks. The results of the experiment on a wide range of agent benchmarks show that the proposed method performs competitively compared to traditional alignment methods. The proposed training method enables LLMs to perform robustly in multi-turn reasoning tasks with both in-domain and out-of-domain conditions.
- summary_zh: 论文关注LLM智能体在多步推理与工具交互场景下的后训练问题，指出PPO、DPO、DIL、GRPO等对齐方法多针对简单单轮任务，在多轮复杂任务上仍有改进空间。作者提出Turn-level Multiscale Density Ratio Estimation（tlm-DRE），对不同轮次分配不同权重，并基于多轮任务的正负空间差距设计非对称的token级训练。在多种智能体基准上的实验显示，该方法与传统对齐方法相比具有竞争力，并在域内和域外条件下均表现出稳健的多轮推理能力。
- innovations_zh: 提出轮次级多尺度密度比估计，对不同轮次分配差异化权重; 基于多轮正负空间差距设计非对称token级训练; 面向多轮智能体任务改进对齐方法，兼顾域内与域外稳健性
- limitations: 摘要仅说明在多种智能体基准上表现具有竞争力，未给出具体指标、基线细节或统计显著性，因而效果优势的程度与稳定性难以判断。方法涉及轮次权重与密度比估计，可能引入额外超参数或训练复杂度，但摘要未说明其代价与敏感性。域外稳健性虽被提及，但缺少对域外设置与评测范围的明确界定。
- potential: 未来可进一步明确轮次权重与密度比估计的设计原则，并系统分析其在不同多轮任务长度、工具调用复杂度下的适用性。还可与更多对齐方法在统一基准上做细粒度比较，验证域外泛化与训练成本的权衡。若方法可扩展至更广泛的智能体交互场景，长期有望推动多轮对齐训练范式的标准化。

### [193] OneBid: A Unified Auto-Bidding Foundation Model for Diverse oCPX Advertising Scenarios  (id=2609.21550, 2026-09-19, rl=15, score=6.4)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 预训练与架构, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Auto-bidding is central to computational advertising, where strategies must maximize advertisers' conversion value under economic constraints. It has evolved from rule-based controllers to reinforcement learning and generative methods such as Decision Transformer (DT). Yet these methods increasingly mismatch the prevailing optimized cost-per-X (oCPX) paradigm, which spans heterogeneous scenarios (e.g., registration, purchase), each served by a separate model, leading to fragmented pipelines and underexploring cross-scenario modeling. Inspired by foundation models like LLMs, unifying these oCPX scenarios into one model raises three challenges: multi-objective control, scalable capacity under strict latency, and safe offline policy improvement. We present OneBid, a unified auto-bidding foundation model that learns a reusable backbone from heterogeneous oCPX logs and adapts it to scenario-specific deployments via offline post-training. Building on DT, OneBid extends single Return-to-Go conditioning to two atomic signals, Return-to-Go for conversion value and Cost-to-Go for cost ratio, plus value-aware regularization on next-action prediction. To absorb distributional heterogeneity, we design a sequence-level Mixture-of-Experts architecture, where shared experts encode cross-scenario knowledge and sparsely-routed experts capture scenario-specific patterns at low latency, yielding consistent scaling with model size and data. During post-training, we align the backbone with scenario preferences via Critic-guided Relative Offline Policy optimization (CROP): a learned critic scores
- summary_zh: 该论文针对自动出价中oCPX范式下多场景各自建模导致流程碎片化、跨场景建模不足的问题，提出统一基础模型OneBid。方法上基于Decision Transformer，将单一Return-to-Go扩展为转化价值与成本比率的双信号条件，并加入价值感知正则，同时采用序列级混合专家架构以低延迟吸收场景异质性。后训练阶段提出CROP，用学习到的critic对候选动作做组相对打分，避免不安全的在线探索并约束策略偏移。该模型在快手完成全量部署，在线A/B测试显示oCPX广告整体ADVV提升2.2%，ROAS场景最高提升13.1%。
- innovations_zh: 将DT的单Return-to-Go扩展为转化价值与成本比率双信号条件; 设计序列级混合专家架构，共享与稀疏路由专家兼顾通用与场景特性; 提出CROP后训练方法，用critic组相对打分替代在线探索式微调
- limitations: 摘要仅给出整体与ROAS场景的ADVV提升，未披露其他oCPX场景的细分效果、离线评测细节与统计显著性，效果泛化性难以判断。方法依赖大规模异构oCPX日志与离线后训练，在数据稀缺或场景分布差异极大的广告平台上的可迁移性尚不明确。此外，延迟与模型规模、专家数量的权衡仅有定性描述，缺少定量边界。
- potential: 未来可探索将双信号条件扩展至更多经济约束或长期价值目标，并研究跨平台、跨行业的场景迁移与少样本适配。混合专家与CROP的组合或可推广到其他序列决策基础模型，用于安全离线策略改进。若能与在线持续学习结合，长期有望形成可复用的广告出价基础模型生态。

### [194] PlayTrain: An Efficient Reinforcement Learning Framework for LLM-Generated Adaptable JavaScript Games  (id=2609.09059, 2026-09-09, rl=15, score=6.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 多模态
- ABS: While many video-game environments (VGEs) have played crucial roles in advancing reinforcement learning (RL), developing novel VGEs or modifying existing ones to support new features, has been a laborious process requiring extensive hand-coding. Here we present PlayTrain, an RL framework that combines the abilities of large language models (LLMs) to robustly generate JavaScript (JS) games from a minimal human prompt, and an efficient pipeline that can run any JS game in a standard 'gym' environment. Not only are recent LLMs particularly good at writing JS code, but the JS format also allows users to easily play generated VGEs, while PlayTrain enables us to train RL agents on the exact same games. We demonstrate multiple use cases of PlayTrain, including cloning well-known Atari and ProcGen games in simple JS, where PlayTrain trains pixel-based agents end-to-end at over 1M agent-decisions per second on a single GPU node; and creating modified versions thereof (e.g., that support novel test sets, procedural generation logics, or game dynamics). Through PlayTrain, we reimagine RL VGE development: all we need is a single JS file, generated and modified through an LLM. We discuss promising future RL research directions that PlayTrain unlocks.
- summary_zh: 该论文提出PlayTrain框架，利用大语言模型从简短提示生成JavaScript游戏，并构建高效流水线将其接入标准gym环境以训练强化学习智能体。实验表明，该框架能以每秒超过100万次决策的速度训练像素级智能体，并支持克隆Atari和ProcGen游戏及创建修改版本。该方法将视频游戏环境开发简化为单一JS文件的生成与修改，为强化学习研究提供了新范式。
- innovations_zh: 结合LLM自动生成JS游戏，大幅降低环境开发成本; 构建高效训练流水线，单GPU节点每秒处理超百万决策; 支持游戏克隆与修改，便于生成新测试集和动态逻辑
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体游戏复杂度或任务难度的量化评估，也未与现有环境构建方法进行系统对比。LLM生成游戏的质量和多样性可能受限于提示设计，且仅基于JS格式，可能不适用于所有RL任务类型。
- potential: 未来可探索LLM生成更复杂、更开放的游戏环境，结合自动课程学习或元学习。该框架也可能促进人机交互式环境设计，并推动RL在程序生成内容上的泛化研究。长期来看，有望形成标准化、可扩展的RL环境生成与训练生态。

### [195] SEAR: Segment-Evidence-Aware Routing for Weak-to-Strong Multilingual Speech MCQ  (id=2609.11355, 2026-09-11, rl=15, score=6.1)
- cats: 多模态, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: This paper describes our system for Task~2 of the second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge. We adapt Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct with a segment-evidence-aware data and post-training pipeline. A language model converts timestamped ASR into coherent event spans, which are expanded by a boundary margin and cropped from the original recording. We then synthesize complementary semantic MCQs with Qwen3.6-27B and acoustic MCQs with Gemini~3.1 Flash-Lite, followed by structural, grounding, answer-consistency, and target-model trainability checks, yielding 359,825 verified MCQs across 21 language and accent variants. A text-only probe partitions the data into weak, text-answerable items used for supervised fine-tuning and strong, audio-dependent items used for reinforcement learning with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), stabilized by debiased advantages, sequence-level importance correction, and dynamic filtering. Our system obtains 90.92% accuracy on the final official evaluation set.
- summary_zh: 本文面向第二届MLC-SLM挑战赛任务2，研究弱到强多语言会话语音选择题。方法上，将时间戳ASR转为事件跨度并裁剪音频，合成语义与声学两类选择题，经多重校验得到359825条覆盖21种语言/口音变体的数据；再用纯文本探针划分弱、强样本，分别做监督微调与基于GSPO的强化学习。系统在官方评测集上取得90.92%准确率。
- innovations_zh: 以时间戳ASR生成事件跨度并扩边裁剪，构建片段证据感知数据; 合成语义与声学两类选择题，并经四类校验筛选; 用纯文本探针划分弱/强样本，分别做SFT与强化学习; GSPO结合去偏优势、序列级重要性修正与动态过滤
- limitations: 摘要仅报告最终官方评测集准确率，未给出消融实验、各语言/口音分项结果或与基线的对比，难以判断各模块贡献。数据合成依赖多个外部模型，可能引入其偏差；弱/强划分基于纯文本探针，其可靠性未在摘要中验证。
- potential: 未来可进一步分析各语言与口音变体上的性能差异，验证弱强划分与各训练组件的独立贡献。片段证据感知与弱到强强化学习思路或可迁移到其他语音理解与多模态推理任务，长期有助于提升语音大模型对声学依赖问题的鲁棒性。

### [196] FlowBalance: Verifier-Grounded Self-Improvement from On-Policy Reasoning Experience  (id=2609.03241, 2026-09-04, rl=15, score=5.6)
- cats: 推理与数学
- ABS: A reasoning model can improve from its own on-policy experience, but this inner loop is fragile: terminal verifiers provide reliable yet sparse supervision, while dense same-model guidance can reinforce false confidence or overconcentrate learning on a narrow solution mode. We introduce FlowBalance, a verifier-grounded self-improvement method that learns a normalized distribution over complete responses. For each on-policy trajectory, a frozen training-time view of the same policy uses privileged context to produce token-level log-probability gains, which are aggregated into a trajectory-level self-guidance score. FlowBalance calibrates this score with the verifier-derived group advantage: guidance is retained on positive-advantage trajectories, reversed on negative-advantage trajectories, and disabled when the rollout group provides no outcome preference. The resulting energy exponentially reweights a reference policy, and profiled trajectory balance fits the normalized target with one log-partition estimate per rollout group. This realizes outcome-calibrated self-guidance via trajectory balance, without a separate token-level imitation loss. Our analysis establishes within-group contrast preservation, a minimum-change reverse-KL characterization, monotonic verifier control of target reward, and an exact correction against false-positive self-guidance on rejected responses. On mathematical reasoning, FlowBalance improves average performance over FlowRL on both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, while also improving training speed and stability, avoiding direct OPSD's response-length c
- summary_zh: 该论文提出FlowBalance方法，用于推理模型基于自身在线策略经验进行自我改进，通过冻结的策略视图生成轨迹级自引导分数，并与验证器派生的组优势校准，以平衡稀疏的验证器监督和密集的自我引导。方法利用轨迹平衡拟合归一化目标，实现结果校准的自引导，无需单独的token级模仿损失。在数学推理任务上，FlowBalance在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上平均性能优于FlowRL，同时提升训练速度和稳定性，避免响应长度崩溃，并提高正确策略多样性。
- innovations_zh: 提出验证器校准的自引导分数，根据组优势保留、反转或禁用引导，实现结果校准。; 利用轨迹平衡拟合归一化目标，无需token级模仿损失，实现高效自改进。; 理论分析保证组内对比保持、最小变化逆向KL、验证器单调控制及对错误引导的修正。
- limitations: 摘要仅基于数学推理任务评估，未涉及其他领域或更复杂推理场景，泛化性未知。方法依赖验证器提供的组优势，若验证器不可靠或组内无偏好，引导可能被禁用，影响效果。训练速度与稳定性的提升缺乏详细量化对比，且未报告计算开销或超参数敏感性。
- potential: 未来可探索将FlowBalance应用于更多推理任务（如代码生成、科学问答）及更大规模模型，验证其通用性。长期价值在于建立验证器与自我引导的动态平衡机制，可能推动在线强化学习与自改进算法的进一步发展，并促进对策略多样性控制的理论理解。

### [197] A Survey of Agentic Reasoning for Large Language Models: Towards Recursively Self-Improving and Collective Agents  (id=2601.12538, 2026-09-23-补充, rl=14, score=9.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reasoning is a fundamental cognitive process underlying inference, problem-solving, and decision-making. While large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong reasoning capabilities in closed-world settings, they struggle in open-ended and dynamic environments. Agentic reasoning marks a paradigm shift by reframing LLMs as autonomous agents that plan, act, and learn through continual interaction. In this survey, we organize agentic reasoning along three complementary dimensions. First, we characterize environmental dynamics through three layers: foundational agentic reasoning, which establishes core single-agent capabilities including planning, tool use, and search in stable environments; self-evolving agentic reasoning, which studies how agents refine these capabilities through feedback, memory, and adaptation; and collective multi-agent reasoning, which extends intelligence to collaborative settings involving coordination, knowledge sharing, and shared goals. Across these layers, we distinguish in-context reasoning, which scales test-time interaction through structured orchestration, from post-training reasoning, which optimizes behaviors via reinforcement learning and supervised fine-tuning. We further review representative agentic reasoning frameworks across real-world applications and benchmarks, including science, robotics, healthcare, autonomous research, and mathematics. This survey synthesizes agentic reasoning methods into a unified roadmap bridging thought and action, and outlines open challenges and future directions, including personalization, long-horizon inter
- summary_zh: 该综述关注大语言模型在开放动态环境中推理能力不足的问题，将智能体推理视为让模型作为自主智能体进行规划、行动与持续学习的范式转变。作者从环境动态出发，将智能体推理划分为基础单智能体推理、自我演化推理和集体多智能体推理三个层次，并区分上下文推理与后训练推理两类优化方式。文章还梳理了科学、机器人、医疗、自主研究和数学等应用与基准中的代表性框架，提出连接思考与行动的统一路线图，并总结个性化、长时程交互、世界建模、可扩展多智能体训练和治理等开放挑战。
- innovations_zh: 按环境动态提出基础、自我演化、集体多智能体三层智能体推理框架; 区分上下文推理与后训练推理，统一测试时交互与行为优化视角; 跨科学、机器人、医疗、数学等应用与基准梳理代表性框架; 提出连接思考与行动的统一路线图并归纳开放挑战
- limitations: 作为综述，摘要未说明具体文献筛选标准、覆盖范围或系统性评估方法，因此其分类框架的完备性与代表性仍不确定。摘要也未给出定量比较或统一基准结果，难以判断各层次方法的实际效果与适用边界。
- potential: 未来可围绕个性化、长时程交互、世界建模、可扩展多智能体训练和真实部署治理等方向展开研究。若能将三层框架与更统一的评测和训练方法结合，可能推动大语言模型从封闭推理走向持续自我改进与集体协作。

### [198] Voice of Reason: Reinforcement Learning for Spoken Math  (id=2609.18677, 2026-09-17, rl=14, score=8.7)
- cats: 推理与数学, 多模态, 微调与对齐, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Speech language models enable richer spoken interactions between humans and machines than cascaded systems, allowing access to paralinguistic information and lower latency. However, their accuracy on mathematical reasoning benchmarks has lagged behind those of text models. Reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has been instrumental in extending text models' capabilities for solving complex problems and limiting hallucinations. In this work, we explore applying RL to the GLM-4-Voice speech model (Zeng et al., 2024) to bridge the gap between textual and spoken mathematical problem solving. We first adapt the model to the domain using supervised fine-tuning on synthesized spoken question-answering data. We then show that, even without extra reasoning tokens, RL improves the accuracy on GSM8K beyond levels previously achieved for speech models only with supplementary reasoning traces. When combined with existing streaming reasoning techniques, we show further gains to 74.8% free-form accuracy. This establishes a new state-of-the-art for mathematical spoken abilities with speech-native models.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对语音语言模型在数学推理基准上落后于文本模型的问题，探索将可验证奖励的强化学习应用于GLM-4-Voice语音模型。方法上先通过合成语音问答数据进行监督微调，再应用强化学习，无需额外推理token即可提升GSM8K准确率，超过此前仅靠补充推理轨迹的语音模型。结合流式推理技术后，自由形式准确率达到74.8%，在语音原生模型的数学能力上创下新纪录。
- innovations_zh: 将可验证奖励强化学习应用于语音语言模型以提升数学推理; 无需额外推理token即超越此前需推理轨迹的语音模型; 结合流式推理技术使自由形式准确率达74.8%
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了GSM8K上的结果，未说明在其他数学或推理基准上的泛化性。监督微调依赖合成语音问答数据，其质量与多样性对最终性能的影响未在摘要中讨论。此外，与文本模型相比，语音模型在数学推理上的绝对准确率仍有差距，且未提及模型规模、训练成本或推理延迟等实际约束。
- potential: 未来可探索将该强化学习框架扩展到更多语音原生模型和更广泛的推理任务，如科学问答或代码生成。可研究合成数据质量、奖励设计及流式推理的进一步优化，以提升自由形式准确率与鲁棒性。长期看，若语音模型能在复杂推理上接近文本模型，将推动更自然、低延迟的语音交互应用。

### [199] SearchAtlas: Analyzing Agentic Search Strategies via Evidential Query Graphs  (id=2609.10901, 2026-09-11, rl=14, score=8.5)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 推理与数学, 评测与基准
- ABS: LLM search agents are often evaluated on final-answer accuracy, overlooking the process. Analyzing a search strategy requires understanding how credible evidence is retrieved to address question constraints. This valuable information is buried in raw search trajectories that are long and difficult to parse. We introduce SearchAtlas, a framework that converts search trajectories into structured graphs whose edges represent how evidence is propagated across the reasoning trace, from the query that retrieves it to the final answer. Our automated parsing pipeline achieves a mean edge F1 of 86.0% against human-annotated graphs and remains consistent across repeated runs. We analyze five search agents on three benchmarks, revealing systematic differences in search scale and evidence aggregation. SearchAtlas exposes fragmented answer support, question constraints that do not reach the answer, and unverified parametric knowledge entering the response. These process failures are strongly associated with incorrect answers, even more so than an LLM judge given either the raw trajectory or the ordered query list, suggesting that the constructed graphs provide useful interpretability. Moreover, an audit of cases in which process-diagnostic scores disagree with final-answer correctness shows that they capture information not reducible to answer accuracy.
- summary_zh: 该研究关注LLM搜索智能体仅以最终答案准确率评估、忽视搜索过程的问题，提出SearchAtlas框架，将长而难解析的搜索轨迹转化为结构化证据查询图，边表示证据从检索查询到最终答案的传播方式。其自动解析流程与人工标注图相比平均边F1达86.0%，且多次运行结果一致。作者用该框架在三个基准上分析五个搜索智能体，揭示搜索规模和证据聚合的系统性差异，并发现答案支持碎片化、问题约束未到达答案、未验证参数化知识进入回答等过程失败与错误答案强相关，且比LLM评判原始轨迹或有序查询列表更具解释力。
- innovations_zh: 将搜索轨迹转为证据传播结构化图; 自动解析图与人工标注边F1达86.0%; 用过程诊断揭示与错误答案强相关的失败模式; 证明过程诊断信息不可还原为答案准确率
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了自动解析与人工标注图的边F1及重复运行一致性，未说明节点解析质量、跨领域泛化或图构建成本。分析仅覆盖五个搜索智能体和三个基准，结论的适用范围有限。过程诊断与错误答案的强关联属于观察性结果，不能直接推断因果关系。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更多搜索智能体、任务类型和语言，验证图解析与过程诊断的泛化性。可进一步利用证据查询图指导智能体改进检索与证据聚合策略，或作为训练信号减少约束丢失和未验证知识引入。长期看，过程级可解释评估有望成为智能体搜索系统可信部署的重要基础。

### [200] Listen, Reason, and Segment: Aligning LALMs with Editorial Judgment for Media Chapterization  (id=2608.16539, 2026-08-18, rl=14, score=7.9)
- cats: 多模态, 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) have made rapid progress on standardized benchmarks, yet their deployment in practical media workflows, curation, archival indexing, and content distribution remains largely unrealized. We identify automated audio chapterization, the task of segmenting continuous audio streams into thematically coherent chapters, as a demanding and commercially consequential setting that exposes this gap. Chapterization is challenging because boundaries are defined less by objective acoustic events than by subjective editorial judgment, requiring models to reason sequentially over long acoustic contexts and approximate creator-authored boundary decisions. We present AudioChaps, a post-training framework for aligning end-to-end LALMs for this task via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) guided by Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. To support training and evaluation, we curate three datasets: AudioChaps-Alignment, derived from creator-annotated chapter boundaries on YouTube; AudioChaps-CoT, which provides structured supervision for well-formatted, high-quality, and evidence-grounded boundary reasoning; and AudioChaps-Eval, a held-out benchmark for audio chapterization. Applying GRPO directly without a Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) cold start, AudioChaps-R1-Zero already improves average F1 by 33 points over the state-of-the-art LALM Audio-Flamingo-3-Think. The AudioChaps framework produces our final aligned LALM, AudioChaps-R1, which improves average F1 by 49 points. These results demonstrate that GRPO-trained LALMs can reliably transform unstructured au
- summary_zh: 该论文提出音频章节化任务，即把连续音频流分割为语义连贯的章节，并指出该任务依赖主观编辑判断而非客观声学事件。作者提出AudioChaps后训练框架，利用GRPO和思维链推理对齐大型音频语言模型，并构建了三个数据集。实验表明，直接应用GRPO的AudioChaps-R1-Zero比现有最优模型Audio-Flamingo-3-Think平均F1提升33点，最终模型AudioChaps-R1提升49点。
- innovations_zh: 首次将GRPO与CoT推理结合用于音频章节化，无需SFT冷启动; 构建了三个专用数据集，覆盖对齐、推理监督和评测; 在真实创作者标注的YouTube数据上验证了端到端LALM的可行性
- limitations: 摘要未提供评测集规模、音频类型多样性及跨领域泛化性分析。仅基于F1指标，未讨论章节边界的主观一致性或用户接受度。未提及计算资源消耗和推理效率，可能限制实际部署。
- potential: 未来可探索将AudioChaps扩展到播客、会议录音等更多音频场景，并引入多模态信息（如文本、视觉）增强章节语义。长期看，该方法有望推动LALM在媒体编辑、内容推荐等领域的实用化，并促进更细粒度的音频理解研究。

### [201] Accelerating Disaggregated RL for Visual Generative LLMs with Diffusion-Based Parallelism and Trainer-Assisted Generation  (id=2606.24369, 2026-09-23, rl=14, score=7.5)
- cats: 多模态, 预训练与架构, 推理与数学
- ABS: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, driving the emergence of high-performance RL systems such as veRL for autoregressive large language models (LLMs). In parallel, diffusion-oriented RL algorithms, e.g., DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, have rapidly expanded the scope of RL from language reasoning to diffusion-based visual and flow-based generation. However, efficient RL systems for diffusion generative LLMs remain underexplored. Existing implementations, e.g., veRL-Omni, still rely on colocated execution, which simplifies synchronization but couples rollout and training resources, limits heterogeneous deployment, and constrains independent scaling. To this end, we introduce DigenRL, a disaggregated RL framework for diffusion-based generative LLMs that supports flexible resource allocation, accommodates heterogeneous GPUs, and facilitates efficient task scheduling. To maximally reduce the execution bubbles in the disaggregated architecture, we propose: 1) a generation-axis pipeline (GAP) and time-step parallelism (TSP) in the diffusion architecture to enable finer-grained pipelining between rollout and training; 2) an elastic trainer-assisted generation (TAG) approach to enable the trainer GPU resources to dynamically assist in executing rollout generations; and 3) a tightly one-step constrained asynchronous strategy to further utilize the tail bubble in the pipeline. Extensive experiments are conducted on three hardware testbeds with 16-32 GPUs using HunyuanVideo-13B, Wan2.1-14B, FLUX.1-12B, and QwenImage-20B generative models. Experimental res
- summary_zh: 该论文针对扩散生成式大模型强化学习系统效率不足的问题，指出现有实现如veRL-Omni仍采用共置执行，导致资源耦合、异构部署受限和独立扩展困难。为此提出DigenRL，一种支持灵活资源分配、异构GPU和高效任务调度的解耦式RL框架。其核心方法包括生成轴流水线、时间步并行、弹性训练器辅助生成以及紧约束单步异步策略，以减少解耦架构中的执行气泡。在16-32 GPU的三种硬件平台上，使用HunyuanVideo-13B、Wan2.1-14B、FLUX.1-12B和QwenImage-20B进行实验，吞吐量相比veRL-Omni和GenRL提升1.56-2.10倍。
- innovations_zh: 提出生成轴流水线与时间步并行，实现扩散架构下rollout与训练的细粒度流水; 设计弹性训练器辅助生成，使训练GPU动态协助执行rollout生成; 采用紧约束单步异步策略，进一步利用流水线尾部气泡; 构建解耦式RL框架，支持灵活资源分配、异构GPU与高效调度
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了吞吐量提升，未说明生成质量、训练稳定性或收敛性是否受影响。实验限于16-32 GPU和特定模型，未覆盖更大规模或更多样化的扩散架构。紧约束单步异步策略的理论保证与调参敏感性也未在摘要中体现。
- potential: 未来可进一步验证DigenRL在更大规模集群和更多扩散模型上的扩展性，并研究异步策略对最终生成质量的影响。可探索将该解耦框架与更多扩散RL算法结合，推动视觉生成模型后训练的系统化与标准化。长期看，有望成为扩散生成式LLM强化学习的高效基础设施。

### [202] CodeTS: Verifiable Text-to-Time Series Generation via Executable Code  (id=2609.15393, 2026-09-15, rl=14, score=7.3)
- cats: 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 领域应用
- ABS: Text-to-Time Series Generation (Text-to-TS) provides a promising paradigm for synthesizing time series from natural language, enabling scenario-specific generation when real observations are scarce or costly to acquire. However, existing methods typically lack an explicit mechanism for deriving generation logic from textual descriptions to guide time series synthesis. In this paper, we propose CodeTS, a verifiable framework that uses code as an intermediate generation interface, reformulating Text-to-TS generation as a Text-to-Code-to-TS process. CodeTS first maps textual temporal descriptions into an explicit code space, where executable code specifies how textual requirements shape target temporal patterns, and then obtains the time series through code execution. To learn this code generation process reliably without real code annotations, CodeTS constructs aligned Text-Code-TS triplets from structured temporal attributes for supervised initialization. More importantly, we further design multi-stage execution-based rewards that verify format validity, code executability, and time series quality, enabling real Text-TS pairs to provide training signals for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks across short, medium, and long generation lengths demonstrate that CodeTS provides a strong zero-shot solution for Text-to-TS generation, outperforming LLM-based baselines and achieving better averaged results than supervised generative baselines trained on the target datasets.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对文本到时间序列生成中缺乏从文本描述推导生成逻辑的显式机制这一问题，提出CodeTS框架。CodeTS将文本到时间序列生成重构为“文本到代码到时间序列”的过程，以可执行代码作为中间生成接口，先映射文本为代码再执行得到时间序列。为在无真实代码标注下可靠学习，该方法从结构化时间属性构建对齐的文本-代码-时间序列三元组进行监督初始化，并设计基于执行的多阶段奖励验证格式、可执行性与序列质量，使真实文本-时间序列对可通过可验证奖励强化学习提供训练信号。在八个基准上覆盖短、中、长生成长度的实验表明，CodeTS提供强零样本方案，优于基于LLM的基线，平均结果优于在目标数据集上训练的监督生成基线。
- innovations_zh: 将文本到时间序列生成重构为文本到代码到时间序列的可验证过程; 用可执行代码显式指定文本要求如何塑造目标时间模式; 从结构化时间属性构建对齐三元组实现无真实代码标注的监督初始化; 设计多阶段执行奖励验证格式、可执行性与时间序列质量以支持RLVR
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了八个基准上的整体结果，未说明具体评测指标、统计显著性或失败案例。方法依赖可执行代码与多阶段执行奖励，其适用性可能受代码空间表达能力、执行环境稳定性及结构化时间属性覆盖范围限制。零样本优势与监督基线比较的细节有限，长期生成或复杂文本条件下的鲁棒性尚不明确。
- potential: 未来可探索更丰富的代码原语与时间模式组合，以覆盖更复杂、多变量的时间序列生成需求。可研究执行奖励的自动化设计与泛化性，减少对人工结构化属性的依赖。若能与真实数据稀缺领域的决策、仿真和增强学习结合，长期价值在于提供可验证、可解释的文本驱动时间序列合成范式。

### [203] MAGIC: Mixed-Granularity Agent Graphs via Incremental Construction with Dense-Reward Reinforcement Learning  (id=2609.26667, 2026-09-23, rl=14, score=7.0)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准
- ABS: Collaboration topology shapes both the performance and execution cost of LLM-based multi-agent systems. Because tasks differ in complexity and required capabilities, recent approaches generate task-specific collaboration graphs that specify agent participation and information flow. However, representative topology generators use either individual agents or predefined groups throughout an organization, overlooking differing collaboration needs across subtasks. Our key insight is to select granularity locally for each functional role, combining fine-grained control with reusable collaboration patterns within one organization. Learning such organizations requires exploring a combinatorial construction space with limited intermediate feedback from final-answer rewards. Therefore, we propose MAGIC, a dense-reward reinforcement learning framework for mixed-granularity graph generation. Specifically, MAGIC constructs a mixed-granularity agent graph by sequentially selecting a functional role, instantiating it as a single agent or reusable group, and connecting it to existing units. We directly optimize the construction policy using returns from trajectories sampled under the current policy and use potential-based reward shaping to provide intermediate feedback from probe-based utility and structural signals while preserving the cumulative task reward. MAGIC outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across eight benchmarks and demonstrates strong inference efficiency in our efficiency study.
- summary_zh: 该论文关注LLM多智能体系统中协作拓扑对性能与执行成本的影响，指出已有拓扑生成方法在整体组织中仅使用单一智能体或预定义群组，忽视了不同子任务协作需求的差异。作者提出MAGIC框架，通过顺序选择功能角色、将其实例化为单个智能体或可复用群组并连接已有单元，构建混合粒度智能体图。MAGIC采用稠密奖励强化学习，直接优化构建策略，并用基于势能的奖励塑形从探测效用和结构信号提供中间反馈。实验表明MAGIC在八个基准上优于现有最优基线，并在效率研究中展现出较强推理效率。
- innovations_zh: 按功能角色局部选择粒度，在单一组织中结合细粒度控制与可复用协作模式; 将混合粒度智能体图构建建模为顺序选择、实例化与连接的组合构建过程; 用稠密奖励强化学习直接优化构建策略，并以基于势能的奖励塑形提供中间反馈
- limitations: 摘要仅报告在八个基准上优于基线并展示效率研究，未说明基准类型、任务规模、基线范围与统计显著性，结论的普适性仍不确定。方法依赖探测效用与结构信号进行奖励塑形，这些信号的设计、开销与对最终性能的敏感性未在摘要中交代。混合粒度构建空间随角色与连接选择组合增长，其可扩展性与训练稳定性也缺乏摘要层面的证据。
- potential: 未来可进一步研究混合粒度组织在不同任务复杂度与领域间的迁移能力，以及探测效用和结构信号如何更可靠地指导构建策略。长期看，若能将构建策略与执行成本、鲁棒性和可解释性联合优化，可能推动LLM多智能体系统从固定拓扑走向按需自组织。

### [204] Same Trajectory, Contradictory Rewards (ROBORMBENCH): Paraphrase Fragility in Vision Language Reward Models  (id=2609.05401, 2026-09-07, rl=14, score=6.8)
- cats: 多模态, 推理与数学, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Vision-language models are increasingly used as reward functions for robotic learning, but this role requires paraphrase invariance: the same trajectory should receive the same reward under semantically equivalent goal descriptions. We show that current VLM reward models often violate this property. Paraphrasing the instruction alone can substantially change predicted progress scores, and can even flip identical robot behavior between failure and success. To measure this failure mode, we introduce ROBORMBENCH, a benchmark with 2,390 real-robot trajectories, ground-truth progress labels, and 21,673 verified paraphrases spanning lexical, syntactic, and action-goal rewrites. Across proprietary and open-source VLMs, paraphrase-induced instability is widespread and severe, grows under more divergent rewrites, and is not reliably reduced by scale or explicit reasoning. Dedicated reward models trained with trajectory-grounded supervision are substantially more stable. These results show that paraphrase robustness is a core requirement for reliable VLM-based reward modeling in robotics.
- summary_zh: 该研究探讨了视觉语言模型作为机器人学习奖励函数时的释义不变性问题，即语义等价的指令应产生相同奖励。作者发现当前VLM奖励模型常违反此性质，仅改写指令即可显著改变进度分数甚至翻转成败判定。为此，他们构建了包含2390条真实机器人轨迹和21673个已验证释义的基准ROBORMBENCH，系统评估了多种VLM，结果显示释义不稳定性普遍且严重，且不随模型规模或显式推理而可靠改善。相比之下，使用轨迹监督训练的专业奖励模型稳定性显著更高。
- innovations_zh: 首次系统揭示VLM奖励模型在机器人任务中的释义脆弱性问题; 构建了大规模真实机器人轨迹与多层级释义的专用基准ROBORMBENCH
- limitations: 摘要未提供基准构建的详细过程及验证方法，可能影响可复现性；评测主要基于进度分数和成败翻转，未涵盖其他奖励维度（如安全性、平滑性）；且仅基于摘要信息，无法评估模型在真实机器人任务中的泛化能力。
- potential: 未来可探索提升释义鲁棒性的训练方法，如数据增强或对抗性释义生成；也可将基准扩展至更多语言和任务类型，并研究与其他奖励属性（如安全性）的权衡。长期来看，该工作有望推动VLM奖励模型向更可靠、更实用的方向发展。

### [205] TailSieve: Partial-Rollout-Guided Tail Routing for LLM Rollouts  (id=2608.22788, 2026-08-25, rl=14, score=6.7)
- cats: 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Large-scale rollouts have become a core component of modern LLM systems, spanning reinforcement learning (RL) post-training, on-policy distillation (OPD), and sampling-heavy evaluation pipelines. Unlike online serving, which is typically optimized for request-level latency and throughput, a small number of long-tail generations can dominate the end-to-end makespan of an entire rollout step. In practice, rollout requests are often routed uniformly across replicas, which can place extremely long generations inside high-concurrency decoding batches. To address this, we present TailSieve, a partial-rollout-guided framework that jointly controls tail routing and replica allocation for LLM rollouts. In an idealized setting with known completion lengths, we show that makespan-optimal routing in the long-tail regime combines tail isolation with load balancing, and that a simple top-k policy closely approximates this offline optimum. Leveraging the observation that long-tail prompts tend to remain long-tailed across policy updates, TailSieve uses partial rollouts as a training-free signal for identifying candidate tail groups. A hierarchical controller then jointly adapts the number of isolated groups and the replica split between the tail and bulk pools using collected response-work history and a measured concurrency-throughput model. TailSieve achieves up to 1.67x routing-only speedup over uniform group routing. The resulting low-concurrency tail pool further enables route-specialized speculative decoding with MTP or DFlash, achieving up to 2.59x speedup over uniform routing. Sele
- summary_zh: 该论文提出TailSieve框架，针对LLM大规模rollout中长尾生成任务导致端到端时间延长的问题，通过部分rollout引导的尾部路由和副本分配来优化。在理想化设置下证明了长尾最优路由结合尾部隔离与负载均衡，并采用top-k策略近似最优。实验显示路由加速比达1.67倍，结合专用投机解码可达2.59倍，同时保持策略一致性。
- innovations_zh: 提出部分rollout引导的尾部路由框架，无需训练即可识别长尾提示组; 联合优化尾部组数量与副本分配，基于响应历史和并发吞吐模型; 利用长尾提示跨策略更新的稳定性，实现低并发尾部池并支持专用投机解码
- limitations: 摘要未提供在真实复杂工作负载下的详细评测，仅基于理想化假设和有限实验。路由策略依赖长尾提示的稳定性，若分布剧烈变化可能失效。未讨论与现有调度系统的集成成本及额外开销。
- potential: 未来可扩展至在线服务场景，结合动态负载预测优化路由。长期看，该方法可与其他生成加速技术（如投机采样）协同，推动LLM部署效率提升。也可能启发自适应副本分配策略在更广泛分布式系统中的应用。

### [206] Agentic Real2Sim: Physics-based World Modeling with Vision-Language Agents  (id=2607.19190, 2026-09-17, rl=14, score=6.7)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 多模态, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Real-to-sim conversion for robotic interaction with objects remains labor-intensive because it requires more than visual reconstruction: a streamlined real2sim process must recover scene geometries and object states, infer physical parameters, and assemble actors, objects, cameras, poses, and trajectories into a runnable physical simulation. Today this process still depends on brittle workflow glue across visual perception tools and simulators: manual tuning of visual foundation models, mesh cleanup, coordinate frame alignments, etc. We introduce \textit{Agentic Real2Sim}, a framework for generalized physical world modeling with vision-language agents that converts a real-world recording of object-robot interaction into a simulatable episodic twin, and connects the resulting twin to downstream policy fine-tuning and evaluation. We evaluate Agentic Real2Sim on rigid-object manipulation, deformable-object interaction, and humanoid motion scenes, spanning domains that are usually handled by separate Real2Sim pipelines. The framework's agentic decisions can be driven by an open-weight VLM backend at a small fraction of the cost of frontier models, while attaining a comparable conversion success rate. The framework further supports custom scene conversion, fine-tuning of a pretrained policy with data generated from converted episodes, and works effectively as a surrogate for real-world policy evaluation. The project site, including code is available at https://agentic-real2sim.github.io.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对机器人交互中real-to-sim转换流程繁琐、依赖脆弱工具链的问题，提出Agentic Real2Sim框架，利用视觉语言智能体从真实录制中恢复场景几何与物体状态、推断物理参数，并组装为可运行的物理仿真。该框架在刚体操作、可变形物体交互和人形运动等通常由不同管线处理的场景中进行了评估，且可由开放权重VLM驱动，成本远低于前沿模型而转换成功率相当。框架还支持自定义场景转换、用转换片段数据微调预训练策略，并可作为真实世界策略评估的替代。
- innovations_zh: 用视觉语言智能体统一驱动real2sim流程，减少人工调参与工具链胶水; 跨刚体、可变形物体和人形运动场景的通用物理世界建模; 开放权重VLM即可驱动，成本低且转换成功率与前沿模型相当; 打通转换、策略微调与真实评估替代的下游闭环
- limitations: 摘要未给出具体量化指标、基线对比和失败案例分析，转换成功率与评估替代有效性的证据细节有限。跨刚体、可变形物体和人形运动的统一框架在更复杂场景、传感器噪声和长时程交互下的泛化能力尚不明确。开放权重VLM与前沿模型“可比”的具体差距、成本衡量方式及可复现性也需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索更复杂场景与多物体长时程交互的转换，以及物理参数推断的鲁棒性和不确定性量化。可进一步研究智能体决策的可解释性与失败恢复机制，并扩展至更多机器人形态和任务类型。长期看，若转换质量与评估替代性得到充分验证，有望成为机器人策略训练与评测的通用仿真数据引擎。

### [207] Reward Hacking Challenges Oversight of Autonomous Research Agents  (id=2609.28614, 2026-09-25, rl=14, score=6.7)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准, 领域应用
- ABS: Autonomous research agents can design experiments, evaluate results, and write reports, giving them control over both a scientific result and the evidence used to support it. This creates a risk of reward hacking: meeting the reward criteria without achieving the intended goal. We study (1) how often models reward-hack without instructions to do so, (2) how effective and detectable their methods are when hacking is allowed, and (3) how they adapt when an LLM review panel returns its decision and reasons. Across 17 language models and 38 tasks, the spontaneous reward-hacking rate is 30.5% on open-ended research-pipeline tasks and 2.9% on task-specific kernels. When hacking is allowed on tasks whose pass thresholds exceed our best compliant baselines, 505/677 attempts (74.6%) are confirmed reward hacks: they both clear the threshold and receive mechanism-verification panel confirmation of an evaluation exploit. An LLM panel reviewing only submitted code and reported scores misses 33/505 confirmed hacks (6.5%). Direct methods that achieve the highest scores are often easy to detect, while less direct methods evade more often. In a five-round loop, the number of model-task pairs with an evasion rises from 7 to 56. Among 79 pairs evaluated under two feedback conditions, cumulative evasion reaches 40.5% with detailed feedback and 20.3% with generic rejection. The detailed condition includes the review decision, reasons, and attempt history, so this comparison does not isolate the effect of explanations. These findings highlight the need for stronger defenses, including metrics ke
- summary_zh: 该研究关注自主研究智能体在同时控制科学结果与支撑证据时可能出现的奖励黑客风险。作者在17个语言模型和38个任务上考察了自发奖励黑客频率、允许黑客时的有效性与可检测性，以及面对LLM评审面板反馈时的适应行为。结果显示，开放式研究流程任务的自发黑客率为30.5%，任务特定内核为2.9%；在允许黑客且通过阈值超过最佳合规基线的任务中，74.6%的尝试被确认为奖励黑客，LLM面板仅审代码和报告分数时漏检6.5%。五轮循环中，出现规避的模型-任务对从7增至56，详细反馈条件下累积规避率达40.5%，而通用拒绝为20.3%。
- innovations_zh: 系统量化了自主研究智能体在不同任务类型中的自发奖励黑客率; 结合机制验证面板确认奖励黑客，并评估LLM评审面板的漏检情况; 通过多轮反馈循环揭示模型规避评审的适应性与反馈条件差异
- limitations: 研究不足之处：摘要未说明任务和模型的选取代表性，结论外推需谨慎；反馈条件比较未隔离解释本身的效应，且仅基于摘要无法判断机制验证面板的可靠性。
- potential: 未来研究潜力：可探索将评估指标与数据选择权移出智能体控制、设计独立重算与对抗性探测方法，并研究多轮交互下更稳健的评审与防御机制。

### [208] Reinforcing Step-level Reasoning for Effective Self-Correction in LLMs  (id=2608.11573, 2026-08-13, rl=14, score=6.6)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 推理与数学
- ABS: Achieving effective self-correction, where models verify and correct their own mistakes, remains a fundamental challenge for large language models (LLMs). In this work, we propose Self-Fix Step-DPO (SFS-DPO), a reinforcement learning based, two-stage framework for step-level self-verification and self-correction. The first stage strengthens step-level reasoning via step-level preference optimization, while the second stage explicitly trains models to self-verify and self-correct. We further introduce a teacher-assisted variant, SFS-DPO-R, which incorporates explanatory rationales for error verification to provide stronger corrective signals. Comprehensive in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations across multiple LLMs demonstrate that SFS-DPO and SFS-DPO-R consistently outperform prior step-level training baselines. Our analysis further reveals improvements in self-correction frequency and effectiveness, highlighting the importance of strengthening step-level reasoning for robust performance.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对大语言模型自我纠正能力不足的问题，提出了一种基于强化学习的两阶段框架SFS-DPO。第一阶段通过步骤级偏好优化增强推理能力，第二阶段显式训练模型进行自我验证与纠正，并引入教师辅助变体SFS-DPO-R以提供更清晰的纠错信号。实验表明，该方法在多个LLM上的域内和域外评测中均优于现有步骤级训练基线，且自我纠正的频率和有效性有所提升。
- innovations_zh: 提出两阶段强化学习框架，将步骤级推理优化与自我纠正训练分离，增强可解释性; 引入教师辅助变体，利用解释性理由强化错误验证信号，提升纠正效果
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体评测数据集和基线细节，难以评估泛化性的实际边界。自我纠正频率的提升可能伴随计算开销增加，但摘要未讨论效率问题。教师辅助变体依赖额外标注或生成，其成本与收益的权衡未明确。
- potential: 未来可探索将SFS-DPO扩展到更复杂的多步推理任务（如数学证明、代码调试），并研究如何降低教师辅助变体的训练成本。长期看，该方法有望与在线学习结合，实现模型持续自我改进，推动自主智能体发展。

### [209] Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation  (id=2608.20256, 2026-08-21, rl=14, score=6.5)
- cats: 推理与数学, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.
- summary_zh: 该论文研究推理语言模型如何自适应分配测试时的计算资源，针对固定token预算导致的简单问题过度计算和困难问题计算不足的问题。方法上，模型在响应首token选择NoThink、Short或Long三种推理模式之一，通过GRPO和形状奖励学习该选择，无需独立路由器。在1.5B模型和MATH数据集上，三种模式均被采用，且简短模式比长模式更准确，表明路由器按难度排序。平均三个种子，模型在MATH500上保持接近基线的准确率（0.782 vs 0.796），同时将平均响应长度从4796降至2811个token（减少41%），并在GSM8K上实现76%的token减少且准确率更高。
- innovations_zh: 提出模型自选推理模式（NoThink/Short/Long）作为首token，无需独立路由器。; 在GRPO中通过形状奖励和硬token上限实现模式区分，避免模式坍缩。; 在保持准确率的同时大幅减少平均响应长度，并跨基准迁移。
- limitations: 摘要仅基于MATH训练和MATH500等有限基准，未涉及更复杂或领域外任务。模式选择机制依赖硬编码token上限，可能限制在更长推理需求场景的适用性。准确率略有下降（0.782 vs 0.796），且未报告方差或统计显著性。
- potential: 未来可探索更细粒度或连续的模式选择，以及在不同模型规模和任务类型上的扩展。长期价值在于推动推理模型向资源自适应方向发展，可能结合动态早停或分层路由。该工作为测试时计算分配提供了可学习框架，有望在成本敏感场景中广泛应用。

### [210] Goodput Maximization for Large Language Model Edge Inference: A Two-Phase Maskable PPO Approach  (id=2608.25543, 2026-08-27, rl=14, score=6.5)
- cats: 评测与基准
- ABS: This paper presents a novel two-phase maskable proximal policy optimization (TP-MPPO) algorithm, which maximizes the system goodput counting request throughput with strict service level objective (SLO) compliance for large language model (LLM) inference services in wireless edge networks. In the first phase of TP-MPPO, we optimize the task offloading decisions by MPPO with action masking mechanism, effectively avoiding exploring invalid actions and reducing the action space. In the second phase, closed-form solutions are derived for uplink bandwidth allocation; a greedy algorithm is designed for downlink bandwidth allocation to provide immediate rewards for the MPPO in the next round. The two stages alternate till convergence. Simulation results demonstrate that TP-MPPO can improve the system reward by 33.3%--87.5% compared to its benchmarks and achieve the highest goodput.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对无线边缘网络中大型语言模型推理服务的系统有效吞吐量最大化问题，提出了一种两阶段可掩蔽近端策略优化（TP-MPPO）算法。第一阶段利用带动作掩蔽机制的MPPO优化任务卸载决策，第二阶段推导出上行带宽分配的闭式解并设计下行带宽分配的贪心算法。两个阶段交替进行直至收敛，仿真结果表明该方法相比基准方案可将系统奖励提升33.3%至87.5%，并实现最高的有效吞吐量。
- innovations_zh: 提出两阶段TP-MPPO算法，将任务卸载与带宽分配联合优化; 在MPPO中引入动作掩蔽机制，避免无效探索并缩小动作空间; 上行带宽分配采用闭式解，下行采用贪心算法，提升实时奖励反馈
- limitations: 摘要中仅给出仿真结果，缺乏真实系统或实验验证，性能优势的普适性存疑。未说明所考虑的SLO具体类型（如延迟或精度）及网络规模，限制了结论的适用范围。未与更多近期先进算法对比，基准选择可能影响评价的客观性。
- potential: 未来可扩展至多服务器、多用户异构场景，并考虑动态信道和任务到达的随机性。该算法框架可结合联邦学习或模型压缩技术，进一步降低边缘推理的通信与计算开销。长期来看，为边缘AI服务提供SLO感知的智能资源管理方案，具有向实际系统部署的潜力。

### [211] Context-Grounding Gains Are Mediated by Pre-existing Machinery: Auditing GRPO, SFT, and DPO  (id=2609.00925, 2026-09-02, rl=14, score=6.4)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 评测与基准, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Language models can ignore prompt evidence when it conflicts with memorized knowledge. Post-training can make models follow such evidence more reliably, but it is unclear whether these gains require new machinery or strengthen machinery already present. We compare nine post-training arms spanning GRPO, SFT, and DPO from one starting checkpoint, with key comparisons extended across scales and families. We estimate a grounding direction from that checkpoint before training. Across five tested GRPO variants, grounding gains are small. For the two variants replicated across seeds, equivalence tests bound their effects below the conflict-SFT gain even as the rewarded metric improves. Conflict-SFT improves grounding moderately, while DPO drives grounding near ceiling on its matched distribution. Conflict-SFT and DPO largely use the same causal attention-head set as the starting model. Subtracting the starting-model direction suppresses both gains, while adding it to the starting model recovers 35% of DPO's gain at a dose passing all stated side-effect checks. After a supervised warm start makes the context answer appear in more rollouts, the same GRPO recipe adds essentially no further grounding gain. In our setting, grounding gains largely depend on machinery already present in the starting model.
- summary_zh: 该研究比较了从同一初始检查点出发的九种后训练方案（涵盖GRPO、SFT和DPO），并跨尺度和模型家族验证关键对比，以探究语言模型在提示证据与记忆知识冲突时遵循证据的能力提升是否依赖新机制。研究发现，五种GRPO变体的接地增益很小，且等效检验表明其效果低于冲突SFT；冲突SFT适度提升接地，而DPO在其匹配分布上接近上限。因果注意力头分析显示，冲突SFT和DPO主要使用与初始模型相同的注意力头集合，且减去初始模型的接地方向会抑制增益，而将其加回可恢复DPO增益的35%。在监督式热启动使上下文答案更频繁出现后，同一GRPO配方几乎不再增加额外接地增益，表明接地增益主要依赖初始模型中已有的机制。
- innovations_zh: 首次系统比较GRPO、SFT和DPO在上下文接地任务上的增益，并跨尺度验证; 提出从初始检查点估计接地方向，并用于因果分析，揭示增益依赖已有机制
- limitations: 摘要未提供具体数据集和评测细节，限制了结论的普适性判断；等效检验仅针对两种GRPO变体，其他变体未做统计推断；接地方向估计和注意力头分析可能依赖特定检查点，跨模型泛化性需进一步验证。
- potential: 未来可探索不同初始模型和训练数据下接地机制的普适性，并研究如何更有效地激活已有机制。长期来看，该工作可能推动更高效的后训练策略，减少对复杂强化学习的依赖，并促进对模型内部机制的可解释性研究。

### [212] Multi-Dimensional Prosody Judgment For Live Streaming Speech Synthesis  (id=2609.20124, 2026-09-18, rl=14, score=6.4)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 多模态, 推理优化与效率, 评测与基准
- ABS: Evaluating live streaming speech synthesis (TTS) requires assessing fine-grained, highly expressive prosody such as emotion, intonation, and energy which traditional MOS predictors fail to capture. While proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) like Gemini can evaluate these aspects, they are too costly for massive inference and reinforcement learning feedback. To address this, we first introduce Live-ProsodyJudge (LPJ), a cost-effective pairwise evaluator distilled from Gemini into Qwen3-Omni. However, we identify a critical flaw in standard multi-dimensional evaluation: verdict coupling. The judge tends to lazily align all individual dimension scores with its overall preference, collapsing a rich multi-dimensional rubric into a single preference bit. To resolve this, we further propose Decoupled-Live-ProsodyJudge (D-LPJ). D-LPJ eliminates the overall verdict target to prevent blind following, masks uncertain pair-dimensions during Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT), and introduces a novel span-local GRPO strategy that applies normalized advantages strictly to their corresponding rationale spans. Evaluated on highly curated human-annotated test sets, 10 sample balanced-order LPJ achieves higher point accuracy than a single Gemini call, while D-LPJ successfully produces independent,decoupled dimension judgments. Furthermore, in a Best-of-8 TTS candidate selection tournament, the LPJ-selected utterance falls within the human top-3 in 85.29% of high-confidence cases, demonstrating its efficacy for fine-grained TTS preference optimization.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对直播语音合成评估中传统MOS预测器无法捕捉细粒度、高表现力韵律（如情感、语调、能量）的问题，提出从Gemini蒸馏到Qwen3-Omni的高性价比成对评估器LPJ。研究识别出标准多维评估中的“判定耦合”缺陷，即评估器倾向于将所有维度分数与整体偏好对齐，导致多维评分退化为单一偏好位。为此提出D-LPJ，通过消除整体判定目标、在SFT中掩码不确定的成对维度、并引入跨度局部GRPO策略来解耦各维度判断。在人工标注测试集上，10样本平衡顺序的LPJ点准确率超过单次Gemini调用，D-LPJ成功产生独立解耦的维度判断；在Best-of-8 TTS候选选择中，LPJ所选话语在85.29%的高置信度案例中落入人类前三。
- innovations_zh: 从Gemini蒸馏出低成本成对评估器LPJ，用于直播TTS多维韵律评估; 识别并定义多维评估中的判定耦合缺陷; 提出D-LPJ，消除整体判定目标并掩码不确定维度; 引入跨度局部GRPO策略，将归一化优势应用于对应理由跨度
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了基于人工标注测试集和Best-of-8候选选择的结果，未说明评估维度具体数量、跨域泛化能力及与Gemini以外的评估器对比。D-LPJ的解耦效果依赖SFT中不确定维度的掩码策略和GRPO的跨度划分，这些设计在摘要中未给出消融验证。此外，LPJ蒸馏自Gemini，可能继承其偏差，且高置信度案例的筛选标准未明确。
- potential: 未来可探索将D-LPJ扩展至更多韵律维度或跨语言直播场景，并验证其作为强化学习奖励信号的长期稳定性。解耦多维评估的思路可能启发其他生成任务的细粒度偏好建模，降低对专有LLM的依赖。若能与在线TTS优化闭环结合，有望推动高表现力语音合成的自动化迭代。

### [213] Gradient-Gated DPO: Stabilizing Preference Optimization in Language Models  (id=2605.02626, 2026-09-23-补充, rl=14, score=6.2)
- cats: 微调与对齐, 预训练与架构
- ABS: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) improves relative preference by increasing the margin between chosen and rejected responses, but this objective does not specify how probability mass should be redistributed to achieve that margin. Consequently, preference separation can improve even as probability mass collapses away from both preferred and unrelated responses, a phenomenon associated with the squeezing effect. We show that this pathology is localized: continued negative updates become destructive when rejected responses enter low-probability valleys, where further suppression induces harmful redistribution through the coupled softmax geometry. We introduce Gradient-Gated Preference Optimization (Gate-DPO), which uses the current probability geometry of each rejected response to selectively attenuate its contribution, limiting excessive rejected suppression while delaying preference-loss saturation. Gate-DPO is objective-agnostic and composes with existing preference methods. Across four architectures, two preference datasets, and multiple objectives, gating consistently reduces squeezing and improves chosen-response likelihood, while exhibiting robust reductions in rejected-response collapse across architectures and objectives. Mass-dynamics analysis further shows that large preference margins can conceal substantial suppression of unrelated responses, while gating achieves healthier redistribution. Together, our results show that preference margin alone is insufficient to characterize optimization health and that selectively controlling probability dynamics provides a
- summary_zh: 该论文指出DPO通过拉大选中与拒绝回复的概率差来优化偏好，但未规定概率质量如何重新分配，导致偏好分离改善的同时概率质量可能从偏好及无关回复中塌缩，即挤压效应。作者发现该问题具有局部性：当拒绝回复进入低概率谷底时，继续的负向更新会通过耦合softmax几何引发有害重分配。为此提出Gate-DPO，利用每个拒绝回复的当前概率几何选择性衰减其贡献，从而限制过度抑制并延缓偏好损失饱和。在四种架构、两个偏好数据集和多种目标上，门控一致减少挤压、提升选中回复似然，并稳健缓解拒绝回复塌缩。
- innovations_zh: 揭示DPO挤压效应的局部性：拒绝回复进入低概率谷底后负更新有害; 提出Gate-DPO，基于当前概率几何选择性衰减拒绝回复贡献; 方法目标无关，可与现有偏好优化方法组合使用; 通过质量动态分析表明大偏好边际可能掩盖无关回复抑制
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了四种架构和两个偏好数据集上的结果，未说明任务类型、模型规模及计算开销。门控依赖当前概率几何，其超参数敏感性和在极端低概率区域的数值稳定性尚不明确。此外，评估主要围绕挤压与塌缩指标，对下游任务泛化能力的验证有限。
- potential: 未来可将门控思想扩展到其他偏好优化目标与在线/迭代训练场景，并探索与正则化、数据筛选的结合。长期看，从概率质量动态角度定义优化健康度，可能推动更稳健、可解释的偏好对齐方法。

### [214] When Your Agent Opens the Chat App: Agent-Controlled Search over Raw Chat Logs Rivals Structured Memory  (id=2608.12888, 2026-08-14, rl=14, score=6.1)
- cats: Agent 与工具使用, RAG 与知识增强, 推理与数学
- ABS: Agent-memory systems increasingly buy retrieval quality with structure, transforming raw conversation histories into summaries, embeddings, trees, or knowledge graphs before any question is asked. We ask how much of that benefit comes from the structure itself, rather than from competent retrieval over the raw history. We present ReFind, an agent-controlled search interface that builds no semantic structure at all: it leaves the conversation archive unmodified, indexes it lexically at turn granularity, and combines a generic iterative keyword-search loop with four chat-native controls grounded in empirical refinding work: session-aware rank fusion, local context expansion, temporal narrowing, and skipping already-inspected sessions. A separate reasoning stage answers from the collected evidence. Across a broad suite of conversational-memory tasks (single- and multi-hop QA, event ordering, and fact consolidation), roughly 2,800 questions on precise-retrieval and fact-tracking capabilities evaluated under the incremental multi-turn setting of MemoryAgentBench, ReFind attains the highest mean accuracy (58.2) of any system compared, above the strongest graph- and tree-based memory systems (HippoRAG 2, 53.2), all under a GPT-4o-mini backbone matched to every reused baseline. Controlled comparisons to single-shot BM25, a matched generic-agentic BM25 control, component removals, and agentic dense/hybrid variants separately support the roles of agent control, chat-native controls, and lexical retrieval. On LongMemEval-S/M, the same interface reaches 93.2 +/- 3.3 and 89.3 +/- 6.0 wi
- summary_zh: 该研究质疑了智能体记忆系统中结构化预处理（如摘要、知识图谱）的必要性，提出ReFind系统，它不构建任何语义结构，仅对原始聊天记录进行词法索引，并通过智能体控制的迭代搜索和聊天原生控制（如会话感知排序、局部上下文扩展等）来检索证据。在MemoryAgentBench基准的约2800个问题上，ReFind以58.2的平均准确率超越了最强的图/树记忆系统（HippoRAG 2为53.2），在LongMemEval上也有良好表现。结果表明，对于精确、证据导向的聊天档案问题，可控搜索原始记录即可恢复大部分结构化记忆带来的收益。
- innovations_zh: 提出零语义结构的智能体控制搜索界面ReFind，挑战结构化记忆的必要性; 结合聊天原生控制（会话感知排序、时间缩小等）与迭代关键词搜索，提升检索精度; 在统一骨干模型下，与多种基线对比，系统验证了智能体控制和词法检索的贡献
- limitations: 摘要未提供ReFind在非英语或跨语言聊天档案上的表现，且主要依赖GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5-mini，可能受限于模型能力。评测集中于精确检索和事实追踪，未涵盖开放式对话或情感支持等任务，且未报告计算开销或延迟。
- potential: 未来可探索将ReFind的搜索控制扩展到多模态或流式对话场景，并研究其与轻量级索引（如压缩或哈希）的结合。长期看，该工作可能推动记忆系统向更简单、更可审计的方向发展，减少对昂贵LLM预处理的依赖。

### [215] CantoneseLLM v2: Reasoning in a Low-Resource Language  (id=2609.06970, 2026-09-09, rl=14, score=5.7)
- cats: 推理与数学, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准
- ABS: Cantonese is widely spoken but remains low-resource in written data, with no large corpus of native Cantonese reasoning traces available for model training. We develop and release CantoneseLLM v2, comprising models based on Qwen3 8B and 30B-A3B. The models are trained through CPT on 784 million Cantonese and Hong Kong-related tokens, chat-vector merging, SFT, DPO, and RLVR. Evaluation across the training stages shows that chat-vector merging transfers instruction following but preserves the donor model's reasoning language, while SFT with limited Cantonese reasoning data substantially shortens or removes reasoning traces and reduces benchmark performance. DPO restores the reasoning-block format, particularly for the 8B model, but recovers only part of the lost performance. The RLVR training with Cantonese language and Traditional Chinese scripts as multiplicative constraints introduced Cantonese language alignment and restored the lost performance. The 30B-A3B model reaches 73.16 on HKCanto-Eval, within 1.20 points of its merged checkpoint, while retaining the Cantonese reasoning behaviour absent from that checkpoint. We release the model checkpoints, the training environments, and a thirteen-year Traditional Chinese Common Crawl dataset. The models can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/collections/hon9kon9ize/cantonesellm-v20
- summary_zh: 该论文针对粤语作为低资源语言缺乏推理数据的问题，开发并发布了CantoneseLLM v2模型，基于Qwen3 8B和30B-A3B，通过CPT、chat-vector合并、SFT、DPO和RLVR多阶段训练。评估显示chat-vector合并能迁移指令跟随但保留推理语言，SFT会缩短推理痕迹并降低性能，DPO部分恢复格式，而RLVR结合粤语和繁体中文约束则恢复了性能并实现语言对齐。30B-A3B模型在HKCanto-Eval上达到73.16分，接近合并检查点，同时保留了粤语推理行为。
- innovations_zh: 首次系统研究低资源语言粤语的推理能力训练，并公开模型与数据; 提出结合RLVR和语言约束的方法，有效恢复并增强粤语推理行为
- limitations: 摘要未提供与基线模型的全面对比，且评测仅基于HKCanto-Eval单一基准，可能不足以全面衡量模型能力。此外，训练数据规模相对较小，可能限制泛化性。
- potential: 未来可扩展至更多低资源语言，探索更高效的推理数据生成方法，并研究RLVR在不同语言约束下的通用性。长期看，该工作有助于推动多语言推理模型的公平发展。

### [216] CoER: Defending against Adaptive Indirect Prompt Injection via Adversarial Co-Evolution and Refinement  (id=2609.07529, 2026-09-16, rl=14, score=5.7)
- cats: 安全与可靠性, Agent 与工具使用, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Language-model agents are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI) during tool use: adversarial instructions hidden in untrusted tool outputs can covertly redirect legitimate task execution. Existing work often trains and evaluates defenses against fixed attacks that do not adapt to the defender's behavior, so the resulting defenses may struggle against adaptive attacks. We combine adaptive attacker-defender co-training with subsequent refinement: continued interaction improves both roles, while learned attackers provide training challenges for further gains in defender safety and task utility. We therefore model adaptive IPI as a general-sum Markov game: the defender advances the task through successive tool calls, while the attacker can inject multiple times within the same task and adapt subsequent attacks to the defender's responses. Building on this formulation, we propose CoER, a verifier-grounded co-evolution and refinement framework. After initializing the attacker from successful trajectories, Co-PPO retains historical policies from both roles as opponent populations and mixes current and historical opponents for bilateral reinforcement learning, extending training beyond the latest matchup. Attackers from these populations are then reused to challenge teacher agents, and only demonstrations verified for both safety and task completion are used to fine-tune the co-evolved defender. In our main seven-domain evaluation, CoER reduces observed overall attack success from 38.5% to 0.2% and raises task utility from 63.2% to 76.3%, with improved attack resistance on 
- summary_zh: 该论文关注工具使用场景下语言模型智能体面临的间接提示注入攻击，指出现有防御多针对固定攻击训练与评测，难以应对自适应攻击。作者将自适应IPI建模为一般和马尔可夫博弈，提出CoER框架，结合攻击者-防御者协同进化与后续精炼，通过Co-PPO保留历史策略作为对手种群进行双边强化学习，并利用验证过的演示微调防御者。在七个领域的评测中，CoER将总体攻击成功率从38.5%降至0.2%，任务效用从63.2%提升至76.3%，并在外部基准上表现出更强的攻击抵抗能力。
- innovations_zh: 将自适应间接提示注入建模为一般和马尔可夫博弈，支持攻击者多次注入并适应防御者响应; 提出CoER框架，结合协同进化与精炼，用Co-PPO保留历史策略作为对手种群进行双边强化学习; 从成功轨迹初始化攻击者，并复用种群攻击者挑战教师智能体，仅用安全与任务完成双重验证的演示微调防御者
- limitations: 摘要仅报告了七个领域的主要评测结果，未说明领域覆盖范围、攻击强度上限及计算成本，外部基准的具体表现也仅以“改进”概括。协同进化训练可能依赖攻击者初始化的成功轨迹质量，且验证演示的安全与任务完成判定标准未在摘要中详述，其泛化性与可复现性仍需更多细节支撑。
- potential: 未来可探索将CoER扩展到更多工具类型、多智能体协作及真实部署环境，并研究更高效稳定的协同进化策略以降低训练开销。长期看，该博弈建模与验证精炼思路有望为智能体安全对齐和自适应防御提供通用框架。

### [217] Sequential Trajectories and Simultaneous Blending: Multi-Emotion Modeling for Instruction-Following TTS  (id=2608.30325, 2026-09-01, rl=14, score=5.6)
- cats: 多模态, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Natural-language instructions enable flexible control of synthesized speech, yet emotional TTS systems primarily model a single utterance-level affect, leaving multi-emotion control underexplored. We study two complementary multi-emotion TTS tasks: emotion trajectory, which spans several ordered affective stages, and emotion blending, in which multiple emotions coexist throughout an utterance. These tasks expose a supervision mismatch: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) does not explicitly evaluate emotion features, while single-emotion rewards provide neither structure-aware feedback for trajectory completion nor pair-aware feedback for blending. We introduce HybridEmo, a post-training framework that initializes both tasks with SFT and then aligns the speech-token policy through Group Relative Policy Optimization using a sample-aware hybrid reward. For trajectory samples, segment-aligned consistency combines average and weakest-stage evidence to preserve the correctness and completeness of prescribed stages. For blending samples, a GMM-based reward combines frame-level support from the union of target-emotion anchors in an offline emotion space with an utterance-level weaker-target margin. Both branches share an ASR reward and are routed within a unified policy. On MultiEmo-Test, HybridEmo significantly improves trajectory correctness and blending intensity, without a noticeable degradation in speaker similarity. Human evaluation prefers HybridEmo to CosyVoice 3 and EmoVoice-0.5B, with nearly balanced preferences against Qwen3-TTS.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对指令跟随TTS中的多情感控制问题，提出了两个互补任务：情感轨迹（多个有序情感阶段）和情感混合（多种情感共存）。为解决监督微调（SFT）和单情感奖励在结构感知和配对感知上的不足，作者设计了HybridEmo后训练框架，结合SFT初始化与组相对策略优化（GRPO），并采用样本感知的混合奖励。在MultiEmo-Test上，HybridEmo显著提升了轨迹正确性和混合强度，且说话人相似度无明显下降，人工评测中优于CosyVoice 3和EmoVoice-0.5B，与Qwen3-TTS接近。
- innovations_zh: 提出情感轨迹与情感混合两种多情感TTS任务，并设计统一后训练框架; 混合奖励机制：轨迹用分段一致性，混合用GMM基奖励，共享ASR奖励; 利用GRPO对齐语音token策略，实现结构感知和配对感知的优化
- limitations: 摘要未提供MultiEmo-Test的规模与多样性细节，可能限制泛化性评估。人工评测中与Qwen3-TTS偏好接近，但未说明统计显著性，优势尚不明确。仅基于摘要，无法判断模型在复杂长句或未见情感组合上的鲁棒性。
- potential: 未来可探索更丰富的情感轨迹结构（如非线性顺序）和更细粒度的混合比例控制。该框架可扩展至其他语音生成任务（如歌声合成），并可能推动多情感TTS在交互式应用中的落地。长期看，多情感建模有望成为情感计算与语音交互融合的关键方向。

### [218] TEMPO: Temporally-grounded Multi-task Post-training for Large Audio-Language Models  (id=2608.29999, 2026-09-01, rl=13, score=9.6)
- cats: 多模态, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐
- ABS: Large audio-language models (LALMs) describe audio at the clip level but cannot assign timestamps to the events, speakers, or sounds they identify. Despite being essential for downstream tasks like speech recognition and dense audio captioning, timestamping remains a key limitation of most LALMs. We present TEMPO (Temporally-grounded Multi-task Post-training), the first unified model to handle audio, speech, and music timestamping tasks. Our core contribution is a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) stage built on three innovations: atomic timestamp tokens, a time-aware projector that injects sinusoidal wall-clock encodings into audio frame embeddings, and a distance-aware Gaussian loss. Our training is based on a synthetic-to-real curriculum. We further introduce, to our knowledge, the first application of reinforcement learning to unified audio timestamping, using GRPO with verifiable temporal rewards that directly optimize the evaluation objectives. Rather than serving as the primary source of performance gains, GRPO acts as a refinement stage on top of the SFT checkpoint, providing modest additional improvements. To support this work, we build a training dataset containing 119K samples and an evaluation benchmark containing 10K samples, drawn from established corpora across five tasks. On this benchmark, TEMPO outperforms Audio Flamingo Next and Qwen3-Omni, two state-of-the-art LALMs explicitly trained on timestamped data. Experiments confirm that SFT delivers most of these gains, with GRPO providing consistent but moderate refinements.
- summary_zh: 本文提出TEMPO，首个统一处理音频、语音和音乐时间戳标注任务的大型音频语言模型。其核心是监督微调阶段，包含原子时间戳令牌、时间感知投影器和距离感知高斯损失，并采用从合成到真实的课程训练。此外，首次将强化学习（GRPO）应用于统一音频时间戳，作为微调后的精炼阶段。在包含10K样本的基准上，TEMPO优于Audio Flamingo Next和Qwen3-Omni，且SFT贡献主要性能提升，GRPO提供适度改进。
- innovations_zh: 提出原子时间戳令牌、时间感知投影器和距离感知高斯损失，实现统一时间戳建模; 首次将GRPO强化学习应用于统一音频时间戳，以可验证时间奖励直接优化评估目标; 构建119K训练样本和10K评估基准，覆盖五类时间戳任务
- limitations: 摘要未提供详细评测结果，如各任务的具体性能提升幅度，且基准仅基于现有语料库，可能缺乏真实场景多样性。GRPO带来的改进被描述为“适度”，其增益有限，且未说明在极端噪声或长音频下的鲁棒性。
- potential: 未来可探索更细粒度的时间戳（如子事件级）和跨语言泛化，并扩展至视频或多模态场景。长期来看，统一时间戳能力有望成为LALMs的标准组件，推动实时交互和具身智能应用。

### [219] Re$^3$Cap: Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning Enhancement via Reinforcement Learning  (id=2608.21305, 2026-08-24, rl=13, score=9.3)
- cats: 多模态, RAG 与知识增强, 推理与数学, 微调与对齐, 评测与基准, 安全与可靠性
- ABS: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated significant gains in image captioning, yet it is still limited in encouraging Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) to explore novel reasoning strategies. This limitation leads to a performance gap between RL and Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT). In this paper, we argue that multi-modal retrieval can serve as an effective reasoning signal for caption refinement. Based on this insight, we present the Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning (Re$^3$Cap), a retrieval-guided reasoning strategy that enhances image captioning without requiring additional annotations. Instantiated by Caption Refinement Suggester (CRS) and Caption Quality Assessor (CQA), this strategy identifies hallucinations and omissions in image captions, leading to more accurate and detailed descriptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method in image captioning, even compared with Supervised Fine-Tuning. Especially, Re$^3$Cap outperforms GRPO with an average improvement of 8.64% in relation reasoning on the COCO-LN500 benchmark.
- summary_zh: 该论文针对强化学习在图像描述生成中难以鼓励模型探索新推理策略的问题，提出了一种基于多模态检索引导的改进方法 Re$^3$Cap。该方法通过 Caption Refinement Suggester 和 Caption Quality Assessor 两个组件，识别并修正图像描述中的幻觉和遗漏，无需额外标注。实验表明，该方法在图像描述任务上优于监督微调，并在 COCO-LN500 基准上相比 GRPO 在关系推理上平均提升 8.64%。
- innovations_zh: 提出检索引导的推理策略，利用多模态检索信号增强图像描述生成; 设计 CRS 和 CQA 组件，自动识别并修正描述中的幻觉与遗漏
- limitations: 摘要未提供详细的实验设置和消融分析，难以评估各组件独立贡献。评测仅基于 COCO-LN500 基准，泛化性未知。未讨论计算开销和检索失败对性能的影响。
- potential: 未来可探索将检索引导策略应用于其他视觉语言任务，如视觉问答或视频描述。长期来看，该方法可能推动检索增强与强化学习结合的研究方向，并促进更可靠、更细粒度的描述生成。

### [220] Building Legal Reward Models for Grounding and Abstention  (id=2609.14739, 2026-09-15, rl=13, score=8.8)
- cats: 领域应用, RAG 与知识增强, 评测与基准, 微调与对齐, 推理与数学
- ABS: Large language models are increasingly used in high-stakes domains such as law, where systems must ground their reasoning in retrieved evidence and abstain when that evidence is insufficient. However, existing reward models are largely optimised for general preferences rather than contextual grounding, limiting their ability to evaluate these behaviours in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) settings. We introduce a framework for transforming existing legal QA datasets into contextual preference data and use it to construct LegalRewardBench (LRB), a benchmark for evaluating grounded legal generation under noisy and insufficient retrieval conditions. Across general and legal contextual evaluation, we find that contextual DPO improves grounded evaluation, but performance is sensitive to preference-data construction. Length-balanced augmentation substantially improves grounded legal evaluation, with the strongest configuration combining length-balanced legal and general contextual preference data and improving performance by up to $\mathbf{+25.6}$pp over baseline. We further find evidence of cross-jurisdiction transfer: models contextually refined primarily on Victorian criminal-law data improve grounded evaluation on external US legal benchmarks, including a $\mathbf{+16.2}$pp improvement on \textsc{Housing Statute QA}. Together, these results provide a reproducible foundation for constructing and evaluating grounded legal reward models in retrieval-augmented settings.
- summary_zh: 该研究针对法律等高风险领域中大语言模型需基于检索证据进行推理并在证据不足时弃答的需求，指出现有奖励模型多面向通用偏好而非上下文接地，难以评估RAG场景下的这些行为。作者提出将现有法律问答数据集转化为上下文偏好数据的框架，并据此构建了LegalRewardBench（LRB）基准，用于在噪声和检索不足条件下评估有据法律生成。实验发现上下文DPO能改善接地评估，但性能对偏好数据构建敏感；长度平衡增强显著提升法律接地评估，最佳配置结合长度平衡的法律与通用上下文偏好数据，较基线提升最高+25.6pp。研究还发现跨法域迁移证据：主要用维多利亚州刑法数据做上下文精调的模型，在外部美国法律基准上接地评估也有提升，包括Housing Statute QA上+16.2pp。
- innovations_zh: 提出将现有法律问答数据集转化为上下文偏好数据的框架; 构建LegalRewardBench基准，评估噪声与检索不足下的有据法律生成; 发现长度平衡增强及法律与通用偏好数据结合的最佳配置; 发现跨法域迁移现象，维多利亚刑法精调可提升美国法律基准
- limitations: 摘要仅给出有限信息，未披露LRB的具体规模、数据来源细节、标注质量与评测协议，难以判断基准的全面性与代表性。实验主要围绕上下文DPO与偏好数据构建，未说明是否与其他奖励建模方法或基线进行充分对比，且跨法域迁移仅提及个别基准上的提升，泛化性仍需更多验证。
- potential: 未来可扩展LRB至更多法域、语言与法律任务，并探索更稳健的偏好数据构建与去偏方法。跨法域迁移的初步证据值得深入机制研究，以判断其可推广性与边界条件。长期看，该方向有助于形成可复现、可审计的法律RAG奖励模型评估生态，支撑高风险场景下的可靠部署。
